A spectral energy budget shows that what aggregation removes from a band, no linear spectral filter can recover. Crowding alone says nothing about the benefit. That is decided by how much label energy the valley removes, which we measure for every graph. SpecValley achieves strong results across heterophilic benchmarks, and a controlled ablation over aggregation operators and downstream filters attributes the gain to aggregation rather than the filter.

</details>


### [102] [DailyWildfireCAN: A Dataset and Benchmark for Next-Day Wildfire Spread Prediction in Canada](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14207)
*Mahacine Ettahri,Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Daniel Aloise,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DailyWildfireCAN——面向加拿大次日野火蔓延预测的多模态、多时相数据集与基准，覆盖 2017–2024 八个火季共 2,440 起野火事件，90 米空间分辨率、24 小时时间分辨率，整合 33 个跨模态特征，并建立单时相与多时相模型对比及多维评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大拥有广袤北方森林，气候变暖使极端火季的频率与严重程度上升，野火带来严峻的生态与社会经济挑战。次日火蔓延预测有助于火情管理，但需刻画气象、可燃物与地形之间的复杂交互。深度学习是有前景的建模途径，但其发展依赖代表性数据；现有研究主要依赖以美国为中心的数据集，加拿大火环境严重缺乏代表性。

Method: 构建 DailyWildfireCAN 数据集：追踪多日火事件连续的过火进展，空间分辨率 90 m、时间分辨率 24 h，以保留火线几何、局部可燃物差异与地形效应等细尺度信息；覆盖 2017–2024 八个火季、2,440 起野火事件；整合 33 个特征，涵盖地形、气象、土地覆盖、植被与森林结构、人类影响指数及卫星影像等多模态数据。在此基础上建立综合基准，对比单时相与多时相预测模型，并提出多维评估框架。

Result: 提供加拿大全域次日火蔓延预测的标准数据集与基准；实验覆盖单时相与多时相两类预测模型；评估框架从野火特征、地理区域、时间条件三个维度的鲁棒性，空间预测质量，以及对参考标签变化的敏感性等方面进行多维考察。

Conclusion: DailyWildfireCAN 填补了加拿大野火环境在深度学习火蔓延预测研究中的数据空白，凭借高分辨率、多模态、多时相设计与系统化基准，为改进日尺度野火蔓延预报研究提供了数据与方法基础。

Abstract: Wildfires represent a critical ecological and socio-economic challenge in Canada, where vast boreal forests and a warming climate have amplified the frequency and severity of extreme fire seasons. Predicting next-day wildfire spread can support fire management, but requires capturing complex interactions among weather, fuels, and terrain. Deep learning has emerged as a promising approach to learning these relationships from heterogeneous environmental data, yet its development and evaluation depend on representative datasets. Existing deep learning research on wildfire spread relies predominantly on US-focused datasets, leaving Canadian fire environments underrepresented. To address this gap, we present DailyWildfireCAN, a multi-modal, multi-temporal dataset and benchmark for predicting next-day wildfire spread across Canada. The dataset tracks continuous fire progression over multi-day events at a high spatial and temporal resolution of 90-m and 24-hour, respectively. This resolution preserves fine-scale information relevant to fire-edge geometry, local fuel variations, and terrain effects, supporting research toward improved daily wildfire spread forecasting. DailyWildfireCAN covers 2,440 wildfire events across eight fire seasons 2017-2024 and integrates 33 features across diverse modalities, including topography, meteorology, land cover, vegetation and forest structure, human influence indices, and satellite imagery. We establish a comprehensive benchmark comparing mono-temporal and multi-temporal prediction models, accompanied by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing robustness across wildfire characteristics, geographic regions, and temporal conditions, spatial prediction quality, and sensitivity to reference label variations.

</details>


### [103] [ViGA-FLAME: Aligning Latent Visual and Geometric Representations for Robust 3D Head Expression Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14218)
*Wen Hao He,Jiacheng Wu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViGA-FLAME：两阶段框架，先用PG-VAE将规范头部表面与FLAME参数编码为统一潜空间，再用ViGA把预训练视觉编码器的语义特征映射进该潜空间，实现单图鲁棒3D头表情估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像估计3D头部表情需将视觉外观映射到面部几何。现有前馈方法在严重面部形变与大视角下鲁棒性不足，缺乏将语义视觉特征与结构化几何先验有效对齐的机制。

Method: 第一阶段：参数化-几何变分自编码器（PG-VAE）学习规范头部表面与FLAME参数的统一表示，通过几何与参数双重监督及两条解码分支间的一致性约束，连接表面细节与参数结构。第二阶段：可训练视觉-几何适配器（ViGA）将预训练语义视觉编码器特征映射至该潜空间，采用非对称注意力将图像语义特征对齐到PG-VAE潜空间中的结构化几何信息，且不修改原始视觉流。

Result: 将图像证据锚定于该潜先验后，ViGA-FLAME在严重面部形变与大视角条件下实现鲁棒的3D头部表情估计，以高效方式优于竞争性前馈方法。

Conclusion: 以结构化几何潜先验对齐语义视觉特征的两阶段设计，可有效提升单图3D头表情估计的鲁棒性，兼顾可解释参数恢复与细节表面重建。

Abstract: Estimating 3D head expression from a single image requires mapping visual appearance to underlying facial geometry. We propose ViGA-FLAME, a two-stage framework that aligns semantic visual features with a structured geometric latent space. First, a Parametric-Geometric Variational Autoencoder (PG-VAE) learns a unified representation of canonical head surfaces and FLAME parameters, supporting both detailed surface reconstruction and interpretable parameter recovery. Supervision on geometry and parameters, together with consistency between the two decoding branches, connects surface detail with parametric structure. Second, a trainable Visual-Geometric Adapter (ViGA) maps features from a pretrained semantic visual encoder into this latent space. Asymmetric attention enables ViGA to align semantic image features from the visual encoder with the structured geometric information represented in the PG-VAE latent space, without altering the original visual stream. By grounding image evidence in this latent prior, ViGA-FLAME enables robust 3D head expression estimation across severe facial deformations and large viewing angles, efficiently improving robustness over competing feed-forward methods.

</details>


### [104] [Location Matters: Sparse Latent-Variance Attacks for Face Privacy against Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14254)
*Shuting Yang,Shuxin Li,Linyan Dai,Xinwei Zhang,Dong Huang,Renyang Liu,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAL：仅扰动5%空间位置的稀疏对抗保护框架，在保持保护图像高质量的同时有效破坏扩散模型的身份个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型可仅凭少量参考照片学习并复现个人身份，带来未授权与误导性内容生成风险。主动防御通过在发布前向人脸图像加入对抗扰动来干扰下游个性化的身份习得；但现有方法多依赖稠密 L∞ 约束扰动，其累积失真在人脸结构周围可见，而近期提升图像质量的方法往往牺牲防护效果。

Method: 提出稀疏对抗保护框架 SEAL，仅修改人脸图像中5%的空间位置。方法包含：(1) 可微 top-k 掩码方案，联合优化扰动位置与扰动值；(2) 面向方差的目标，作用于冻结 VAE 编码器的隐分布，破坏身份一致的表示；(3) 显著性引导的分散惩罚，抑制扰动在感知显著人脸区域聚集。

Result: 在 CelebA-HQ 与 VGGFace2 上，SEAL 在对比方法中取得最佳保护图像质量：PSNR 超过 41 dB、SSIM 0.97、LPIPS 至多 0.07；同时经扩散个性化后身份相似度降至至多 0.15。

Conclusion: 稀疏位置优化能够在保持高视觉质量的同时实现强身份保护；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can learn and reproduce an individual's identity from only a few reference photos, creating risks of unauthorized and misleading content generation. Proactive defenses address this risk by adding adversarial perturbations to facial images before release, thereby disrupting identity acquisition during downstream personalization. However, existing methods typically rely on dense $L_\infty$-bounded perturbations, whose accumulated distortions can become visible around facial structures. Recent methods improve protected-image quality, but often at the cost of reduced protection effectiveness. In this paper, we propose Sparse Encoder Attack on Latent Variance (SEAL), a sparse adversarial protection framework that modifies only 5% of the spatial locations in a facial image. SEAL combines a differentiable top-k masking scheme that jointly optimizes perturbation locations and values with a variance-oriented objective over the frozen VAE encoder's latent distribution to disrupt identity-consistent representations. A saliency-guided dispersion penalty further discourages perturbation concentration in perceptually salient facial regions. Experiments on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2 show that SEAL achieves the best protected-image quality among the compared methods, exceeding 41 dB PSNR with 0.97 SSIM and at most 0.07 LPIPS, while reducing identity similarity to at most 0.15 after diffusion personalization. These results demonstrate that sparse location optimization enables strong identity protection while preserving high visual quality. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Lvxan/SEAL

</details>


### [105] [AutoThreshold: Joint Confusion-Profile Learning for Adaptive Mask Binarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14279)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AutoThreshold后处理策略，通过预测各分数区间上正负像素的联合质量，直接参数化阈值索引的混淆计数，从而在无需重训练的情况下为任意F_β目标选取阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一验证集调优得到的固定阈值在密集分数分布发生偏移时容易失效（brittle），需要一种对分数分布漂移稳健、且可随评价指标（F_β）灵活切换的阈值选择方法。

Method: AutoThreshold：在分数分箱上联合预测正、负像素的联合质量（joint mass），避免分别估计患病率与类条件率再相乘；采用Softmax归一化与累积解码，得到连贯且单调的混淆剖面（confusion profiles）；再用插件式（plug-in）解码器为任意F_β目标选出阈值，无需重训练网络头。在Kvasir三个独立训练管线迁移到四个息肉数据集上验证（Joint vs 连续Otsu），并在参考管线上用三个head种子比较，加入direct-utility Ridge基线；另在KITTI Road上用leave-one-corruption-family-out评估Joint与标量回归。

Result: 外部macro Dice上Joint比连续Otsu高0.0080±0.0046，3/3次运行均为正增益；参考管线上三个种子为0.6324±0.0027，比Otsu高0.0085；direct-utility Ridge为0.6342，配对差值区间跨零。同一Joint检查点在F_0.5、Dice、F_2上均优于Otsu。KITTI Road上Joint较标量回归提升0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101]，而direct utility高0.0022。

Conclusion: 联合混淆预测是一种有用且可随指标重定目标的归纳偏置，但作者明确不宣称其达到Bayes最优的F_β决策。

Abstract: A single validation-tuned threshold can become brittle when dense-score distributions shift. We introduce AutoThreshold, a post-hoc policy that predicts the joint mass of positive and negative pixels over score bins. This target directly parameterizes threshold-indexed confusion counts, avoiding products of independently estimated prevalence and class-conditional rates. Softmax normalization and cumulative decoding yield coherent, monotone confusion profiles; a plug-in decoder then selects a threshold for any $F_β$ objective without retraining the head. Across three independently trained Kvasir pipelines transferred to four polyp datasets, Joint exceeds continuous Otsu by $0.0080 \pm 0.0046$ external macro Dice, with positive gains in 3/3 runs. On the reference pipeline, three head seeds achieve $0.6324 \pm 0.0027$, 0.0085 above Otsu; direct-utility Ridge achieves 0.6342, with the paired difference interval spanning zero. The same Joint checkpoints outperform Otsu for $F_{0.5}$, Dice, and $F_2$. On developmental KITTI Road leave-one-corruption-family-out evaluation, Joint improves over scalar regression by 0.0082 [0.0064, 0.0101], while direct utility is 0.0022 higher. These results establish joint confusion prediction as a useful, metric-retargetable inductive bias, without claiming Bayes-optimal $F_β$ decisions.

</details>


### [106] [Textual Response-Aware Guidance for Zero-Shot Adversarial Robustness of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40309)
*Yubo Cui,Xianchao Guan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出文本响应感知引导（TRAG），利用预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应来约束视觉表示漂移，从而在对抗微调中更好地保持图文对齐，实现领先的干净与鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）零样本泛化强但易受对抗攻击。现有对抗微调依赖预训练模型引导以保持图文对齐，但两类代表性子引导方式存在互补缺陷：匹配视觉特征对各方向等权约束，覆盖面广但缺乏语义侧重；匹配预测分布侧重语义相关方向，却受限于候选文本与预测概率。二者均可能损害预训练图文关系并降低零样本性能。

Method: 提出 TRAG（Textual Response-Aware Guidance）。以预训练文本编码器对输入嵌入局部变化的响应为依据约束视觉表示漂移：围绕语义锚点，从响应的方向与幅度构建锚点级约束，再利用预训练的图像—锚点相关性进行聚合，得到图像自适应的约束项。

Result: 跨多种架构与任务的大量实验表明，方法在干净性能与鲁棒性能上均达到当前最优；进一步分析显示 TRAG 有助于保持预训练图文关系。

Conclusion: TRAG 通过文本编码器响应的锚点级约束与图像自适应聚合，克服了特征匹配与分布匹配两类引导的局限，在提升对抗鲁棒性的同时更好地保留图文对齐与零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit strong zero-shot generalization but remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Existing adversarial fine-tuning methods often use pre-trained model guidance to preserve image--text alignment while improving robustness. Our analysis of visual representation drift reveals complementary limitations of two representative forms of guidance. Matching visual features constrains all directions equally, providing broad coverage without semantic emphasis. Matching prediction distributions prioritizes semantically relevant directions, but remains limited by the candidate texts and prediction probabilities. These limitations can hinder preservation of pre-trained image--text relationships and degrade zero-shot performance. We therefore propose Textual Response-Aware Guidance (TRAG), which constrains visual representation drift using the pre-trained text encoder's responses to local variations in input embeddings. TRAG constructs anchor-level constraints from the response directions and magnitudes around semantic anchors, then aggregates them using pre-trained image--anchor relevance, yielding an image-adaptive constraint. Extensive experiments across architectures and tasks demonstrate state-of-the-art clean and robust performance. Further analysis shows that TRAG helps preserve pre-trained image--text relationships.

</details>


### [107] [UniFlow-RS: Unified Generative Prediction for Heterogeneous Remote-Sensing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14281)
*Jianlong He,Liang Liao,Jing Xiao,Chaofeng Chen,Gui-Song Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniFlow-RS，用单一共享的潜空间流匹配预测器统一异构遥感任务的预测过程，并引入任务-模态自适应时间步采样（TMAS）协调联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感基础模型虽在表征层面趋于统一，但预测机制仍高度任务特定：独立的解码器、预测头与目标函数导致预测过程在不同任务间割裂，因此希望让异构遥感任务共享同一预测过程。

Method: 两个关键设计：其一，将异构任务输出投射到公共潜空间中的结构化视觉目标，由同一预训练流预测器建模，并用任务相关条件化适配不同输入配置；其二，以统一的 Flow Matching 目标替代任务特定损失，并提出 TMAS，依据各任务-模态组的在线学习状态动态分配训练时间步。

Result: 单一共享模型覆盖 6 个任务、13 个数据集，在多个基准上超过近期任务专用 SOTA，其余基准保持竞争力；同一 checkpoint 在未见数据集、任务设定、遮挡条件及近红外假彩色影像上具备强零样本迁移能力，无需目标域适配。

Conclusion: 统一的潜空间流匹配预测范式可在不依赖任务特定解码器与损失的情况下支撑多样遥感任务，且泛化性良好，为遥感基础模型的预测端统一提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Remote-sensing foundation models increasingly unify representations across sensing modalities and downstream tasks, yet their prediction mechanisms remain largely task-specific. Separate decoders, prediction heads, and objectives leave prediction fragmented across tasks. This naturally raises a question: can heterogeneous remote-sensing tasks share the prediction process? UniFlow-RS answers this question with a single latent flow-matching predictor shared across tasks. This requires addressing two key challenges: unifying heterogeneous prediction spaces and harmonizing joint optimization across tasks. First, we cast heterogeneous task outputs into structured visual targets in a common latent space, allowing them to be modeled by the same pretrained flow predictor. Task-dependent conditioning further accommodates differences in input configurations. Second, we optimize all tasks with a common Flow Matching objective, eliminating task-specific prediction losses. To mitigate optimization mismatch across tasks and modalities, we introduce Task-Modality Adaptive Timestep Sampling (TMAS), which dynamically allocates training timesteps according to the online learning state of each task-modality group. Using a single shared model, UniFlow-RS handles six tasks and thirteen datasets, surpassing recent task-specific state-of-the-art methods on several benchmarks while remaining competitive on the others. Beyond these benchmarks, the same checkpoint demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer across unseen datasets, task settings, occlusion conditions, and near-infrared false-color imagery, without target-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [108] [TaoMate-R1: Temporal Role Specialization for Streaming Multi-Reference Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14307)
*Qijun Gan,Chenwei Zhang,Xinyang Li,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TaoMate-R1，一种流式多参考视频生成器，通过按时间角色（建场 / 延续）特化低秩适配器，在冻结骨干上实现更稳定的长时流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多参考视频生成需先用指定人物与物体建立场景，再在指令变化下延续。现有"按操作划分"的参数共享方案（把去噪与上下文写入在所有 chunk 上分开）并非最优的适配参数组织方式，需要探索更合适的共享结构。

Method: 在冻结骨干上为场景建立与场景延续分别配置独立的低秩适配器；每个适配器对自身角色同时执行去噪与上下文写入；采用 Self Gradient Forcing，通过角色所构建的历史联合训练两个角色。并用固定回放的梯度分析刻画两种共享方案所允许的更新方向。在相同"双适配器"预算下与按操作特化方案对比。

Result: 在 32 个多参考案例上比较两种方案；在 10 条约两分钟、指令变化且共享教师音频条件的流上，TaoMate-R1 将平均边界亮度跳变从 1.96 降至 0.613，边界特征相似度更高；在短交互、长流与公开参考组合上取得更高的平均 CLIP 与 DINO 时间相似度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，按"场景建立 / 场景延续"分组组织适配参数，更适合流式多参考视频生成；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Streaming multi-reference video generation establishes a scene from specified people and objects, then continues it under changing instructions. We introduce TaoMate-R1, a streaming multi-reference generator that organizes adaptation parameters through temporal role specialization. It assigns separate low-rank adapters to scene establishment and continuation on a frozen backbone. Each adapter performs both denoising and context writing for its role; Self Gradient Forcing jointly trains the roles through the history they construct. This allocation contrasts with operation-based specialization, which separates denoising from context writing across all chunks. A fixed-replay gradient analysis characterizes the update directions admitted by the two sharing schemes. On 32 multi-reference cases, we compare the schemes at the same two-adapter budget. Across ten approximately two-minute streams with changing instructions and shared teacher-audio conditioning, TaoMate-R1 reduces the mean boundary luminance jump from 1.96 to 0.613, with higher boundary feature similarity. It also achieves higher mean CLIP and DINO temporal similarity across short interactions, long streams, and public reference compositions. These results support grouping adaptation parameters by scene establishment and continuation for streaming multi-reference generation. Our code and model are released at https://github.com/TaoLiveAIGC/TaoMate-H3.

</details>


### [109] [What Factors Matter for Collection-Level Counting with Human-AI Collaboration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14380)
*Seoyun Jeong,Logan Lawrence,Owen L. Martin,Benjamin Koger,Daniel Sheldon,Subhransu Maji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在统一的人在回路（human-in-the-loop）计数框架下，系统比较了12个计数任务中的模型、估计器与自适应策略选择问题，指出多模态模型（MMM）可作为免训练强基线，微调检测器在大标注预算与重复部署时更优，并发现用模型预测优先选择人工核验单元可显著提升估计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研与政策决策常需估计大规模图像集合中目标对象的总量。逐一人工标注不可行，而原始模型预测存在偏差（尤其在域偏移下）。已有的人在回路估计方法虽结合不完美预测与有限人工核验，但对实践者在新部署中如何选择模型、估计器与自适应策略缺乏指导。

Method: 构建统一的人在回路计数框架，在覆盖生物医学显微、地面与航拍野生动物调查、城市结构俯视影像的12个任务上开展受控比较；对比现代多模态模型的免训练使用与微调检测器，比较均匀采样与基于模型预测优先采样两种人工标注分配策略，并检验用小型试点集估计各模型排序质量（ranking quality）的可行性。

Result: 现代多模态模型为多数任务提供强免训练起点；微调检测器在更大标注预算与重复部署场景更有优势；以模型预测优先决定人工标注单元（而非均匀采样）可进一步提升估计效率；小型试点集即可估计模型的排序质量并据此选择策略。在航拍叉角羚调查的实际部署中，任务特定自适应方法在median 3.2%图像核验量下即满足±5%的容差要求。

Conclusion: 该工作为集合级计数中模型、估计器与采样/自适应策略的选择提供了实证指南，并提出用试点集快速评估模型排序质量以指导新任务策略选择；实地部署验证了方法在严格容差下的高效率。

Abstract: Many scientific and policy decisions require estimating the total number of target objects across a large image collection. Exhaustive human labeling is often infeasible, while raw model predictions can be biased, especially under domain shift. Recent human-AI estimation methods combine imperfect model predictions with limited human verification to obtain reliable collection-level estimates. However, existing work provides little guidance on how practitioners should choose among models, estimators, and adaptation strategies for a new deployment. We study these choices within a common human-in-the-loop counting framework across 12 tasks spanning biomedical microscopy, ground-level and aerial wildlife surveys, and overhead imagery of urban structures. Our results show that modern multimodal models provide a strong, training-free starting point for many tasks, while fine-tuned detectors become more advantageous at larger labeling budgets and across repeated deployments. Estimation efficiency improves further when model predictions are used to prioritize which units humans label, rather than sampling uniformly. We also find that each model's ranking quality can be estimated with a small pilot set, yielding practical guidance for selecting strategies on new counting tasks. In a field deployment on aerial pronghorn surveys, a task-specific adaptation of these methods meets the survey's $\pm 5\%$ tolerance after reviewing a median of only $3.2\%$ of collected images.

</details>


### [110] [OnlyAct: Extracting Action Representations from Foundation Models for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14420)
*Runjia Li,Minghao Chen,Junyu Xie,Philip Torr,Andrea Vedaldi,Tomas Jakab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标签的对比式适配框架，从预训练基础模型V-JEPA 2的帧对嵌入出发，通过“同运动异场景拉近、同场景异运动推远”重塑潜空间几何，从而在不引入重建瓶颈的情况下提取动作信息并丢弃场景信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型受限于动作监督：动作标注稀缺、规范不统一、无法随无标签视频预训练语料扩展。主流替代方案是联合训练动作编码器（将相邻帧压缩为潜动作）与解码器（由前一帧和潜动作重建下一帧），但存在两个问题：一是从零学习动作空间限制了对未见域的泛化；二是重建瓶颈在表达性与解耦之间难以权衡——约束过紧丢失细节，过松则在域偏移下泄漏场景信息。

Method: 不走从零学习动作的路线，而以预训练基础模型V-JEPA 2为起点（其帧对嵌入经大规模多样视频预训练后已蕴含丰富动作信息），目标是提取动作、剔除场景。为此提出无标签对比式适配框架：将不同场景但相同运动的帧对在嵌入空间中拉近，将相同场景但不同运动的帧对推远，从而在不施加重建瓶颈的前提下重塑预训练空间的几何结构。

Result: 提出统一评测协议，涵盖三个维度——动作表达性、场景不变性、动作条件视频生成质量——并在机器人操作、相机导航、游戏、驾驶等基准上评测，其中多数基准在适配阶段未见。与无标注潜动作方法相比，本方法表示显著更具表达性，同时场景不变性相当；所得世界模型能将演示动作更忠实地迁移到新场景。

Conclusion: 以预训练基础模型嵌入为起点、用无标签对比目标做几何重塑，可在摆脱重建瓶颈与动作标注依赖的同时兼顾动作表达性与场景不变性，并带来更强的跨场景动作迁移能力，为可扩展的视频世界模型训练提供了新起点。

Abstract: Video world models promise general-purpose interactive simulators, but their training is limited by action supervision: action labels are scarce, fragmented across incompatible specifications, and do not scale with the unlabeled video pretraining corpus. A common workaround jointly trains an action encoder to compress consecutive frames into a latent action and a decoder to reconstruct the next frame from the previous frame and that latent, forcing the latent to capture only new information. This has two limitations: learning the action space from scratch can limit generalization to unseen domains, and the bottleneck trades expressiveness for separation, with tighter settings losing nuance and looser ones risking scene leakage under domain shifts. We argue for a different starting point: rather than learning actions from scratch, we start from a pretrained foundation model (V-JEPA 2), whose embeddings of a frame pair already carry substantial action information after large-scale, diverse video pretraining. The remaining challenge is to extract the action while discarding the scene. We propose a label-free contrastive adaptation framework that pulls together frame pairs sharing the same motion under a different scene and pushes apart pairs from the same scene with different motion, reshaping the geometry of the pretrained space without imposing a reconstruction bottleneck. We introduce a unified evaluation protocol along three axes (action expressiveness, scene invariance, and action-conditioned video generation quality) across robot manipulation, camera navigation, game, and driving benchmarks, most of which are unseen during adaptation. Compared with annotation-free latent-action methods, our representations are substantially more expressive while matching their scene invariance, and they yield world models that transfer demonstrated actions to new scenes more faithfully.

</details>


### [111] [Metric-Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization for Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14436)
*Luka Dragar,Peter Peer,Vitomir Štruc,Borut Batagelj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先预测后解释"解耦流水线：视觉基础模型集成负责真伪分类，Qwen3-VL-8B生成决策条件化的证据解释，并对假图额外做无图文本压缩生成通俗摘要；再用指标对齐的GRPO优化两个解释器，在XPlainVerse上复杂BERTScore-F1由0.686提升至0.712，提交结果复杂BERTScore-F1排名第一、总榜第七。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器多以真/假二分类为唯一评测目标，而实际应用需要给出预测背后的视觉证据解释；联合视觉-语言模型在真伪判断上表现不佳，甚至为被篡改图像生成"真实"的叙述。因此需要将判别与解释解耦，并按挑战评测指标直接优化解释质量。

Method: 作为ACM MM 2026可解释深度伪造检测挑战赛提交方案，基于XPlainVerse基准训练与评测。流程：(1) 视觉基础模型集成对图像做真/假分类；(2) Qwen3-VL-8B生成以预测结果为条件的视觉证据解释；(3) 对假图，第二个Qwen3-VL-8B在不使用图像的条件下将解释压缩为非专家可读的简短摘要；(4) 采用与评测指标对齐的GRPO分别优化两个解释器。

Result: 同等条件下，GRPO使复杂BERTScore-F1从0.686提升至0.712，并同时改善接地性（实体F1与证据F1）；提交方案在复杂BERTScore-F1单项排名第一，总榜排名第七。

Conclusion: 将分类与解释解耦、并以评测指标为奖励做强化学习微调，可有效提升深度伪造解释的忠实度与可读性；该方案在挑战赛中验证了其竞争力。局限在于仅报告单一基准与挑战赛设定下的结果，跨域泛化性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors are commonly evaluated on real/fake classification alone, but real-world applications require explanations of the visual evidence behind each prediction. Joint vision-language models struggle with authenticity assessment, producing "real" narratives for manipulated images. To address this, we propose a decoupled predict-then-explain pipeline as our submission to the ACM Multimedia 2026 Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge, with methods trained and evaluated on the XPlainVerse benchmark. A vision-foundation-model ensemble first classifies the image, then a Qwen3-VL-8B model generates a decision-conditioned explanation of the visual evidence. For fake images, a second Qwen3-VL-8B compresses that text into a short non-expert summary without using the image. We further apply metric-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to optimize both explainers against the challenge criteria. Under matched conditions, GRPO raises complex BERTScore-F1 from 0.686 to 0.712 and also improves grounding (entity and evidence F1); our submission ranked first on complex BERTScore-F1 and seventh overall.

</details>


### [112] [A Lightweight Differentiable Method for Real-Time Skin Rendering and Digitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14451)
*Mario Marquez-Balduque,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Jorge Lopez-Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可微分的屏幕空间次表面散射（SSSS）框架，从单光单视角轻量采集直接估计可用于实时渲染管线的皮肤散射参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有皮肤逆向渲染依赖离线路径追踪估计物理属性，其结果难以直接映射到游戏引擎等交互应用所使用的近似着色器，存在离线物理参数与实时渲染管线之间的鸿沟。

Method: 构建可微分SSSS框架，联合优化空间变化的base贴图与散射距离；按面部区域分别估计参数以刻画局部皮肤厚度差异，生成可混合的profile map；采用低成本单光源、单视角采集装置；优化由聚焦次表面散射主导区域的masked loss驱动。另用合成数据、多光源装置验证单光源假设、倾斜视角验证角度一致性。

Result: 在覆盖Monk肤色量表的45名真实个体数据集上验证，具备人口统计学泛化性；实验表明方法能从轻量输入中有效恢复散射参数，生成与标准实时着色器直接兼容的逼真皮肤外观。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了离线逆向渲染与实时渲染之间的差距，实现了面向生产级实时引擎的皮肤外观获取，并具实用性与泛化性。

Abstract: Inverse rendering for skin typically relies on offline path tracing to estimate physical properties, which fail to map directly to the approximate shaders utilized in interactive applications, such as game engines. We bridge this gap by directly estimating subsurface scattering parameters tailored for real-time rendering pipelines. We introduce a differentiable Screen-Space Subsurface Scattering (SSSS) framework that enables the joint optimization of spatially varying base textures and scattering distances. To account for local variations in skin thickness, we estimate these parameters across distinct facial regions, generating a blended profile map ready for production runtime engines. Our pipeline utilizes a low-cost, single-light, single-view capture setup designed for accessible and practical appearance acquisition. Optimization is driven by a masked loss function targeting regions where subsurface scattering effects are dominant. We validate our approach on a real-world dataset of $45$ individuals across the Monk Skin Tone Scale, ensuring demographic generalization. Furthermore, we complement this evaluation with synthetic data, a multi-light setup validating our single-light assumption, and a tilted-view configuration confirming angular consistency. Results demonstrate that our method effectively recovers scattering parameters from lightweight inputs, yielding realistic skin appearance directly compatible with standard real-time shaders.

</details>


### [113] [C$^2$-Routing: Complementary Cropping and Compression for Efficient Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14469)
*Guorui Lu,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 C²-Routing，针对 VQA 中视觉 token 预算问题，自适应地在裁剪与压缩两种视觉输入削减策略间进行路由，并配合安全门控机制，在 RTX 3090 上以 1,024 token 上限为基线，实现端到端 FLOPs 平均再降 36.53%、视觉 token 再降 42.36%，任务分数平均相对仅下降 0.77%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA 给视觉语言模型（VLM）带来显著计算开销，需要模型无关、可用于黑盒场景的输入削减方法。单纯设定视觉 token 上限虽能降低计算，但会丢弃回答问题所需信息；且单一削减策略难以适配不同场景。作者观察到裁剪与压缩具有互补性：裁剪突出小目标与局部细节，全图压缩更利于保留大目标或分布式证据的上下文。

Method: 提出 C²-Routing：依据输入图像与问题自适应选择裁剪或压缩策略。由于两种策略仍可能丢失关键信息，进一步设计安全门控（safety gate），利用原始输入与削减输入下预测的联合正确性分布来指导回退决策，从而保护精度。所有训练与评估均在 RTX 3090 消费级 GPU 上完成；为衡量超越 token 上限的额外收益，采用同样 1,024 视觉 token 上限的全图基线进行对比。

Result: 在 3 个 VLM 与 18 个数据集（覆盖局部细节、全局场景、场景文字与文档）上广泛评测。在视觉预算已被上限约束的前提下，C²-Routing 平均进一步降低端到端 FLOPs 36.53%、视觉 token 42.36%，而任务分数平均相对下降仅 0.77%。

Conclusion: 裁剪与压缩的互补性可通过输入条件化的自适应路由有效利用；配合基于联合正确性分布预测的安全门控回退机制，可在模型无关、黑盒可用且消费级 GPU 可负担的条件下，以极小精度代价换取显著的视觉 token 与计算节省。

Abstract: Visual Question Answering (VQA) imposes substantial computational overhead on vision-language models (VLMs), motivating model-agnostic input reduction methods that can be readily applied across diverse VLMs, including in black-box settings. Simply enforcing a visual-token cap reduces computation, but it can discard information needed to answer the question. A single reduction strategy also struggles to accommodate different scenarios. Our key observation is that cropping and compression are complementary: cropping highlights small targets and local details, whereas whole-image compression better preserves context for large targets or distributed evidence. Based on this observation, we propose C$^2$-Routing, which adaptively selects cropping or compression according to the input image and question. Since both strategies may still discard key information, we further design a safety gate that uses the predicted joint correctness distribution under original and reduced inputs to guide fallback and protect accuracy. To accommodate consumer GPU resource constraints, all training and evaluation experiments run on RTX 3090 GPUs. To measure gains beyond token capping, we use full-image baselines with the same cap of 1,024 visual tokens. We conduct extensive evaluation on three VLMs and 18 datasets spanning local details, global scenes, scene text, and documents. With the visual budget already capped, C$^2$-Routing further reduces end-to-end FLOPs by 36.53% and visual tokens by 42.36% on average, with a mean relative task-score decrease of only 0.77%.

</details>


### [114] [BoviShift: A Six-Year Multi-Attribute Audit of Visual Cattle Phenotyping Across Animals and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14476)
*Mobasshir Bhuiya Shagor,Nilambar Halder Tonmoy,Md Saef Ullah Miah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发布BoviShift数据集（2021–2026六个年度队列，2,657头牛、10,610张静态图像、2,566段环绕视频，覆盖九个记录品种），并系统评估图像随机划分与牛只不重叠划分、以及未来年度队列上的迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉可为牲畜性状估计提供可扩展的非接触手段，但相关图像与视频帧之间存在强相关性，图像随机划分会让同一动物出现在训练与测试集，造成评估不可靠；实际部署还需迁移到未来年份捕获的动物。现有资源缺乏跨连续年度队列、同时包含物理与视觉属性的评测基准。

Method: 构建BoviShift语料库（六个连续年度队列，2021–2026）；设计三种划分策略、四种评估配置：同一开发队列上的图像随机划分与牛只不重叠（cattle-disjoint）划分，以及2025未来年评测和锁定的2026未来年评测；采用固定ResNet-50多任务基线，评估体重、年龄、身高、品种、毛色五个属性；并进行单种子多任务消融、静态视图聚合以及零样本视频帧聚合分析。

Result: 图像随机划分与牛只不重叠划分的差异依任务而异，并非普遍存在泄漏惩罚；未来队列表现随属性与队列变化：品种性能相对池化评估下降；2025年体重与年龄的均值性能未超过常数基线；身高仍高于基线；毛色向2025迁移良好但在2026出现退化。单种子消融中多任务训练改善全部五个点估计，静态视图聚合有选择性收益，零样本视频帧聚合暴露了静态到视频的域差。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，BoviShift是目前时间跨度最长的牛类视觉资源，将连续年度队列与物理和视觉属性结合，为牲畜视觉模型的泄漏控制评估与跨年度泛化研究提供了基准，并表明未来队列迁移性能具有显著的属性与队列依赖性。

Abstract: Computer vision offers a scalable, non-contact means of estimating livestock traits, but correlated images and video frames make reliable evaluation difficult. Image-random splitting can place views of the same animal in both training and test sets, while deployment further requires transfer to animals captured in future years. We introduce BoviShift, a corpus reconstructed from six consecutive annual cohorts of an operational cattle marketplace collected from 2021 to 2026. It contains 2{,}657 cattle, 10{,}610 still images, and 2{,}566 walk-around videos across nine recorded breed categories. We evaluate three splitting strategies through four configurations: image-random and pooled cattle-disjoint splits over the same development cohorts, followed by separate future-2025 and locked future-2026 evaluations. Using a fixed ResNet-50 multi-task baseline, we assess weight, age, height, breed, and coat color. Differences between image-random and cattle-disjoint splitting are task-dependent rather than a universal leakage penalty. Future-cohort behavior is also attribute- and cohort-specific: breed declines relative to pooled evaluation; mean weight and age performance fails to beat constant baselines in 2025; height remains above baseline; and color transfers well to 2025 but degrades in 2026. Multi-task training improves all five point estimates in a single-seed ablation, still-view aggregation helps selectively, and zero-shot video-frame aggregation exposes a still-to-video domain gap. To our knowledge, BoviShift is the longest-spanning cattle-vision resource combining consecutive annual cohorts with physical and visual attributes.

</details>


### [115] [RS-JEPA: Remote Sensing Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture with Scan-Path Views for Cross-Sensor Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14496)
*Tanishq Rachamalla,Srishti Kaushik,Muhammad Attique Khan,Gustau Camps-Valls,Swalpa Kumar Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RS-JEPA：基于共享 Vision Mamba 编码器的紧凑联合嵌入预测框架，将同一图像的两种扫描顺序视为两视图进行跨传感器自监督检索，3.88M 参数下取得最高 F1@5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨传感器遥感检索（SAR-光学、PAN-MS）缺乏人工标签。现有自监督方法存在缺陷：重建传感器特定的像素细节、仅与共配准孪生图像对齐、或依赖 118–173M（高达 398M）参数的巨型 Transformer，模型冗余且未充分利用可观的自监督信号。

Method: 1) 以共享的 Vision Mamba（状态空间）编码器处理两种传感器图像；2) 提出 scan-path views：利用状态空间编码器按有序 patch 序列读图的特性，将同一图像两种读取顺序作为像素完全相同但顺序不同的两视图，由一种顺序的可见 patch 预测另一顺序的隐藏 patch；3) 提出 Adversarial View Coupling (AVC)，学习最难的可见 patch 划分以保持预测难度；4) 引入邻域一致性损失，对齐两种传感器的场景相似度结构。

Result: 在 SAR-光学（BEN-14K、CBRSIR-VS）与 PAN-MS（DSRSID）基准上，于 12 个复现方法中取得最高 F1@5：分别为 72.92、80.35、94.43；检索网络仅 3.88M 参数（训练时 14.57M），比既有 JEPA 检索器少 8–16 倍。

Conclusion: 阅读顺序（scan order）可作为自监督视图的有效来源；轻量 SSM 编码器结合预测式联合嵌入即可在跨传感器检索中超越重型 Transformer 方案。局限：仍依赖共配准图像对训练（与既有方法相同）。

Abstract: Cross-sensor remote sensing image retrieval aims to find scenes with the same content across heterogeneous sensors, such as SAR and optical or panchromatic (PAN) and multispectral (MS) imagery, without manual labels. Existing self-supervised methods either reconstruct sensor-specific pixel detail, align each image only with its co-registered twin, or rely on large transformers with 118-173M parameters (and up to 398M). We introduce RS-JEPA, a compact joint-embedding predictive framework built on a Vision Mamba encoder shared by both sensors. Since a state-space encoder reads an image as an ordered sequence of patches, we propose scan-path views, which treat two reading orders of the same image as two views with identical pixels and predict the hidden patches in one order from the visible patches in the other.To keep this prediction challenging, we propose Adversarial View Coupling (AVC), which learns the hardest split of the visible patches, and a neighbourhood consensus loss that aligns the scene similarities of both sensors. Extensive experiments on SAR-optical (BEN-14K, CBRSIR-VS) and PAN-MS (DSRSID) benchmarks demonstrate that RS-JEPA achieves the highest F1@5 among twelve re-implemented methods (72.92, 80.35 and 94.43) with a retrieval network of only 3.88M parameters (14.57M in training, 8-16times fewer than prior JEPA retrievers). Like prior methods, RS-JEPA trains on co-registered pairs. Our results highlight reading order as a source of self-supervised views. code: https://github.com/Tanishq251/RSJEPA.

</details>


### [116] [Measuring Robustness and Efficiency in a Connectome-Constrained Fly Visual System Model: Linking an Adversarial and a Perceptual Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14498)
*Alexandre Garcia-Duran,Alejandro Rodriguez-Garcia,Manuel Molano-Mazón,Alexandre Hyafil,Srikanth Ramaswamy,Janne K. Lappalainen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文以受果蝇突触连接组约束的连续时间递归网络FlyNet为对象，在可微3D渲染的碰撞检测任务上，与CNN、RAFT及无连接组约束的sCTRNN/CTRNN对比，考察"脑式布线"在更低算力下的鲁棒性。结论：连接组约束主要带来效率（低边数）而非鲁棒性；FlyNet的对抗失效与其位置/速度表征崩溃有关，且与reverse-phi错觉共享同一低层运动统计量这一电路脆弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物视觉系统在代谢与布线约束下仍能可靠计算，而人工视觉系统能耗高且对对抗扰动敏感。核心问题是：具备类脑布线的网络是否在更低计算代价下更鲁棒，此前缺乏系统基准。作者希望通过"连接组约束"这一生物学先验，检验效率与鲁棒性之间的权衡关系，并借生物约束模型反推生物感知脆弱性的机制假设。

Method: 构建任务优化、连续时间递归、受果蝇突触连接组（edge count）约束的网络FlyNet；在可微3D渲染管线内设定碰撞检测任务；与CNN、RAFT、以及不受连接组约束的sCTRNN与CTRNN进行对照；评测维度包括：常见视频损坏（corruptions）、白盒对抗攻击、FLOPs成本；并设计反事实纹理（counterfactual textures，与原始纹理等距于训练分布但恢复正确分类）以排除"纹理陌生性"解释；进一步用reverse-phi错觉作机制对照，并仅优化该低层运动统计量（无网络访问）以诱发分类失败。

Result: (1) FlyNet对对抗攻击脆弱，但相对其计算量，对常见视频损坏的鲁棒性优于RAFT，且FLOPs最多低五个数量级；(2) CTRNN类（无连接组约束）在几乎所有扰动下都比FlyNet更鲁棒，说明连接组约束带来的是效率优势（源自低边数）而非鲁棒性；(3) 递归性提升RAFT对白盒攻击的鲁棒性，时序上下文提升对常见损坏的鲁棒性；(4) 反事实纹理下FlyNet的位置与速度表征可恢复，排除"不熟悉纹理"为失效原因；(5) reverse-phi错觉同样破坏这些表征，并反转与对抗纹理相同的低层运动统计量；仅优化该统计量即可诱发分类失败，表明两类扰动归因于同一电路脆弱性。

Conclusion: 连接组约束的模型主要价值在于效率而非鲁棒性；但这类生物约束模型可作为"可检验假设的生成器"，把对抗失效机制上升为对生物感知脆弱性（如reverse-phi类现象）的电路级解释，从而在人工与生物视觉之间建立机制层面的双向验证路径。

Abstract: Biological visual systems compute reliably under tight metabolic and wiring constraints, whereas artificial vision systems consume far more energy and can be sensitive to adversarial perturbations. Whether networks with brain-like wiring are more robust at lower computational cost is unclear. We address this by benchmarking a task-optimized, continuous-time recurrent neural network of the fly visual system constrained by its synaptic connectome (FlyNet) against artificial neural networks. We compare it against CNNs, RAFT, and continuous-time recurrent neural networks without detailed connectome constraints (sCTRNN, CTRNN) on a collision-detection task within a differentiable 3D rendering pipeline. FlyNet is adversarially vulnerable but, relative to its compute, more robust to common video corruptions than RAFT, while using up to five orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs. The CTRNNs remain more robust than FlyNet across almost all tested perturbations, suggesting that connectome constraints benefit efficiency, through their low edge count, over robustness. Recurrence improves robustness to white-box attacks in RAFT and temporal context improves robustness to common corruptions. FlyNet's position and velocity representations degrade under adversarial textures but recover under counterfactual textures that restore correct classification while staying equally far from training, ruling out unfamiliar textures as the cause of failure. The reverse-phi illusion similarly disrupts these representations and inverts the same low-level motion statistic as the adversarial textures. Optimizing this input statistic alone, without access to the network, suffices to induce classification failure, attributing both perturbations to the same circuit vulnerability. Connectome-constrained models can thus surface adversarial failure mechanisms as testable hypotheses about biological perceptual vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [117] [Toward Foundation Detection Models via Example-Based Grounding in EM-DETR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14518)
*Sheethal Bhat,Bogdan Georgescu,Awais Mansoor,Florin C. Ghesu,Sasa Grbic,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合多源异构标注数据构建跨域医学病灶检测基础模型的方法，结合师生训练策略与EM-DETR实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的医学CAD系统性能高度依赖大规模、标注完善的数据集，但医学病灶检测领域缺乏全面标准化的标注，现有数据集异构性强，限制了鲁棒基础模型的构建。

Method: 整合多个同时标注解剖结构与异常病灶的医学影像数据集；提出师生（student-teacher）训练策略以有效融合标签空间异构的数据集，缓解灾难性遗忘并改善特征学习；在原有基于样例的EM-DETR框架基础上进行改造，使其能联合学习多个领域特定数据集，实现细粒度、样例驱动的跨模态检测。

Result: 在胸部X线（CXR）四项关键发现上，图像级AUC超越此前SOTA约1个百分点；在CXR与乳腺摄影上取得领先的mAP50，较单数据集训练的EM-DETR提升2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: 该工作为利用异构数据构建可扩展、鲁棒且具备良好泛化能力的医学影像基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: DeepLearning (DL)-based Computer-Aided Diagnostic (CAD) systems have shown great promise in supporting clinicians, but their performance heavily depends on the availability of large, well-annotated datasets. In medical lesion detection, the scarcity of comprehensive, stan dardized annotations and the heterogeneity of existing datasets limit the development of robust foundation models. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to building a multi-domain foundation model for le sion detection by integrating diverse medical image datasets annotated for both anatomical structures and abnormalities. We first propose a student-teacher training strategy to effectively combine datasets with heterogeneous label spaces, mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and im proving feature learning. In addition, building on our previously pro posed example-based EM-DETR framework, we adapt it to jointly learn from multiple domain-specific datasets, enabling fine-grained, example driven detection across modalities. Our model achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results, with image-level AUC scores exceeding previous SOTA by 1% point on four key findings in CXRs. We achieve a leading mAP50 in CXRs and mammography, surpassing EM-DETR trained on a single dataset by 2-3% points. This approach paves the way for scalable foundation models in medical imaging that leverage heterogeneous data while maintaining robust and generalizable performance.

</details>


### [118] [Medical Image Segmentation: Foundation Model Adaptation versus Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14519)
*Emre Kutlusan,Yongmin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将DINOv2基础模型以从仅调偏置到全量微调的多种策略适配到结肠镜、乳腺超声与皮肤镜分割任务，并在训练源留出数据与多机构外部数据集上系统比较，发现适配后的基础模型在所有外部数据集上泛化更好，但所需适配容量很小，且由成像域而非适配方法决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型被期望替代逐任务的医学分割模型，但现有比较多在训练分布内进行、且仅用单一配置缺乏依据的任务专用基线，难以证明在新临床站点上的表现；同时"需要适配多少"及"由什么决定"尚不清楚。

Method: 以DINOv2为基础模型，采用从仅微调偏置项到全量微调的谱系适配策略；与从头训练的U-Net、ResU-Net对比；涵盖结肠镜、乳腺超声、皮肤镜三个成像域；评估包括训练源留出数据与来自其他机构的独立数据集。

Result: 适配后的基础模型在每个外部数据集上泛化均更优；在训练分布内、训练数据充足时，任务专用模型仍具竞争力且边界勾画更精确，而基础模型更常漏检小病灶；仅需适配基础模型的很小一部分参数，所需容量由成像域决定而非适配方法；此外发现一个广泛使用的息肉测试集与其他基准存在图像重复。

Conclusion: 当模型需跨站点部署时，基础模型适配是有必要的，且适配容量应依据目标域来选择；数据集重复问题提示部分既有基准结论需重新审视。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large natural-image collections are increasingly adapted for medical image segmentation, in the expectation that they will replace models trained for each task. Most comparisons, however, use data from the training distribution and a single task-specific baseline whose configuration is rarely justified, and therefore provide limited evidence of performance at new clinical sites. It also remains unclear how much of a foundation model must be adapted, and what determines this. We adapted a foundation model, DINOv2, with strategies ranging from tuning only its bias terms to full fine-tuning, and compared it with U-Nets and ResU-Nets trained from scratch. Experiments covered colonoscopy, breast ultrasound and dermoscopy, with testing on held-out data from the training source and on independent datasets from other institutions. The adapted foundation model generalised better on every external dataset. Within the training distribution, the task-specific models remained competitive when training data were abundant, and delineated boundaries more precisely, while the foundation model more often missed small lesions. Only a small fraction of the foundation model required adaptation, and the capacity needed was determined by the imaging domain rather than by the adaptation method. A widely used polyp test set was also found to duplicate images from another benchmark. Foundation-model adaptation is therefore warranted where models must work across sites, with capacity selected on the target domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Auditable Human-Agent Teams for Autonomous X-ray Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12933)
*Gunik Luthra,Ronald J. Pandolfi,Benjamin T. Diroll,Ricky Ronquillo,Ruipeng Li,Christopher B. Murray,Marcus M. Noack,Kevin G. Yager,Esther H. R. Tsai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文展示了基于编码智能体基础设施在同步辐射光源上对纳米颗粒超晶格开展自主X射线实验，并通过完整对话记录审计决策溯源与认知分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环自主实验可加速科学发现，但实现成本高、可及性受限；智能体系统有望降低门槛，然而在物理仪器上持续运行的可行性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 以经科学方法验证的编码智能体基础设施为依托，在人类定义且动态演化的目标下执行自主同步辐射X射线实验；保存完整的人—智能体与智能体—智能体对话记录，据此重建决策溯源并量化认知劳动分工。

Result: 三次实验中61%–67%的消息涉及解释或判断；方法与科学类贡献占比由首个以搭建为主的实验的46%升至后续实验的56%–61%；59%–68%的纠错涉及多方参与，约半数决策纳入了其他参与者的输入。

Conclusion: 结果为多智能体科学系统的设计原则提供依据，并可指导智能体框架（harness）、模型训练及可及的自主实验平台建设。

Abstract: Closed-loop autonomous experimentation can accelerate scientific discovery, but implementation effort limits accessibility. Agentic systems may lower this barrier, although sustained operation on physical instruments remains largely unexplored. Here, using coding-agent infrastructure grounded in validated scientific methods, we demonstrate autonomous synchrotron X-ray experiments on nanoparticle superlattices under evolving, human-defined objectives. Complete human-agent and agent-agent transcripts enable an audit that reconstructs decision provenance and characterizes the division of cognitive labor. Across three experiments, 61-67% of messages involved interpretation or judgment, while the share of methodological and scientific contributions increased from 46% in the setup-focused first experiment to 56-61% in later experiments. Provenance showed that 59-68% of error corrections involved multiple participants and that about half of all decisions incorporated the input of another participant. These results may guide design principles for multi-agent scientific systems and inform future agent harnesses, model training, and accessible autonomous experimentation.

</details>


### [120] [EarthRecord: From Pixels to Land Change with Multimodal Agents over Four Decades of Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12934)
*Haoran Zhang,Huizhen Sun,Hanbin Wen,Jie Leng,Jiaxin Zhu,Zhaohua Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EarthRecord 基准：基于 56 个站点约四十年的 Landsat、MODIS、VIIRS、Sentinel-2 原始多传感器影像，考察多模态智能体端到端完成土地变化分析的能力。238 个问题覆盖 10 类分析任务，并设"无影像闭卷"与"单卫星数据篡改"两项对照。评测 12 个多模态智能体显示：领先者能端到端完成多数分析，但在需要从影像直接制图的任务上表现不佳；影像使答案从先验知识转向像素，但尚未真正对齐地表变化——篡改单颗卫星数据后约半数"正确答案"随之改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体已能用工具完成复杂数据分析，但其将数十年卫星影像转化为可信土地变化分析的能力（对地球观测科学至关重要）尚未被量化。现有基准存在两类缺陷：一是在精选/裁剪好的影像上提问，二是直接向智能体提供预处理后的输入，绕开了云掩膜、跨传感器协调等真实档案分析起点。

Method: 构建基于原始影像记录的基准 EarthRecord：56 个站点、四十年 Landsat/MODIS/VIIRS/Sentinel-2，云掩膜与跨传感器协调交由智能体自行处理；设计 238 个土地变化问题、覆盖 10 类分析任务；以三类留出参考评分（全球产品、多方法一致性、目视解译）；设置两项控制实验——闭卷（移除影像记录）与仪器配对（只改动某一卫星数据而地表不变）。评测 12 个多模态智能体。

Result: 12 个多模态智能体中，领先者能端到端完成多数分析流程，但当答案需要由影像制图（mapping from imagery）时表现下降。进一步分析表明：提供影像可使答案由先验知识转向像素证据，但尚未追踪真实地表变化——在单卫星数据被篡改的对照中，约一半正确答案随篡改而改变。

Conclusion: EarthRecord 提供了一个有原则的平台，用于度量并推动智能体从"像素"走向"土地变化"这一能力跃迁，并揭示当前智能体仍依赖影像的表层统计而非真实地表信号。

Abstract: While multimodal agents now carry out complex data analysis with tools, their ability to turn decades of satellite imagery into reliable land-change analysis, crucial for Earth observation science, remains unmeasured. Existing benchmarks fall short: they pose questions on curated images or give agents preprocessed inputs, leaving out the raw multi-sensor archives where such analysis begins. We introduce EarthRecord, a benchmark built on the raw record of 56 sites: four decades of Landsat, MODIS, VIIRS, and Sentinel-2 data, with cloud masking and cross-sensor harmonization left to the agent. It poses 238 land-change questions across ten analysis tasks, scored against withheld references from global products, agreement among methods, and visual interpretation. Two controls test what answers rest on: the closed book removes the record, and instrument pairs alter one satellite's data while nothing changes on the ground. Evaluations of twelve multimodal agents reveal that leading agents complete most analyses end to end but falter when an answer requires mapping from imagery. Further analysis shows that the record moves answers from prior knowledge to the pixels but not yet to the real change on the ground: when one satellite's data are altered, half of the right answers follow the alteration. EarthRecord thus provides a principled platform for measuring and advancing agents from pixels to land change.

</details>


### [121] [Train on prefill, monitor at decode: Using sparse autoencoders for topic monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12935)
*Rishikesh Mallagundla,Pranav Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TopicScope框架，在SAE特征上训练因果主题分类器，为生成过程中的每个token分配主题标签，可在预填充阶段低成本训练并部署于解码阶段进行在线主题监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成时运行的激活监控器仅用于检测有害内容，且局限于2-3个类别，无法在整个回复过程中逐token标注主题。需要一种细粒度、多类别的在线主题监控方法。

Method: 构建合成多主题对话数据集（覆盖7个主题），从SAE（稀疏自编码器）特征上训练三种检测器架构的因果主题分类器，为每个token分配主题；并在Gemma-3 1B与4B上，用预填充（prefill）隐藏状态提取的特征训练，测试其向解码（decoding）阶段的泛化能力，并做扰动鲁棒性测试与无SAE消融实验。

Result: 在Gemma-3 1B与4B上，基于预填充隐藏状态特征的监控器可泛化至解码阶段；对插入跑题旁白、删除主题或重排章节等扰动鲁棒，macro-F1最多下降5个点；消融显示无SAE时达到基线性能的分类器需大数倍规模。

Conclusion: 紧凑的主题监控器可在预填充阶段廉价训练并部署于生成过程；该工作还表明SAE的用途可超越安全分类，扩展到更细粒度的在线语义监控。代码、数据集与检查点已开源。

Abstract: Topic monitoring is the task of labelling the topic of each token in a model's response during generation. Activation-based monitors that run at generation time have so far been trained to detect harmful content and are limited to two or three classes. We introduce TopicScope, a framework for training causal topic classifiers on SAE features that assign a topic to every token, so that the label can change partway through a response. We train three detector architectures on synthetic multi-topic conversations covering seven topics. Across Gemma-3 1B and 4B, monitors trained on features extracted from prefill hidden states were able to generalise to decoding. We also found that the classifiers are robust to perturbations, losing at most 5 macro-F1 points when off-topic asides are inserted, topics are removed or sections are reordered. Ablations conducted to understand the contribution of the SAE revealed that classifiers reaching baseline performance without it were several times larger. Our findings show that compact topic monitors can be trained inexpensively in prefill and deployed during generation. More broadly, our work shows where SAEs are useful beyond safety classification. Our code, dataset and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/Mercity-AI/SAE-Guardrails and https://huggingface.co/collections/Mercity/online-guardrails-using-sparse-autoencoders-6ac602940468c62fefb2b9e8

</details>


### [122] [LandslideFM: A Foundation Model Towards Unified Landslide Perception and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12936)
*Shanwen Wang,Sirui Wang,Xin Sun,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个统一滑坡基础模型 LandslideFM，将视觉感知任务与上下文语义推理连接，并构建首个滑坡智能分析基准 LandslideVL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能滑坡检测模型局限于孤立的视觉任务、依赖大量标注数据，且缺乏深度分析与场景理解能力。

Method: 采用"源一致性渐进信任（Source-Agreement Graduated Trust）"半监督学习框架，利用有限标注数据与大量无标注数据适配多视觉任务；再将预测的滑坡区域作为显式空间引导，输入经参数高效微调（PEFT）适配的视觉-语言模型，实现综合滑坡推理；同时构建包含视觉图像与新引入文本提示的基准 LandslideVL。

Result: 在公开数据集与 LandslideVL 上的实验表明，LandslideFM 在定量与定性指标上均表现优越，在专业滑坡推理与场景分析上超过通用 VLM。

Conclusion: 验证了"感知—推理"统一建模范式的有效性，为滑坡智能分析提供了可复用的基础模型与评测基准，代码已开源。

Abstract: Intelligent landslide detection is essential for disaster prevention. However, existing models primarily focus on isolated visual tasks, require extensive labeled data, and lack deep analytical and scene-understanding capabilities. To address these limitations, we propose LandslideFM, the first unified foundation model that connects visual perception tasks with contextual interpretation of landslides. Specifically, LandslideFM adapts to multiple visual tasks via a Source-Agreement Graduated Trust semi-supervised learning framework that leverages limited labeled data alongside abundant unlabeled data. Furthermore, predicted landslide regions provide explicit spatial guidance to a Vision-Language Model adapted through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, enabling comprehensive landslide reasoning. To support training and evaluation in this domain, we also present LandslideVL, the first benchmark for intelligent landslide analysis, combining visual imagery and newly introduced textual prompts. Comprehensive experiments across public datasets and LandslideVL demonstrate that LandslideFM achieves superior quantitative and qualitative performance. Most importantly, LandslideFM surpasses general-purpose VLMs in specialized landslide reasoning and scene analysis. Our code is released at https://github.com/wangshanwen001/LandslideFM.

</details>


### [123] [Tracing Psychometric Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12937)
*Chih-Hao Hsu,Feng-Chun B. Chou,Pin-Hao A. Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文用跨人格（cross-persona）范式检验大模型是否"内部表征"了心理构念，还是仅输出近似心理测量结构的行为。行为层面，大模型复现并放大人类跨量表相关；内部层面，较大instruct模型的方向几何与人类相关矩阵对齐，且几何强度与行为放大相关（r=0.68），但探针-行为斜率存在"读出衰减"缺口，不足以证明所测方向中介了跨人格回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是表征性 vs. 行为模仿性：LLM究竟在内部表征了心理构念，还是只生成表面符合心理测量结构的输出。既有工作多停留在输出层面的量表效度复现，缺少对内部几何与行为输出之间关系的直接检验。

Method: 1) 跨人格范式：Llama 3 与 Qwen 2.5 两家族、0.5B–14B、base 与 instruct 共14个变体；以272名台湾大学生的真实作答为锚，给定其中1个量表作答，预测其余6个量表的反应。2) 行为分析：比较模型复现的跨量表相关与人类七量表相关（并对人类相关做测量衰减校正）。3) 内部几何：对比方向分析（contrastive direction analysis）检验内部几何与人类相关矩阵的对齐程度。4) 激活探针：匹配的activation-probing范式估计探针斜率并与行为斜率比较；用合成对照检验ridge探针的斜率膨胀，并以另一分析得到的信度代理作为基线做校正。5) 关联分析：几何强度—行为放大、探针斜率—行为斜率的相关与偏相关（控制log模型规模）。

Result: 行为层面：较大变体复现并放大人类跨量表相关，放大效应在测量衰减校正后仍存在。内部层面：较大instruct变体出现与人类矩阵对齐的几何结构，base变体在该测定下几乎无对齐。跨模型上，几何强度与行为放大相关（r=0.68, p=0.008；控制log规模的偏相关 r=0.69）。探针斜率变化幅度小于行为斜率（1.38–1.77 vs 0.52–1.53）；合成对照显示ridge探针会膨胀斜率，扣除基于信度代理的基线后校正斜率仍与行为相关（r=0.88）。

Conclusion: 在所用测定下可确立"可解码性"与"几何对齐"，但存在被称为"读出衰减"（readout attenuation）的探针-行为缺口，因此不能断言所测方向中介了跨人格回答；表征性与行为模仿性之间仍未被决定性区分。

Abstract: Do large language models represent psychological constructs internally, or do they only generate outputs that resemble psychometric structure? We address this question using a cross-persona paradigm across 14 model variants from two families (Llama 3 and Qwen 2.5, 0.5B-14B, base and instruct). Given responses on one psychological scale from real individuals (N = 272 Taiwanese university students), models predict responses on six other scales. At the behavioral level, larger variants reproduce and amplify human cross-scale correlations; amplification persists after correcting the seven-scale human correlations for measurement attenuation. We then examine whether this structure is reflected internally. Contrastive direction analysis finds geometry aligned with the human matrix in larger instruct variants, but little alignment in base variants under this assay. Across models, geometry strength is associated with behavioral amplification (r = 0.68, p = 0.008; partial r = 0.69 controlling for log size). To relate internal structure to output behavior, a matched activation-probing paradigm shows that probe slopes vary less than behavioral slopes (1.38-1.77 vs 0.52-1.53). A synthetic control shows that ridge probing can inflate these slopes; after subtracting a baseline based on a reliability proxy from another analysis, adjusted slopes remain associated with behavior (r = 0.88). We term the measured probe-behavior gap readout attenuation. These results establish decodability and geometric alignment under the tested assays, without showing that the measured directions mediate the cross-persona answers.

</details>


### [124] [Graph Visualizations in Prompts for Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12938)
*Luiz do Valle Miranda,Andreas L. Opdahl,Grzegorz J. Nalepa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定KGQA流水线中，用1500道WebQSP/CWQ问题与6个VLM对比"纯文本提示"与"文本+图可视化"提示，发现附加子图图像平均提升1.85 F1，收益主要来自低复杂度问题的精确率提升，且高度依赖图布局而非模型规模；据此提出按子图大小与结构自适应决定是否可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作较少系统回答"多模态输入何时、以何种方式真正改善VLM的知识图谱问答"，且往往混淆模型规模、问题复杂度与视觉呈现方式等因素。本文希望以细粒度、受控的实验厘清视觉提示在KGQA中的有效边界与作用机制。

Method: 构建统一受控评估框架：覆盖WebQSP与CWQ共1500个问题、6个视觉语言模型，在同一KGQA流水线内进行逐题（per-question）的文本提示 vs 文本+图可视化提示配对比较；以推理复杂度、模型规模、图像布局为自变量分析性能变化；采用混合效应模型与回归分析检验各因素的贡献；并比较多种渲染方式（纯图像、Graphviz层级布局、结构化程度较低的渲染、将大子图拆分为多条分离路径）。

Result: 附加图图像平均带来+1.85 F1提升，其中低复杂度问题的精确率提升显著（+4.65 F1）；多模态收益与模型规模关系不强，但对图布局高度敏感：仅图像设置导致性能大幅下降，Graphviz层级布局优于组织性差的渲染，将大子图拆分为分离路径收益最大。

Conclusion: 视觉提示主要通过提升低复杂度情境下的预测精确率来助力KGQA，前提是视觉设计符合认知与知觉约束；为兼顾准确率与推理效率，应采纳自适应视觉提示策略——仅对小规模、视觉稀疏且布局良好的子图附加图示意，避免在复杂查询上引入视觉噪声与token开销；并将结论与视觉记号设计理论相联系，为后续多模态VLM提示中的图编码设计提供原则性指导。

Abstract: We study when and how multimodal input improves knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) in large vision--language models. Using a controlled evaluation over 1,500 WebQSP and CWQ questions and six VLMs within a fixed KGQA pipeline, we compare text-only prompting against text-plus-graph visualization at the per-question level and analyze performance changes as a function of reasoning complexity, model size, and image layout. Adding a graph image yields an average gain of 1.85 F1 points, driven by strong precision improvements on low-complexity questions (+4.65 F1). Mixed-effects and regression analyses show that these multimodal gains do not depend strongly on model scale, but are highly sensitive to graph layout: image-only settings sharply degrade performance, hierarchical Graphviz layouts outperform less organized renderings, and decomposing large subgraphs into separated paths yields the largest improvements. Taken together, the results indicate that visual prompting primarily aids KGQA by enhancing prediction precision in low-complexity contexts, provided that visual design respects cognitive and perceptual constraints. To optimize both accuracy and inference efficiency, we argue for adaptive visual prompting policies that selectively append diagrams to small, visually sparse subgraphs with well-structured layouts, avoiding unnecessary visual clutter and token overhead on complex queries. We conclude by relating these findings to a visual notation design theory, outlining how principled diagram encoding can guide future multimodal VLM prompting.

</details>


### [125] [Reinforcement Learning based control of Cancer Chemotherapy treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12940)
*Jayakody Kankanamalage Chamani Shiranthika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习在癌症化疗序贯治疗决策中的应用：先用传统Q-learning构建化疗仿真，再提出基于模型架构的离线强化学习方案，结合Conservative Q-Learning（CQL）与Model-Based Offline Policy Optimization（MBOP），在真实人类结肠癌临床数据上生成最优治疗策略，实验表明该Chemo RL模型能有效处理化疗给药调度问题，可辅助初级肿瘤医生决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已从Atari、Go、Chess等复杂游戏扩展到现实场景建模，个性化医疗与动态治疗方案（DTR）是新兴研究方向。从观察性真实临床数据中估计最优动态治疗方案的需求日益增长，推动强化学习算法向更高级版本发展；化疗给药调度属于典型的序贯决策问题，适合用强化学习建模。

Method: ①综述用于序贯治疗最优策略生成的前沿强化学习技术，包括现有在线强化学习方法；②通过传统Q-learning构建癌症化疗仿真；③进一步采用离线强化学习生成最优策略，提出数据驱动方案，基于真实人类结肠癌临床数据；④核心创新为基于模型（model-based）架构的离线强化学习实现，融合Conservative Q-Learning与Model-Based Offline Policy Optimization算法（Chemo RL模型）。

Result: 根据实验结果与评估，所提出的Chemo RL模型能够较好地应对癌症化疗治疗调度问题，可作为支持性模型辅助初级肿瘤医生进行治疗决策。

Conclusion: 离线、基于模型的强化学习（CQL+MBOP）在真实临床数据上可行且有效，为化疗序贯治疗方案优化提供了数据驱动的实用框架，具备临床决策支持的潜力；摘要未报告具体量化指标（如生存期、剂量偏差等），相关验证细节有待补充。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning has achieved tremendous success in recent years. Extending its scope from the traditional complex games such as Atari, Go, Pokeman, and Chess, reinforcement learning has caught researchers' attention on modelling real-life scenarios. Personalized medicine and the dynamic treatment regimen are two such preliminary research fields; researchers are focusing their mindsets currently. Estimating the optimal dynamic treatment regimens from the observational real clinical data is being popularized among the researchers, widening its scope towards more advanced versions of reinforcement learning algorithms.
  This research is about a research study that focused on reinforcement learning techniques that have been approached in the context of cancer chemotherapy treatments. Primarily this thesis addresses the result of the study by means of the state-of-the-art on reinforcement learning techniques used in generating the optimal policy for sequential treatments, including the existing online reinforcement learning approaches. Thereby a simulation for cancer chemotherapy via traditional Q-learning would be presented. Secondly, the study will extend towards using offline reinforcement learning in the optimal policy generation. Thus, the proposed solution follows a data-driven approach, which works on the real human colon cancer clinical data.
  The novelty of the proposed solution resides in the implementation of an offline reinforcement learning approach by means of a model-based architecture using the conservative Q-learning and the model-based offline policy optimization algorithm. As per the experimental results and the evaluations, it was revealed that the proposed Chemo RL model caters well the problem of cancer chemotherapy treatment scheduling and serves as a supportive model for the junior oncologists in deciding their treatment decisions.

</details>


### [126] [AI Governance Should Prioritize Control and Knowledge Boundaries Over Limiting Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12941)
*Hendrik Luuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"能力回路"（IKC+KC+C）框架，将AI智能体现实影响分解为智能介导的发现、程序性知识与直接控制三条路径，并通过NK适应度景观仿真检验智能驱动"快速起飞"的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主行动AI智能体迅速普及使治理问题紧迫；同时"快速起飞"辩论往往将智能本身视为决定性变量，缺乏对影响路径的分解与形式化，需澄清智能、知识与控制各自的作用边界。

Method: 构建能力回路框架（IKC + KC + C），以经典快速起飞辩论为压力测试，形式化"智能介导快速起飞"的合取充分条件；将认知智能操作化为假设选择效率，在NK适应度景观上以随机仿真检验六种覆盖该合取链的情景，并辅以解析上界。

Result: 解析上界与仿真一致表明：智能驱动的加速受限于竞争者智能水平之比，与任务难度无关；出现"快速起飞困境"——大的加速倍率仅在假设空间含噪时成立，而绝对时间尺度仍受关键能力测试耗时约束，故大倍率不等于快速进展；当强制授权延迟相对智能体无产出搜索时间足够大时，制度性瓶颈对超级智能体减速效果尤为显著。

Conclusion: 主导性安全杠杆在于限制控制权与设定知识边界，而非单纯限制智能，此结论适用于任意能力水平的智能体系统；纯数字领域会削弱这些瓶颈，因此应支持刻意"把物理纳入回路"的架构设计。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of autonomously acting AI agents makes the governance of agentic systems urgent. We propose the competence circuit, summarized as IKC + KC + C, that decomposes an agent's real-world impact into three pathways: intelligence-mediated discovery, procedural know-how, and direct control. Using the canonical fast-takeoff debate as a stress test, we formalize the conditions for intelligence-mediated fast takeoff as a conjunction jointly sufficient for fast catastrophic supremacy, showing that most rely on rapid knowledge acquisition or control attainment rather than intelligence alone. We operationalize epistemic intelligence as hypothesis-selection efficiency and validate this framework via stochastic simulation on NK fitness landscapes across six scenarios spanning the conjunctive chain. An analytic bound supported by the simulations shows that intelligence-driven speedup is capped by the ratio of competing intelligence levels regardless of task difficulty. The simulations also expose a fast-takeoff dilemma: a large intelligence-driven speedup multiplier materializes only when the hypothesis space is noisy, but the absolute timescale remains constrained by the time required for capability-critical tests, so a large multiplier need not imply rapid progress. Simulations also indicate that institutional chokepoints disproportionately slow down superintelligent agents when mandatory authorization delays are large relative to the negligible time these agents spend on unproductive search. We argue that the dominant safety levers are restricting control and enforcing knowledge boundaries rather than limiting intelligence alone, and this applies to agentic systems at any capability level. Purely digital domains weaken these bottlenecks, strengthening the case for architectures that intentionally "put physics in the loop."

</details>


### [127] [Tabular Foundation Models vs Tuned Gradient Boosting for Telecom Churn: A Profit- and Calibration-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12942)
*Ismail Hossain Polas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在5个电信流失数据集、10个种子划分上，用调优GBDT重评TabPFN系列与TabICL v2：TabPFN-3.5在Cell2Cell(+1.9%)与Iranian(+7.6%)上显著获得更高EMP，但收益可能源于同一数据集的重采样；TabICL v2仅在Iranian上优于最佳调优GBDT，并对Orange严重低估流失率；上线障碍主要来自权重许可而非评分成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基准宣称表格基础模型(TFM)在客户流失预测上明显优于树集成，但其对比对象是未调参的GBDT，且仅评估排序类指标；而留存营销活动的真实判据是金额收益。因此需要以调优后的基线、以利润导向指标重新检验该结论。

Method: 对比TabPFN-2.5、TabPFN-3.5、TabPFN-3.5-Fast、TabICL v2与经Optuna调参的GBDT，在5个公开电信流失数据集、10个带种子的划分上评估；将问题拆为排序能力、期望最大利润(EMP)、概率驱动的阈值策略三层；采用考虑划分结构的检验并对20项预先声明的比较做Holm校正；并做更大调参预算与去重感知划分的稳健性检验。

Result: TabPFN-3.5相对最佳调优GBDT在Cell2Cell(1.9%)和Iranian(7.6%)上EMP显著更高，且对三个调优GBDT分别成立；Orange上10/10划分领先(14.3%)但Holm p=0.155不显著。TabICL v2仅在Iranian上胜过最佳GBDT，且对Orange在7.3%基础流失率上低估2.1个百分点。重调基线使原报告的6.1个PR-AUC点差距缩小至4.0点但未消失；更大调参预算与去重划分不改变主要结论。

Conclusion: TFM的利润优势存在但弱于此前宣传，且依赖数据集、统计上多为不显著，划分源自同一数据集的重采样，结论不能外推为独立运营商层面的证据。TabPFN-3.5权重的License v1.0许可是其从实验走向生产的主要障碍，而非评分成本。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) predict from a labelled context in a single forward pass. A recent benchmark reported that they decisively outperform tree ensembles for customer churn, but it compared them with untuned gradient-boosted trees (GBDTs) and scored only ranking metrics, while a retention campaign is judged in money. We re-evaluate TabPFN-2.5, TabPFN-3.5, TabPFN-3.5-Fast and TabICL v2 against Optuna-tuned GBDTs on five public telecom churn datasets over 10 seeded splits. We separate three questions: whether a model ranks churners well, whether that ranking earns more expected maximum profit (EMP), and whether its probabilities can drive a threshold policy. With a split-aware test and Holm correction over 20 pre-declared comparisons, TabPFN-3.5 earns significantly more EMP than the best tuned GBDT on Cell2Cell (1.9%) and Iranian (7.6%), and these two gains also hold against each of the three tuned GBDTs separately. On Orange it is ahead in all 10 splits (14.3%), but the gain is not significant after correction (Holm p = 0.155). The splits resample the same datasets, so these results do not stand for independent operators. TabICL v2, the earlier benchmark's leader, beats the best tuned GBDT only on Iranian, not all three of them, and underpredicts Orange churn by 2.1 points on a 7.3% base rate. On the datasets and splits where we tested them, a substantially larger tuning budget and a duplicate-aware split leave the main conclusions unchanged. Retuning the baseline cuts the earlier headline gap from 6.1 to 4.0 PR-AUC points without closing it. The licence of the TabPFN-3.5 weights we used (License v1.0), not scoring cost, is what stands between its gains and production use.

</details>


### [128] [SetPrint: Black-Box LLM Fingerprinting via Setwise Correspondence of Response Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12950)
*Jinmo Han,Ju Yeon Kang,Jimin Hong,Sunghwan Ahn,Yonghyeon Jun,Nam Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练黑盒指纹方法SetPrint，同时利用扰动响应变化的集合内关系与集合间对应关系，在LeaFBench上稳定优于现有免训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒指纹需仅凭输出判断目标模型是否源自源模型。对每个查询做扰动可得两个模型的响应变化集合，其中既有集合内变化关系、也有集合间变化对应关系两种互补信息，但现有方法未能同时充分利用两者。

Method: SetPrint为免训练方法：将每个响应变化表示为其所属集合平均变化的相对量（刻画集合内关系），再比较两模型在同一扰动下诱导的变化（刻画集合间对应），联合建模两类信息以判定模型间派生关系。

Result: 在LeaFBench上，SetPrint持续优于现有免训练黑盒指纹方法；消融分析表明性能提升同时归因于集合内关系与集合间对应关系的贡献。

Conclusion: 集合内与集合间两类信息联合使用是提升黑盒指纹有效性的关键，SetPrint提供了一种简单且无需训练的实现路径。

Abstract: Black-box LLM fingerprinting aims to determine whether a target model was derived from a source model when only the output responses to input queries are observable. One way to reveal this relationship is to perturb each query and observe the resulting responses of both models, yielding two corresponding sets of response variations. These sets provide two complementary sources of information: the relations among response changes within each set, and the correspondence of response changes between the two sets. Existing approaches, however, have not fully exploited both. We introduce \textbf{SetPrint}, a training-free method that jointly captures both within-set relations and between-set correspondence. SetPrint expresses each response change relative to the mean change of its own set, and then compares changes from the two models that were induced by the same perturbation. On LeaFBench, SetPrint consistently outperforms existing training-free black-box fingerprinting methods. Further analyses attribute these gains to both within-set relations and between-set correspondence.

</details>


### [129] [Agent Architecture Cost-Success Trade-offs: A Controlled Evaluation across Enterprise-Style SOPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12953)
*Subhrangshu Nandi,Hitesh Arora,Yunfei Bai,Udita Patel,Chirag Shah,Hannah Kwon,Wei Zhang,Weian Chen,Chloe Ruoqi Li,Alberto Sconfienza,Ayush Goyal,Yasmine Lee,Ming Xiong,Prathap Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定模型、任务与工具、仅变架构的受控实验中，比较四种 LLM Agent 架构（8 个实验组）于 13 个企业 SOP 上的表现，结论是：不存在通用最优架构，架构选择依赖 SOP；额外架构成本需由任务成功率增益证明；实现质量比"单/多智能体"选择更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业越来越多地用 LLM Agent 执行标准作业流程（SOP，即长流程、多分支、工具密集型工作流），但缺乏在同等条件下比较不同 Agent 架构的受控证据，导致架构选型缺少依据。论文旨在提供可复现的对照证据与选型指导。

Method: 控制变量实验：模型、任务、工具全部固定，仅改变架构。比较四种设计——函数调用单 Agent、技能增强 Agent、集中式多智能体编排、triage swarm（分诊式群体），共 8 个实验组；覆盖 13 个企业 SOP。同时提出"技能定义质量评分量表"，据此构建质量匹配的高、低两套技能语料库，以分离技能质量与架构的混淆效应。结果以任务成功率为主指标，并报告 token 消耗与延迟，含统计显著性检验。

Result: (1) 无通用最优架构：单个 SOP 上成功率从最难的不足 10% 到最简单的接近 100%；总体上各架构无可统计区分的差异，附加结构仅在复杂、多分支 SOP 上呈现有方向性但未达显著的正向趋势。(2) 成本需被收益证明：所有协同型实验组（多智能体或技能增强）消耗 token 为单 Agent 的 1.4–4.7 倍、延迟为 2.6–5.8 倍，而收益普遍偏小且很少显著，使单 Agent 成为高效默认方案；高质量技能可提升成功率（67.4% → 73.1%），低质量技能则低于基线。(3) 实现质量优先：一个看似合理的多智能体实现反而逊于单 Agent，但对其做针对性重构后，成为唯一显著优于基线的实验组（75.6% vs. 67.4%）。

Conclusion: 架构选型应以 SOP 复杂度为依据而非追求"最强架构"；引入多智能体或技能层须以可测量的成功率增益为其 token 与延迟开销正名；工程实现质量（而非单/多智能体的范式选择）是决定性能的关键变量，应优先投入实现与技能质量的打磨。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly use large language model agents to execute standard operating procedures (SOPs): long, branching, tool-intensive workflows. Yet there is little controlled evidence for choosing among competing agentic architectures. Holding the model, tasks, and tools fixed and varying only the architecture, we compare four designs (a function-calling single agent, a skill-augmented agent, centralized multi-agent orchestration, and a triage swarm; eight arms) across 13 enterprise SOPs, and introduce a skill-definition quality rubric to build matched high and low-quality skill corpora. Three takeaways emerge. (1)~\textbf{There is no universal best architecture; the right choice is SOP-dependent.} On a given SOP, task success ranges from under $10\%$ on the hardest to near $100\%$ on the easiest; no architecture is statistically separable in aggregate, and added structure only plausibly pays off on complex, branching SOPs (a directional, not yet significant, trend). (2)~\textbf{Added architecture cost must be justified by a task-success gain.} Every coordinated arm (multi-agent or skill-augmented) spends $1.4$--$4.7\times$ the tokens and $2.6$--$5.8\times$ the latency of the single agent for gains that are usually small and rarely significant, making the single agent the efficient default; high-quality skills do lift success (to $73.1\%$ from $67.4\%$) while degraded skills fall below it. (3)~\textbf{Implementation quality outweighs the single- versus multi-agent choice.} A plausible multi-agent implementation loses to the single agent, yet a targeted redesign of it becomes the only arm to significantly beat the baseline ($75.6\%$ vs.\ $67.4\%$).

</details>


### [130] [Physics2Weight: Direct Neural Network Architecture and Weight Generation from Physical Laws Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12954)
*Shogo Sato,Tomoyasu Shimada,Kazuhiko Murasaki,Takashi Sano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Physics2Weight (PtW) 框架，将权重空间编程(WSP)扩展到偏微分方程求解，通过直接生成网络权重并编辑架构实现无梯度优化求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型已支持权重空间编程(WSP)，可无需梯度优化直接生成网络权重，但将其扩展到物理问题仍具挑战，难点在于同时保证解的精度与物理一致性。

Method: PtW基于控制方程与初边值条件直接生成神经网络权重并编辑网络架构，引入物理约束提示(physics-constrained prompting)、物理引导的架构编辑(physics-guided architecture editing)，并结合WSP的迭代权重精化，在无梯度优化条件下提升精度与物理一致性。

Result: 在热传导、对流、Burgers和Allen–Cahn四个PDE上，求解误差低于传统物理信息神经网络(PINN)；但在非线性PDE上满足控制方程方面仍存在局限。

Conclusion: PtW验证了将权重空间编程用于PDE求解的可行性，在精度上优于PINN，但对非线性PDE的物理一致性（方程残差满足）仍有不足，是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled weight-space programming (WSP), which directly generates neural network weights without gradient-based optimization. However, extending WSP to physical problems remains challenging, particularly in achieving both solution accuracy and physical consistency. In this paper, we propose Physics2Weight (PtW), a framework that extends WSP to solving partial differential equations by directly generating neural network weights and editing architectures based on governing equations and initial and boundary conditions. PtW introduces physics-constrained prompting and physics-guided architecture editing, combined with iterative weight refinement from WSP, to improve solution accuracy and physical consistency without gradient-based optimization. Experiments on four PDEs, namely the heat, advection, Burgers', and Allen--Cahn equations, demonstrate lower solution errors than conventional physics-informed neural networks, while revealing limitations in satisfying the governing equations for nonlinear PDEs.

</details>


### [131] [Embodied Science Agents: Making Embodied Experimental Design Learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12958)
*Yihang Che,Zhongwei Yu,Yang Li,Xingyu Lu,Hongxia Yu,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出现有自驱动实验室（SDL）存在"语言精细化但物理接地缺失"的悖论，提出需在认知、实验、具身三个维度嵌入可学习性，并构建具身科学智能体ESA，在废水处理自主实验中将脱色率从82%提升至99.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型推动AI for Science走向"智能体化科学"，实现该愿景须打通计算推理与物理实验的闭环，SDL是关键技术载体。但现有SDL将大模型作为"无身体的规划器"输出符号化工作流交由独立机器人执行，虽提升了语言层面的复杂度，却切断了物理接地，从架构上阻碍了具身实验直觉的形成。

Method: 提出三轴可学习性框架：认知可学习性（学什么）、实验可学习性（在哪搜索）、具身可学习性（如何行动）。据此设计ESA，包含三个协同组件：数字智能体（基于记忆的推理与贝叶斯优化，提供认知与实验可学习性）、具身智能体（可泛化的仪器操控，提供具身可学习性）以及具身统一接口（实现无缝协同）。

Result: 在自主废水处理实验中，ESA在超过2亿种可能条件中进行搜索；经15轮连续实验，最终溶液透光率由82%提升至99.7%，残余染料浓度降低98.5%，实现近乎完全脱色；其习得的视觉运动策略可泛化至不同仪器摆放位置。

Conclusion: 将"可学习性"贯穿认知、实验与具身三轴、并统一数字智能与物理具身，是破解SDL现有悖论的有效路径；ESA验证了该思路在真实化学实验中的可行性，为下一代面向自主科学发现的SDL提供了有前景的范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large models have accelerated AI for Science toward agentic science, where AI agents autonomously conduct scientific discovery. Realising this vision requires closing the loop between computational reasoning and physical experimentation, positioning self-driving laboratories (SDLs) as critical enablers. However, existing SDLs exhibit a fundamental paradox: foundation model integration has increased linguistic sophistication while severing physical grounding. By treating models as disembodied planners that output symbolic workflows for separate robotic systems, current architectures inherently block the development of embodied experimental intuition. We argue that overcoming this paradox requires embedding \emph{learnability} along three interdependent axes: cognitive learnability (learning what to investigate), experimental learnability (learning where to search), and embodied learnability (learning how to act). Building on this view, we introduce the Embodied Science Agent (ESA), which unifies digital intelligence with physical embodiment through three synergistic components: a Digital Agent that provides cognitive and experimental learnability via memory-based reasoning and Bayesian optimisation, an Embodied Agent that provides embodied learnability via generalisable instrument control, and an Embodied Unified Interface for seamless coordination. We validated ESA in autonomous wastewater treatment experiments, navigating over 200 million possible conditions. Through 15 continuous rounds, ESA raised the final solution transmittance from 82\% to 99.7\%, corresponding to a 98.5\% reduction in residual dye concentration and near-complete decolourisation, while its learned visuomotor policies generalised across varying instrument placements, a promising step toward next-generation SDLs for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [132] [Monitoring One Channel Is Not Communication Control: Causal Evidence for Adaptive Reallocation of Joint Message--Route Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12959)
*Liu Hung Ming,Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在端到端博弈中，部分监控会改变智能体联合"消息—路由"编码的分配（选择性迁移至影子路由），但不会消除通信本身或其语义性；完整监控仅移除了大部分选择性迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分"监控是否改变通信方式"与"监控是否消除通信动机"这两件事，检验可见通道监控下学习智能体的行为变化。

Method: 发送者与接收者在无通信监督下学习离散协议与路由策略；两批各20个种子的独立实验；对比无监控/部分在线覆盖/完整覆盖；采用在线部分覆盖与影子路由占比测量；冻结干预（消息—路由联合打乱、仅路由、结果前新鲜确认）；另设修正的"显式沉默成本"实验（18/20完全沉默）并检验严格完全抑制门控。

Result: 两批20种子中分别为20/20与19/20形成协议。部分覆盖使影子路由占比相对无监控提升.701［.574,.826］与.575［.412,.737］，而任务准确率保持.905/.913，冻结消息的带符号贡献保持.592/.563。完整覆盖移除大部分选择性迁移但未移除通信。冻结干预显示路由具语义性：联合打乱改变.668的接收者预测，仅路由保留.388准确率（随机为.25）。修正沉默成本使18/20策略完全沉默、平均发送降至.050，但严格完全抑制门控因两个种子保留通信而失败；语义规避与操作性门控亦未通过。

Conclusion: 证据支持部分监控下联合消息—路由编码的自适应再分配，而非开放世界路由发现、涌现加密或普遍性防御。

Abstract: Monitoring a visible channel can change how learned agents communicate without removing why communication is useful. We test this distinction in an end-to-end game where a sender and receiver learn a discrete protocol and route policy without communication supervision. In two independent 20-seed batches, 20/20 and 19/20 protocols form. Online partial coverage increases shadow-route share over no monitoring by .701 [.574,.826] and .575 [.412,.737], while task accuracy remains .905/.913 and signed frozen message contribution remains .592/.563. Complete coverage removes most selective migration without removing communication. Frozen interventions, including a fresh pre-outcome confirmation, show that route is semantic: joint message--route shuffling changes .668 of receiver predictions and route alone retains .388 accuracy versus .25 chance. A corrected explicit-silence cost makes 18/20 policies fully silent and lowers mean sending to .050, but its strict complete-suppression gate fails because two seeds retain communication. Failed semantic-evasion and operational gates further bound the result. The evidence supports adaptive reallocation of a joint message--route code under partial monitoring, not open-world route discovery, emergent encryption, or a universal defense.

</details>


### [133] [IIT Patna at "ICPR 2026 TVRID Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12960)
*Abhinav Kumar,Swagatam Pati,Advait Deshmukh,Aditya Mehrotra,Bibek Das,Kumari Priya,Utsav Kumar Nareti,Saba Akram,Chandranath Adak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛论文：基于顶视深度相机的隐私保护行人重识别（Re-ID），报告两支前十名队伍的方法，深度轨道上取得93.51% mAP与90.42% CMC@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶视相机可避免采集正面外观，深度传感进一步削弱颜色等身份线索，从而在隐私保护前提下实现行人重识别；竞赛旨在系统评测该设定下跨视角（平视、上坡、下坡、斜视）与跨模态（RGB/Depth）的识别性能。

Method: 使用4台同步Intel RealSense D455相机采集86个身份的RGB与Depth流；指标为mAP与CMC@1，覆盖同相机内/外、上坡-下坡、平视-斜视场景。论文介绍两支前十队伍方法：Methodology 1（abhinavkumar03）聚焦深度表征与监督，Methodology 2（sjkdgfhl_）同时处理RGB与Depth轨道。

Result: 147人注册、33份最终提交、31人进入排名。Methodology 1在Depth上93.51% mAP/90.42% CMC@1，列28人中第4；Methodology 2在RGB上99.63%/99.35%，Depth上86.26%/80.34%。二者均显著超过57.19%的Depth轨道平均mAP，Methodology 1较Methodology 2高7.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 深度表征与针对性监督对隐私保护Re-ID至关重要；深度模态可作为顶视Re-ID的有效且隐私友好的主干信息源。摘要未说明具体网络结构与训练细节，仅给出竞赛性结论。

Abstract: Privacy-aware person re-identification (Re-ID) from top-view cameras replaces frontal appearance with anthropometric shape, while depth sensing further reduces color-based identity cues. The ICPR 2026 TVRID competition evaluates this setting using 86 identities captured by four synchronized Intel RealSense D455 cameras with RGB and Depth streams across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. Performance is measured using mAP and CMC@1 across within-camera in/out, ascent-descent, and flat-oblique scenarios. The competition had 147 registered participants and 33 final submissions. Three teams from IIT Patna participated: 'abhinavkumar03' and 'sjkdgfhl_' ranked 5th and 7th among 31 ranked participants, and 'vivek katuri' reached 77.67% CMC@1 / 85.20% mAP on the RGB track. This paper presents the methods of the two top-10 teams. Methodology 1 (abhinavkumar03) achieves 93.51% mAP and 90.42% CMC@1 on Depth, ranking 4th of 28, while Methodology 2 (sjkdgfhl_) reaches 99.63%/99.35% on RGB and 86.26%/80.34% on Depth. Both substantially outperform the 57.19% track-average Depth mAP, with Methodology 1 yielding a 7.3-point gain over Methodology 2. These results demonstrate the importance of depth representation and supervision for privacy-preserving Re-ID.

</details>


### [134] [RBLA+: Efficient Rank-Specific LoRA Adapter Extraction for Federated Fine-Tuning with Heterogeneous Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12961)
*Shuaijun Chen,Omid Tavallaie,Xin Chen,Wentao Gao,Nguyen Tran,Albert Y. Zomaya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RBLA+ 在异质 LoRA 秩的联邦微调中，用紧凑 QR/core-SVD 分解在不形成稠密更新的前提下，为每个客户端提取秩受限、Frobenius 误差最小的 LoRA 适配器，并在上传/广播中统一缩放约定，精度优于 RBLA 且提取比稠密部分 SVD 更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
