Our code is available at https://github.com/ydyjya/Neuron-Level-Specialization-in-MoE

</details>


### [201] [Personalized Memory Rubrics: Learning What to Remember per User via Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14017)
*Yupeng Han,Kai Zhang,Shuochen Liu,Ze Liu,Xianquan Wang,Zhihong Pan,Xiangyu Zhao,Qi Liu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Personalized Memory Rubrics (PMR) 框架，将长程个性化智能体的终端反馈转化为细粒度、逐步、逐用户的信用分配，实现记忆更新策略与评估量规的协同演化，且部署时零推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程个性化智能体需持续决定记忆的增、改、删，但学习最优更新策略困难：1）传统训练仅依赖终端问答准确率，反馈信号粗糙，对所有中间更新一视同仁；2）更新效用本质上是用户特定的，相同记忆操作在不同用户画像下可能产生相反效果，而现有方法依赖全局共享的评估标准，至多提供单一标量中间奖励，无法刻画这种异质性。

Method: PMR 将评估分解为通用层（捕获跨画像的失败模式）与逐用户自适应层（以通用基线之上的专门 delta 建模个体特质）。通过 Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment 在 token 级别重分配信用：在成功轨迹上选择性放大被验证的决策，在失败轨迹上回退到标准策略优化以避免不可靠归因。量规与记忆策略在闭环中协同演化，并带有反循环性保证。

Result: 在四个基准上的大量实验表明，PMR 相对强记忆增强基线取得一致性提升；同时因部署时无需量规或外部评判器，推理开销为零。

Conclusion: 细粒度、用户自适应的量规式信用分配可有效改进长程个性化记忆更新策略，且不引入额外推理成本，为个性化智能体的记忆学习提供了实用范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon personalized agents maintain an evolving memory by continuously deciding what to add, modify, or discard. However, learning an optimal update policy remains challenging. Conventional training relies solely on terminal question-answering accuracy, yielding a coarse feedback signal that treats all intermediate updates identically. Furthermore, update utility is inherently user-specific, as identical memory actions may yield opposite effects across different user personas. Existing methods fail to address this heterogeneity, relying on globally shared evaluation standards with at most a single scalar intermediate reward. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{Personalized Memory Rubrics} (\textbf{PMR}), a framework that translates terminal feedback into fine-grained, per-step, and per-user credit assignment. PMR decomposes evaluation into a universal layer capturing cross-persona failure patterns and a per-user adaptive layer modeling individual traits as a specialized delta over the universal baseline. Through \textbf{Rubric-Guided Credit Assignment}, PMR redistributes credit at the token level, selectively amplifying validated decisions on successful trajectories while falling back to standard policy optimization on failed ones to avoid unreliable attribution. The rubric and memory policy \textbf{co-evolve} in a closed loop with anti-circularity guarantees. Crucially, PMR incurs zero inference overhead, operating without any rubric or external judge during deployment. Extensive experiments across four benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over strong memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [202] [Hx2Dx: From Anamnesis to Diagnosis - A Multimodal Arena for Conversational Clinical Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14122)
*Federico Ravenda,Mirko Cesarini,Daniele Montagnani,Volodymyr Karpenko,Antonietta Mira,Andrea Raballo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式诊断基准 Hx2Dx ARENA（1000 例 NEJM 病例，从病史采集/查体到检查直至诊断），发现所有 LLM 在交互式场景下诊断准确率均低于静态场景，主要瓶颈在于"会问什么"的假设驱动式信息采集能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 诊断评测多为静态：直接给定已凝练关键发现的小病例或病例报告，只衡量"在已选证据上的推理"，忽略了获取证据的提问过程。而真实临床诊断是迭代式问诊（病史采集、查体、开检查、解释结果）逐步缩小鉴别诊断的过程，因此需要交互式评测来衡量模型的主动信息采集能力。

Method: 构建 Hx2Dx ARENA 交互式基准，包含来自 NEJM 的 1000 个临床病例；每个病例模拟一次完整诊疗接触，从初始主诉开始，经病史采集（Hx）、体格检查到诊断性检查，最终给出诊断（Dx）。在此基准上以多种配置评测开源与闭源 LLM，并与"一次性给出完整病例"的静态设置进行对比。

Result: 交互设置下各模型表现差异显著；且所有模型相对静态设置均出现诊断准确率下降。提问质量是模型间差距与准确率损失的主要来源：问题越有针对性、越能构建完整临床图景，诊断表现越好。

Conclusion: LLM 在相关信息齐备时推理能力良好，但在假设驱动的信息采集中（即"知道该问什么才能知道"）仍受限。缩小这一"问诊能力"缺口是其安全融入真实诊断流程的前提。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve expert-level diagnostic accuracy in static evaluations, where the model receives a curated vignette or case report that already condenses the diagnostically relevant findings. Such evaluations measure inference over already selected evidence; the evaluations do not account for the inquiry used to obtain it. In clinical practice, however, diagnosis is an iterative process of inquiry. Clinicians take the patient's history, or anamnesis (Hx), through targeted questioning, examine the patient, and request and interpret tests, progressively narrowing the differential until they reach a final diagnosis (Dx). We introduce Hx2Dx ARENA, an interactive benchmark of 1'000 clinical cases from the New England Journal of Medicine (NEJM). Each case is a simulated encounter that proceeds from initial presentation through history taking and physical examination to diagnostic workup. We evaluate open- and closed-source LLMs under multiple configurations. Performance varies considerably across models in the interactive setting, and every model loses diagnostic accuracy relative to the static setting, where the full case is presented upfront. The ability to ask the right questions accounts for much of both the gap between models and the loss of accuracy: diagnosis improves as questions become more targeted and build a more complete clinical picture. LLMs thus reason well once the relevant data are available but remain limited in hypothesis-driven information gathering, in knowing what to ask in order to know. Closing this gap is a prerequisite for their safe integration into real-world diagnostic workflows.

</details>


### [203] [BARS: Budget-Aware Sequential Sampling with Refusal for Majority Identification under Unknown Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14171)
*Shenxi Liu,Yiwei Li,Mingyang Zhao,Yuhang Tian,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出BARS框架，将LLM自一致性中的提前停止问题形式化为"未知分布+预算+目标错误率"下的多数识别问题，给出带理论保证的certified模式和基于经验校准的practical模式，实现按题目难度自适应的采样与拒答决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复采样+多数投票能有效提升LLM自一致性，但成本高；已有的提前停止变体虽能省成本，却无法从答案分布中给出正确的置信度估计，即缺乏对"是否已经可靠地识别出多数答案"的原则性判断依据。

Method: 将问题形式化为带预算与目标错误率的未知分布下的多数识别（majority identification），提出BARS顺序规则：通过检验top-2答案概率间隙（top-two gap）进行序贯认证，输出三种结果——提前停止、预算受限的多数投票、或在可行性无法建立时拒答（refusal）。certified模式对联合回答错误率与"拒答本可在预算内处理的实例"给出可证界；practical模式（实验中采用）用经验校准替代这些界。

Result: 在数千个种子的已知分布合成实例上，复现了由难度驱动的行为，并量化了certified模式的理论代价。GSM8K上practical模式相比固定8次采样减少22.3%的采样量（同一候选池，含被拒答问题），代价是拒答18.8%的问题。MATH上输出答案准确率提升至82.5%，代价是对模型本身无法解决的难题中有半数诚实拒答。

Conclusion: BARS将自一致性采样转化为有统计保证的序贯决策问题，用"认证—投票—拒答"三态输出替代不可靠的启发式停止；拒答被严格界定为对该规则与预算的陈述而非题目不可答，从而为实践者提供何时扩大预算或更换模型的明确信号，在效率与准确性之间取得可度量的权衡。

Abstract: Repeated sampling with majority selection is an effective means of enforcing self-consistency in large language models. Recently, a variant has also been introduced that terminates early to save costs when the output distribution is favorable. But it still cannot yield a correct confidence estimate from the answer distribution. For this issue, we formalize this as majority identification under an unknown distribution with a budget and a target error rate, and propose BARS, a sequential rule certifying the top-two gap and returning an early stop, a budgeted majority vote, or a refusal when feasibility cannot be established. A refusal is a statement about the rule and its budget, not a claim that a question is unanswerable, and it tells practitioners when to widen the budget or switch models. A certified mode carries proven bounds on the joint answering error and on refusing an instance the budget could handle, while a practical mode, used in our experiments, replaces them with empirical calibration. On synthetic instances drawn from known distributions over thousands of seeds, the difficulty-driven behavior is recovered and the price of the certified mode is measured.On GSM8K the practical mode draws 22.3\% fewer samples than fixed-8 sampling over the same pool, refused questions included, at the cost of declining 18.8\% of the questions. On MATH, it greatly improves the accuracy of its output answers into 82.5\% at the cost of honestly refusing to answer half of the difficult questions that the model itself is unable to solve.

</details>


### [204] [Cross-Border Fulfillment Center Selection via Doubly Robust Estimation and Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14174)
*Kriti Mahajan,Patrick Bloebaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨境履约中心（XB FC）选择模型：融合预测、双稳健估计与线性规划的多阶段优化框架，为每条源-目的运输弧选择最优源国FC子集；在大型电商三条生产级国际航线上，相较人工专家策略实现配送时效降低8.5–51.1%、物流成本降低4.4–48.3%，5/5达标率最高提升6.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨境电商需在既有国内网络固定不变的约束下，为每条跨境运输弧选择源国发货FC。该决策需同时权衡时效、成本、利润与发货量等多目标，人工专家规则难以在高维组合空间内取得有效权衡，缺乏系统化的多目标优化方法。

Method: 多阶段优化框架：①需求/时效预测；②双稳健（doubly robust）因果估计以评估候选FC的潜在结果；③线性规划在固定国内网络约束下选择源FC子集，目标为最小化配送时间与成本、最大化利润与发货量。评估采用聚合离线回放（offline replay）与货运级反事实最近邻匹配两种方式，并以Pareto前沿分析对比单目标排序策略与无约束LP。

Result: 基于某大型电商12个月货运数据、三条生产规模国际航运走廊：相对人工专家定义的FC选择策略，配送时间降低8.5–51.1%，物流成本降低4.4–48.3%，5/5目标合规率（5天、5美元）最高提升6.5个百分点；Pareto分析显示模型在效率-再分配前沿上稳定优于单目标排序策略与无约束LP；全部结果统计显著。

Conclusion: 多阶段优化（预测+双稳健估计+LP）可有效替代人工规则进行跨境源FC选择，在时效、成本与服务水平多目标间取得更优Pareto权衡，并在生产规模数据上验证了显著且稳健的收益，具备落地价值。

Abstract: We introduce the cross-border (XB) fulfillment center (FC) Selection Model: a multi-stage optimization framework combining forecasting, doubly robust estimation, and linear programming to select optimal source FCs. For each source-destination shipping arc, the objective is to choose a subset of source-country FCs that minimize delivery time and cost while maximizing profit and shipped units under a fixed domestic network. We evaluate the model on three production-scale international shipping corridors for a large e-commerce company using 12 months of shipment data, employing both aggregate offline replay and shipment-level counterfactual nearest-neighbor matching. Across all shipping corridors, the XB FC Selection Model achieves 8.5-51.1% reductions in delivery time and 4.4-48.3% reductions in logistics cost relative to human expert-defined FC selection policies, with up to 6.5 percentage-point improvements in 5/5 target compliance (5 days, $5 USD). Pareto analysis shows the model consistently dominates single-objective ranking strategies and unconstrained LP on the efficiency-reallocation frontier, with all results statistically significant.

</details>


### [205] [An Augmented Lagrangian Approach to Lifted Bellman Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14183)
*Jongchan Park,Hyukjun Yang,Narim Jeong,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用乘子法（method of multipliers）求解Lifted Bellman Linear Programming (LBLP)，将ALBUM中固定的铰链惩罚系数改为由对偶上升学习的乘子，仅保留一个惩罚参数ρ，提出AL-LBLP。在无需目标网络、单个Q与V的条件下，best-of-N版本在50个state-based OGBench任务上平均成功率60.1%，长视野任务humanoidmaze-giant达81.5%、antmaze-giant达79.8%，且batch size增至8192仍不崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD critic依赖自举目标并通常需要目标网络；LBLP虽将离线RL的样本内最优值表述为数据集上的线性规划，但其神经求解器ALBUM把约束松弛为固定系数的铰链惩罚，理论保证只是"预先选定系数下的条件性保证"，系数需手动调参且缺乏自适应平衡。

Method: 用乘子法求解同一LBLP：约束系数变为通过对偶上升学习的乘子，仅保留单一惩罚参数ρ。凸情形下不动点即LBLP的KKT对，任一驻点处约束权重与目标精确平衡。critic可支撑Gaussian actor、flow actor与best-of-N采样；针对Gaussian actor提出优势归一化的DDPG+BC目标。整体仅用单个Q、单个V，且不使用目标网络。

Result: AL-LBLP + best-of-N在50个state-based OGBench任务上平均成功率60.1%；长视野任务上humanoidmaze-giant达81.5%、antmaze-giant达79.8%；batch size扩展至8192时不出现性能崩溃。

Conclusion: 以乘子法替代固定惩罚系数，使LBLP的约束权重自适应于对偶上升并在驻点处精确平衡目标，从而在无目标网络、单Q单V的简洁结构下取得强性能与良好的批量扩展性，为离线RL的LP式critic提供了有KKT依据的实用求解方案。

Abstract: Temporal-difference critics regress onto a bootstrapped target and usually need a target network. Lifted Bellman Linear Programming (LBLP) instead expresses the in-sample optimal values of offline RL as the solution to a linear program over the dataset. Its neural solver ALBUM relaxes the constraints into hinge penalties with fixed coefficients, and its guarantees are conditions on coefficients chosen in advance. We solve the same program with the method of multipliers: the coefficients become multipliers learned by dual ascent, with one penalty parameter $ρ$. In the convex case the fixed points are the KKT pairs of LBLP, and at every stationary point the applied constraint weights balance the objective exactly. The critic can serve a Gaussian actor, a flow actor, and best-of-N sampling, and for the Gaussian actor we propose an advantage-normalized DDPG+BC objective. With a single $Q$ and $V$ and no target network, our AL-LBLP with best-of-N reaches 60.1\% average success on the 50 state-based OGBench tasks. On long-horizon tasks, it reaches 81.5% on humanoidmaze-giant and 79.8% on antmaze-giant. It does not collapse up to batch size 8192.

</details>


### [206] [Joint Quantization and Dynamic Partitioning for Context-Aware Edge Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14196)
*Zhu Jiaqi,Jain Prakriti,Katare Dewant,Kumar Saurabh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向车联网（CAV）的边端协同编排框架，联合优化ViT模型分割点、逐层量化位宽与执行放置，以MILP在时延与精度约束下最小化计算与通信能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV上部署ViT需联合管理计算与V2V通信；静态分割推理采用固定切分点，无法适应时变链路带宽、处理负载与多流并发流量。

Method: 将联合分割与混合精度分配建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），同时决策模型分割点、每层量化位宽和异构车载计算节点上的执行放置，目标为最小化计算与通信能耗，受端到端时延与任务质量约束。在Jetson Orin Nano(8GB)、Orin NX(16GB)、AGX Orin(32GB)上以OPV2V多流负载（K∈{2,4,6,8}）评测。

Result: 相对Uniform INT8分割基线，V2V通信能耗降低33.3%–36.0%；K=8时端到端任务完成时延在Orin Nano上降低34.0%、Orin NX上降低33.1%；相对FP16执行，mAP50检测精度仅下降3.1–4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 动态联合的分割与混合精度编排可在多流车载场景下显著降低通信能耗与时延，同时将精度损失控制在可接受范围内，优于静态均匀量化分割方案。

Abstract: Deploying vision transformer models across connected and automated vehicles (CAVs) requires joint management of computation and Vehicle-to-Vehicle (V2V) communication. Static split-inference methods use fixed partition points and cannot adapt to time-varying link bandwidth, processing load, and concurrent multi-stream traffic. This paper proposes an edge orchestration framework that jointly selects model partition points, per-layer quantization bitwidths, and execution placement across heterogeneous vehicular compute nodes. The joint partitioning and mixed-precision allocation problem is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP) that minimizes compute and communication energy subject to end-to-end latency and task-quality constraints. The framework is evaluated on Jetson Orin Nano (8~GB), Jetson Orin NX (16~GB), and Jetson AGX Orin (32~GB) nodes using OPV2V-driven multi-stream workloads with $K \in \{2,4,6,8\}$. Across the evaluated configurations, the proposed formulation reduces V2V communication energy by $33.3$--$36.0\%$ relative to a Uniform INT8 split. At $K=8$, end-to-end task completion latency is reduced by $34.0\%$ on the Orin Nano and $33.1\%$ on the Orin NX relative to the same baseline, while limiting the reduction in 3D detection accuracy to $3.1$--$4.1$ percentage points in $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ relative to FP16 execution.

</details>


### [207] [Breakdown of Autoregressive Multi-Step Reasoning Under Concentrated vs. Diffuse Attention Head Dropout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14209)
*Karol Struniawski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文借鉴神经科学中清醒期皮层“off-state”现象，研究注意力头掩蔽在时间上聚集（burst）与均匀分散（diffuse）时对LLM多步推理的影响，发现集中的扰动显著更强地破坏GSM8K推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哺乳动物新皮层在长时清醒中出现局部、短暂的神经元静默（off-states）；作者希望检验类似“时空集中式”扰动是否同样会不成比例地破坏自回归LLM的顺序推理，从而为Transformer容错性提供实证基准。

Method: 在GSM8K上选取250对派生样本，对Qwen3-8B与LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct两个8B模型比较两类等预算扰动：集中式注意力头掩蔽突发 vs. 逐token i.i.d. 扩散dropout（onset参数B=0.10，约覆盖5.7%脆弱头-词元体积）；并用分层五分位匹配把相同累计预算均匀分散到各生成步。另做N=100探索性因子实验，分离单步dropout密度（多token连续性）两因素；辅以McNemar精确检验与错误类型分析。

Result: 集中突发致显著退化：Qwen3-8B由91.2%降至78.8%（Δ=-12.4pp，McNemar p=5.5e-6），LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct由84.0%降至76.8%（Δ=-7.2pp，p=0.0198）；同等预算均匀分散后精度恢复至91.2%与84.0%。因子实验中单步dropout密度与急性下降相关（-10.0pp，p_holm=0.0425），多token连续性呈不显著复合趋势（-7.0pp，p_holm=0.1892）。错误分析：Qwen的失败多为格式内推导错误（条件精度下降-11.4pp）；LLaMA有22.2%的不一致损失缺少最终答案行，条件差距收窄至-5.0pp。

Conclusion: 自回归推理链对集中式结构性扰动尤为脆弱，等预算下“聚集”比“分散”危害大得多；结果为Transformer架构的容错性与鲁棒性评估提供了可复现的经验基准，并提示训练/推理中应避免时间上高度聚集的组件失效。

Abstract: During extended wakefulness, mammalian neocortical networks experience localized periods of transient neuronal silence termed off-states. Here, we examine how analogous spatiotemporally concentrated disruptions affect sequential reasoning in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs). Evaluating 250 paired derivations on the GSM8K benchmark across two 8B models (Qwen3-8B and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct), we contrast clustered bursts of attention head masking against budget-matched diffuse i.i.d. dropout. Concentrated bursts induce substantial reasoning degradation: at onset parameter $\mathcal{B}=0.10$ (scheduling $\sim 5.7\%$ of vulnerable head-token volume), Qwen3-8B accuracy drops from $91.2\%$ under diffuse noise to $78.8\%$ under burst dropout ($Δ= -12.4$ percentage points [pp], exact McNemar $p = 5.5 \times 10^{-6}$), while LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct drops from $84.0\%$ to $76.8\%$ ($Δ= -7.2$ pp, $p = 0.0198$). Conversely, dispersing the identical cumulative budget uniformly across generation steps via stratified quintile matching yields substantially milder degradation (retaining $91.2\%$ in Qwen and $84.0\%$ in LLaMA). In an exploratory factorial dissociation ($N=100$), single-step dropout density is associated with an acute drop in a single schedule draw ($-10.0$ pp on Qwen, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.0425$), while multi-token continuity exhibits an inconclusive compounding trend ($-7.0$ pp, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.1892$). Error analysis reveals that Qwen failures reflect derivation errors within formatted responses (conditional accuracy drop of $-11.4$ pp), whereas in LLaMA $22.2\%$ of discordant losses lack a final answer format line (conditional gap narrows to $-5.0$ pp). These findings demonstrate that autoregressive reasoning chains are particularly vulnerable to concentrated structural disruptions, providing empirical benchmarks for fault tolerance in transformer architectures.

</details>


### [208] [A Cross-Scenario Survey and Analysis of Self-Evolving Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14256)
*Jingjing Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，将多模态智能体的自进化机制置于统一框架下，考察其在多模态推理、多模态生成、GUI与Web导航、具身操作四类场景间的非对称迁移问题，并将各机制刻画为以任务结构资源换取稀缺要素的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体自改进机制均在单一场景内设计与评估，其适用前提未经过跨场景迁移检验，导致机制的可迁移性缺乏系统说明，跨场景迁移呈现明显非对称性且成因不明。

Method: 提出四条判据与"自指"差异项来形式化自进化多模态智能体；以"经验生成—反馈获取—更新巩固"三阶段自进化闭环为分析框架，依据rollout数量上限与反馈保真度上限定位各场景；在每个场景内对比自进化循环与固定循环，剥离场景固有局限与设计固有局限；进而将每个机制重构为"以任务结构资源（如输出等价性、可校验结果）为前提提升某稀缺要素"的权衡，并给出可迁移、有条件、受阻、冗余四类判定。

Result: 形成覆盖四类场景的机制迁移判定表：识别出稀缺要素与结构资源在相反迁移方向上的不同组合是非对称性的来源，并指出若干判定目前仍未经实证检验。

Conclusion: 机制迁移的非对称性源于不同场景对稀缺要素与结构资源的组合差异；论文提出可采用匹配预算的测量方案来检验并可能推翻现有判定，为自进化智能体的跨场景设计与评估提供统一视角。

Abstract: Recent work shows that multimodal agents increasingly self-improve without recurring human annotation. However, each self-improvement mechanism is designed and evaluated within a single scenario, leaving its preconditions unverified under cross-scenario transfer. This survey investigates the structural source of asymmetric mechanism transfer across four scenarios: multimodal reasoning, multimodal generation, GUI and Web navigation, and embodied manipulation. We first formalize the self-evolving multimodal agent by four criteria and a differentia of self-reference, and adopt the three-stage self-evolving loop of experience generation, feedback acquisition, and update consolidation as our analytical framework to position the scenarios along ceilings on rollout count and feedback fidelity. Comparing self-evolving with fixed loops within each scenario separates the limitations that the scenario imposes from those inherent in the self-evolving design. We then recast each mechanism as a trade-off that raises a scarce factor by presupposing a task-structural resource, such as output equivalence or checkable outcomes, and judge it portable, conditional, blocked, or redundant across scenarios. We attribute the asymmetry to divergent combinations of scarce factors and resources across opposite transfer directions, and identify the matched-budget measurements that could overturn these still untested verdicts.

</details>


### [209] [Recurrent Modular Structure in Spiking Neural Network Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14262)
*Vikram Ramavarapu,Himanshu Udupi,Ashish Gautam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出社区循环脉冲神经网络（CoRe SNN）：将循环网络划分为功能固定的社区，控制器社区接收刺激、每个动作对应一个社区、潜在社区承载内部状态；脉冲活动表现为社区间"行走"，到达社区即动作。局部三因子学习，并用动态模块化（新规则Map-STDP）、部分模块化（SBM初始化）与完全模块化（封隔）三种方式获得社区。在CartPole上Map-STDP提升学习、可压缩性更高；以循环动作社区替代TD-STDP actor-critic的前馈actor，在CartPole与LunarLander上优于、Acrobot上持平前馈网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经回路具有模块化且循环连接的结构（密集社区、社区间稀疏连接），而用于强化学习的脉冲神经网络大多为前馈结构；在循环SNN中如何获得功能模块、模块能带来什么收益尚不清楚，且缺乏与皮层微回路更接近、能量更高效且生物可解释的替代方案。

Method: 构建CoRe SNN：循环网络划分为固定角色社区（控制器社区、每动作一个社区、潜在状态社区），动作为社区间脉冲"行走"的终点；全部学习为局部三因子规则。比较三种模块化获得方式：(1) 动态模块化——新提出的Map-STDP学习规则在训练中促进模块结构；(2) 部分模块化——随机块模型(SBM)初始化部分施加模块性；(3) 完全模块化——封隔(sealing)完全强制。并在三种脉冲神经元模型、CartPole及TD-STDP actor-critic框架（替代前馈actor）上评估。

Result: CartPole上，跨三种脉冲神经元模型，Map-STDP相对无该规则的同一网络提升学习效果，并在其中两个模型上匹配完全模块化（封隔社区）表现，且使观测到的脉冲行走更可压缩。用循环动作社区替代TD-STDP actor-critic的前馈actor后，在CartPole与LunarLander上优于前馈网络，在Acrobot上与之持平。模块化强度依赖任务：完全模块化在Acrobot上收益最大，而LunarLander上动态、部分与完全三种方式表现相近。

Conclusion: 循环模块化脉冲网络可匹配甚至超越前馈方法，同时提升生物可解释性与能量效率；模块化施加强度应依任务调节，动态模块化（Map-STDP）可作为无需硬性约束的有效折中。

Abstract: Biological neural circuits are both modular and recurrent, with dense communities sparsely linked to one another. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) for reinforcement learning are usually feed-forward, and it is unclear how to obtain functional modules in a recurrent SNN or what they buy. In this work, we introduce a class of spiking networks, called Community Recurrent SNNs (CoRe SNNs): recurrent networks partitioned into communities with fixed roles. A controller community receives the stimulus, each action has its own community, and latent communities carry internal state. Spiking activity is then a walk between communities, and the action is the community the walk reaches. All learning is local and three-factor, and the recurrent communities are closer to cortical microcircuits than a feed-forward actor. We compare three regimes for obtaining the communities: dynamic modularity, in which a new learning rule we introduce, Map-STDP, encourages modular structure during learning; partial modularity, in which a stochastic block model (SBM) initialization enforces it partly; and full modularity, in which sealing enforces it fully. On CartPole, across three spiking neuron models, Map-STDP improves learning over the same network without it matches full modularity (sealed communities) on two of the three models, and makes the observed spike walk more compressible. Replacing the feed-forward actor of a TD-STDP actor-critic with recurrent action communities improves on the feed-forward network on CartPole and LunarLander, and matches it on Acrobot. How strongly to enforce modularity depends on the task: full modularity helps most on Acrobot, while on LunarLander the dynamic, partial and full regimes perform alike. Overall, these recurrent modular networks can match or even improve upon feed-forward approaches, with improvements in bio-plausibility and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [210] [LM-SimBench: A Unified Framework, Dataset, and Benchmark for Large Language Model Training Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14285)
*Yu Dong,Hong Li,Qiang Sun,Xu Zhou,Jinbiao Huo,Qixiu Cheng,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LM-SimBench：一个用于评估 LLM 训练模拟器的统一框架、性能剖析数据集与基准，涵盖 10 个稠密/MoE 模型家族、23 种模型设置、348 个可行配置，支持多维度并行策略的模拟精度比较与误差诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接用分布式训练跑批来评估候选并行策略在大规模下成本过高；性能模拟虽廉价，但现有模拟器在建模的训练阶段和评测负载上各不相同，难以进行受控对比，缺乏统一的评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建统一框架，将模拟过程分解为从模型编译到调度级聚合的功能阶段，把执行表示与代价估计器同可度量评测目标连接；2) 依据该框架在 Ascend NPU 集群上剖析 10 个稠密与 MoE 模型家族的 23 种模型设置；3) 产出含 348 个可行配置的数据集，覆盖 5 个并行维度与激活重计算，并提供配置、rank、算子、事件、kernel 六个粒度视图；4) 对代表性训练模拟器开展评测。

Result: LM-SimBench 可支持模拟器预测精度对比、配置选择能力评估与预测误差诊断；GPU-NPU 配对实验验证了该数据集与评测协议的跨平台适用性，并实现了由 NPU 引导的 GPU 配置选择。

Conclusion: LM-SimBench 为 LLM 训练模拟器提供了可复现、细粒度、跨平台的统一评测基准，缓解了模拟器间难以受控比较的问题，并为并行策略选择提供了数据与协议支撑。

Abstract: Evaluating candidate parallelization strategies through distributed training runs makes strategy selection prohibitively expensive at scale. Performance simulation offers a lower cost alternative, yet controlled comparisons among existing simulators remain difficult because they differ in the training stages they model and the workloads on which they are evaluated. We introduce LM-SimBench, a unified framework, profiling dataset, and benchmark for evaluating LLM training simulators. The framework decomposes simulation into functional stages from model compilation to schedule level aggregation, connecting execution representations and cost estimators to measurable evaluation targets. Following this framework, we profile 23 model settings from 10 dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLM families on an Ascend Neural Processing Unit (NPU) cluster. The resulting dataset contains 348 feasible configurations that jointly vary five parallelism dimensions and activation recomputation, with six views spanning configuration, rank, operator, event, and kernel granularity. Evaluations of representative training simulators demonstrate how LM-SimBench supports comparisons of prediction accuracy, assessment of configuration selection, and diagnosis of prediction errors. Matched GPU-NPU experiments validate the cross-platform applicability of the collection and evaluation protocol and demonstrate NPU-guided GPU configuration selection.

</details>


### [211] [Multimodal Abstention That Preserves Acquired Correct Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14311)
*Jinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReFrain 框架，将"弃答修复"建模为在"新丢失正确答案预算"约束下最大化不可答问题的正确弃答率，在 Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上实现零预算零回归、0.5% 预算下正确弃答 74.05% 且仅损 8 个正确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为任务适配后的多模态模型加入弃答能力（如拒答不可答问题）往往会破坏适配阶段获得的正确作答，导致"能力回退"。作者希望在不牺牲已获得能力的前提下引入弃答，因此把弃答修复形式化为：在限制新丢失正确答案数量的预算下，最大化不可答问题上的正确弃答率。

Method: ReFrain（Regression-Free Abstention）由三部分组成：(1) 在首 token logits 上训练线性探针作为弃答判别器；(2) 在留出的图像聚类上做阈值校准；(3) 确定性 veto 机制，将拒答输出替换为界面中显示的"none of the above"选项，其余回答保持不变。所有对比方法均从同一 answering-SFT checkpoint 出发，分别统计丢失的正确答案、修复的错误与保留的 SFT 增益。

Result: Qwen2.5-VL-3B 上，零预算下 ReFrain 保留全部 57 个 SFT 增益答案、零损失，正确弃答率提升 7.30 个百分点；0.5% 校准预算下达到 74.05% 正确弃答、仅 8 次损失并保留 55 个增益；CLIP-UP EmbLoRA 为 64.65%，但损失 62、修复 41、保留增益 40。同一探针用默认阈值则损失 298 个正确答案。冻结模型与阈值后，在 228 个独立问题聚类上确认：0.5% 点达到 66.05% 正确弃答且零损失，领先 EmbLoRA 32.72 个百分点。普通拒答 SFT 正确弃答更高但回归代价更大。

Conclusion: 弃答能力可以在几乎不牺牲既有正确作答的前提下加装：轻量探针加阈值校准加确定性 veto 即可实现"零回归"目标，且效果显著优于基于嵌入/LoRA 的弃答方案与常规拒答 SFT，并在独立聚类上得到确认。

Abstract: Adding abstention to a task-adapted multimodal model can discard correct answers, including capabilities acquired during adaptation. We formulate abstention repair as maximizing correct abstention on unanswerable questions under a budget on newly lost correct answers. We propose ReFrain (Regression-Free Abstention), which combines a linear probe on first-token logits, threshold calibration on held-out image clusters, and a deterministic veto that replaces rejected outputs with the displayed "none of the above" option while preserving all other responses. "Regression-free" denotes zero loss on the calibration set at the zero-budget target. All abstention methods in our main comparison start from the same answering-SFT checkpoint; we separately track lost correct answers, repaired errors, and retained SFT gains. On Qwen2.5-VL-3B, ReFrain at zero budget retains all 57 SFT-gained answers with no losses and improves correct abstention by 7.30 percentage points. At a 0.5% calibration budget, it reaches 74.05% correct abstention with 8 losses and retains 55 gains; CLIP-UP EmbLoRA reaches 64.65% with 62 losses, 41 repairs, and 40 retained gains. The same probe at its default threshold loses 298 correct answers. With models and thresholds frozen, confirmation on 228 independent question clusters yields 66.05% correct abstention and no losses at the 0.5% point, exceeding EmbLoRA by 32.72 percentage points. Ordinary refusal SFT achieves higher correct abstention at greater regression cost. Code and reproduction materials: https://github.com/ModeLiberaL/ReFrain.

</details>


### [212] [Don't Just Trust Your Memory: Evidence-Linked Memory Reduces Memory-Induced Degradation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14320)
*Yukyeong Lee,Yumin Choi,Jinheon Baek,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WikiTrace，一种"证据可溯源"的记忆系统，让 LLM 智能体在执行前能把每条经验教训回溯到其来源轨迹与成败归因，从而缓解记忆诱导的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部记忆通过将过往任务轨迹蒸馏为简明经验来提升 LLM 智能体，但也会造成"记忆诱导退化"：智能体因遵循错误的经验，或误用、误读正确经验，而在本可完成的任务上失败。根因在于经验仅由"成功/失败"标签的轨迹推断而来，却通常脱离原始轨迹单独提供，智能体无从核验。

Method: WikiTrace 将每条经验存储为简洁洞见，并链接到：(1) 该轨迹为何成功或失败的归因摘要；(2) 原始轨迹本身。新任务中智能体从所有洞见出发，当某条洞见缺少行动所需细节、与其他洞见冲突、或源自失败任务时，借助带终止工具的链接机制下钻，补齐细节或修正洞见后再行动。

Result: 在五个智能体基准上验证，WikiTrace 一致优于现有记忆方法与"仅提供相同洞见"的对照基线，同时降低了记忆诱导退化。

Conclusion: 为记忆附加可核验的证据链（归因摘要+原始轨迹）并按需下钻，是缓解记忆诱导退化、提升记忆增强智能体鲁棒性的有效途径；代码已开源。

Abstract: External memory enables LLM agents to improve from experience by distilling trajectories of past tasks into concise lessons for new tasks. However, memory can also lead to memory-induced degradation, where an agent fails on tasks it would solve without memory, often because it follows a lesson that is incorrect, or misapplies or misreads a valid one. This is because lessons are inferred from trajectories labeled only with task success or failure, yet are typically provided without the trajectories they came from, leaving the agent no way to check them. To address this, we introduce WikiTrace, an evidence-linked memory system designed to let agents check each lesson against its original trajectory. Specifically, motivated by the finding that a trajectory often still holds the evidence needed to catch an erroneous lesson, WikiTrace stores each lesson as a concise insight linked to a summary of why its trajectory succeeded or failed and to the raw trajectory itself. Then, on a new task, the agent starts from all insights and, whenever one lacks details needed to act, conflicts with another, or comes from a failed task, descends through its links with terminal tools to fill in missing details or correct the insight before acting. We validate WikiTrace on five agentic benchmarks, showing that it consistently outperforms existing memory approaches and a baseline given the same insights alone, while also reducing memory-induced degradation. Our code is available at https://github.com/yukyeongleee/wikitrace

</details>


### [213] [When Should Agents Remember? Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14326)
*Runxuan Tang,Haoyu Gao,Yuyan Ding,Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Zecheng Sheng,Liwei Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution (FGSE)：将经验教训表示为带前置条件、行为改变、预期效果与验证器的结构化假设，仅在临时状态中试用并通过迁移/证伪/回归探针检验后再提交、修正或拒绝，从而让记忆更新更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体长期运行于富验证器环境，现有自我改进机制常把单次回合的经验教训直接写入记忆，使"局部成功"成为可复用性与安全性的不可靠信号。核心问题：何时应把经验驱动的行为更新纳入持久化智能体状态。

Method: FGSE为验证器支撑的框架：每个候选教训被形式化为结构化假设（前置条件、行为改变、预期效果、验证器）；先仅在临时状态中应用，再通过目标迁移探针、证伪探针与归档回归探针测试，依据实测增益与风险决定提交、修正或拒绝。

Result: 在Web、App、工具使用与长期记忆基准上取得较强表现：WebArena成功率39.8，AppWorld平均完成率78.8，τ-Retail/τ-Airline的Pass^1为0.724/0.487；同时证伪失败降至5.0%，回归损伤1.3%，有害提交更新3.6%。

Conclusion: 把记忆更新当作假设进行检验，可支持更可靠的长期适应；验证器门控的准入机制在提升任务表现的同时显著降低风险。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in long-horizon, verifier-rich environments, but current self-improvement mechanisms often write episode-derived lessons directly into memory, making local success an unreliable signal for reusable and safe knowledge. This paper addresses the core question of when an experience-derived behavioral update should be admitted into persistent agent state. We propose Falsification-Gated Self-Evolution (FGSE), a verifier-grounded framework that represents each candidate lesson as a structured hypothesis with an explicit precondition, behavior change, expected effect, and verifier. FGSE applies the hypothesis only in a temporary state, tests it on target-transfer, falsification, and archived-regression probes, and then commits, refines, or rejects it according to measured gain and risk. Across web, app, tool-use, and long-term memory benchmarks, FGSE achieves strong task performance, including 39.8 WebArena success rate, 78.8 AppWorld average completion, and 0.724/0.487 Pass$^1$ on $τ$-Retail/$τ$-Airline, while reducing falsification failures to 5.0%, regression damage to 1.3%, and harmful committed updates to 3.6%. These results suggest that testing memory updates as hypotheses supports more reliable long-term adaptation.

</details>


### [214] [CLADE: Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence in Retrieval-Augmented Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14355)
*S M Rayeed,Charles V. Stewart,Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"可解析性不足"（resolvable insufficiency）这一独立情形：检索到的段落正确相关，但仅给出比问题所需更粗粒度的类别（如上位病种），文本本身无法确定细粒度答案，但该类别可在领域本体中映射为一份候选答案短列表。作者发现语言模型读到这类"仅含类别"的段落时倾向于直接作答而非弃答或继续检索，称之为Clade现象，并证明常规手段难以消除，最后评估本体能否把粗粒度段落转化为精确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于上下文充分性的研究把粒度偏粗的段落一律视为"不充分"，忽视了其中蕴含可利用的类别线索；同时实际RAG系统中模型面对这类段落往往"自信作答"，造成大量看似有据实则错误的回答，因此需要单独刻画这一问题并探索解决方案。

Method: 在PrimeKG生物医学问题上构造"仅含疾病家族"的粗粒度段落，与无信息段落对比测量弃答率变化；用反事实实验（将精确病名替换为上位家族名 vs. 删除该句）确认因果；测试置信度过滤、增大模型规模、显式指令弃答等简单修复；在GloBI生态物种互作数据上复现；最后检验基于本体的候选列表覆盖率和端到端恢复流程（字符串匹配 vs. 模型选择家族）。

Result: 仅含家族段落使弃答率相对无信息段落下降13.8个百分点，猜测错误率约50%；反事实替换使弃答率较删除句子下降17.4个百分点；置信度过滤、更大模型、显式弃答指令均无法消除Clade，且在GloBI上复现。本体列表中含真答案的比例达98.5%，但字符串匹配仅在19.8%的页面中定位到该家族；让模型选择家族可提升至30.0%，端到端恢复率从10.0%升至26.8%。

Conclusion: 粗粒度但相关的段落构成一类可被本体解析的"可解析性不足"，模型对此表现出顽固的Clade式猜测行为，简单干预无效。本体候选列表覆盖率高但检索/定位环节是瓶颈；论文明确了模型应停止猜测并查询本体的时机，同时指出家族定位与端到端恢复等步骤仍未解决。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented systems often retrieve passages that are accurate and relevant but state the answer at a coarser level of granularity than the question requires. For example, a question may ask which specific disease a drug treats (familial hyperlipidemia), while the retrieved passage mentions the broader disease family (hyperlipidemia). Prior work on context sufficiency treats such coarse passages simply as insufficient. We argue that they form a distinct case, which we call resolvable insufficiency: text alone cannot settle the fine-grained answer, but the broader category it mentions maps to a short list of candidate answers in a domain ontology. When language models read these coarse, family-only passages, they exhibit a phenomenon we call Clade (Category-Level Answering from Deficient Evidence): they answer instead of abstaining or searching further. On biomedical questions from PrimeKG, family-only passages reduce abstention by 13.8 points compared to uninformative passages, and the resulting guesses are wrong about half the time. A counterfactual test confirms the cause: replacing an exact disease name with its broader family name lowers abstention by 17.4 points compared to deleting the sentence. Simple fixes like confidence filtering, scaling to a larger model, and explicitly instructing the model to abstain cannot resolve Clade, and this replicates on ecological species interactions in GloBI. Finally, we test whether an ontology can turn coarse passages into exact answers. Given the correct family, an ontology list includes the true answer in 98.5% of cases, but string matching finds that family on the page for only 19.8%. Letting the model choose the family raises this to 30.0% and end-to-end recovery from 10.0% to 26.8%. Overall, our results show when language models should stop guessing and consult an ontology, and highlight which steps of that process remain unsolved.

</details>


### [215] [Cognitive Mapping of Learning by Thinking Mechanisms in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14382)
*Dong Han,Jin Zhang,Kunyue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知性LbT框架"，将人类"思考中学习"(Learning by Thinking, LbT)的四种机制——反思推理、合成记忆形成、内部模拟、概念自组织——映射到LLM的计算过程，并用代表性方法（CoT、自一致性、自我反思、ToT搜索、Self-Instruct、self-taught reasoning）加以论证：这些方法可在无新增标注样本下近似实现部分机制并提升任务表现，但并不等于参数层面的持久学习，同时伴随幻觉扩散、模型崩溃、概念漂移与高计算成本等特征性失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益表现出类似人类"思考中学习"的能力，即在不引入新外部输入的情况下，通过解释、推理等内部过程获得新洞见；但关于这些能力背后认知机制及其边界的系统性理论解释仍不充分，缺乏可比较、可预测的分析框架。

Method: 概念性/理论分析为主：构建Cognitive LbT Framework，将四种认知机制逐一映射到LLM的计算过程；选取代表性技术方法作为例证（chain-of-thought prompting、self-consistency、self-reflection、Tree-of-Thoughts search、Self-Instruct、self-taught reasoning）；并归纳LLM与人类认知之间的四种结构性不对称（生成者与监控者同一、无外部接地的闭环学习、符号性而非感觉运动性模拟、推理期计算成本），以解释上述失败模式。

Result: 所选方法可在功能上近似对应部分LbT机制，并在不增加标注样例的情况下改善某些任务表现，但并不总构成参数层面的持久学习；例如Self-Instruct从175个种子任务生成约52,000条指令，在Super-NaturalInstructions上报告33%的绝对提升(Y. Wang et al., 2023)。同一批机制也带来典型失败：幻觉扩散、模型崩溃、概念漂移与高计算代价，且这些失败源于四个结构性不对称而非孤立技术缺陷。

Conclusion: 该框架给出了关于LLM中LbT何时成功、何时失败的可检验命题，并为设计更可靠的自主学习系统提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly exhibit capabilities that resemble human Learning by Thinking (LbT), the acquisition of new insight through internal processes such as explanation and reasoning, without new external input. A systematic account of the cognitive mechanisms behind these capabilities, and of their limits, remains underdeveloped. This paper proposes a Cognitive LbT Framework that maps four mechanisms (Reflective Reasoning, Synthetic Memory Formation, Internal Simulation, and Conceptual Self-Organization) onto computational processes in LLMs. Drawing on representative methods, including chain-of-thought prompting, self-consistency, self-reflection, Tree-of-Thoughts search, Self-Instruct, and self-taught reasoning, the analysis argues that selected methods can functionally approximate aspects of these mechanisms and can improve performance on some tasks without additional labeled examples, although this does not always constitute durable learning in model parameters. For example, Self-Instruct generated approximately 52,000 instructions from 175 seed tasks and reported a 33% absolute improvement on Super-NaturalInstructions (Y. Wang et al., 2023). The same mechanisms also produce characteristic failures, including hallucination diffusion, model collapse, concept drift, and high computational cost. These failures are not isolated technical defects but follow from four structural asymmetries between LLMs and human cognition: the identity of generator and monitor, closed learning loops without external grounding, symbolic rather than sensorimotor simulation, and the cost of inference-time computation. The framework yields testable propositions about when LbT in LLMs should succeed or fail and offers a structured basis for designing more reliable autonomous learning systems.

</details>


### [216] [An Energy-Based Perspective on Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14383)
*Bo Dai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该教程以能量模型（EBM）为统一视角，将机器学习中判别模型、生成模型、表征学习、强化学习及推理时计算等众多方法统一为"能量—推理—学习"三要素的不同选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EBM通常只被视为众多模型家族中的一种，缺乏跨领域的统一解释框架。作者希望用一个视角串联机器学习的各个侧面，解释现有方法、组织它们，并启发新算法。

Method: 论文从EBM三要素展开：能量（模型表示什么）、推理过程（采样或优化能量）、学习方法（拟合能量到数据）。先系统介绍采样、变分推断，以及MLE、噪声对比估计、伪似然、score matching、对抗训练等学习方法，并指出它们共享对比式结构；再将各领域方法映射为三要素的不同组合。

Result: 判别模型、GAN、归一化流、扩散与自回归模型、对比学习、word2vec、BERT、去噪自编码器、扩散表征、EBM策略与世界模型强化学习、逆强化学习、规划，以及带验证器的推理时计算，均被证明是EBM，差异主要在于对配分函数与采样的处理方式。

Conclusion: 统一的EBM框架可解释并组织已有方法；通过替换三要素之一而保持其余不变可推导新算法，且能量组合为推理时融合多个已学模型提供了原则性途径。

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) define a distribution through a scalar energy, $p_θ(x)\propto\exp(-E_θ(x))$, and are usually presented as one model family among many. This tutorial has a broader goal: beyond introducing EBMs, it uses them as a single lens that connects the different aspects of machine learning. Any EBM is specified by three choices, the energy, which determines what the model represents; the inference procedure, which samples from or optimizes the energy; and the learning method, which fits the energy to data. We first develop these components, including sampling, variational inference, and learning by maximum likelihood, noise contrastive estimation, pseudolikelihood, score matching, and adversarial training, and show that the learning methods share one contrastive structure. We then show that discriminative models, generative models such as GANs, normalizing flows, diffusion and autoregressive models, representation learning methods such as contrastive learning, word2vec, BERT, denoising auto-encoders, and diffusion representations, reinforcement learning with EBM policies and world models, inverse reinforcement learning, and planning, as well as inference-time computation with verifiers, are all EBMs that differ in these three choices, mostly in how they handle the partition function and sampling. This view explains existing methods, organizes them into a common framework, and suggests new algorithms by replacing one component while keeping the others, with composition of energies as a principled way to combine learned models at inference time.

</details>


### [217] [Bits or permutations? Spending a fixed inference budget on weight precision or on option-order averaging in small local language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14413)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在模拟权重量化下，量化本身不会显著加剧选项顺序敏感性，但4比特时答案槽偏斜明显上升；以"权重字节×前向次数"为统一成本单位，成本匹配下仅在≥6比特时增加顺序平均才优于增加比特，最优策略是6比特权重＋最多四次循环顺序、每次一次前向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重量化可省内存，平均多个选项重排可降低对答案顺序的敏感性；作者追问前者是否使后者变差，以及在固定预算下哪种投入更划算。文献检索（标题级）未发现同时研究量化与选项顺序的工作。

Method: 在Qwen3-0.6B与Qwen3-1.7B上做模拟的round-to-nearest权重量化（8/6/4/3比特，fp16为参照），从A–D字母的logits读取答案；数据集为ARC-Easy、ARC-Challenge、MMLU、HellaSwag各300题。控制实验用同权重SNR的高斯噪声对照。指标包括准确率、答案槽偏斜（slot skew）、翻转率，并与独立均匀误差的准确率匹配基线比较。成本以"权重字节读取数×前向次数"的机器无关单位计价，做成本匹配配对比较。

Result: 6比特相对fp16无损（反而高+1.8点[+1.0,+2.5]与+0.9点[+0.3,+1.5]，作者未解释）；8比特变化-0.7点[-1.1,-0.3]与-0.4点[-0.7,-0.2]。4比特时槽偏斜从0.15升至0.53（0.6B）、0.07升至0.12（1.7B）；等SNR高斯噪声给出0.15与0.37（两次抽样）、0.19（一次抽样），故对照无法判定舍入是否在等功率噪声之外额外带来槽偏斜。翻转率上升但仍低于准确率匹配的独立均匀误差基线。四次循环顺序平均在4比特下为0.6B恢复+12.6点（fp16为+5.3）。成本匹配下，仅当权重≥6比特时"加顺序"才优于"加比特"；决策表归结为：用6比特权重，其余预算用于最多四个顺序、每次一遍前向。

Conclusion: 量化与顺序平均并非简单叠加的此消彼长：低比特下量化引入的槽偏斜上升需谨慎对待，而顺序平均在高比特（≥6）时才是更划算的预算投向。结论基于两个小模型与最简量化器的模拟，属初步结论，需更大量化器与更大模型验证。

Abstract: Local language models can save memory by storing weights in fewer bits, and they can reduce sensitivity to the order of answer options by averaging a few reorderings. We ask whether the first makes the second worse, and which is the cheaper way to spend a fixed budget. On Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B with simulated round-to-nearest weight quantization (8, 6, 4 and 3 bits, fp16 reference), read from the logits of the letters A to D on ARC-Easy, ARC-Challenge, MMLU and HellaSwag (300 items each), 6 bits lose no accuracy against fp16 (they score +1.8 (+1.0 to +2.5) and +0.9 (+0.3 to +1.5) points higher, which we do not explain), and 8 bits change it by -0.7 (-1.1 to -0.3) and -0.4 (-0.7 to -0.2) points. At 4 bits the answer-slot skew rises from 0.15 to 0.53 (0.6B) and from 0.07 to 0.12 (1.7B); Gaussian noise at the same weight SNR gives 0.15 and 0.37 (two draws) and 0.19 (one draw), so the control is inconclusive on whether rounding adds slot skew beyond equal-power noise. Flip rates rise but stay below an accuracy-matched baseline of independent uniform errors. Averaging four cyclic orders recovers +12.6 points at 4 bits for 0.6B (+5.3 at fp16). We price bits and passes in one machine-independent unit, weight bytes read times passes. In cost-matched pairs, extra orders beat extra bits only when the weights have 6 or more bits, and the decision table reduces to: use 6-bit weights and spend the rest of the budget on up to four orders, one pass each. Everything is simulated on two small models with the simplest quantizer; a title-level search found no earlier study of quantization together with option order.

</details>


### [218] [Precision on Demand: Escalating Weight Bits at Uncertain Decoding Steps in a Small Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14418)
*Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种"低精度优先、按置信度选择性升级"的混合精度解码分配策略：以8-bit权重存储、默认用高4位解码，仅当低精度输出分布熵偏高或top-2间隔过小时才以8-bit重算该步。在Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K上，离线回放显示熵信号对argmax翻转有较强判别力（AUC 0.93），但在150道题的live实验中，门控策略与全高/全低精度两端均无法在统计上区分，结论尚未确证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部解码通常为整个生成过程固定单一权重精度，缺乏"按需精度"的机制。作者希望在不显著增加权重读取字节的前提下，把高精度算力集中在真正影响输出（argmax可能翻转）的不确定步骤上，从而在精度-成本之间取得更优的权衡。

Method: 存储8-bit权重的量化模拟，默认用高4位解码；以低精度输出分布的熵（超过阈值）或top-2概率间隔（小于阈值）作为不确定度门控，触发时以8-bit重做该步。模型为Qwen3-0.6B，基准为GSM8K，成本以每token读取的权重字节计。评测包括：36724步回放的离线分析（AUC、门控捕获率对比随机门期望）、150题live实验（全高/全低/熵门控三组配对比较、McNemar检验）。

Result: 离线回放的36724步中，低精度通路熵对argmax翻转排序的AUC为0.93；在15%升级预算下熵门控捕获78%的翻转、间隔门控捕获94%，而等率随机门期望仅15%。Live实验150题：全高64.0%、全低55.3%（McNemar p=0.053）；熵门控（16%升级率）62.7%，权重字节为全高的0.87。门控对全高配对差-1.3（-8.0~+5.3）分，对全低+7.3（0.0~+14.7）分（p=0.080），置信区间均跨零。

Conclusion: 离线证据支持熵/间隔信号能有效预测argmax翻转，但live实验样本量不足，门控策略与两端精度均无统计学差异，无法判定其优劣。作者明确指出若干局限未验证：缺少live随机门控对照、thinking模式下的阶段规则、第二模型以及live KL测量；并提及同为"经验证的猜测"方案可在相同成本模型下达到零KL。整体属初步探索性结果。

Abstract: Local decoding usually fixes one weight precision for the whole generation. We study a different allocation: store 8-bit weights, decode with their top 4 bits, and redo a step at 8 bits only when the low-precision output distribution looks uncertain (entropy above a threshold or a small top-two margin). Quantization is simulated, the model is Qwen3-0.6B, and the benchmark is GSM8K. Cost is counted in weight bytes read per token. On 36724 replayed steps the entropy of the low pass ranks argmax flips with AUC 0.93. At a 15% escalation budget it catches 78% of flips and the margin gate 94%, against the 15% expected for a random gate of equal rate (a computed expectation, not a run). In 150 live problems all-high scores 64.0% and all-low 55.3% (McNemar p=0.053). An entropy gate with a 16% escalation rate scores 62.7% at 0.87 of the all-high weight bytes. Its paired difference is -1.3 (-8.0 to +5.3) points to all-high and +7.3 (+0.0 to +14.7) points to all-low (p=0.080), so the live data do not separate the gate from either end. Verified speculation reaches zero KL at similar bytes in the same cost model. A live random control, a thinking-mode phase rule, a second model and live KL were not run.

</details>


### [219] [Evaluating LLM-Generated World Models for Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14419)
*Souhaila Djaffal,Amna Jamal,Gagana Madhugiri Devaraja,Anidipta Pal,Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出44个场景的基准，检验5个LLM将开放自然语言情境生成为部分可观测随机博弈形式的世界模型，发现机械有效性虽高但结构保真度低，是必要非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化世界模型可支撑规划、验证与安全推理，但从开放式自然语言情境构建极为困难，多智能体场景中可能存在多种合理形式化。作者质疑：机械有效性是否足以证明生成模型忠实刻画了原意决策问题。

Method: 构建涵盖博弈、情境与文化三类设置的44个场景基准，用5个LLM生成部分可观测随机博弈形式的世界模型；沿三个维度评估：机械有效性、参照相对的结构一致性、语义标准；共分析410次首轮生成，分三条实证轨道。

Result: 参照博弈轨道中134/135个模型通过严格机械审计，但置换不变结构F1均值仅0.366，仅28/134个机械有效模型与人工参照完全匹配；文化轨道中明确写出国家名会改变多数LLM的结构表示，而自动语义分数变化很小。

Conclusion: 可执行性是LLM生成世界模型的必要非充分标准；机械有效的模型在下游神经符号推理与规划所依赖的结构上仍可能显著偏离，需引入结构一致性与语义层面的评估。

Abstract: Formal world models can support planning, verification, and safety reasoning, but constructing them from open-ended natural-language situations is difficult, particularly in multi-agent settings where several formalizations may be plausible. We study whether five LLMs can generate such world models as partially observable stochastic games and whether mechanical validity is sufficient to establish that the resulting model faithfully represents the intended decision problem. We introduce a benchmark of 44 scenarios spanning game, situational, and cultural settings and evaluate generated world models along three dimensions: mechanical validity, reference-relative structural agreement, and semantic criteria. Across three empirical tracks, we analyze 410 first-pass generations. In the reference-backed game track, 134/135 generated world models pass a strict mechanical audit, yet mean permutation-invariant structural F1 is only 0.366, with just 28/134 mechanically valid models exactly matching the authored reference. In the cultural track, explicitly naming the country changes the generated structural representation for most LLMs while producing only small changes in automated semantic scores. These results show that executability is a necessary but insufficient criterion for LLM-generated world models: models that are mechanically valid can still differ substantially in the structure used by downstream neuro-symbolic reasoning and planning.

</details>


### [220] [Re-Search: Dissecting LLM Ideation Through a Two-Player Questioning Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14487)
*Muhan Gao,Hangxiao Zhu,Xinqi Su,Yu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Re-Search 基准，将研究构思形式化为"二十问"游戏，用模型自问自答+二元 oracle 反馈的方式刻画 LLM 的迭代式想法搜索过程，发现瓶颈在"生成"而非"判断"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 LLM 科研构思能力的评测多为"一次性生成-打分"，把迭代搜索过程隐去，因此无法揭示模型如何探索研究想法空间、在哪一步停滞、失败原因何在。作者希望把构思与执行解耦，并显式追踪想法随反馈的演化轨迹。

Method: 构建 Re-Search：从 200 篇 ICML 2026 论文中，仅保留研究问题作为输入，要求模型通过提出是/否问题、接收二元 oracle 反馈（反馈来自论文自身信息）逐步重发现论文的核心想法；共 100 轮问题预算。评测六个近期 LLM，并对候选问题集合与假设空间做受控干预（提供好问题/好想法），以区分"生成"与"判断"能力；同时定性分析构思轨迹。

Result: 几乎所有进展集中在前 30 问（共 100 问），这 30 问基本决定最终结果，之后多数模型进入平台期。干预实验显示瓶颈在于生成而非判断：给出好想法或好问题时模型能识别，但独立产出困难。轨迹分析显示两类典型失败模式——过度利用单一方向（over-exploitation）与过度分散探索过多方向（over-exploration）。

Conclusion: 为 autoresearch 系统提出设计原则：既然模型能判断但难以生成，则用外部来源的想法与问题丰富搜索空间，可能比单纯提升单一模型的生成能力更有效；同时需在探索-利用之间做更好调度，并把预算集中于早期高信息量提问。

Abstract: Research ideation is iterative: researchers refine their ideas step by step based on feedback. Yet existing evaluations obscure this search process, making it difficult to understand how LLMs explore the space of research ideas and what limits their progress. We introduce Re-Search, which adapts the format of Twenty Questions to research ideation: given only the research problem of a published paper, a model must rediscover the paper's core idea by asking yes-or-no questions. Built from 200 ICML 2026 papers, Re-Search tracks how a model's idea evolves as it asks questions and receives binary oracle feedback, isolating ideation from execution. Across six recent LLMs, nearly all progress happens within the first 30 of 100 questions, which largely decide how the game ends; after that, most models plateau. Through controlled interventions on the candidate questions and the hypothesis space, we find that the bottleneck is generation, not judgment: models recognize a good idea or a good question when one is offered, but often cannot produce it on their own. We further analyze the ideation traces and find that models fail by either over-exploiting one direction or over-exploring too many. These findings point to concrete design principles for autoresearch systems: since models can judge candidates they cannot produce, enriching the search with externally sourced ideas and questions may be more effective than improving any single model's generation ability.

</details>


### [221] [ReTrace: Distilling Tool-Using Agents into Planner-Free Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14492)
*Kaiyuan Hou,Yang Ping Kuo,Indranil Bhattacharya,Rohith Nama,Arushi Gupta,Carlos D. Castillo,Prathap Ramachandra,Xiaofan Jiang,Francesco Carbone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReTrace 将自然语言 SOP 与少量 agent 运行日志转化为可复用工作流，由代码而非 LLM 选择下一步，在接近 agent 成功率的前提下显著降低延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程自动化需在新输入上反复执行同一 SOP。LLM agent 能遵循自然语言 SOP，但每一步都要调用 LLM，规模化时带来高延迟与高成本。

Method: 利用 agent 的 reasoning（每次工具调用时写出的解释）揭示动作背后的条件，而工具调用本身不含此信息。ReTrace 抽取每步条件，跨运行合并为紧凑的规则文档，再由规则文档生成可执行工作流；仅最后一步依赖平台，因此规则文档可编译到不同编排平台，并支持基于训练失败的修复（repair）。

Result: 生产平台 79 个流程：完成率 93.8%（agent 96.8%），每任务成本降低 24%；τ²-Bench：任务成功率 75.8%（agent 80.6%），耗时 1.3s 而非 170s，三轮修复将 k=2 工作流从 66.7% 提升到 81.2%；SOP-Bench 8 个流程：未修复时 80.6% 低于 few-shot 生成的 83.6%，修复后达 87.8%（0.40s/任务），agent 为 85.5%（46.6s）。

Conclusion: 以代码替代逐步 LLM 决策可在大幅降低延迟和成本的同时保持接近原 agent 的成功率，且抽取条件、生成工作流、失败修复的流水线具备跨平台可移植性。

Abstract: Business process automation runs the same Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) again and again on new inputs. Large Language Model (LLM) agents can follow an SOP written in natural language, but they call an LLM to choose every step of every run, which adds latency and cost at scale. We present ReTrace, which turns an SOP and a few logged agent runs into a reusable workflow in which code, not an LLM, selects the next step. The agent's reasoning, the explanation it writes with each tool call, often states why it took an action; the tool calls alone do not. ReTrace extracts the condition behind each step, merges these conditions across runs into a compact rule document, and generates an executable workflow from it. Only this last stage depends on the platform, so rule documents can be compiled for different orchestration platforms. On $79$ procedures from our production platform, ReTrace workflows reach $93.8\%$ completion, against $96.8\%$ for the agent, at $24\%$ lower cost per task. On $τ^2$-Bench, they reach $75.8\%$ task success (agent: $80.6\%$) and run in $1.3$s instead of $170$s; three rounds of repair on training failures lift $k{=}2$ workflows from $66.7\%$ to $81.2\%$. On eight SOP-Bench procedures, ReTrace without repair trails few-shot generation ($80.6\%$ vs. $83.6\%$), but repair from labeled training failures lifts it to $87.8\%$ at $0.40$s per task, compared to $85.5\%$ in $46.6$s for the agent.

</details>


### [222] [Comparative Analysis of Agent Evaluation Frameworks: Stability, Detection, and Discovery in Enterprise Analytics Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14493)
*Howard Zhang,Chuck Arvin,Logan Brown,Mihir Bhatia,Neelu Choudhary,Lily Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种LLM分析型智能体评测框架（基于执行轨迹的LLM评判、纯文本LLM评判+红队测试、确定性启发式），通过三阶段实验表明：评测框架可获取的信息量决定评测质量，功能评测与安全评测互补，LLM评测能发现人工评审遗漏的细微错误，建议采用"轨迹评测+对抗红队"双框架策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署基于LLM的分析型智能体时，需要在复杂多工具工作流中可靠检测失败，但现有评测框架的稳定性、已知风险检出能力与未知风险发现能力缺乏系统性对比证据，因此需要厘清不同评测范式各自的价值与边界。

Method: 在受控研究环境中，基于冻结的执行轨迹，对两个分析型智能体应用三种代表不同评测范式的框架：①基于轨迹的LLM评判；②纯文本LLM评判结合红队测试；③确定性启发式。研究分三阶段：阶段一通过重复测量量化评测框架稳定性；阶段二在72个有真值标注的测试用例上衡量已知风险检出；阶段三评估未知风险发现能力。

Result: 阶段一：97%的配置满足稳定性阈值，且稳定性模式与智能体无关。阶段二：基于轨迹的框架取得最佳精确率-召回率平衡（F1=0.90）；纯文本评测召回率相当，但误报多5倍。阶段三：发现24个新型失败，且功能测试与安全红队测试的发现零重叠。

Conclusion: 评测框架可获取的信息决定评测质量；功能评测与安全评测具有互补性；基于LLM的评测框架能够暴露人工评审遗漏的细微错误。建议采用双框架策略：以基于轨迹的评测做功能评估，以对抗性红队测试做安全验证。

Abstract: Deploying LLM-based analytics agents in enterprise settings requires evaluation frameworks that can reliably detect failures across complex, multi-tool workflows. We present a three-phase comparative study of three evaluation frameworks, each representing a distinct evaluation paradigm (trace-based LLM judging, text-only LLM judging with red-teaming, and deterministic heuristics), applied to two analytics agents in a controlled research setting using frozen execution traces. Phase 1 quantifies evaluation harness stability through repeated measurements, finding that 97% of configurations meet the stability threshold and that stability patterns are agent-independent. Phase 2 measures known risk detection on 72 ground-truth test cases, where the trace-based framework achieves the best precision-recall balance (F1=0.90) while text-only evaluation matches on recall but generates 5x more false alarms. Phase 3 evaluates unknown risk discovery, uncovering 24 novel failures with zero overlap between functional testing and security red-teaming. Our results demonstrate that (1) the information available to the evaluation harness determines evaluation quality, (2) functional and security evaluation are complementary, and (3) LLM-based evaluation harnesses surface subtle errors that human reviewers miss. We recommend a two-framework strategy: trace-based evaluation for functional assessment and adversarial red-teaming for security validation.

</details>


### [223] [Contrastive Discovery: Open-Ended Scientific Discovery over Competing Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14514)
*Ziang Liu,James J. Kim,Yijia Dai,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"对比式发现"（Contrastive Discovery）：用"对比集"（问题+互斥且穷尽的竞争性解释）替代单一假设，以证据对信念分布的再分配作为实验打分依据，使LLM智能体能从同一证据中把被否定的假设解析为具体替代解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM科研智能体多采用逐点式（pointwise）假设评估，仅检验h与其补集¬h。证据否定h时，智能体只知道某个解释不太可能，却无法得知证据支持哪种替代解释，导致信息浪费与问题无法定论。

Method: 引入对比集：将科学问题与其互斥且穷尽的候选解释配对；每个实验按"证据在集合上引起信念重分配的程度"打分；当信念集中于某一解释时判定问题解决。该方法仅改变智能体提议与打分的单元，故可直接嵌入已有智能体，用于重估已收集证据或指导后续搜索。

Result: 六个领域中，信念位移最大的逐点候选有89%属于否定结果；在相同证据下，对比集可将其中73%解析到具体替代解释。以对比集指导搜索时，同等实验预算下解决的问题比逐点惊奇度多67%。盲测中38位人类专家在78%的已解决问题上与系统选择同一解释；对逐点智能体否定假设即停止的问题，这一比例为82%。

Conclusion: 显式引入竞争性解释可使发现智能体在相同证据与验证预算下解决更多科学问题，并提升与专家判断的一致性。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for open-ended, data-driven scientific discovery, yet most of them evaluate hypotheses pointwise, testing an empirical claim $h$ only against its complement $\neg h$. When evidence rejects $h$, the agent learns that one explanation is unlikely but not which alternative the evidence supports. To recover that alternative, we introduce Contrastive Discovery, which replaces the single hypothesis with a contrast set, a scientific question paired with mutually exclusive and collectively exhaustive competing explanations. Each experiment is scored by how its evidence redistributes belief across the set, and the question is resolved when belief concentrates on one explanation. Because contrast sets change only the unit an agent proposes and scores, an existing agent can use them to re-evaluate evidence it has already collected or to guide its search. Across six domains, $89\%$ of the pointwise candidates with the largest belief shifts are rejections. On the same evidence, contrast sets resolve $73\%$ of these candidates to a specific alternative. When contrast sets guide search, the agent resolves $67\%$ more questions than pointwise surprisal under the same experiment budget. In a blinded study, $38$ human experts chose the same explanation on $78\%$ of resolved questions and on $82\%$ of questions where the pointwise agent rejected its hypothesis and stopped. Together, these results support explicit competing explanations as a way for discovery agents to resolve more scientific questions from the same evidence and validation budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Maze Runner: Autonomous Maze Navigation with Line-Based Localization and Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12949)
*Alvaro J. Gaona,Lucas Gómez-Velayos,Peter Pasuy-Quevedo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Maze Runner：一个基于 C++ 与 ROS 2 的开源自主导航栈，在 Gazebo 仿真中实现迷宫场景下的建图/定位、规划与安全控制全链路闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迷宫导航面临三大难点：稀疏且重复的几何特征导致定位困难、窄通道内的路径规划、以及在状态估计或地图不准确时仍需保证安全控制。现有方案缺乏端到端整合与可复现实现。

Method: 感知端用 RANSAC 加总体最小二乘从每帧 2D LiDAR 扫描中提取带不确定度的墙面直线；同一前端馈入两个估计器——基于迷宫网格构建线地图的 EKF 定位，以及在线建图的 EKF SLAM 变体。规划在膨胀占据栅格上进行，采用 A* 加视线捷径，或前向式 Hybrid A*。控制采用非线性 MPC 跟踪路径，并在世界坐标系 LiDAR 点上施加离散时间控制障碍函数（CBF）约束以保证与墙面及未建图障碍物的安全距离。算法置于无 ROS 依赖、带单元测试的核心库中，整体由单一可复现环境构建运行。

Result: 仿真中完成 135 m 迷宫路线：已知地图定位 RMSE 为 1.0 cm，SLAM 模式下为 24 cm；规划器出路径耗时 6 ms；NMPC 平均求解 11.7 ms，始终未超 80 ms 预算。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"线段特征前端 + EKF/EKF-SLAM + 栅格规划 + NMPC 与 CBF 安全约束"的组合可在实时约束下可靠完成迷宫自主导航，且核心库与运行环境可复现。

Abstract: Maze navigation requires localization from sparse, repetitive geometry, planning through narrow corridors, and control that stays safe when the estimate or the map is wrong. We present Maze Runner, an open autonomy stack in C++ and ROS 2 that addresses these needs end to end on a simulated Husarion ROSbot XL with a 2D LiDAR in Gazebo. Perception extracts wall lines from each scan with RANSAC and total least squares, with per-line uncertainty. The same line front end feeds two estimators: an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) that localizes against a line map built from the maze mesh, and an EKF SLAM variant that builds the line map online. Planning runs online on an inflated occupancy grid with A* plus line-of-sight shortcutting, or with a forward-only Hybrid A*. A nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) tracks the plan while discrete-time Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) on world-frame LiDAR points keep the robot clear of walls and unmapped obstacles. The algorithms live in a ROS-free, unit-tested core library, and the whole stack builds and runs from a single reproducible environment. In simulation, the robot drives a 135 m route through the maze with 1.0 cm localization RMSE against the known map and 24 cm in SLAM mode, the planner returns a path in 6 ms, and the NMPC solves in 11.7 ms on average, never exceeding its 80 ms budget.

</details>


### [225] [CAFE: Closed-Loop Autonomous Task Formulation and Execution without Instance-Specific Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12983)
*Mingjia Chen,Xuewen Zhao,Philip Dames,Qi Mao,Fei Xie,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CAFE框架，在预定义的清洁、安全、收纳/整理类别下，实现从场景观察中自主发现任务并执行，无需实例级目标或动作序列，通过在线记忆与三阶段LLM（Draft-Complete-Audit）联动完成任务制定与执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景驱动的机器人自主性不仅需要执行任务，还需从观察中选择任务。现有方法通常依赖实例级目标或预定义动作序列，缺乏在类别级约束下自主形成任务并闭环修正的能力。

Method: CAFE维护关于物体、功能区与执行状态的在线记忆；规则引导的Trigger-LLM基于累积证据判断何时触发任务制定；Draft、Complete、Audit分别负责提出目标、补全执行细节、检查依赖关系；执行结果回写记忆以支持纠错与后续发现。在AI2-THOR的12个场景中，以coverage、constraint与execution指标比较3种骨干模型与多个任务复杂度层级，并进行消融控制实验。

Result: Tri-LLM在三种骨干模型和各级任务复杂度下均取得高于基线的GCR分数；控制实验考察了完成、审计、功能区、反馈、触发与提示示例各组件的作用；LimoCobot实机轨迹展示了收纳、清理及抓取失败后的恢复过程。

Conclusion: 共享场景状态是连接任务发现、执行与结果驱动修正的关键机制，可使机器人在类别级目标下实现自主的任务制定与执行闭环。

Abstract: Scene-driven robot autonomy requires selecting tasks from observations as well as executing them. We study scene-driven task formulation and execution under predefined cleaning, safety, and storage/organization categories, without instance-specific goals or action sequences. CAFE maintains online memory of objects, functional zones, and execution states. A rule-guided Trigger-LLM admits formulation from accumulated evidence; Draft, Complete, and Audit propose goals, resolve execution details, and check dependencies. Outcomes update memory for correction and subsequent discovery. Evaluation in 12 AI2-THOR scenes compares three backbones and task-complexity tiers using coverage, constraint, and execution metrics. Tri-LLM records higher GCR scores than the evaluated baselines across three backbones and task-complexity tiers. Controls examine completion, auditing, zones, feedback, triggering, and prompt examples. LimoCobot traces illustrate storage, cleanup, and recovery from a failed grasp. The study examines how shared scene state connects task discovery to execution and outcome-driven revision.

</details>


### [226] [RoboACE: Adversarial Co-Evolution for Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13034)
*Delin Zhao,Zhengrong Yue,Junlin He,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboACE 提出一个在冻结基础模型之上联合演化课程与执行框架的方法，通过交替更新让课程侧与执行侧互相促进，在 LIBERO 系列四个评测面板上优于所评估的控制器与智能体系统，LIBERO-PRO 上达到 74.0% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体可借助执行反馈改进可复用技能、规划流程与恢复策略，但固定的练习来源使搜索新弱点的方向保持不变；而有效练习依赖智能体当前能力，因此选择练习的流程本身也应可持续修正。

Method: RoboACE（Robot Adversarial Co-Evolution）围绕冻结的基础模型联合演化课程框架与执行框架：课程框架利用累积的能力证据提出有信息量的挑战，执行框架以可复用的修订作出响应并扩展执行能力；采用交替更新，一侧固定、另一侧变化，被接纳的挑战形成固定快照用于比较执行端的修订效果。初始条件为精简的 LIBERO 练习条件集合。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 系列评测面板上，冻结部署优于所评估的控制器与智能体系统；LIBERO-PRO 上成功率达 74.0%，较初始部署提升 15.0 个百分点，较其 VLA 控制器的直接执行提升 13.5 个百分点。案例研究记录了证据引导的练习选择、抽屉技能落地（grounding）与控制器干预方面的变化。

Conclusion: 在保持基础模型冻结的前提下，通过课程与执行框架的对抗式协同演化可显著提升机器人部署的泛化与成功率，说明"选择练习的流程"本身可修正这一设计是有效的。

Abstract: Robot agents can improve reusable skills, planning procedures, and recovery strategies from execution feedback, yet a fixed practice source leaves the search for new weaknesses unchanged. Useful practice depends on the agent's current capabilities, so the procedure that chooses practice must also be revisable. We present RoboACE (Robot Adversarial Co-Evolution), a framework that jointly evolves curriculum and execution harnesses around frozen foundation models. The curriculum harness uses accumulated capability evidence to propose informative challenges, while the execution harness responds with reusable revisions that expand execution capabilities. Alternating updates hold one side fixed as the other changes, and admitted challenges form fixed snapshots for comparing execution revisions. Starting from a compact set of LIBERO practice conditions, the resulting frozen deployment outperforms the evaluated controllers and agent systems across four LIBERO-family panels. On LIBERO-PRO, it reaches 74.0% success, exceeding the initial deployment by 15.0 percentage points and direct execution by its VLA controller by 13.5 points. Case studies document changes to evidence-guided practice selection, drawer-skill grounding, and controller intervention.

</details>


### [227] [Robo-MLT: Dual-System Robotic Manipulation for Long-Horizon Medical Laboratory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13039)
*Zequan Zeng,Kai Wang,Delvin Ce Zhang,Huaping Liu,Zhipeng Yuan,Junqin Yang,YunLong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双系统框架Robo-MLT，将显式流程监控与学习式动作生成结合，实现医疗实验室长时程自动化操作的可靠执行与中断恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗实验室自动化要求机器人按协议顺序完成整个工作流，并能应对执行中的变化。仅靠动作层正确无法保证流程正确：机器人可能混淆视觉相似的阶段，或人工干预后停滞。因此需要在动作生成之外引入显式的工作流级监控。

Method: 双系统框架：(1) 视觉-语言规划器依据协议与初始工作区观测，组合出有序原子技能序列；(2) 多帧执行监控器判断当前技能是否完成或不可行，进而推进流程或触发重规划；(3) 技能条件的视觉-语言-动作策略生成操作轨迹；(4) 异步运行时协调策略与监控器。在血气分析与血样试管分拣两个任务上，与4个策略基线及1个基于VLM的监控基线对比，每方法50个名义配置、20个样本移除配置。

Result: Robo-MLT任务成功率达82%与70%，分别超过π0.5基线30与22个百分点。当操作员移除正在处理的样本后，系统能修正剩余计划并继续处理其他样本。

Conclusion: 在所评估条件下，显式工作流监控使长时程实验室操作更可靠、更具可恢复性。

Abstract: Reliable medical laboratory automation requires robots to complete entire workflows in protocol order while handling changes during execution. A robot may execute each motion correctly yet fail the workflow by confusing visually similar stages or stalling after human intervention. We present Robo-MLT, a dual-system framework that couples explicit workflow monitoring with learned action generation. A vision--language planner composes an ordered atomic-skill sequence from the protocol and the initial workspace observation. A multiframe execution monitor detects when the active skill has completed or become infeasible, then advances the workflow or triggers replanning. A skill-conditioned vision--language--action policy generates the manipulation trajectories, and an asynchronous runtime coordinates it with the monitor. We evaluate Robo-MLT against four policy baselines and a VLM-based monitor baseline on blood gas analysis and blood sample tube sorting, with 50 nominal configurations per method and 20 sample-removal configurations. Robo-MLT achieves task success rates of 82\% and 70\%, exceeding $π_{0.5}$ by 30 and 22 percentage points, respectively. After an operator removes the sample being handled, the system revises the remaining plan and continues processing the other samples. These results indicate that, under the evaluated conditions, explicit workflow monitoring makes long-horizon laboratory manipulation more reliable and recoverable.

</details>


### [228] [ASPIRE: An Agentic System-Identification Platform for Iterative Reasoning and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13114)
*Erik Derner,Jiří Kubalík,Robert Babuška*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ASPIRE 框架，由大语言模型智能体自主完成非线性系统辨识全流程（方法选择、执行与验证）。在移动机器人、含干摩擦旋转系统与实验室发酵罐三套实测系统上共 30 次运行全部产出有效模型，单次成本 0.96–3.38 美元，作者自评 150 项清单中 132 项完全满足、18 项部分满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统辨识依赖专家在辨识方法选择、先验物理知识融入与模型验证上作出判断，门槛高、流程繁琐。作者希望将这一专家决策过程交由 LLM 智能体承担，从而降低人工依赖并实现"给定数据（与可选系统描述、用途、物理要求）即得可用模型"的自动化辨识。

Method: ASPIRE 让 LLM 智能体选择并执行辨识方法，从实测数据（可辅以系统描述、模型预期用途、以形式化或自然语言表述的物理要求）中构建闭环形式的非线性静态或动态模型。每个模型输出包含参数、估计记录、各项的物理解释，以及在智能体不可见的测试数据上计算的误差；形式化物理要求由独立程序校验。系统支持全自主与交互两种模式，智能体可提问，用户可要求追加分析与新一轮辨识。

Result: 在移动机器人、含干摩擦的旋转系统、实验室发酵罐三个实测对象上，每对象各设"有/无系统描述"两种条件、每任务 5 次运行。30 次运行全部给出有效模型，单次成本 0.96–3.38 美元。作者按 150 项评价条目评估 30 个推荐模型：132 项完全满足、18 项部分满足；最常见缺陷为模型结构选择相关的表述不准确或缺乏支撑（出现于 9 个模型）。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体可端到端驱动非线性系统辨识，输出在准确性与可追溯文档上总体可靠且成本可控，具备自主与交互协作能力。但结构选择论证的忠实性仍是主要薄弱环节，且评估为作者自评、样本量与系统类型有限，尚需独立第三方与更广泛基准的验证。

Abstract: System identification requires expert decisions on the identification method, incorporation of prior physical knowledge, and model validation. We propose ASPIRE, a framework for nonlinear system identification in which a large language model agent selects and executes the identification method. ASPIRE constructs closed-form, nonlinear static or dynamic models from measured data, optionally supplemented by a system description, the intended use of the model, and physical requirements expressed formally or in natural language. Each model includes its parameters, an estimation record, an interpretation of its terms, and an error evaluated on test data inaccessible to the agent. Formal physical requirements are verified independently. ASPIRE operates autonomously or interactively, allowing the agent to ask questions and the user to request further analyses and identification rounds. We evaluate ASPIRE on three measured systems: a mobile robot, a rotational system with dry friction, and a laboratory fermenter. Each system is tested with and without a description, in five runs per task. All 30 runs delivered valid models, at a cost of 0.96 to 3.38 US dollars per run. An assessment by the authors found that, for the 30 models recommended by the agent, one per run, the interpretation of the model and the documentation of its structure selection and parameter estimation fully satisfied 132 of 150 assessed items and partly satisfied 18. The most frequent discrepancy, found in nine models, concerned inaccurate or unsupported claims about model structure selection.

</details>


### [229] [DOBB: Dynamic Oriented Bounding Boxes for Multi-Agent Collision Avoidance under State Uncertainty and Heterogeneous Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13142)
*Pedro Antonio Alarcon Granadeno,Jane Cleland-Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动态定向包围盒（DOBB），将状态估计、不确定性估计与智能体响应特性映射为动态、方向相关的几何安全裕度，从而提升去中心化避碰在分布外扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的去中心化避碰方法仅依赖训练域随机化，或将不确定性与能力估计直接输入策略，使策略需自行推断这些量应如何改变安全交互所需的物理间隔，导致避碰行为与训练分布中学到的关系相耦合，难以在状态估计不确定和异构动力学下保持可靠。

Method: 构建在线几何表示DOBB：由当前状态估计、不确定性与智能体特定响应特性共同生成动态、方向依赖的安全裕度；推导同时刻画智能体DOBB间相对构型与交互严重程度的几何特征，并设计保持该交互物理可解释性的奖励函数。

Result: 在四个评估环境中，DOBB取得最低平均总失效率为2.6%；在执行动力学与状态估计不确定性的分布外偏移下性能保持，所有测试条件下基于碰撞的失效率均不超过2.3%。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性源于安全裕度的几何抽象：将不确定性、能力差异转化为显式几何量，而非交由策略隐式推断，从而降低对训练中所遇不确定性与执行机制的耦合，提升分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning-based decentralized collision avoidance must remain reliable under state-estimation uncertainty and heterogeneous agent dynamics. Existing methods typically address these factors through training-domain variation or by exposing uncertainty and capability estimates to the policy at runtime. However, both leave the policy to infer how these quantities should alter the physical separation required for safe interaction, which can couple collision-avoidance behavior to relationships learned from the training distribution. We introduce the Dynamic Oriented Bounding Box (DOBB), an online geometric representation that maps the current state estimate, uncertainty, and agent-specific response characteristics into a dynamic, direction-dependent safety margin. We derive geometric features that jointly characterize the relative configuration and severity of interactions between agents' DOBBs, along with a reward function that preserves the physical interpretation of those interactions. Across four evaluation environments, DOBB achieves the lowest mean total failure rate of 2.6%. This performance persists under out-of-distribution shifts in actuation dynamics and state-estimation uncertainty, with collision-based failure remaining at or below 2.3% in every tested condition. We attribute this robustness to the geometric abstraction of the safety margin, which is less coupled to the specific uncertainty and actuation regimes encountered during training.

</details>


### [230] [How Much of Your VLA Is kNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13160)
*Denis Grachev,Damien LaRocque,Omar Moured*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅通过检索邻近示范并复用其动作块（无需策略训练）即可在 LIBERO 上达到 91.7% 成功率；同时提出离线指标 LAC 衡量表征空间邻近样本的未来动作一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在 LIBERO 仿真基准上成功率已超 95%，但其性能究竟有多少来自对训练示范之外的真实泛化并不清楚，需要重新审视该基准所衡量的能力。

Method: 构建免训练的检索基线：检索邻近示范并复用其动作块。在 LIBERO-Plus 扰动下比较各策略下降幅度；对 9 个公开 VLA 进行微调分析，考察骨干网络作为检索键的质量及输出动作与示范的接近程度；提出离线指标 Local Action Consistency (LAC)，度量表征空间邻近观测是否对应相似未来动作，并用于指导检索键训练。

Result: 检索基线在 LIBERO 上达 91.7% 成功率；在 LIBERO-Plus 扰动下其性能下降幅度为所有评估策略中最小；微调主要使 VLA 的视觉-语言骨干成为更好的检索键，而 VLA 输出动作仍接近示范动作；LAC 与检索成功率呈正相关，可有效指导检索键训练。代码与评测已开源。

Conclusion: LIBERO 上的高成功率很大程度上可由示范复用解释，泛化成分有限；LAC 作为离线诊断与训练信号具有实用价值，并提示需重新设计真正衡量泛化的评测协议。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models now exceed 95% success on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, yet it remains unclear how much of this performance requires generalization beyond the training demonstrations. In this work, we show that retrieving nearby demonstrations and reusing their action chunks achieves a success rate of 91.7 % on LIBERO without policy training. Under LIBERO-Plus perturbations, retrieval shows the smallest drop of all evaluated policies. Across nine publicly available VLAs, fine-tuning mostly makes the vision-language backbone a better retrieval key, and the actions of the VLAs remain close to the demonstrations. In addition, we introduce Local Action Consistency (LAC), an offline metric that measures whether nearby observations in representation space are also associated with similar future actions. We show that LAC tracks the success rate of retrieval and can guide the training of a retrieval key. Our evaluation and source code are available at https://github.com/sereact/lerobot_knn.

</details>


### [231] [Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control for Underactuated Upright Physical Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13204)
*Darren Biskup,Jeonghan Yu,Zibo Yu,Sunil K. Agrawal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRF-MPC框架，将实时地面反作用力与压力中心融入人体质心动力学模型，用滚动时域QP优化辅助力/力矩，在欠驱动4缆RobUST上以半数量执行器实现优于8缆全驱动阻抗基线的人体直立辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有康复机器人多采用反应式跟踪控制，执行预设动作，未显式纳入用户瞬时的人-地交互信息，导致辅助"僵硬"、不透明且能量效率低。

Method: 构建GRF-MPC：把在线GRF与CoP测量直接嵌入人体质心动力学模型，形成实时二次规划（QP），在滚动时域内优化辅助力与力矩；部署于RobUST的欠驱动4缆配置，并与全驱动8缆笛卡尔阻抗基线在6名健康受试者、近300次试验中对比（任务含脉冲扰动、弓步轨迹、自主稳定极限）。

Result: 扰动下平面质心峰值误差降低19.6%，轨迹跟踪误差降低24.1%，净机械功减少28.7%，归一化自主偏移面积增大45.9%；底层QP求解耗时<1 ms，支持100 Hz重规划。

Conclusion: 将实时人-地交互纳入预测控制可产生更强、更透明的物理辅助，且仅需一半执行器即可超越全驱动阻抗基线，验证了GRF感知MPC在直立辅助中的有效性。

Abstract: Many rehabilitation robots execute prescribed motions using reactive tracking controllers that do not explicitly incorporate the user's instantaneous human-ground interaction. This paper presents Ground Reaction Force-Aware Model Predictive Control (GRF-MPC), a framework for upright physical assistance. By integrating live GRF and center of pressure (CoP) measurements directly into a human centroidal dynamics model, GRF-MPC formulates a real-time quadratic program (QP) that optimizes assistive forces and moments over a receding horizon. We deployed GRF-MPC on the Robotic Upright Stand Trainer (RobUST) in an underactuated 4-cable configuration and evaluated it against a fully actuated 8-cable Cartesian impedance baseline in nearly 300 trials across six healthy participants. Across tasks spanning impulsive perturbations, lunge trajectories, and voluntary limits of stability, GRF-MPC reduced peak planar center-of-mass (CoM) error by 19.6% during perturbations, lowered trajectory tracking error by 24.1%, required 28.7% less net mechanical work, and permitted a 45.9% greater normalized voluntary excursion area. The underlying QP solves in under 1 ms, comfortably enabling 100 Hz replanning and demonstrating that incorporating live human-ground interaction into predictive control yields stronger, more transparent physical assistance with half the actuators.

</details>


### [232] [Latent Barrier Steering: Hierarchical Safety for Generative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13227)
*Renhao Zhang,Mingzhe Li,Bruno Castro da Silva*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Barrier Steering（LBS），一种面向 flow-matching 生成式规划的分层语义到路径安全框架：先在行为潜在空间导向更安全的 rollout，再仅对残余违规施加路径空间 CBF-QP 校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向扩散/flow-matching 规划器的控制障碍函数（CBF）安全过滤器只在采样过程中或采样后对生成样本做局部修复，适合小违规；当新约束封锁了整个被选中的行为模式（而非局部扰动）时，这类局部修复容易失效。

Method: 将行为选择与路径认证解耦，采用两级协同干预：(1) 将生成器的行为潜在表示导向安全裕度更大的 rollout；(2) 解码导向后的潜在表示，并用路径空间 CBF 二次规划（QP）仅处理残余违规。理论上证明潜在导向可增大路径空间校正器可用的安全裕度。

Result: 给出有限时间恢复保证，并表明校正负担下降；在导航与机器人末端执行器规划任务上，相比路径级安全过滤器，LBS 在保持安全性的同时提升了目标到达率并减少了校正负担。

Conclusion: LBS 通过潜在层语义导向与路径层 CBF 校正的分工，缓解了行为模式级约束冲突下的局部修复失效问题，在安全性与任务完成度之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce Latent Barrier Steering (LBS), a hierarchical semantic-to-path framework for safe flow-matching-based generative planning. Existing control-barrier-function (CBF) safety filters for diffusion and flow-matching planners act locally on generated samples, either during sampling or after prediction. Such repair is effective for small violations, but can struggle when a new constraint blocks the whole selected behavior mode rather than merely perturbing it locally. LBS decouples behavior selection from path certification through two coordinated interventions: it first steers the generator's behavior latent toward rollouts with larger safety margin, then decodes the steered latent and applies a path-space CBF quadratic program (QP) only for residual violations. We show that latent steering increases the margin available to the path-space corrector, yielding finite-time recovery guarantees and reduced correction burden. Experiments across navigation and robot end-effector planning tasks show that LBS preserves safety while improving goal reaching and reducing correction burden compared with path-level safety filters.

</details>


### [233] [Incorporating World Models in Decision Making for Next Best View Selection in Active Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13229)
*Nikita Sarawgi,Omey M. Manyar,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向下一最佳视角(NBV)选择的部署适配方法：冻结预训练世界模型，仅用少量本地标定数据学习一个"传感器回波适配器"，估计预测表面上的测量缺失概率，从而优先选择能填补实测重建空洞的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效机器人3D重建需以尽量少的物理采集选出能带来新表面测量的视角。预训练世界模型可指导视角选择，但其预测表面在实际传感器工作条件下未必能产生有效深度测量（如结构光传感器在特定表面上的测量缺失），导致标称选择策略反复对准却无法测得的目标区域。

Method: 模块化部署适配框架：保持预训练预测器固定不动，从有限的本地标定数据中训练一个轻量传感器回波适配器，对每个预测表面逐点估计测量丢包（dropout）概率；将该估计接入NBV选择流程，使效用评估偏向于更可能填补实测重建空洞的视角。方法核心是"冻结预测+局部适配"的解耦设计。

Result: 在真实机器人平台上使用结构光传感器开展实验：适配后的方法相比标称选择在实测表面覆盖率上更高，且采集次数减少60%；能恢复标称方法反复指向但始终未能测到的表面区域，验证了适配对系统性测量偏差的纠正能力。

Conclusion: 机器人可在数据稀缺条件下复用预训练预测模型，仅通过少量本地标定将决策适配到实际运行条件；预测能力与部署条件之间的失配可由轻量适配层解耦处理，为NBV及更广泛的感知决策提供可迁移范式。

Abstract: Efficient robotic 3D reconstruction requires selecting views that contribute new surface measurements with few physical captures. Pretrained world models can guide this choice, but a predicted surface may not produce valid depth measurements under the operating conditions of the deployed sensor. We present a deployment adaptation methodology for next-best-view selection that learns a sensor-return adapter from limited local calibration data while keeping the pretrained predictor fixed. The adapter estimates measurement dropout over each predicted surface, allowing the selection method to favor views likely to fill gaps in the measured reconstruction. In a real-robot experiment with a structured-light sensor, the adapted method achieves higher measured surface coverage with 60% fewer captures than nominal selection. The adapted method recovers a surface region that the nominal method repeatedly targets but fails to measure. These results suggest that robots can reuse pretrained predictive models while adapting their decisions to local operating conditions in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [234] [Curie's Jarvis: Autonomous Robotic Chemistry Experiments from Printed Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13251)
*Rumi Loghmani,Bozhang Song,Victor Alvarado,Aliyah Redding,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.RO
