TL;DR: 该论文提出Curie's Jarvis系统，使机械臂能够在为人类布置的普通实验台上完成化学实验：通过拍摄打印纸质操作说明读取步骤，无需固定容器位置或基准标记即可定位容器，并通过读取四个相机视角下的标签区分外观相同的容器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验室自动化依赖脚本化流程、固定容器位置或基准标记（fiducial markers），与人类实验操作方式相反，难以适用于为人类设计的普通实验台。作者希望让机器人像人一样直接在"人类布置"的实验台上操作。

Method: Curie's Jarvis包含多项能力：(1) 从打印说明书照片中读取实验步骤；(2) 在无固定位置、无标记的普通实验台上找到容器；(3) 通过四个相机视角读取标签区分外观相同的容器；(4) 在每个步骤执行前重新定位目标；(5) 根据所处理对象的类型决定执行方式（包括速度）。系统在Franka Panda机械臂上实现。

Result: 在Franka Panda机械臂上，Curie's Jarvis无需人工干预，完成了一套儿童化学实验套件中某协议的5/5次运行以及另一协议的4/5次运行；在160次针对打印和手写标签的定位中，系统对所有容器的识别均正确。

Conclusion: 该系统推动机器人向"像人一样在实验台上进行化学实验"迈进一步，展示了在非结构化人类工作环境中执行实验流程的可行性。

Abstract: A person running a bench experiment works from a printed instruction sheet, tells identical containers apart by the labels written on them, and looks at the bench again before each step. Lab automation usually works the other way around: the procedure is written as a script, and containers stand at fixed positions or carry fiducial markers. We present Curie's Jarvis, a system that lets a robot arm carry out a chemistry experiment on a bench set up for people. It reads the steps from a photo of the printed sheet, finds containers on an ordinary bench with no fixed positions or markers, tells identical-looking containers apart by reading their labels in four camera views, locates its target again before every step, and sets how each step is carried out, including its speed, from what is being handled. On a Franka Panda arm, Curie's Jarvis completed 5 of 5 runs of one protocol from a children's chemistry kit and 4 of 5 runs of another, with no human intervention, and in 160 locates on printed and handwritten labels it identified every container correctly. This brings robots a step closer to doing bench chemistry the way people do.

</details>


### [235] [Force-Closure Condition for Cooperative Multi-robot Pushing of Rectangular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13268)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文推导了矩形物体在四个无摩擦推动接触下的平面力封闭充要条件闭式判据$(l_1+l_3-b)(l_2+l_4-a)<0$，并给出不满足时的接触调整规则，将其嵌入全位姿轨迹跟踪框架验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作操作物体时，推动接触点的布局决定能否实现力封闭，从而决定智能体能否对物体施加任意力与力矩。现有工作缺乏针对常见矩形物体在四无摩擦接触下可事先验证的显式力封闭判据。

Method: 解析推导矩形（长b、宽a）在四个无摩擦接触点（边缘位置$l_1,l_2,l_3,l_4$）下的平面力封闭充要条件，得到闭式不等式判据，并利用两因子符号判断需调整的对置接触对；将该判据作为几何可行性层集成到全位姿轨迹跟踪控制框架中，在接触力分配前完成接触构型校验；通过静态力旋量可行性测试与狭窄通道浮动体仿真进行验证。

Result: 静态力旋量可行性测试验证了判据的正确性；狭窄通道浮动体仿真中，保持力封闭构型使$x$、$y$和展开偏航角的RMS跟踪误差分别从12.05 m、18.11 m、535.96°降至2.24 m、2.66 m、2.69°。

Conclusion: 所提闭式条件与物体质心无关，可廉价地在力分配前校验接触布局，两因子符号提供了明确的调整方向；将其作为几何可行性层嵌入控制框架能显著降低跟踪误差，说明接触构型的力封闭性对协作操作控制性能至关重要。

Abstract: In cooperative object manipulation, the placement of pushing contacts determines whether force closure can be achieved and agents can generate arbitrary forces and torques on the object correspondingly. This study derives a necessary and sufficient closed-form condition for the planar force closure of a rectangular object, a common object shape in everyday settings, under four frictionless pushing contacts. For a rectangle of length $b$ and width $a$, force closure holds if and only if $(l_1+l_3-b)(l_2+l_4-a)<0$, where $l_1,l_2,l_3,l_4$ are the four edge contact locations. This condition is independent of the object's center of mass and is used to verify contact configurations before contact force allocation. When the condition is not satisfied, the signs of the two factors indicate which opposing contact pair should be adjusted. The condition and adjustment rule are then integrated into a full-pose trajectory-tracking framework as a geometric feasibility layer to demonstrate their use in control. Static wrench feasibility tests validate the proposed condition, while narrow-passage floating-body simulations show that maintaining a force-closure configuration reduces the RMS tracking errors in $x$, $y$, and unwrapped yaw from $12.05$ m, $18.11$ m, and $535.96^\circ$ to $2.24$ m, $2.66$ m, and $2.69^\circ$, respectively.

</details>


### [236] [RaMOD: Moving-Object Detection from Raw mmWave Radar Cubes under Ego Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13271)
*Yuan Fang,Long Zhuang,Lingzi Xing,Xijia Wei,Jingqi Jiang,Fangzhan Shi,Simon Julier,Kevin Chetty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RaMOD 是一个基于原始距离-方位-多普勒（RAD）立方体的实时运动目标检测与速度估计框架，通过显式估计自车运动抑制静态结构多普勒响应，并处理多普勒卷绕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车载感知流水线把毫米波雷达输出压缩为稀疏点云或二维投影，浪费了原始 RAD 立方体信息；同时自车运动使世界静态结构产生多普勒响应，且多普勒卷绕造成径向速度混叠，增加运动目标检测与测速难度。

Method: RaMOD 逐帧显式估计自车运动，用估计的雷达平移速度预测静态结构的多普勒区域并抑制该区域回波；将抑制后的立方体在时间维度编码为多尺度特征，预测带类别标签的 RAD 空间框；对每个目标在原始多普勒与解卷绕假设下预测径向与纵向速度，并给出多普勒卷绕概率。

Result: 在自建 BRIDGE-Liv 数据集上，RaMOD 在 RAD 立方体、RA、RD 三种检测设置下均优于所有对比的原始雷达方法；IoU 0.30 时立方体 mAP 达 80.8%，比最强 RAD 立方体基线高 15.7 个百分点；速度 MAE 1.128 m/s，多普勒卷绕判定准确率 99.87%。

Conclusion: 直接在原始 RAD 立方体上建模、结合自车运动补偿与多普勒解卷绕假设，可显著提升毫米波雷达运动目标检测与速度估计性能，说明原始雷达数据仍有较大利用潜力。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar provides weather-robust sensing and direct Doppler measurements, yet automotive perception pipelines often reduce its output to sparse point clouds or two-dimensional projections, leaving raw range-azimuth-Doppler (RAD) cubes underutilized. Ego motion induces Doppler responses from world-static structures, while Doppler wrapping aliases radial velocities, complicating moving-object detection and velocity estimation. We present RaMOD (Radar-based Moving-Object Detection), a real-time framework for moving-object detection and velocity estimation from raw RAD cubes acquired by a vehicle-mounted single-chip radar. RaMOD explicitly estimates frame-wise ego motion and uses the estimated radar translational velocity to predict the Doppler region of world-static structures and suppress returns within it. It temporally encodes the suppressed cubes into multi-scale features to predict RAD-space boxes with class labels. For each detected object, it predicts radial and forward velocities under raw-Doppler and Doppler-unwrapped hypotheses, together with Doppler-wrapping probabilities. On our BRIDGE-Liv dataset, RaMOD outperforms all compared raw-radar methods across RAD-cube, RA, and RD detection settings, achieving 80.8% cube mAP at IoU 0.30, 15.7 percentage points above the strongest RAD-cube baseline. Velocity MAE is 1.128 m/s, with 99.87% Doppler-wrapping accuracy.

</details>


### [237] [Large Language Model Explainability for Planning Lunar Rover Trajectories under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13292)
*Michelle Ho,Maximilian Adang,Zachary Booth,David E. Astle,Margaret Hansen,Edward Balaban,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式助手，用于解释行星漫游车在概率不确定性下的规划轨迹，通过ReAct接口调用工具与检索函数，90.9%的回复忠实于规划器输出，较零样本提示提升近65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学团队目前手工迭代求解并验证漫游车多目标轨迹规划，任务规划期时间线与约束频繁变动使其难以实施；蒙特卡洛树搜索等采样规划器虽能加速，但输出缺乏对科学目标的溯源依据，团队无法据此验证方案。

Method: 构建模拟器（受NASA VIPER任务启发）与ReAct可查询接口配对框架，接口调用精选动作工具与检索函数；LLM不介入规划决策环，仅用于规划后解释，以领域词汇解释轨迹并按用户指定条件重跑规划器比较备选方案。

Result: 在包含方案对比、可行性与查询类问题的查询库上评估，助手90.9%的回复忠实于规划器输出，相比同一LLM无工具访问的零样本提示提升近65%。

Conclusion: 将LLM置于规划后解释环节而非决策回路，可提升可信度，验证了基于LLM系统对不确定性感知通行轨迹实现可信可解释性的潜力。

Abstract: Planning planetary rover trajectories under probabilistic uncertainty is a multi-objective optimization problem that science teams currently iteratively solve and verify by hand. This process becomes impractical when timelines and constraints frequently shift throughout the mission planning phase. Sampling-based planners such as Monte Carlo tree search can speed up this process, but their outputs do not preserve traceability to the science objectives that justify each plan, which the science team needs to verify. We propose an interactive assistant based on a large language model (LLM) that can explain planner trajectories using domain vocabulary and re-run the planner under user-specified conditions to compare alternatives. Previous work has used LLMs inside planner decision-making loops, but we explore using them for post-planning explanation, where they are more likely to be trusted since they do not directly influence planner decisions. Our framework pairs a simulator inspired by the NASA Volatiles Investigating Polar Exploration Rover (VIPER) mission with a ReAct queryable interface that calls curated action tools and retrieval functions. We evaluate the assistant on a query bank consisting of plan comparisons, feasibility, and lookup queries. Experiments show that 90.9% of the assistant's responses are faithful to the planner's outputs, a nearly 65% improvement over zero-shot prompting of the same LLM without tool access, demonstrating the potential for trustworthy explainability of uncertainty-aware traverses with an LLM-based system.

</details>


### [238] [Does Haptic Connection to a Healthy Partner Improve Motor Learning in Chronic Stroke? A Pilot Study of Robot-Based Dyadic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13307)
*Erica L. Waters,Natalia Stroutinsky-Sobotka,Reem M. Azar,Frances S. Shofer,Pamela Z. Cacchione,Rochelle J. Mendonca,Michelle J. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该预实验比较慢性脑卒中患者在单独与双人（机器人触觉配对）条件下学习复杂追踪任务的表现，发现仅单独组出现短期追踪误差下降且不稳健、两组均无清晰保留，但双人组训练中表现、努力与运动复杂度更高；基线追踪误差与认知、运动评分强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人触觉配对（两名用户通过机器人交换力）被认为可通过促进运动变异性、提供隐式沟通通道和促进社交互动来增强运动学习，是机器人辅助神经康复的潜在手段；但针对神经系统人群的研究很少，且尚无包含全面临床评估的工作。

Method: 开展慢性脑卒中个体的预实验：训练前完成认知、运动与感觉测评组合；在单独与双人条件下学习复杂追踪任务，评估追踪误差、移动距离、平均绝对功率、样本熵等关键运动学指标变化，并对临床评分与基线追踪误差作相关分析。

Result: 基线追踪误差与较低认知评分、较高运动评分强相关（r > 0.6）；仅单独组在评估时追踪误差下降（Δ = -5.33，95%CI [-10.25, -0.40]，p = 0.027），但剔除一名离群被试后该结论被削弱；两组均无明确保留效应；双人组训练期间表现、努力程度与运动复杂度显著更高。

Conclusion: 所选任务过难（可能与其高认知负荷有关），未能显现任一组的学习效应；样本量与卒中异质性亦为局限。配对训练提升了与更优运动学习相关的运动复杂度与努力；病变部位、认知、感觉与病程等临床特征可能影响配对训练的潜在获益。

Abstract: Purpose: Robot-based haptic dyads, where two users exchange forces through robots, have been proposed for robot-assisted neurorehabilitation. This intervention could increase motor learning by promoting motor variability, providing an implicit communication channel, and facilitating social interaction. Despite the potential benefits, few studies have investigated robot-based haptic dyads for neurological populations, and none have included a comprehensive clinical assessment. Design/Methodology/Approach: We present a pilot study investigation of individuals with chronic stroke learning a complex tracking task in solo and dyad conditions. Prior to training, participants completed a battery of cognitive, motor, and sensory assessments. We assessed changes in key kinematic outcomes including tracking error, distance traveled, mean absolute power, and sample entropy. We also performed a correlation analysis of clinical scores with baseline tracking errors. Findings: Baseline tracking error was strongly correlated (r > 0.6) with lower cognitive scores and higher motor scores. Only the solo group demonstrated lower tracking error at assessment (Δ = -5.33, 95%CI [-10.25, -0.40], p = 0.027), but this result was tempered by the removal of an outlier participant. Neither group demonstrated clear retention. The dyad group showed significantly elevated performance, effort, and movement complexity during training. Research Limitations/Implications: The selected task was too difficult to reveal learning for either group, which may be related to its high cognitive demand. Sample size and stroke heterogeneity were also limitation. Originality/Value: Dyadic training increased movement complexity and effort, which are associated with better motor learning. Clinical characteristics such as lesion location, cognition, sensation, and chronicity may influence potential benefits of dyadic training.

</details>


### [239] [PneuGen: Task-Driven Agentic Co-Design of Soft Pneumatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13344)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Perla Maiolino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PneuGen——一个基于大语言模型（LLM）智能体的软体机器人协同设计框架，通过"设计智能体+批评智能体"迭代提出与修正设计程序，并结合模块响应模型与有限元仿真实现高效设计空间探索，在抓取与形状匹配任务上显著优于传统计算优化基线，并完成真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人任务驱动设计涉及几何、形态、材料属性与驱动方式等多因素耦合，设计空间庞大复杂；现有计算优化方法在优化与仿真两端均计算负担沉重，探索效率低。

Method: PneuGen 采用智能体协同设计框架：设计智能体从参数化组件库生成设计程序；批评智能体分析仿真诊断信息并给出针对性修改建议；拟合的模块响应模型提供几何-压力-变形的可复用物理先验；设计程序自动校验后转换为有限元仿真，支持迭代评估。

Result: 在基本形状与困难物体的抓取任务，以及需要多方向平面与三维变形的形状匹配任务中，框架显著优于既有计算优化基线；消融实验表明批评智能体与模块响应模型对高效设计均有贡献；并在真实世界形状匹配与抓取任务中展示了智能体生成的软体机器人设计。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体结合物理先验与仿真反馈，可作为软体机器人任务驱动设计的高效协同设计范式，并具备向真实场景迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Task-driven design of soft robots requires the consideration of factors such as geometry, morphology, material properties, and actuation, resulting in a large and complex design space that is challenging to explore. Computational optimisation methods leverage signals from simulation to iteratively improve designs to achieve task objectives. However, these methods typically demand high computational load for both optimisation and simulation. We present PneuGen, an agentic co-design framework for soft robots that leverages Large Language Model (LLM) agents to search over the soft robot design space efficiently. A designer agent proposes design programs from a parametric component library, and a separate critic agent analyses simulation diagnostics and recommends targeted revisions. Fitted module response models provide reusable physical priors relating geometry and pressure to deformation. Design programs are automatically validated and converted into finite-element simulations, enabling iterative evaluation. Across grasping tasks on primitive shapes and challenging objects, as well as shape-matching tasks that require multi-directional planar and 3D deformation, the proposed framework significantly outperforms prior computational optimisation baselines. Ablation experiments also highlight the utility of the critic agent and the module response model in facilitating efficient design. We additionally demonstrate the agent-generated soft robot designs in real-world shape-matching and grasping tasks.

</details>


### [240] [AHEADance: Anticipatory Humanoid Dance from Streaming Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13370)
*Tianhu Peng,Yukun Wang,Lingfan Bao,Yiyang Jia,Chengxu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AHEADance，一种机器人原生的流式舞蹈生成器，通过 ListenAhead（基于冻结音乐模型的可信度加权未来音乐预测来条件化每个动作块）与 Commit Forcing（从模型自身已提交的动作块训练续接）实现人形机器人在音乐流式播放中提前生成并续接舞蹈动作，并在 Unitree G1 实机上落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人随流式音乐跳舞存在本质时序矛盾：每个动作块必须在听到对应音乐之前提交，同时又必须从已提交的动作继续。已有音乐驱动舞蹈方法多为离线、非因果生成，无法满足这种因果约束，且续接易出现低活动（动作停滞）问题。

Method: 1) ListenAhead：以已听音乐与冻结音乐模型对即将到来音乐的预测作为条件，预测按目标时间对齐并按可靠性加权；2) Commit Forcing：以模型自身已提交的动作块为历史训练续接，同时更新潜在历史与下一块的解码边界；3) 采用全身跟踪器在实体人形机器人上执行生成动作；4) 构建三个分别在真实 G1 舞蹈上验证过的音乐评估器，分别考察同步性、歌曲与风格匹配。

Result: 未来音乐预测在节拍时序上几乎与真实未来同等准确；引入后生成与音乐同步性更好、动作能量更多集中于歌曲节拍频率。Commit Forcing 将低活动比例从 83.5% 降至 10.3%，达到真实舞蹈水平。在实体 Unitree G1 上实现音乐播放时起舞、停止时归于静止，且无跌倒。

Conclusion: 因果流式约束下的舞蹈生成可通过"预测未来音乐 + 自条件续接训练"有效解决，配合经真实舞蹈验证的评估体系与全身跟踪执行，使机器人原生流式舞蹈在实际硬件上可行。项目主页：https://ihcr.github.io/AHEADance。

Abstract: A humanoid that dances to streamed music must commit each motion chunk before it has heard the music that chunk accompanies, and must continue from motion it has already committed. We present AHEADance, a robot-native streaming dance generator executed on a humanoid robot by a whole-body tracker. ListenAhead conditions each chunk on heard music and a frozen music model's forecasts of the coming music, aligned to their target times and weighted by reliability. The forecasts carry the timing of upcoming beats almost as well as the actual future; with them, the generator is better synchronized with the music and concentrates more motion energy at its song's beat frequency. Commit Forcing trains continuation from the model's own committed chunks, updating both the latent history and the decoded boundary of the next chunk; it reduces low activity from 83.5% to 10.3%, the level of real dance. Three music evaluators, each validated on real G1 dance, test synchronization, song and style. On a physical Unitree G1, it dances while the music plays and comes to rest when it stops, without a fall. Project page: https://ihcr.github.io/AHEADance

</details>


### [241] [Fastener-centric acquisition and AND/OR graph representation of state-dependent disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13379)
*Shiki Iwase*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以紧固件为中心的虚拟拆卸框架，从CAD派生虚拟装配体中获取与装配状态相关的拆卸操作知识，并组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph用于规划与设计评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计阶段的拆卸性评估需要产品结构与各装配状态下可用操作的知识，但CAD装配体并不直接给出状态相关的拆卸操作描述。

Method: 将共同释放同一约束的紧固件归为操作级集合，避免枚举任意单紧固件顺序；通过状态相关的可见性与工具可达性检查、扭矩驱动的虚拟移除试验评估可拆卸性；紧固件移除后，用图推理识别候选运动单元，并经由初始运动筛选、路径生成与物理仿真验证其分离可行性；将操作、状态、依赖关系、验证路径及仿真属性组织为Transformed AND/OR Graph。

Result: 受控玩具装配体的穷举验证在引入工具可达性与优先约束后未发现分支违规；54组件OpenManipulator Chain夹爪实验展示了状态相关的紧固件移除与零件/子装配分离操作，可见性与工具可达性评估剪枝了51.4%的紧固件集合候选评估，且移除阻碍组件可解锁原本不可达的紧固件操作。

Conclusion: 操作级分组与筛选使详细物理验证仅集中于机械上合理的候选项；结果表明可在制造前从CAD派生虚拟装配体获取产品特定拆卸知识，支撑后续拆卸推理与面向拆卸的设计评估。

Abstract: Design-stage disassemblability assessment requires knowledge of product structure and the operations available in each assembly state, but CAD assemblies do not directly specify state-dependent disassembly operations. This paper proposes a fastener-centric virtual-disassembly framework that acquires such knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies. Fasteners jointly releasing the same constraint are grouped into operation-level sets, avoiding enumeration of arbitrary individual-fastener orders. Their removability is evaluated through state-dependent visibility and tool-access checks and torque-driven virtual-removal trials. After fastener removal, graph-based reasoning identifies candidate moving units, whose separation is evaluated by initial-motion screening, path generation, and physics-based validation. The acquired operations, states, dependencies, validated paths, and simulation-derived attributes are organized into a Transformed AND/OR Graph for subsequent planning and design evaluation. Exhaustive verification with a controlled toy assembly found no branching violations for introduced tool-accessibility and precedence conditions. Evaluation with a 54-component OpenManipulator Chain gripper demonstrated state-dependent fastener-removal and part- or subassembly-separation operations. Visibility and tool-accessibility evaluations pruned 51.4% of fastener-set candidate evaluations, while removal of obstructing components enabled previously inaccessible fastener operations. Operation-level grouping and screening thereby limited detailed physical validation to mechanically plausible candidates. The results demonstrate the feasibility of acquiring product-specific disassembly knowledge from CAD-derived virtual assemblies before manufacture, supporting subsequent disassembly reasoning and design-for-disassembly evaluation.

</details>


### [242] [Discrete Model-Based Diffusion for Combinatorial Optimization in Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13382)
*Haejoon Lee,Thomas Marshall Vielmetti,Xinyi Wang,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无训练的离散模型引导扩散方法 Discrete MBD，直接优化离散变量并具理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体/多机器人系统的离散决策属NP-hard组合优化，需秒级求解。现有去噪扩散方法存在两难：MBD在采样优化中无需训练但仅限连续空间；离散扩散组合求解器需离线训练。

Method: 提出 Discrete MBD：一种无训练的采样优化（SBO）方法，通过蒙特卡洛采样近似类别型反向转移分布，从而直接在离散变量上执行扩散优化；并给出有限步下找到全局最优解概率的下界分析。

Result: 理论上下界随去噪步数增加收敛至1；在通用整数规划及异构多机器人联盟形成与调度实验中，几乎全部设置优于SBO基线，解质量与混合整数规划、强化学习相当，计算开销仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: Discrete MBD以无训练、低成本方式统一了连续MBD的免训练优势与离散求解能力，为近实时组合优化提供有效方案。

Abstract: Decision making in multi-agent and multi-robot systems often involves discrete decision variables, leading to NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems that must be solved within seconds for near real-time implementation. Denoising diffusion has emerged as a powerful tool for such problems, but existing approaches face a trade-off: Model-Based Diffusion (MBD) incorporates diffusion in sampling-based optimization (SBO) without training but only over continuous spaces, while existing diffusion-based combinatorial solvers operate over discrete spaces but require offline training. To this end, we propose Discrete Model-Based Diffusion (Discrete MBD), a training-free SBO method that directly optimizes discrete variables by approximating categorical reverse transition distributions through Monte Carlo sampling. We establish a finite-step lower bound on the probability of finding a global minimizer and show that this bound converges to one as the number of denoising steps increases. Extensive experiments on generic integer programs and heterogeneous multi-robot coalition formation and scheduling demonstrate that Discrete MBD outperforms SBO baselines in nearly all settings while achieving solution quality competitive with that of mixed-integer programming and reinforcement learning methods at a fraction of their computational cost.

</details>


### [243] [Rhythmic-Semantic Co-Speech Gestures for Emotional Dialogue in Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13472)
*Christian Arzate Cruz,Edwin C. Montiel-Vázquez,Stefanos Gkikas,Miguel Mejia Tobar,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出情感感知的节奏-语义协同语音手势系统，可基于文本与机器人内部情感状态实时生成图标手势与节拍手势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动机器人系统多只生成节拍式运动或语义性图标手势，二者难以兼得；而协同语音手势对提升参与度与语音理解很重要，因此需要统一生成兼具节奏与语义的手势并融入情感。

Method: 将"节拍-图标手势生成"任务在情感条件下形式化；采用轻量模型预测图标手势的时间位置与强度，剩余时段以情感条件化的节拍手势按合适强度填充；在BEAT2数据集上训练，并部署于社交机器人Haru。

Result: 60人线上用户研究表明，该方法在语音-手势对齐性上表现更强，且相较基线方法更受被试偏好。

Conclusion: 情感条件化的节奏-语义联合建模可在实时条件下提升机器人协同语音手势的语义一致性与用户接受度。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures can increase engagement and improve speech understanding. Yet, most data-driven robot systems generate either rhythmic beat-like motion or semantically meaningful iconic gestures, but not both. We present an emotion-aware rhythmic-semantic co-speech gesture system that generates iconic and beat behaviors in real time from text and the robot's internal emotional state. We formalize the task of beat-iconic gesture generation using emotion and propose lightweight models that predict iconic timing and intensity, then fill the remaining spans with emotion-conditioned beats at an appropriate intensity level. We train on the BEAT2 dataset and deploy the system on the social robot Haru. In an online user study with 60 participants, our method shows stronger speech-gesture alignment and is preferred over baseline methods.

</details>


### [244] [ExRL: Sample-Efficient Online Adaptation of Long-Chunk Policies via Execution Length Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13482)
*Xu Luo,Jiaying Yang,Hao Wu,Junlin Xie,Zijin Hong,Ji Zhang,Lianli Gao,Jingkuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExRL，将对长动作块策略的强化学习适配从“优化动作”转移到“优化每个预测动作块的执行长度”，从而显著降低适配空间的维度，实现样本高效的在线适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长动作块（long action chunk）策略已成为高性能机器人策略的关键组成部分，但用现有RL方法对其进行微调时，需要优化的动作块维度极高，导致在线样本需求巨大；而真实场景中交互数据采集成本高昂，使这种适配难以落地。

Method: ExRL（Execution at the Right Length）：在基础策略之上附加一个轻量级adapter，用于预测每个预测动作块的执行长度（即执行多少步后切换到下一个块）。适配只在该低维执行长度空间中进行，采用off-policy RL算法训练；执行长度的表达能力随块大小增长，但待优化维度保持较低。

Result: 在仿真基准与真实机器人任务中，ExRL 能够对多种不同的长动作块策略实现样本高效的适配，且只需极少的任务特定超参数调优。

Conclusion: 将RL适配的目标从高维动作块转为低维执行长度，是一种简单且通用的方案，可显著缓解长块策略在线适配的样本效率瓶颈，具备在真实机器人场景中实用的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting long action chunks, often spanning dozens of timesteps, has become a key ingredient in increasingly capable robot policies. In this paper, we find that adapting these long-chunk policies with existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods can require many online samples due to the high dimensionality of the action chunks being optimized, making such adaptation impractical in real-world scenarios where interaction data are costly to collect. Our key insight is to shift adaptation from optimizing actions to optimizing how long to execute each predicted action chunk. This keeps the adaptation space low-dimensional while its expressivity grows with the chunk size. We thus introduce Execution at the Right Length (ExRL), a simple approach which augments the base policy with a lightweight adapter that predicts the execution length of each chunk and is trained using off-policy RL algorithms. Across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic tasks, ExRL enables sample-efficient adaptation of a diverse range of long-chunk policies with minimal task-specific hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [245] [Route-Time Conflict Maps for Cooperative End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13494)
*Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Tianle Zhu,Zhipeng Bao,Yiping Li,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: When2Go是一个达到SAE J3216 CDA Class B及以上的协同端到端驾驶系统：车辆广播"实例token"形式的LiDAR检测+预测运动、自身plan与意图轨迹，接收方构建"路径-时间冲突图"并据此对候选速度曲线打分；当双方意图轨迹冲突时，由基于通行权规则蒸馏出的"顺序门"（优先序网络）决定谁先行，双方用相同消息得到相同次序。单模型在InterDrive、V2Xverse、MDrive三个CARLA闭环基准上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同端到端驾驶工作停留在CDA Class A（状态共享）层面：只交换"感知到什么"而非"打算做什么"，因此各自独立规划何时通行，合作受限——双方同时让行会死锁，同时抢行会碰撞。

Method: 1) 共享意图：每车广播LiDAR检测（带预测运动，编码为实例token）、plan与意图轨迹；2) 接收端融合检测、预测与伙伴plan，构建沿自身路径在规划时域上的路径-时间冲突图（占用图），规划器据此为候选速度曲线打分；3) 冲突消解：对冲突的意图轨迹引入"顺序门"——由通行权规则蒸馏得到的优先序网络，双方在同一消息上评估，保证次序一致；4) 模仿V2Xverse中可见仿真真值的规则专家，并将超出2s规划时域、不可观测的停车重新标注，避免学习"无可见原因地等待"。

Result: 单一模型在三个CARLA闭环基准上创SOTA：InterDrive无交通97.8、有交通90.7驾驶分数（已发表最佳90.1/78.9）；公开V2Xverse协议70.0（16个重跑方法最佳59.1）；MDrive 88.2（重跑最佳85.1）；95.0%的车辆无碰撞（对比87.7%）。注：实验中基准的PID控制器改为由When2Go的路点而非路线转向，并关闭对长时间等待车辆的强制推动。

Conclusion: 将协同层级从状态共享提升到意图共享（Class B+）并结合确定性优先序网络，可在多车交互场景中同时消除死锁与碰撞，显著提升闭环驾驶性能，且单一模型即可跨基准泛化。

Abstract: Existing cooperative end-to-end driving works at Class A (status-sharing) of the SAE J3216 classes of cooperative driving automation (CDA): vehicles exchange what they perceive, not what they intend, so each plans alone when to go, which limits cooperation and leads to deadlock when both vehicles yield or to collisions when both go. We present When2Go, a cooperative end-to-end driving system that learns when to go by sharing intent under CDA Class B or higher. Each vehicle broadcasts its LiDAR detections with forecast motion as instance tokens, together with its plan and intended trajectory. The receiver draws the fused detections, forecasts and partner plans into a route-time conflict map, the occupancy along its route over the planning horizon, on which a planner scores candidate speed profiles. Where two intended trajectories conflict, an order gate, a precedence network distilled from a right-of-way rule, decides who goes first; both vehicles evaluate it on the same messages and so reach the same order. When2Go imitates the rule-based expert of V2Xverse, which sees the simulator's ground truth; stops that When2Go cannot observe within its 2 s planning horizon are relabelled, so that it does not learn to wait without an observable cause. For our runs only, the benchmarks' PID controller steers by When2Go's waypoints instead of the route, and its forced push of long-waiting vehicles is off. With one model, When2Go sets the state of the art on three closed-loop CARLA benchmarks: 97.8 and 90.7 driving score on InterDrive without and with traffic (best published 90.1 and 78.9), 70.0 on the public V2Xverse protocol (best of sixteen rerun methods 59.1) and 88.2 on MDrive (best rerun 85.1), with 95.0% of its vehicles collision-free (87.7%).

</details>


### [246] [DeRep: Learning Depth Representations for End-to-End Navigation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13499)
*Hanyu Jin,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出DeRep，一种面向导航的表征学习方法，将局部鸟瞰图可达性监督与深度重建结合进行编码器预训练，并在冻结编码器条件下用线性探针、流匹配模仿学习和PPO三种下游策略学习方式进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端杂乱环境导航依赖紧凑视觉表征以支持避障与目标导向运动。现有方法多将表征与策略联合学习或通过观测重建学习，未显式规定表征应保留何种信息；且通常仅用单一策略学习器评估，难以判断表征收益是否依赖策略学习方式。因此不同表征学习目标对下游导航性能与鲁棒性的影响仍不明确。

Method: 提出DeRep：结合局部鸟瞰图（BEV）可达性监督与深度重建的表征学习目标。为在受控条件下比较各表征目标，采用统一架构预训练编码器并在下游学习中冻结编码器；分别用线性策略探针、流匹配模仿学习和PPO强化学习评估表征质量。

Result: DeRep在线性策略探针上取得最低预测误差；在全部六种PPO测试条件下优于仅重建目标；模仿学习中两种基于可达性的目标平均成功率均高于仅重建，而仅可达性与联合目标之间平均成功率相近。

Conclusion: 将可达性监督纳入深度表征预训练，在不同下游学习方法和评估条件下均带来一致收益。进一步在Gazebo仿真与真实四旋翼上无需微调部署冻结DeRep编码器的PPO策略，验证了零样本sim-to-sim与sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation in cluttered environments relies on compact visual representations to support collision avoidance and goal-directed motion. Existing methods typically learn these representations jointly with the policy or through observation reconstruction, which does not explicitly specify what information the representation should preserve. Moreover, they are usually evaluated with a single downstream policy learner, although the benefit of a representation may depend on how the policy is learned. It therefore remains unclear how different representation-learning objectives affect downstream navigation performance and robustness. This work proposes DeRep, a navigation-oriented representation-learning method that combines local bird's-eye-view reachability supervision with depth reconstruction. To compare representation objectives under controlled conditions, we pretrain encoders with a common architecture and freeze them during downstream learning. We evaluate the representations using a linear policy probe, flow-matching imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with PPO. DeRep achieves the lowest linear policy probe prediction error and outperforms reconstruction alone across all six tested PPO conditions. In imitation learning, both reachability-based objectives achieve higher mean success rates than reconstruction alone, with similar mean success rates between the reachability-only and joint objectives. These results demonstrate consistent benefits of incorporating reachability supervision into depth representation pretraining across different downstream learning methods and evaluation conditions. We further deploy the PPO policy with the frozen DeRep encoder in Gazebo and on a physical quadrotor without fine-tuning, demonstrating its capability for zero-shot sim-to-sim and sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [247] [Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13517)
*Yaru Gu,Ting Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DFIO将误差状态卡尔曼滤波的更新环节嵌入计算图，实现神经网络伪测量与滤波器的端到端联合优化，在行人惯性里程计上显著降低平移漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惯性里程计因传感器噪声与零偏积分而产生无界漂移。TLIO等方法的神经伪测量与下游滤波器独立训练，训练目标与最终定位性能不一致，导致伪测量未针对滤波后的后验位置优化。

Method: 提出Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry（DFIO）：将误差状态卡尔曼滤波的量测更新阶段直接嵌入计算图，使滤波更新可微；先独立预训练，再以后验位置为监督信号，通过可微量测更新联合优化位移输出头与协方差输出头。在含标定与未标定惯性测量的公开行人运动数据集上验证。

Result: 行人数据集上，DFIO相对TLIO基线将平均绝对平移误差与1秒相对平移误差分别降低13.2%和39.6%；相对其预训练模型分别降低7.7%和17.4%。在未标定智能手机惯性数据上，该方法有效抑制平移漂移，在强噪声条件下保持轨迹一致性。

Conclusion: 将滤波更新纳入可微训练可有效弥合伪测量学习与滤波后定位性能之间的差距，提升惯性里程计的精度与鲁棒性，并对未标定低成本传感器具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate inertial odometry is critical for autonomous systems but suffers from unbounded drift caused by the integration of sensor noise and biases. In approaches such as TLIO, neural pseudo-measurements are trained independently of their downstream filter. This paper introduces Differentiable Filtering for Inertial Odometry (DFIO), a learning framework that embeds the update stages of an error-state Kalman filter directly into a computational graph. Following standalone pre-training, DFIO jointly optimizes the displacement and covariance output heads using posterior-position supervision through differentiable measurement updates. The proposed system is validated on public pedestrian motion datasets featuring both calibrated and uncalibrated inertial measurements. Experimental results on a pedestrian dataset demonstrate that DFIO reduces mean absolute and one-second relative translation errors by 13.2% and 39.6% relative to the TLIO baseline, and by 7.7% and 17.4% relative to its pre-trained counterpart. Furthermore, evaluations on uncalibrated smartphone inertial data indicate that the proposed method effectively mitigates translational drift and maintains trajectory alignment under highly noisy sensor conditions. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [248] [GEMOR-GS: Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13528)
*Yuchen Yang,Ming Ding,Xinyu Wu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GEMOR-GS：通过离线LiDAR-相机外参标定、门控可微渲染与LiDAR投影交替优化、全局重建，以及基于XFeat+RANSAC-PnP+高斯RGB-D warp的在线重定位，构建几何准确、全局精炼、可重入的3DGS地图，服务于LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在持续运行中的LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM需要照片级真实且度量一致的世界表示；3DGS虽能提供显式、可渲染、可查询的场景模型，但现有地图存在几何精度不足、缺乏全局精炼、跟踪丢失后无法重新进入等问题。

Method: 1) 在专用标定段上以直接法对齐离线估计LiDAR-相机外参，并在后续在线/离线流程中复用；2) 在标定几何下，以门控可微渲染与LiDAR投影目标交替优化关键帧位姿与高斯参数；3) 解除全部在线约束，从精炼后的关键帧模式重建最终全局地图；4) 重定位采用XFeat旁路地图、RANSAC-PnP粗定位与高斯RGB-D warp精化的三级流程。

Result: 在1个自采集与5个公开序列上：离线重建在所有配对关键帧上较在线地图提升14.3–19.9 dB；PSNR较Gaussian-LIC2提升约9 dB；留出重定位成功率达86–100%，且与外参质量相关。

Conclusion: GEMOR-GS提升了3DGS地图的几何精度、外观保真度与可重入性，可作为SLAM及下游任务的可靠机器人世界模型。

Abstract: Photorealistic and metrically consistent world representations are a primary requirement for LiDAR--inertial--visual SLAM systems that serve robots over continuous operation. 3D Gaussian splatting makes such representations attainable by supplying an explicit, renderable, and queryable scene model. We present GEMOR-GS (Global Extrinsic-calibration-refined Mapping and Online Relocalization for 3D Gaussian-Splatting SLAM) to make the Gaussian map geometrically accurate, globally refined, and re-enterable, providing a better appearance for robot perception and decision-making. To improve the 3DGS map, the LiDAR--camera extrinsic is first estimated offline by direct-method alignment over dedicated calibration segments and reused by all following online and offline processes. Under the calibrated geometry, keyframe poses and Gaussian parameters are refined alternately under a gated differentiable-rendering and LiDAR--projection objective. The final global reconstruction is then rebuilt from the refined keyframe schema with all online constraints lifted. For the robot SLAM lifecycle, the method recovers the pose from the 3DGS map after tracking loss through an XFeat bypass map, RANSAC-PnP coarse localization, and Gaussian RGB-D warp refinement. Experiments on one self-collected and five public sequences quantify each element: the offline reconstruction wins all paired keyframes against the online map by 14.3--19.9 dB, improves PSNR by approximately 9 dB over Gaussian-LIC2, and achieves 86--100% held-out relocalization success tied to extrinsic quality. The results show that GEMOR-GS improves the geometric accuracy, appearance fidelity, and re-enterability of the 3DGS map, serving as a dependable robot world model for SLAM and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [249] [Few-Shot Residual Adaptation of Pretrained Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13592)
*Jakub Suliga,Pankhuri Vanjani,Nisarga Nilavadi,Wolfram Burgard,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出少样本残差自适应方法，通过自监督学习潜在预测误差的修正量，双位置施加（潜变量预测器内部与预测潜状态上），保持预训练世界模型冻结，在质心与摩擦变化下将真机成功率从近零恢复到90%/100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练潜在世界模型可用于视觉规划与控制，但部署时物理动力学发生变化会导致预测失效。作者希望在不修改预训练权重、也不显式辨识变化物理参数的条件下，仅凭少量交互实现自适应。

Method: 少样本残差自适应：以潜在预测误差为信号进行自监督学习得到修正项，并将其同时施加于潜空间预测器内部及预测出的潜状态上（dual-site），预训练世界模型参数保持冻结；在仿真与真机上针对质心偏移和摩擦改变进行评测，并做单/双位置消融与诊断分析。

Result: 物理变化使冻结模型成功率降至接近零；仅用3个交互回合自适应后，双位置方法在真机上质心偏移下恢复90%成功率、摩擦改变下恢复100%；消融显示双位置优于单位置修正；诊断表明所学修正很大程度在缓解一般性预测失配，而成功控制还依赖自适应中捕捉到的与具体变化相关的信息。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练潜在世界模型可通过少量自监督残差修正实现有效的部署期自适应，且双位置修正更优；但通用失配补偿之外，仍需变化特异信息才能保证控制成功。

Abstract: Pretrained latent world models can support visual planning and control, but their predictions may fail when physical dynamics change at deployment. We study whether such models can adapt from only a few interactions without modifying their pretrained weights or identifying the changed physical parameters. We introduce a few-shot residual adaptation approach that learns self-supervised corrections from latent prediction errors. The corrections are applied both inside the latent predictor and directly to its predicted latent state, while the pretrained world model remains frozen. We evaluate the approach in simulation and on a real robot under center-of-mass and friction shifts. These changes reduce the success of the frozen model to near zero. Using three interaction episodes for adaptation, our dual-site approach recovers 90\% success under the center-of-mass shift and 100\% under the modified friction on the real robot. Ablations show that correcting both locations outperforms single-site adaptation. Our diagnostics further suggest that much of the learned correction reduces a general prediction mismatch, while successful control also depends on shift-specific information captured during adaptation.

</details>


### [250] [From Egocentric Human Video to Robot Manipulation in the Era of Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13610)
*Yi Wang,Jingqiu Wu,Lei Yao,Zhaoshuo Li,Haoyu Zhu,Yirui Zhou,Huaiyuan Xu,Ying Zheng,Chuang Yang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统定义"Ego2Robot learning"（从自我中心人类视频学习机器人操作策略），将其归纳为四类范式——显式本体迁移、显式动作与任务监督、自我中心视频表征预训练、隐式动作与动力学建模——并梳理各类范式的信息抽取方式与适用条件，同时综述相关基准数据集与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类视频（EHV）记录近距离手-物交互与日常活动的时间结构，可作为机器人操作经验的可扩展来源；但缺少机器人动作标签，且存在形态、视角与动作空间差异，因此需要系统梳理如何从中恢复有用监督。

Method: 综述/立场性论文。按"从人类视频中导出何种信息"划分四种学习范式（机器人格式示范、可解释监督、视觉表征、动作与动力学预测模型），逐一分析所提取的EHV信息、机器人策略的使用方式及额外数据需求；并回顾覆盖EHV、机器人示范、合成与仿真资源的基准数据集，建立"EHV任务信息保留度"与"机器人特定grounding"之间的关联分析框架。

Result: 给出四范式的统一对比框架（提取内容、策略利用方式、数据需求差异），汇总相关基准数据集清单，并将EHV数据的贡献归结为任务信息保留程度与训练/部署阶段机器人特定grounding的组合关系。

Conclusion: Ego2Robot学习的关键在于任务信息保留与机器人本体grounding的权衡；未来需应对物理可行性、智能体协同、上下文内适应、受控评测与预测控制等挑战。

Abstract: Egocentric human video (EHV) records close-range hand-object interaction (HOI) and the temporal structure of everyday activities, offering a potentially scalable source of experience for robot manipulation. Using this experience for robot control requires recovering useful supervision without recorded robot actions and accounting for differences in morphology, viewpoint, and action space. We define Ego2Robot learning as the process of learning or adapting robot manipulation policies from EHV, organized into four learning paradigms: explicit embodiment transfer, explicit action and task supervision, egocentric video representation pretraining, and latent action and dynamics modeling. The four paradigms differ in what they derive from human video: robot-format demonstrations, interpretable supervision, visual representations, or predictive models of actions and dynamics. For each paradigm, we trace what EHV information is extracted, how robot policies use it, and when the additional data is required. Besides, we review the benchmark datasets that cover EHV, robot demonstrations, and synthetic and simulated resources. This paper links EHV data's contribution to the task information preserved and the robot-specific grounding used during training or deployment. Finally, we identify challenges in physical feasibility, agent coordination, in-context adaptation, controlled evaluation, and predictive control.

</details>


### [251] [An IoT Based Approach for Platform Independent Positioning Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13634)
*Anh-Van Vu,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出KAILOS的IoT扩展架构，用专用IoT设备替代手机App提供室内定位服务，并给出该设备的软硬件设计，验证IoT路线可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAILOS（2014年发布的完整室内定位方案）基于WiFi信号定位，依赖手机App提供服务，但移动App受操作系统诸多限制，形成应用障碍。

Method: 设计KAILOS的扩展架构，引入IoT设备承担定位与服务功能，以规避移动App的限制；同时给出该IoT设备的硬件与软件设计。

Result: 通过所提出的IoT设备实现与架构验证，证明基于IoT的室内定位方式具有可行性，并有望成为拓展室内定位服务应用场景的关键特性。

Conclusion: IoT化扩展可突破手机App式方案的瓶颈，是推广室内定位服务应用的有效途径。

Abstract: KAIST Indoor Localization System (KAILOS) was introduced in 2014 and became one of the world-first complete indoor positioning solutions. It is mainly based on the WiFi signal to estimate location and provide positioning service via mobile application that has many restrictions by the Operating System. This study proposes an extended architecture of KAILOS to make use of the IoT device for removing the barriers of the mobile-app-based approach and conveniently providing indoor positioning service. Design of the IoT device that contains hardware and software is also presented. Via this study, we prove that the IoT-based approach is promising and can be a crucial feature to widen applications of indoor localization services.

</details>


### [252] [NIF-VIO: Visual--Inertial Odometry Across Inertial and Non-Inertial Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13662)
*Zichao Shu,Lijun Li,Zhuochen Li,Zetao Chen,Jianyu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NIF-VIO，一个可在惯性/非惯性参考系间自适应切换视觉-惯性融合的定位框架，通过一致性驱动的约束隔离与局部重优化提升非惯性条件下的精度与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VIO假设相机与IMU观测在同一惯性参考系下联合一致，但在加速或旋转载体内部，视觉观测反映载体系相对运动、IMU含额外载体诱导分量，导致该假设失效、融合退化。

Method: 以测量一致性自适应调整视觉-惯性融合：惯性条件变化时，先早期隔离约束并局部重优化以抑制误差传播，随后在确认不一致后修复受影响局部几何；非惯性条件下引入参数时间共享的紧凑模型刻画载体运动，同时保留可与视觉互补的惯性信息。

Result: 在公开基准与自采集数据集上，惯性条件下性能具竞争力，非惯性条件下定位精度与可用性提升。

Conclusion: NIF-VIO实现了跨惯性/非惯性参考系的相对局部环境定位，验证了一致性驱动融合策略的有效性。

Abstract: Conventional visual--inertial odometry (VIO) assumes that observations from cameras and an inertial measurement unit (IMU) are jointly consistent in an inertial reference frame. However, this assumption breaks down under non-inertial conditions. Inside accelerating or rotating carriers, visual observations of the interior reflect carrier-relative motion, while IMU measurements contain additional carrier-induced components, which can compromise conventional fusion. We therefore present NIF-VIO for localization relative to the local environment across inertial and non-inertial reference frames. It adapts visual--inertial fusion based on measurement consistency. As inertial conditions change, early constraint isolation is combined with local reoptimization to limit error propagation before inconsistency confirmation and repair the affected local geometry. Under non-inertial conditions, a compact model with temporally shared parameters is introduced to account for carrier motion while preserving inertial information that complements visual constraints. Experiments on public benchmarks and a self-collected dataset demonstrate that NIF-VIO achieves competitive performance under inertial conditions and improved localization accuracy and availability under non-inertial conditions.

</details>


### [253] [Ego Guidance: Enhancing Robot Policy Learning with Action-Free Human Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13685)
*Lin Long,Tianren Ma,Zhong Chu,Di Wu,Maosong Cao,Qixiang Ye,Junbo Zhao,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPAR（Shared Prediction on Action and Representation），一种基于预训练VLM的MoT架构，通过未来视觉状态预测与动作预测共享表示，在无动作标注的第一视角人类视频上学习，从而提升机器人策略学习在分布外任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略学习受限于带动作标注的机器人数据稀缺，而无动作监督的第一视角人类视频规模庞大，但如何有效利用这类视频提升下游机器人策略（尤其是OOD泛化）尚不清晰。

Method: 以预训练VLM为骨干，构建Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，联合进行两个预测目标：动作预测与未来视觉状态预测；使未来视觉状态预测作为独立且互补的学习信号，与动作预测共同塑造共享表示；并进一步分析目标函数选择与数据筛选策略的影响。

Result: 从第一视角视频学到的表示提升了机器人在分布外任务上的下游性能；随视频数据规模增大，增益进一步提升；同时给出了目标函数设计与数据整理方式的实证结论。

Conclusion: 无动作标注的第一视角视频可作为可扩展的机器人学习信号来源，SPAR表明"动作预测+未来状态预测"的联合表示学习是有效路径，并为规模化利用无动作视频提供了实践指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate how human egocentric videos can benefit robot policy learning without action supervision. Building on a pretrained VLM, we propose Shared Prediction on Action and Representation (SPAR), an MoT architecture in which future visual state prediction can provide independent and complementary learning signals alongside action prediction. We find that representations learned from egocentric videos improve downstream performance on out-of-distribution robot tasks, with further gains as video data scales. We also study the effects of objective choices and data curation, providing insights into how to leverage action-free videos for scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [254] [Joint-State Inference for Autonomous Excavators Using Cab-View Vision-Control Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13690)
*Junwei Li,Jian Pan,Mengchi Cai,Shuai Zhao,Xizhuo Jiang,Zheng Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于车载视觉与控制时序信息的挖掘机前端关节状态闭环推断框架，无需额外传感器即可支撑自主挖掘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘依赖可靠的前端机构关节状态反馈，但为现役整机加装专用关节传感器、外置相机或视觉标记会显著增加部署与运维负担。

Method: 框架融合当前驾驶室视角图像的结构线索与历史操纵杆指令的短期运动趋势；为铲斗关节引入滚动状态缓冲以在严重遮挡下保持时序连续性；将机械关节行程约束嵌入学习目标以抑制不可行估计；推断状态与当前图像一并输入基于ACT（Action Chunking with Transformers）的控制策略在线生成动作。

Result: 在AGX Dynamics for Unity仿真环境中开展多工况实验：20次自主挖掘试验任务完成率95%，单步"关节推断+控制"平均时延10.11 ms，铲斗关节推断MAE为0.965°。

Conclusion: 该方案实现了少传感器、计算高效的关节状态推断，可在降低额外感知设施依赖的前提下支撑闭环自主挖掘。

Abstract: Reliable state feedback of excavator front-end mechanisms is essential for autonomous excavation, yet retrofitting existing machines with dedicated joint sensors, external cameras, or visual markers can increase deployment and maintenance burdens. This paper proposes a closed-loop autonomous excavation framework for continuous inference of excavator joint states using onboard visual observations and control-related temporal information. The framework encodes structural cues from the current cab-view image, models short-term motion trends from historical joystick commands, and introduces a rolling state buffer for the bucket joint to preserve temporal continuity under severe occlusion. Mechanical joint-range constraints are incorporated into the learning objective to suppress infeasible estimates. The current cab-view image and inferred states are supplied to an Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT)-based control policy for online action generation. Experiments in an AGX Dynamics for Unity simulation environment under diverse operating conditions evaluate joint-state inference and end-to-end task execution. Across 20 autonomous excavation trials, the framework achieved a 95% task-completion rate, with an average joint-state-inference-and-control latency of 10.11 ms per step. The bucket-joint inference achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.965 degrees. These results demonstrate a sensor-light and computationally efficient joint-state-inference approach that can support closed-loop autonomous excavation with reduced dependence on additional sensing infrastructure.

</details>


### [255] [CIALLO: Learning Shared Intent Representations for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13732)
*Choo Sin Wai,Dake Zhong,Jingye Zhang,Zhifang Liu,Siyao Xiao,Zirong Ding,Yuhong Zhang,Kangjie Chen,Dongbin Zhang,Guorui Song,Guocun Wang,Peng Jiao,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CIALLO 动作编解码器，将动作、视觉变化与语言对齐到短时行为的共享意图空间，实现跨具身知识迁移，并构建 Qwen-CIALLO 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示含可复用操作知识，但跨具身迁移需要弥合控制接口差异的表示；仅靠动作重建（action reconstruction）不能保证不同机器人的相似行为共享同一表示。

Method: CIALLO 在短时行为意图空间内对齐动作、视觉变化与基于观测的语言；采用具身条件解码器将意图映射回各机器人特定动作。先于 8 种具身上预训练，新机器人仅用最多 10 秒单条录像更新具身嵌入完成适配。校准后编解码器冻结，由策略从观测与指令预测意图，用 Qwen3.5-4B 实例化为 Qwen-CIALLO（主干无需大规模机器人动作预训练）。仿真中额外微调解码器。

Result: 真实 SO-101 与单臂 Piper 上，每任务 20 条演示，平均成功率 76.7%（全量微调 π₀ 为 70.0%）；仿真 LIBERO 97.3%、RoboTwin 2.0 52.7%；LIBERO 策略在 LIBERO-Plus 上免微调达 80.8%。

Conclusion: 共享动作编解码器可作为可迁移的控制接口，将具身校准与任务学习解耦，支持高效跨具身迁移。

Abstract: Robot demonstrations contain reusable manipulation knowledge, but transferring this knowledge across embodiments requires representations that bridge differences in control interfaces. Action reconstruction alone does not ensure that similar behaviors across robots share a representation. To address this gap, we introduce CIALLO, an action codec that aligns actions, visual changes, and observation-grounded language in a shared space of short-horizon behavioral intent. An embodiment-conditioned decoder maps this intent back to robot-specific actions, allowing a shared representation to support different control interfaces. After pretraining on eight embodiments, CIALLO adapts to a new robot from one recording of at most 10 seconds by updating only its embodiment embeddings. The calibrated codec then remains frozen while a policy learns to predict intent from observations and instructions. We instantiate this policy with Qwen3.5-4B to form Qwen-CIALLO, whose backbone requires no large-scale robot-action pretraining before policy finetuning. With 20 demonstrations per task, Qwen-CIALLO achieves 76.7\% average success across real SO-101 and single-arm Piper robots, compared with 70.0\% for fully finetuned $π_0$. In simulation, we additionally finetune the action decoder, achieving 97.3\% success on LIBERO and 52.7\% on RoboTwin 2.0. The LIBERO policy also achieves 80.8\% on LIBERO-Plus without further finetuning. Together, these results support a shared action codec as a transferable control interface that separates embodiment calibration from task learning.

</details>


### [256] [FAME-QP: A GPU-Accelerated Quadratic-Programming Solver for Real-Time Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13766)
*Santosh Mohan Rajkumar,Debdipta Goswami,Stephanie Stockar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME-QP 是面向线性模型预测控制中稠密二次规划的 GPU 加速求解器，通过约束相关算子表示、融合的单/双核迭代与 CUDA 图重放，并结合设备校准与问题级计时自动选择执行配置，从而降低求解延迟且保持精度与控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性 MPC 中的稠密 QP 求解对实时性要求高，现有 GPU 求解器在延迟上仍有优化空间；同时需要兼顾求解精度与控制性能，因此作者希望构建一个能自适应硬件与问题特性、低延迟的 GPU QP 求解器。

Method: 提出 FAME-QP，将约束相关的算子表示与融合的单核/双核迭代相结合，并通过 CUDA 图重放执行；利用设备校准与问题特定计时自动选择执行配置；通过缓存算子库（operator bank）选取初始罚参数，并在问题矩阵变化时惰性更新算子。

Result: 数值基准测试以及仿真人形机器人与四旋翼控制实验中，相比同类 GPU 求解器降低了延迟，同时保持了解的精度与控制性能。

Conclusion: FAME-QP 表明，将硬件自适应配置选择、CUDA 图融合迭代与约束相关算子复用相结合，可在实时 MPC 场景中有效降低稠密 QP 求解延迟，具备实际控制应用价值。

Abstract: We present FAME-QP, a GPU-accelerated solver for dense quadratic programs arising in linear model predictive control. FAME-QP combines constraint-dependent operator representations with fused single- and two-kernel iterations replayed through CUDA graphs, automatically selecting the execution configuration through device calibration and problem-specific timing. It probes a cached operator bank to select the initial penalty and updates operators lazily when problem matrices change. Numerical benchmarks and simulated humanoid and quadrotor control experiments demonstrate reduced latency relative to competing GPU solvers while maintaining solution accuracy and control performance.

</details>


### [257] [From Depth to Safe Depth: Uncertainty-Aware Monocular Perception for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13769)
*Saeid Firouzi Daghigh,Saeed Ayat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 单目深度基础模型直接用于移动机器人导航不安全；本文提出一个0.58M参数的轻量插件头，在冻结的深度模型上预测逐像素对数深度修正μ与不确定性σ，并以单侧置信界 \hat d_safe = d·e^{μ-kσ} 提供“安全深度”，把导航成功率从37%提升到70%、碰撞率从63%降到3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度基础模型（如Depth Anything V2 metric）让用单目RGB替代深度传感器变得有吸引力，但现成使用存在安全隐患：在仿真室内导航基准中，标准Nav2栈由该模型驱动时120个回合中有63%发生碰撞，原因是模型系统性高估近处障碍物距离。需要以极低开销把“预测深度”转化为“可用于安全决策的深度”。

Method: 在冻结的深度模型之上加装0.58M参数的小型预测头，逐像素输出对数深度修正量μ与不确定性σ，用β加权的高斯负对数似然训练。导航栈使用单侧置信下界 \hat d_safe = d·e^{μ-kσ}，其中k通过在留出世界上的保形校准（conformal calibration）确定；不确定性同时用于判定哪些像素可被视为地面。与等像素覆盖率的常数安全裕度基线对比。

Result: 深度误差减半（AbsRel 0.262→0.134），近距高估从59.8%降至12.0%；导航成功率37%→70%，碰撞率63%→3%，接近真值深度的2%。同像素覆盖率的常数安全裕度基线成功率仅30%（因过度保守导致机器人停滞），在56个不一致配对回合中不确定性感知方案胜出52个。增益在玻璃、无纹理墙面和暗区同样成立。

Conclusion: 现成单目深度基础模型不足以支撑安全导航，需显式建模不确定性与单侧保守界；一个极轻量、无需微调主干的后处理头即可显著提升安全性，且优于同等保守程度的常数裕度方案，对挑战性材质与低光区域具有泛化性。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models make it tempting to replace depth sensors on mobile robots with a single RGB camera. We show that, used off the shelf, this is unsafe: in a simulated indoor navigation benchmark, a standard Nav2 stack driven by Depth Anything V2 (metric) collides in 63\% of 120 episodes, because the model systematically over-estimates the distance to nearby obstacles. We propose a lightweight plug-in module that turns predicted depth into \emph{safe depth}. A 0.58\,M-parameter head on top of the \emph{frozen} depth model predicts, per pixel, a log-depth correction $μ$ and an uncertainty $σ$, trained with a $β$-weighted Gaussian negative log-likelihood. The navigation stack consumes the one-sided bound $\hat d_{\text{safe}}=d\,e^{μ-kσ}$, where $k$ is set by conformal calibration on a held-out world, and uncertainty also decides which pixels may be treated as floor. The correction halves the depth error (AbsRel $0.262\rightarrow0.134$) and reduces near-range over-estimation from 59.8\% to 12.0\%. In navigation, safe depth raises the success rate from 37\% to 70\% and lowers collisions from 63\% to 3\%, close to the 2\% of ground-truth depth. A constant safety margin calibrated to the same pixel coverage reaches only 30\% success because it freezes the robot; uncertainty-aware safety wins 52 of 56 discordant paired episodes against it. Gains hold on glass, texture-less walls and dark regions.

</details>


### [258] [Softness is something you also need! Why compliance helps control: information metrics of a passive walker with compliant ankle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13779)
*Fabio Bonsignorio,Elisabetta Barletta,Sorin Dragomir,Francesco Esposito,Enrica Zereik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是一篇技术札记，从"范式"角度反思刚性/软体机器人自上而下的笛卡尔式设计，主张以形态计算与涌现行为为核心的信息—动力学框架来建模与调控高顺应性人工体。文中回顾了若干联系信息度量与系统动力学的经典数学结果，并结合作者与学界近期方法，对倒立摆与被动动态行走模型进行相空间体积计算，比较状态依赖蒙特卡洛与再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）方法的估计效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能机器人（无论刚性还是部分顺应）在适应性、灵活性与灵巧性上与自然演化出的动植物仍存在巨大差距，作者认为根源之一是范式问题：多数机器人仍遵循笛卡尔式设计，采用自上而下的控制与清晰的心—身二分；而生物体的组织原则依赖形态计算（morphological computation）以及由松散耦合的具身网络涌现出适配行为，因此需要全新的建模与控制路径。

Method: 1）回顾并阐释将信息度量与智能体动力学相联系的若干奠基性数学结果，并与作者团队及社区近期方法相整合；2）提出用于相空间体积计算的框架，以McGeer罗盘步态为起点，逐步引入膝—踝顺应性、足部摇椅效应，以及具有分布腿部惯性的Ruina式罗盘步态；3）推导标准足跟触地映射（heel-strike maps），据此估计可行相空间体积 W(γ)；4）分别采用状态依赖蒙特卡洛与RKHS方法进行估计与比较。

Result: 给出了倒立摆与被动动态行走模型相空间体积计算的技术性总结：随模型复杂度提升（膝踝顺应性、摇椅足、分布惯性），通过足跟触地映射可估计出对应的可行相空间体积 W(γ)；其中基于RKHS的方法能够提供更深入、更细致的分析，超越了蒙特卡洛估计所能揭示的层面。

Conclusion: 作者据此指出通向完全或部分顺应人工体建模与控制的高效具体途径，并强调对自然界"物理智能"（physical intelligence）的更深入理解；RKHS类方法在相空间体积分析中具有更强的解析能力，是后续工作的方向。

Abstract: A great gap in adaptivity, flexibility, and dexterity still exists between intelligent robots, whether they are made of rigid or (partially) compliant components, and their naturally evolved animal and vegetal counterparts. One reason could be of `paradigm' nature. On the one hand, most robots (both rigid and soft) are still designed by following a `Cartesian' approach, with top-down control and a clear mind-body separation. Designing artificial agents on the basis of the principles that we observe in biology is not an easy task. The principles of organization of animals and plants rely on morphological computation and emergence of fit behaviors from loosely coupled networks of embodied agents.
  Modeling and control require radically new approaches. In this paper we briefly recall and explain some seminal mathematical results linking information metrics and dynamics of the agents, already published years ago; we integrate them with some recent methods developed by us and by the community, we highlight some brief remarks about their relevance and we hint to our current and near future work towards finding concrete and efficient ways for the modeling and control of completely or partially compliant artificial agents and for a better understanding of physical intelligence in nature. We present a technical note summarizing phase-space volume calculations for inverted-pendulum and passive-dynamic walking models, following our proposed framework. Starting from the McGeer compass gait, we introduce knee-ankle compliance, foot-rocker effects, and a Ruina-style compass gait with distributed leg inertia. Standard heel-strike maps are derived and used in the estimation of viable phase-space volumes $W(γ)$, based on a state-dependent Monte-Carlo and Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces methods. We show how the methods based on RKHS allow deeper and more subtle analysis.

</details>


### [259] [RiSE-Drive: Risk-Sensitive Long-Horizon Candidate Evaluation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13790)
*Yu Zhao,Hongyi Duan,Ming Yin,Hongyuan Zhang,Fan Yang,Hongzhi Wang,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiSE-Drive 将端到端自动驾驶的候选轨迹打分从确定性点估计改造为长时程分布式结果评估：先做候选条件化的潜空间未来推演，再估计长时程结果分布，最后用尾部风险感知且抗扰动的准则排序候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多候选轨迹生成与选择的范式下，核心瓶颈已从"如何生成候选"转向"如何在不确定性下评估候选的长时程后果"。现有方法仍采用确定性点分数、启发式子分数或单结果评估，难以刻画延迟失效、尾部风险以及未来合理扰动下的排序不稳定问题。

Method: 框架分三阶段：(1) 候选条件化的潜空间未来 rollout；(2) 估计长时程结果分布，替代单一标量分数；(3) 采用尾部风险感知且对扰动鲁棒的决策准则对候选排序。本质是把候选打分从"确定性 oracle 式评估"重构为"带显式风险控制的分布化结果评估"。

Result: 在 NAVSIM-v1、NAVSIM-v2 基准以及闭环 HUGSIM 基准上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，并显著提升候选选择的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 评估器被引导偏好不仅在名义条件下表现强、而且在长时程推演与安全关键偏差下依然可靠的候选轨迹；风险敏感的长时程分布化评估是提升端到端自动驾驶候选选择质量与稳定性的有效途径。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems increasingly rely on generating multiple trajectory candidates and selecting one for execution. In this setting, a central challenge is no longer only candidate generation, but how to evaluate candidates according to their long-horizon consequences under uncertainty. Existing methods still mainly use deterministic point scores, heuristic subscores, or single-outcome evaluations, which makes it difficult to capture delayed failures, tail risk, and ranking instability under plausible future perturbations. We present RiSE-Drive, a framework for risk-sensitive long-horizon candidate evaluation in end-to-end autonomous driving. RiSE-Drive first performs candidate-conditioned latent future rollout, then estimates long-horizon outcome distributions instead of single scalar scores, and finally ranks candidates using a tail-risk-aware and perturbation-robust decision criterion. This reformulates candidate scoring from deterministic oracle-style evaluation into distributional outcome assessment with explicit risk control. As a result, the evaluator is encouraged to prefer candidates that are not only strong under nominal conditions, but also reliable under long-horizon rollout and safety-critical deviations. Experiments on the NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks, as well as the closed-loop HUGSIM benchmark, show that RiSE-Drive achieves state-of-the-art performance and significantly improves the robustness of candidate selection.

</details>


### [260] [Camera equalisation of an 11.5x drop in environmental illuminance: four imitation-learning policies on an SO-101 arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13810)
*Simon Cowling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在低成本真机平台上，房间照度（lux）与相机实际接收的图像亮度几乎无关；四种模仿学习策略在520 lux与45 lux下成功率无显著差异（40次中20胜 vs 19胜），因此应报告并控制相机设置与图像统计量，而非仅报告lux。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真机基准测试通常用房间照度（lux）作为光照条件的度量，但作者发现这一指标可能无法反映策略实际看到的图像亮度，从而削弱基准测试中光照变化结论的可解释性与可复现性。

Method: 在典型低成本硬件上，使用SO-101机械臂的同一个pick-and-place任务、同一批50条演示，训练ACT、SmolVLA、Diffusion Policy与pi0.5四种模仿学习策略；每种策略在约520 lux与约45 lux（11.5倍差异）下各做10次rollout（共40次/条件），比较成功率并检验策略间敏感性交互效应；同时分析前视与腕部相机的平均图像亮度，两相机均启用自动曝光与自动白平衡且未锁定曝光；另有一次意外重复的10次rollout区块用于检验重复性。

Result: 正常光照下成功20/40，低光照下19/40，变化为-2.5个百分点（95% CI −23 至 +19）；策略间敏感性差异无证据（交互 p = 0.86）。图像亮度仅在前视相机下降3%、腕部相机下降19%，远小于11.5倍的照度差异。重复区块成功数与首块一致（7/10），但10次放置中仅6次位置相同，符合随机水平。

Conclusion: 仅用lux不能描述策略实际观测到的视觉输入；自动曝光/白平衡很可能吸收了照度差异，但本实验未锁定曝光。建议基准测试报告并控制相机设置（曝光、白平衡、增益）以及与训练录制相比的图像统计量，而非只记录房间照度。

Abstract: Real-robot benchmarks of learned visuomotor policies often record room illuminance as the lighting condition. We show that, on a typical low-cost rig, room light and image light can be almost unrelated. Four imitation-learning policies (ACT, SmolVLA, Diffusion Policy and pi0.5) were trained on the same 50 demonstrations of a pick-and-place task on an SO-101 arm. Each was run for ten rollouts at about 520 lux and again at about 45 lux, an 11.5x reduction. Success was 20/40 in normal light and 19/40 in low light: a change of -2.5 percentage points (95% CI -23 to +19), with no evidence that the policies differed in sensitivity (interaction p = 0.86). The recorded video shows why: mean image brightness was only 3% lower in low light on the front camera and 19% lower on the wrist camera. Both cameras were set to automatic exposure and white balance, which most likely absorbed the difference; exposure was not locked in this experiment. An accidental repeat of one 10-rollout block reproduced its success count (7/10) but matched the first block at only 6 of 10 placements, consistent with chance. We conclude that lux alone does not describe what a policy sees. Camera settings, and image statistics compared with the training recordings, are the quantities to report and control. Data and code are public.

</details>


### [261] [WAM is capable in BEHAVIOR-1K: the 2nd-place solution to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13818)
*Yelin He,Jiaquan Ye,Xinming Wei,Wenjie Xiao,Gengming Zhang,Guoting Wei,Xianbiao Qi,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出2026 BEHAVIOR挑战赛（100项家庭移动操作任务）第二名方案（Team IF1001），基于世界动作模型Cosmos 3，通过更丰富的视觉历史、任务进度条件化、任务组与技能专属LoRA适配器、进度估计器与路由机制等改进，在官方私有测试集上取得平均Q-score 0.615、全任务成功率41.2%，并在有限硬件下对100个评测端口并发请求维持超过2 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEHAVIOR挑战赛需在100项长时程家庭移动操作任务上统一建模，单一通用策略难以同时兼顾任务多样性与技能差异；同时官方要求最低1 FPS推理速度，且限制廉价推理手段（禁用RSI与agentic harness方法、不使用闭源模型），需在资源受限条件下实现高效、可并发的多任务策略系统。

Method: 以世界动作模型Cosmos 3为基座，做三方面改进：(1) 引入更丰富的视觉历史输入；(2) 以任务进度作为条件信号；(3) 训练任务组级与技能级LoRA适配器实现参数高效特化。推理阶段：用独立进度估计器提供任务进度上下文；采用任务与进度条件化路由选择任务组或技能专属策略；辅以动作候选聚合（action-candidate aggregation）、rolling inpainting、以及任务特定的时序与夹爪控制。为满足1 FPS要求，不使用递归自我改进（RSI）与agentic harness方法，且不依赖闭源模型。

Result: 在100项任务的3轮自评测中，平均Q-score为0.613、全任务成功率40.9%；官方私有测试集（每任务20个实例）上平均Q-score 0.615、全任务成功率41.2%。工程部署方面，在有限硬件资源下处理100个评测端口的并发请求，平均端到端推理速率超过每端口2 FPS，满足1 FPS最低要求。

Conclusion: 在强基座世界动作模型上叠加任务进度条件化、分层LoRA特化与条件化路由，可在长时程多任务移动操作中取得有竞争力的性能；同时该方案证明了在无闭源模型、无RSI与agentic harness的前提下，开源模型体系也能满足严格实时性约束并支撑大规模并发评测。

Abstract: This work presents the 2nd-place solution (Team IF1001) to the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge, which evaluates mobile manipulation across 100 household tasks. Our method is built on a powerful world action model (WAM), Cosmos 3. We improve Cosmos 3 by using richer visual history, task-progress conditioning, and task-group and skill-specific LoRA adapters. During inference, we apply a separate progress estimator to supply task-progress context, and use task- and progress-conditioned routing to select a task-group or skill-specific policy, complemented by action-candidate aggregation, rolling inpainting, and task-specific temporal and gripper control. To meet the minimum inference requirement of 1 FPS, we use neither recursive self-improvement (RSI) nor agentic harness methods and rely on no closed-source models; during official private-test evaluation, our system handled concurrent requests across 100 evaluation ports with limited hardware resources, sustaining an average end-to-end inference rate above 2 FPS per port. Our method achieves a mean Q-score of 0.613 and a full-task success rate of 40.9%, averaged over 3 self-evaluation rounds across all 100 tasks. On the official private test set, with 20 instances per task, our system achieves a mean Q-score of 0.615 and a full-task success rate of 41.2%.

</details>


### [262] [Game-Enhanced Embodied Learning with Event and Foresight Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13819)
*Lianlei Shan,Han Jin,Yaunchao Ma,Xinpeng Chen,Linhao Zhou,Xin Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究 InternVLA-A1.5 的"事件+预见"游戏预训练：用人类键鼠事件预测与冻结视频模型的历史条件视觉监督进行预训练，在 LIBERO 四个套件上使总体成功率从 85.90% 提升至 90.55%（+4.65 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类游戏过程天然提供"观测—动作—视觉后果"配对数据，可作为机器人策略训练的有益补充。作者希望验证完整游戏预训练阶段能否为 VLA 策略提供更好的初始化。

Method: 对 InternVLA-A1.5 进行事件与预见联合预训练：同时预测记录的键盘/鼠标事件，并通过冻结视频模型施加历史条件视觉监督。采用覆盖保持的锚点选择构建 1 秒目标窗口，并用注意力掩码阻止 teacher-forcing 的未来动作进入预见查询。语料为 20 款游戏、394 段录制、223.36 小时时间跨度、2,072,084 个训练锚点；训练 12,400 步后在相同套件专属微调协议下与公开基座对比。

Result: LIBERO 四个套件合计成功率由 1,718/2,000（85.90%）升至 1,811/2,000（90.55%），+4.65 个百分点；Spatial、Object、Goal、Long 分别提升 9.2、6.8、0.2、2.4 点。40 任务细分为 24 项提升、10 项下降、6 项持平。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整游戏预训练阶段在该评测设定下的实用价值，但任务层面表现异质；且对比包含额外优化，未隔离游戏数据、两项训练目标及训练随机性的各自贡献，属消融不足的实证性结论。

Abstract: Human gameplay supplies paired observations, actions, and visual consequences that can supplement robot-policy training. We study event-and-foresight pretraining for InternVLA-A1.5, combining recorded keyboard/mouse event prediction with history-conditioned visual supervision through a frozen video model. Coverage-preserving anchor selection constructs one-second targets, and an attention mask prevents teacher-forced future actions from entering foresight queries. The selected corpus contains 394 recordings across 20 games, with 223.36 hours of recorded timestamp span and 2,072,084 training anchors. After 12,400 game-pretraining updates, we compare the resulting initialization with the public base under the same suite-specific robot fine-tuning protocol. Across four LIBERO suites, aggregate success increases from 1,718/2,000 (85.90%) to 1,811/2,000 (90.55%), a gain of 4.65 percentage points. Suite gains are 9.2, 6.8, 0.2, and 2.4 points on Spatial, Object, Goal, and Long. The 40-task breakdown shows 24 improvements, 10 declines, and six ties. These results support the practical value of the complete game-pretraining stage in this evaluated setting, while revealing heterogeneous task outcomes. The comparison includes additional optimization and does not isolate the effects of game data, the two objectives, or training variability.

</details>


### [263] [Learn-To-Propagate: Distributionally Robust Motion Planning for Nonlinear Uncertain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13863)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Adrien Banse,Qi Heng Ho,Raphael Jungers,Morteza Lahijanian,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出L2P-DR-Forest，一种面向非线性、初始状态与过程噪声分布均未知的随机系统的分布鲁棒采样规划算法，利用最优传输学习高置信度模糊集，并给出正确性与概率完备性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机系统运动规划方法存在两类局限：一是仅适用于线性动力学且需完全已知底层分布；二是缺乏形式化正确性保证。因此需要一种能处理非线性随机系统、且对未知初始状态与过程噪声分布具有鲁棒性、并具备形式化保证的规划方法。

Method: 基于最优传输理论学习以高置信度包含真实系统分布的模糊集（ambiguity set）；提出高效的模糊集动力学传播方法及其安全性验证方法；将上述技术嵌入新的采样规划算法，在状态空间中生长搜索树森林，并显式考虑与抑制模糊集半径的增长。

Result: 证明了算法正确性，即每个返回的规划均以规定置信度满足安全与目标规格；在一定的可行性与分布假设下证明了概率完备性；在多个机器人基准上的评估显示，该方法显著优于现有方法，同时提供高置信度的形式化保证。

Conclusion: L2P-DR-Forest在未知分布的非线性随机系统上实现了兼具分布鲁棒性、形式化正确性与概率完备性的运动规划，实验验证其性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: Motion planning for stochastic systems aims to find a sequence of control inputs that drives a system to a goal set while avoiding unsafe states with high probability. Existing approaches, however, are either restricted to linear dynamics with perfect knowledge of the underlying distributions or lack formal correctness guarantees. To address these limitations, we propose \emph{Learn-To-Propagate Distributionally-Robust Forest (L2P-DR-Forest)}, a distributionally-robust sampling-based planning algorithm for \emph{nonlinear} stochastic systems with \emph{unknown} initial state and process noise distributions. Our approach leverages recent advances in optimal transport to learn ambiguity sets that contain the true system distributions with high confidence. We develop an efficient method for propagating these sets through the system dynamics and for verifying their safety. We then integrate these techniques into a novel sampling-based planning algorithm to grow a forest of search trees in the system's state space while accounting for and mitigating the growth of ambiguity-sets radii. We prove that the algorithm is correct, i.e., every returned plan satisfies the specifications with the prescribed confidence, and, under some feasibility and distributional assumptions, is probabilistically complete. Evaluations on several robotic benchmarks demonstrate that our algorithm significantly outperforms existing methods while providing formal guarantees with high confidence.

</details>


### [264] [ReACT: Reflection-Consistent Modulation and Cross-Load Consistency for Humanoid Velocity Tracking Under Asymmetric Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13929)
*Xianghui Wang,Aoran Fu,Liyou Luo,Shanze Wang,Xiaoyu Shen,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReACT框架，用于人形机器人单手持物（非对称上肢负载）下的精确速度跟踪：通过反射一致的扰动调制与跨负载跟踪一致性训练，减小速度跟踪偏差与方向漂移，在Unitree G1上实现仿真性能提升，并在真实场景40次无地图导航试验中全部无碰撞到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身抓取、举升与搬运任务都依赖运动控制器准确执行导航策略给出的速度指令。但单手持物会引入持续的非对称上肢负载，导致速度跟踪性能下降；跟踪偏差会累积为位置与航向漂移，增加碰撞与导航失败风险。因此需要一种在非对称负载下仍能保持精确速度跟踪的方法。

Method: 提出ReACT框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 反射一致扰动调制（reflection-consistent disturbance modulation）：利用估计的外力/力矩（wrench）调制actor特征以补偿负载，同时在观测被镜像反射时保持动作对应关系的一致性；(2) 跨负载跟踪一致性训练（cross-load tracking consistency training）：在相同速度指令下对比有负载与无负载条件，促使策略在不同负载条件下产生一致的速度跟踪行为。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的仿真实验表明，在不同非对称负载下速度跟踪精度提升、方向性偏差减小；真实世界实验中，在单侧手持负载且使用固定无地图导航策略的条件下，全部40次导航试验均实现无碰撞到达目标。

Conclusion: ReACT通过负载感知的特征调制与跨负载一致性训练，有效缓解单手持物引起的速度跟踪偏差及其累积漂移，使现有无地图导航策略在真实人形机器人上可靠完成搬运导航任务，说明提升底层运动控制器的跟踪一致性对全身移动操作具有关键作用。

Abstract: In recent years, humanoid loco-manipulation has progressed to whole-body grasping, lifting, and transport. A basic requirement underlying these tasks is carrying the object to its destination, which depends on the locomotion controller accurately executing velocity commands from the navigation policy. However, carrying an object in one hand introduces a persistent asymmetric upper-body load that can degrade velocity tracking. Persistent tracking biases can accumulate into position and heading deviations, increasing the risk of collisions and navigation failure. To address this problem, we present ReACT, a framework for accurate velocity tracking under asymmetric upper-body loads. Reflection-consistent disturbance modulation uses the estimated wrench to modulate actor features for load compensation while preserving action correspondence under reflected observations. Cross-load tracking consistency training compares loaded and unloaded conditions under the same velocity commands to promote consistent velocity tracking across loading conditions. Simulation experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot show improved velocity tracking and reduced directional bias under varying asymmetric loads. Real-world experiments demonstrate collision-free goal reaching in all 40 tested navigation trials under unilateral hand loads with a fixed mapless navigation policy.

</details>


### [265] [Optimization-Based Path Planning for Industrial Manipulators: Experimental Validation in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13983)
*Shiva Ganesh Murali,Valentin Bhend,Deepak Ingole,Gabriele Fadini,Alisa Rupenyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种离线优化式联合路径规划器，将关节路径正则性与自碰撞惩罚同工艺成本/约束统一建模，使新工艺仅通过其参考、残差与容差即可接入；用全路径与边界耦合分块优化处理不同长度参考，用迭代权重调节(IWT)依据KPI与目标的差距确定代价权重。在碳纤维缠绕与机器人3D打印上验证，规划路径满足几何容差与全部KPI指标，并在UR10e上执行（打印不做挤出），记录了关节级跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人增材制造需要同时满足机器人可行性与工艺特定几何要求的关节路径，而现有方法难以在统一框架中兼顾二者，且新工艺接入通常需要重写规划器。

Method: 离线优化式路径规划：目标函数含关节路径正则项、自碰撞惩罚及工艺成本，并施加工艺约束；工艺通过参考轨迹、残差与容差接口进入；针对不同长度参考采用全路径优化与边界耦合的分块优化；采用迭代权重调节(IWT)，根据KPI与其目标的偏差更新代价权重。

Result: 在碳纤维缠绕与机器人3D打印两个任务中，规划路径满足几何容差并达到全部KPI目标；两条路径均在UR10e上实际执行（打印时不挤出材料），并记录了执行过程中的关节级跟踪数据。

Conclusion: 该框架以统一的优化形式实现了机器人可行性与工艺几何的协调，具备良好的工艺可扩展性；IWT可自动确定权重；实验（含关节级跟踪）初步验证了方法的有效性与可执行性。

Abstract: Robotic additive manufacturing requires joint paths that reconcile robot feasibility with process-specific geometry. We develop an offline optimization-based path planner that combines joint-path regularity and self-collision penalties with process costs and constraints, so that a new process enters through its own reference, residuals, and tolerances. Full-path and boundary-coupled chunked optimization handle references of different lengths. Iterative Weight Tuning (IWT) determines the cost weights from the gaps between Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) and their targets. In carbon-fiber winding and robotic 3D printing, the planned paths meet the geometric tolerances and all KPI targets, and both paths are executed on a UR10e, without extrusion for printing, with joint-level tracking recorded during execution.

</details>


### [266] [LaST0.5: A Generalist World Action Model with Interaction-Centric Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14048)
*Xuheng Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Jialin Gao,Hao Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ji Li,Runzhong Huang,Yinxi Wang,Chenyang Gu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Zhonghao Yan,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Peng Jia,Pheng-Ann Heng,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaST0.5 提出以"交互中心物理表征"桥接世界建模与动作生成的 WAM 新范式，通过交互潜空间专家、自适应世界到动作迁移与 14k 小时大规模预训练，在 LIBERO 达 99.5%，并在 10 项 RLBench 任务与真实实验中取得 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models（含 video-first 动作预测与视频-动作联合建模）直接将世界模型的全局物理表征用于动作生成。但全局场景表征会弱化机器人-物体之间的局部交互信息，造成"面向世界演化优化的表征"与"操作任务所需表征"之间的失配。

Method: 1) 在机器人-环境视觉交互线索监督下学习紧凑的交互中心潜空间；2) 在 Mixture-of-Transformers 架构中将该推理空间实例化为潜空间专家，桥接世界专家与动作专家；3) 联合预测未来表征与进度值，实现自适应世界到动作迁移，并利用进度一致性调整动作执行、触发未来重采样；4) 在超 14k 小时大规模机器人数据语料上预训练。

Result: LIBERO 上达 99.5%；在 10 项评测 RLBench 任务上超越此前最优方法；真实世界实验展现稳健的长时序执行与精细灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: 以交互中心物理推理作为可扩展且有效的建模范式，可弥合世界模型与操作策略间的表征鸿沟，显著提升操作性能与泛化性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, endowing policies with strong capabilities for modeling temporal and physical dynamics. Existing WAMs, encompassing both video-first action prediction and joint video-action modeling, directly transfer global physical representations from world models to action generation. However, global scene representations may underemphasize local robot-object interactions, creating a mismatch between representations optimized for world evolution and those required for manipulation. To bridge this gap, we propose LaST0.5, a novel WAM that introduces an interaction-centric physical representation between world modeling and action generation. Specifically, LaST0.5 learns a compact interaction-centric latent space under the supervision of visual interaction cues between the robot and the environment. This reasoning space is instantiated as a latent expert that bridges the world expert and the action expert within a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. Furthermore, LaST0.5 performs adaptive world-to-action transfer by jointly predicting future representations and progress values, using progress consistency to adjust action execution and trigger future resampling. Finally, we pretrain LaST0.5 on a large-scale robotic data corpus comprising over 14k hours of data, establishing interaction-centric physical reasoning as a scalable and effective paradigm. LaST0.5 achieves 99.5% on LIBERO, surpasses the previous state of the art on ten evaluated RLBench tasks, and demonstrates robust long-horizon execution and precise dexterous manipulation in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [267] [RedirectMimic: Local Motion Content Redaction for Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14049)
*Zepeng Wang,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RedirectMimic 通过将 actor 观测到的参考与训练执行所用的目标解耦，用规则生成的编辑参考引导 PPO 跟踪奖励，实现人形机器人局部动作替换，且部署接口不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形运动接口需在满足应用特定运动约束的同时保留有用参考行为。局部内容删改要求控制器在仍接收原始参考的情况下替换特定动作；而移除这些动作的跟踪激励会使替代行为未被指定。

Method: 分离 actor 观测的参考与训练执行目标：由规则生成编辑后的参考，在 PPO 中用于引导跟踪奖励，而部署时的观测与动作接口保持不变。在 Unitree G1 仿真中基于 7,509 条动作片段的预训练追踪器评估姿态定义与速度定义的手臂编辑任务。

Result: 举臂任务中，核心窗口内容出现率降至 2.7%（奖励置零 58.9%、反跟踪 32.8%），核心窗口关节成功率 94.2%；速度定义任务中三种局部编辑方法的快速外摆臂发生率均较低；两任务中 RedirectMimic 替代误差最低，未编辑动作跟踪精度与预训练控制器相当。

Conclusion: 将参考观测与训练目标解耦可有效实现局部动作替换，同时保持原有跟踪能力与部署接口兼容性，优于奖励置零与反跟踪基线。

Abstract: Humanoid motion interfaces should accommodate application-specific movement constraints while retaining useful reference behavior. Local content redaction requires a controller to replace selected movements even when it continues to receive the original reference. Removing the tracking incentive for those movements leaves their replacement unspecified. We introduce RedirectMimic, which separates the reference observed by the actor from the target used to train its execution. A rule-generated edited reference guides tracking rewards during proximal policy optimization, while the deployed observation and action interfaces remain unchanged. We evaluate pose- and speed-defined arm editing in Unitree G1 simulation using a tracker pretrained on 7,509 motion clips. On the overhead-arm task, RedirectMimic reduces core-window content occurrence to 2.7%, compared with 58.9% for reward zeroing and 32.8% for anti-tracking. It achieves 94.2% core-window joint success, combining content suppression, fall-free execution, and local retention. On the speed-defined task, fast outward arm movements occurred at similarly low rates under all three local editing methods. Across both tasks, RedirectMimic achieved the lowest replacement error among these methods and tracked unedited motions with accuracy comparable to that of the pretrained controller. Project page: https://xiaopeng-whu.github.io/RedirectMimic/.

</details>


### [268] [A human-in-the-loop Control Paradigm for Construction Robotics: From VR-Guided Teleoperation to Semi-Autonomous Dual-Arm Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14062)
*Jinhua Chen,Xiaoji Xu,Zhongyuan Liao,Huang Zhenmin,Zhang Ye,Changying Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人在回路控制范式，在VR遥操作、半自主共享控制与规则化自主执行之间进行结构化切换，用于接触密集的双臂建筑任务，并在幕墙安装任务中验证其性能与操作员负荷权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 危险非结构化环境中的建筑任务既需要人的适应性又需要机器人精度，但现有方法常把遥操作与自主控制当作彼此分离的控制模式，缺乏可切换、可协同的统一框架。

Method: 融合沉浸式VR引导、人工定义的可复用技能原语库，以及带显式人机角色分配与指令优先级规则的共享自主框架，实现三种模式间的结构化转换；在双臂平台上开展典型幕墙安装任务验证。

Result: 在标定良好的名义条件下自主执行性能最佳；当环境扰动超出既定自主流程的纠偏能力时，半自主控制韧性更强；且半自主控制相较全遥操作显著降低操作员主观工作负荷。

Conclusion: 有效的建筑机器人控制应依据任务与环境不确定性自适应调整人的介入程度：可重复操作交由自主执行，扰动恢复交由共享控制。

Abstract: Construction tasks in hazardous and unstructured environments require both human adaptability and robotic precision, yet existing approaches often treat teleoperation and autonomy as separate control modes. This paper presents a human-in-the-loop control paradigm that enables structured transitions among VR-guided teleoperation, semi-autonomous shared control, and rule-based autonomous execution for contact-rich dual-arm construction tasks. The proposed system combines immersive VR guidance, a manually defined library of reusable skill primitives, and a shared-autonomy framework with explicit human-robot role allocation and command-priority rules. This integration allows operators to retain supervisory and corrective authority while delegating repetitive and precision-critical actions to the robot. The paradigm is validated on a dual-arm platform through representative facade-installation tasks. Results show that autonomous execution provides the best performance under well-calibrated nominal conditions, whereas semi-autonomous control improves resilience when environmental disturbances exceed the predefined autonomous pipeline's correction capability. Semi-autonomous control also substantially reduces subjective operator workload compared with full teleoperation. These findings demonstrate that effective construction-robot control should adapt human involvement to task and environmental uncertainty, using autonomy for repeatable operations and shared control for disturbance recovery.

</details>


### [269] [DexSeed: Expanding Human Hand-Object Interactions into Robot Demonstrations for Dexterous Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14092)
*Huaijin Pi,Zehong Shen,Yong Wang,Kechun Xu,Changpeng Wang,Dingkun Wei,Xueyang Wang,Wenjia Wang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSeed：从人类手-物体交互数据低成本生成多样化、可物理执行的灵巧机器人演示，用于VLA微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧VLA学习缺乏数据：现有演示生成依赖昂贵遥操作；人类手-物体数据集可复用，但现有迁移方法只能复现给定交互，难以覆盖不同物体与操作位置，且缺乏可执行的臂/手指动作与视觉观测。

Method: 按抓取模式并用共享语言描述组织源序列；采用基于InteractionMesh的重定向并结合手臂轨迹增强，在保持局部手-物体关系的前提下适配机器人手、不同物体几何与抓放位置；手臂跟踪预计算轨迹，由RL手指控制策略产生可执行rollout；将成功rollout的渲染观测与动作同抓取描述配对，用于微调VLA。

Result: 重定向的接触保真度更高，整臂执行比对比基线更可靠；仿真评估与真机部署表明，仅用生成演示微调的VLA能够执行语言指定的抓取。

Conclusion: 通过"保持抓取的序列扩展+物理仿真下的可执行化"路线，可从人类手-物体数据低成本构造灵巧机器人VLA训练数据，并实现语言条件抓取的真实部署。
