Abstract: We study how to generate diverse, physically executable robot demonstrations for dexterous vision-language-action (VLA) learning. Existing demonstration-generation methods typically rely on costly teleoperated examples. Meanwhile, human hand--object datasets provide a reusable source, but existing transfer methods mainly reproduce given interactions. Turning these interactions into VLA training data further requires coverage of different objects and manipulation locations, together with executable arm and finger actions, and visual observations. To achieve this, we present DexSeed, a framework for low-cost generation of dexterous robot data from human hand--object interactions. Our key idea is to expand human sequences while preserving their grasps and realizing the resulting motions under arm-tracking dynamics in physics simulation. Specifically, we organize source sequences by grasp mode with shared language descriptions. InteractionMesh-based retargeting and arm trajectory augmentation adapt these sequences to the robot hand, different object geometries, and pick-and-place locations while preserving local hand--object relationships. An RL finger-control policy produces executable rollouts, while the arm tracks precomputed trajectories. Rendered observations and recorded actions from successful rollouts are paired with grasp descriptions to fine-tune a VLA. Experiments show improved contact fidelity in retargeting and more reliable whole-arm execution than the evaluated baselines. Simulation evaluation and real-world deployment further demonstrate that a VLA fine-tuned solely on the generated demonstrations can execute language-specified grasps. Project page: https://robbyant-research.github.io/DexSeed/.

</details>


### [270] [Model-Free Runtime Measurement of Task-Relevant Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14111)
*Dimitris Panagopoulos,Adolfo Perrusquía,Yang Xing,Weisi Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Acquisition-State Telemetry (AST) 架构，将原始观测映射到任务解析坐标，并用固定因果监视器实时输出"前瞻进展"与"信息摩擦"两路连续信号；在八个领域中以单一未调参配置验证摩擦通道能区分无效停滞与有意义的探索，并在闭环强化学习中通过惩罚信息摩擦显著提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作能够规划信息获取动作并解释已获得的观测，但缺少对"信息收集过程本身是否正常运行"的在线监测。因此系统可能在未解决任务需求的情况下持续运行而不自知，导致任务悬而未决。

Method: 提出模型无关的 Acquisition-State Telemetry（AST）：(1) 领域接口将原始观测映射为任务解析坐标与归一化比较表示；(2) 由固定的因果监视器处理，输出两路连续实时信号——跨未解决需求的前瞻性进度，以及重复证据带来可忽略信息增益时产生的信息摩擦。

Result: 在八个异构领域（搜救对话、空间网格探索、分层能力获取、连续机器人操作、概率意图估计等）中以单一未调整的监视器配置评估：摩擦通道能在任务完成前区分无产出的停滞与有目的的探索，且在任务完成后的活动中保持平静。闭环强化学习早停实验中，惩罚信息摩擦使智能体维持高任务成功率，而未惩罚策略与等运行成本基线均无法完成所需任务。

Conclusion: 信息获取过程本身可作为可观测、可监测的状态；AST 提供了一个模型无关、免调参的通用遥测机制，其摩擦信号既可作为停滞诊断指标，也可作为强化学习的有效引导信号。

Abstract: Autonomous systems increasingly monitor physical kinematics, environmental states and task completion outcomes. While existing work provides methods to plan information seeking actions and interpret acquired observations, current approaches do not monitor whether the live process of information gathering is functioning properly. Consequently, such systems can remain operationally active without resolving outstanding mission requirements. To address this limitation, we introduce Acquisition-State Telemetry (AST), a model free architecture that evaluates ongoing information acquisition from observable evidence relative to declared task requirements. Specifically, a domain interface maps raw observations into task resolution coordinates and normalized comparative representations, which are processed by a fixed causal monitor. This monitor emits two continuous real time signals that quantify (i) prospective progress across unresolved requirements and (ii) informational friction generated when recurrent evidence yields negligible informational gain. We evaluated a single, unadjusted monitor configuration across eight diverse domains including search and rescue dialogue, spatial grid exploration, hierarchical capability acquisition, continuous robotic manipulation, and probabilistic intent estimation. We show that across these domains the friction channel distinguished unproductive stagnation from purposeful exploration prior to task completion, while remaining muted during post completion activity. Furthermore, in closed loop reinforcement learning experiments under early termination constraints, we demonstrate that penalizing informational friction allowed agents to sustain high mission success, whereas unpenalized policies and baselines with matched operational costs failed to complete required tasks.

</details>


### [271] [LEASH: Stay Safe in Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14115)
*Qinfeng Zhu,Weiguang Zhao,Ke Sun,Xihao Guo,Yunxi Jiang,Rui Zhang,Lei Fan,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 RoboRisk——覆盖五类安全风险的具身智能体安全基准（92 个仿真任务 + 13 个真机任务，全部闭环执行），发现六个前沿基础模型危害率普遍较高；并提出 LEASH 安全框架，通过任务门、动作门、提交门三道决策模型审查机制显著降低危害，同时基本保持任务完成率、开销很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用基础模型（GPT、Claude 等）驱动机器人虽推进了具身智能，但带来严重安全风险，而现有评测基准覆盖面不足：最多只覆盖五类风险中的两类，且部分仅评估文本输出而非真实闭环执行，缺乏全面的安全性评估手段。

Method: 1) 构建 RoboRisk 基准，任务分为五类：任务级拒答(T)、规划修复(P)、执行期危害(E)、注入(I)、序列记忆(S)，共 92 个仿真任务与 13 个物理机器人任务，均在闭环中执行；2) 在 RoboRisk 上评测六个前沿基础模型及两个机器人专用 API（Dream API、PokeBot RoboAPI）；3) 提出 LEASH（Lease-Enforced Agentic Safety Harness），用决策模型（Jev 或 OpenAI Decisions API）实现任务门、动作门、提交门三层闸门，分别在视觉上下文中审查任务、每个动作和每个不可逆操作，规划器与三道门共享同一安全策略，可集成进几乎任何智能体架构。

Result: 所评测的六个基础模型（GPT-6 Astra、GPT-6.1 Sol、GPT-6 Luna、Claude Opus 5.5、Gemini 4 Argon、Grok 4.7）总体危害率较高；仿真与真机实验表明，LEASH 在全部六个模型上均有效降低危害，且基本不损失任务完成性能，增加的时间与 token 开销很小。

Conclusion: 现有基础模型直接用于机器人控制存在显著安全隐患，且此前缺乏全面评测；RoboRisk 提供了覆盖五类风险、全闭环的安全基准，LEASH 则作为一种通用、低开销的安全基础设施，可有效提升具身智能体的安全性而不显著牺牲能力。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude have shown strong capabilities in spatial understanding and embodied intelligence. These advances make agentic robotics a promising path toward artificial general intelligence in robotics. However, we find that using foundation models to control robots poses serious safety risks, and comprehensive safety evaluation is still lacking. We introduce RoboRisk, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating safety in agentic robotics. Its 92 simulated tasks and 13 physical-robot tasks fall into five categories: Task-level Refusal (T), Plan Repair (P), Execution-time Hazard (E), Injection (I), and Sequential Memory (S). Prior benchmarks cover at most two of these categories, and several evaluate only text outputs. In contrast, RoboRisk covers all five and executes every task in closed loop. We evaluate six state-of-the-art foundation models on RoboRisk: GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6 Luna, Claude Opus 5.5, Gemini 4 Argon, and Grok 4.7. These models generally show high harm rates. We also evaluate two robot-specific APIs: the Dream API and PokeBot's RoboAPI. We further propose the Lease-Enforced Agentic Safety Harness (LEASH), which offers essential safety infrastructure for agentic robotics and can be integrated into nearly any agentic architecture. LEASH leverages a decision model (Jev or OpenAI's Decisions API) to implement task, action, and commit gates, which review the task, each action, and each irreversible operation in their visual context. The planner and all three gates share a common safety policy. Experiments on RoboRisk in simulation and on physical robots show that LEASH effectively reduces harm across all six foundation models. It largely preserves their task completion performance and adds little time and token overhead.

</details>


### [272] [Koopman Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14186)
*Wenjian Hao,Paulo C. Heredia,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Koopman算子的模型强化学习算法(K-MBRL)，用线性Koopman表示近似未知非线性动力学，结合critic估计未来代价，实现高效策略改进并成功部署于实体四足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制未知动力学的非线性机器人系统时，无模型RL样本效率低，而传统基于模型RL的长时域rollout会累积预测误差、模型依赖的策略梯度计算开销大。

Method: 构建近似策略目标：解析即时代价 + 对一步预测状态处未来代价的估计；用随机Koopman算子学习未知动力学为线性表示，critic函数估计剩余未来代价；线性Koopman动力学将模型相关策略梯度化简为常数矩阵乘积，仅做一步模型传播；给出随机逼近假设下的策略学习收敛性保证。

Result: 在HalfCheetah与四足运动任务上样本效率高、性能优于经典无模型与基于模型基线；单摆起摆任务验证策略梯度近似精度与消融；实体四足机器人完成两后圆足支撑起身、双足行走平衡并安全落地的动作序列。

Conclusion: Koopman线性化+一步预测+critic的组合在保证长期代价信息的同时降低计算与误差累积，学习策略可前馈实时执行无需在线优化，具备仿真到实物的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a Koopman model-based reinforcement learning (K-MBRL) algorithm for controlling nonlinear robotic systems with unknown dynamics. The K-MBRL learns a control policy by minimizing an approximate policy objective that combines the analytical immediate cost with an estimate of the future cost evaluated at the one-step predicted state. To this end, we develop a stochastic Koopman operator-based learning method to approximate the unknown nonlinear dynamics in a linear representation, while a critic function estimates the remaining future cost. This formulation offers two key advantages. First, the linear Koopman dynamics simplify the model-dependent policy gradient computation to a constant matrix product, enabling computationally efficient policy improvement. Second, one-step model propagation mitigates the accumulation of prediction errors associated with long-horizon rollouts while retaining long-term cost information through the critic. The learned policy enables real-time control through feedforward evaluation without online optimization. We establish policy learning convergence guarantees under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Simulations on HalfCheetah and quadruped four-legged locomotion tasks demonstrate strong sample efficiency and improved performance compared with classical model-free and model-based reinforcement learning baselines. A pendulum swing-up task is used to assess policy gradient approximation accuracy and conduct ablation studies on the learning mechanism and key hyperparameters. The proposed K-MBRL is further validated on a physical quadruped robot performing a challenging sequence of maneuvers: rising to an upright posture supported by two rounded rear feet, maintaining balance during bipedal walking, and safely returning to the ground, as shown in Fig.1.

</details>


### [273] [Elastic-WAM: Adaptive Spatiotemporal Imagination for Efficient Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14257)
*Haitian Li,Tianfu Li,Xuxi Zhao,Zizheng Liu,Yangcheng Yu,Yiwen Hou,Zeyu Xiao,Mutian Xu,Ziqian Yang,Haonan Chen,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Elastic-WAM，用一个统一的时空精化需求场自适应分配世界模型精化计算量（时间维度的去噪深度与空间维度的未来区域），在降低推理开销的同时提升机器人操控成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-Action Models依赖迭代视频去噪预测未来视觉动态，推理开销大；现有部分未来去噪方法采用固定精化预算，但实验观察发现最优精化深度随任务与状态变化，且未来不同区域对动作生成的相关性不同，固定预算不合理。

Method: 构建统一的时空精化需求场，控制三件事：何时停止世界精化、每步去噪推进多远、刷新哪些区域。该场用共享时间基与空间幅值参数化，并通过三阶段、以动作为锚的监督过程学习；推理时用学到的场引导循环稀疏世界精化。

Result: 在多个仿真基准与真实世界长程操控任务上取得state-of-the-art控制性能，同时降低推理延迟。

Conclusion: 自适应、时空联合的计算分配优于固定精化预算，可兼顾World-Action Model的控制效果与推理效率。

Abstract: World-Action Models improve robot control by predicting future visual dynamics, but iterative video denoising incurs substantial inference overhead. Recent approaches reduce this cost through partial future denoising, yet typically rely on fixed refinement budgets. Our empirical observations reveal that preferred refinement depths vary across tasks and states, while different future regions exhibit varying relevance to action generation. Motivated by these findings, we propose Elastic-WAM, which adaptively allocates world refinement computation across temporal denoising time and spatial future space. A unified spatiotemporal refinement demand field governs when to stop world refinement, how far to advance each denoising step, and which regions to refresh. We parameterize this field with a shared temporal basis and spatial amplitudes, and learn it through a three-stage action-grounded supervised procedure. At inference, the learned field guides recurrent sparse world refinement to improve task success while reducing inference overhead. Evaluations across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world long-horizon manipulation demonstrate state-of-the-art control performance with reduced inference latency.

</details>


### [274] [From Prediction to Action: Learning Physical Context for Deformable Linear Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14274)
*Chengxiao Dong,Alessio Caporali,Hongyu Lan,Gianluca Palli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究可变形线性物体（DLO）平面单抓取形状控制中"预测效用"与"决策效用"的区分，提出Geometry-Pointer-Flow（GPF）几何策略主干，并系统比较真值参数、估计参数与交互历史表示在预测与控制中的不同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征研究多以预测精度衡量优劣，但对操作决策而言，预测得准的表征未必最利于选动作。作者希望厘清物理上下文表征在"前向预测"与"实际控制"两种目标下的价值差异，避免仅凭预测精度选表征。

Method: 提出 GPF 几何接地策略，将抓取点选择与抓取条件下的运动生成解耦；以其几何单模态版本对比 DLO 专用扩散策略。在 GPF 主干上分别以真值物理参数、估计参数、学习到的交互历史表征作为物理上下文，评估前向预测与已执行控制两类指标；全部策略仅在仿真训练，零样本迁移至实体机器人，无需真实数据训练或微调。

Result: 仅几何版 GPF 相比 DLO 专用扩散策略单步形状误差降低 32.8%，参数量少 18.7 倍，推理约快 193 倍。预测方面，真值参数使下一步形状误差较纯几何降低 15.8%，估计参数略优于交互历史。控制方面该排序不成立：估计参数无可检测的单步控制增益，而历史条件 GPF 在 470 组配对仿真试验中相对仅几何版降低执行误差 9.9%；闭环仿真中交互历史提升严格阈值达成率，并在硬件上取得最强零样本表现。

Conclusion: 物理上下文应按其支撑的决策质量而非仅预测精度来评估；交互历史表征在决策上优于更"准确"的参数估计，说明预测效用与决策效用存在实质性分离。

Abstract: A representation that accurately predicts a deformable object's response is not necessarily the one most useful for choosing manipulation actions. We study this distinction between predictive utility and decision utility in planar single-grasp shape control of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs). As a common decision backbone, we introduce Geometry-Pointer-Flow (GPF), a geometry-grounded policy that separates grasp-location selection from grasp-conditioned motion generation. Its geometry-only variant reduces one-step executed shape error by 32.8% relative to a DLO-specific diffusion policy, while using 18.7 times fewer parameters and achieving approximately 193 times faster inference. On this backbone, we compare physical context represented by ground-truth parameters, estimated parameters, and learned interaction-history representations, for both forward prediction and executed control. For prediction, ground-truth physical parameters reduce next-shape error by 15.8% over geometry alone, and estimated parameters are slightly more predictive than interaction history. This ordering does not carry over to control: estimated parameters provide no detectable one-step control gain, whereas history-conditioned GPF reduces executed error by 9.9% relative to geometry-only GPF across 470 paired simulation trials. In closed loop, interaction history improves strict-threshold attainment in simulation and yields the strongest zero-shot performance on hardware. All policies are trained exclusively in simulation and transferred to the physical robot without real-world training or fine-tuning. These results show that physical context should be evaluated by the decisions it supports rather than by predictive accuracy alone. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [275] [ForeseeDrive: Future-Token Conditioning for Anticipatory Generative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14286)
*Shaokai Wu,Xunchu Zhou,Pinzhi Wang,Bo Dong,Yuting Hu,Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Xiao Cui,Qi Kong,Hongtao Lu,Yue Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForeseeDrive 将未来场景演化转化为可部署的"查询索引 token"接口，用其预条件候选轨迹生成，在 NAVSIM/nuScenes/Bench2Drive 上取得 89.2 PDMS、0.41m L2、76.50 驾驶分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式驾驶规划器虽能表征多样自车未来，但下游打分器只能在已采样候选中选择，无法恢复未被采样的机动（absent maneuver），存在候选集覆盖不足的固有限制。

Method: 不做稠密未来状态重建，也不对固定候选菜单打分；从日志中 actor 运动、占据演化、路线交互与 TTC 裕度导出 query-indexed tokens，并在部署时由当前上下文预测同一 token 空间；tokens 在候选轨迹生成之前条件化轨迹几何；辅以 zero-token 门控残差路径、候选保留损失抑制无谓漂移，以及 TTC-margin guard 针对安全边界穿越。

Result: NAVSIM navtest 达 89.2 PDMS；nuScenes val 平均 L2 0.41m、碰撞率 0.05%；Bench2Drive 驾驶分 76.50。DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM 诊断显示：zero-token 精确等价 no-op；保持约束下 aligned 优于 shuffled 的 selected-PDM 小幅优势；oracle-best 质量参差；TTC 边界失效呈局部化。

Conclusion: 基准结果与受控机制测试共同支持"未来条件化生成"作为可落地的预判接口；同时诊断暴露 oracle-best 质量不稳定与 TTC 边界局部失效等局限，代码随补充材料提供。

Abstract: Generative driving planners can represent diverse ego futures, but a downstream scorer can choose only among sampled candidates; it cannot recover an absent maneuver. We present ForeseeDrive, which turns future scene evolution into a deployable interface for shaping candidate generation. Rather than reconstructing dense future states or scoring a fixed menu, ForeseeDrive derives query-indexed tokens from logged actor motion, occupancy evolution, route interactions, and TTC margins, and predicts the same token space from present context at deployment. The tokens condition trajectory geometry before candidates materialize. A zero-token-gated residual path and candidate-preservation loss limit unnecessary drift, while a TTC-margin guard targets brittle safe-to-unsafe boundary crossings. Under their respective benchmark protocols, ForeseeDrive reaches 89.2 PDMS on NAVSIM navtest, 0.41\,m average L2 and 0.05\% collision rate on nuScenes validation, and a 76.50 driving score on Bench2Drive. Controlled DiffusionDrive/NAVSIM diagnostics show an exact zero-token no-op, a small aligned-over-shuffled selected-PDM advantage under preservation, mixed oracle-best quality, and localized TTC-boundary failures. Together, the benchmark results and bounded mechanism tests support future-conditioned generation as a practical interface for anticipation. Code is included in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [276] [Gaussian Process-Augmented Model Predictive Contouring Control for 6-DoF Trajectory Tracking with a Holonomic AUV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14373)
*David Dörner,Giancarlo Troni,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种六自由度非线性MPCC控制器，用高斯过程学习的残差动力学补偿名义模型误差，并通过零阶鲁棒优化嵌入优化问题，实现25Hz实时控制，仿真与水池实验验证其优于标称MPCC和PID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型全驱动AUV可在任意视角近距离检测水下结构，但需在状态与输入约束下精确跟踪位姿。MPCC能自然处理这些约束，却依赖精确动力学模型，而水下动力学建模困难，模型误差会削弱控制性能。

Method: 为全驱动AUV设计全六自由度非线性MPCC；用高斯过程回归学习名义模型的残差动力学，并将学习到的残差通过零阶鲁棒优化方案嵌入最优控制问题，以保持控制器实时性（25Hz）。

Result: 仿真消融实验表明残差项的选择对控制器设计至关重要；基于MOLA AUV的仿真与真实水下实验表明，所提控制器性能优于标称MPCC和经现场验证的PID控制器。

Conclusion: GP残差补偿结合鲁棒MPCC可在保持实时性的同时提升全驱动AUV在模型不精确条件下的位姿跟踪性能，是一种可行且有效的水下近距检测控制方案。

Abstract: Small, holonomic Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) enable close-range inspection of underwater structures from arbitrary viewpoints, requiring accurate position and attitude tracking under state and input constraints. Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC) naturally handles these requirements, but relies on an accurate dynamics model, which is difficult to obtain underwater. In this paper, we present a nonlinear MPCC in all six Degrees of Freedom (DoF) for a holonomic AUV and alleviate the inaccuracies in the dynamics by augmenting the nominal model employed with residual dynamics learned via Gaussian Process (GP) regression. The learned residual is embedded in the optimal control problem through a zero-order robust optimization scheme, which keeps the controller real-time capable at 25 Hz. A simulation ablation shows that residual selection is essential when designing the controller, and simulation and in-water experiments with the Multimodality, Observing, Low-cost, Agile (MOLA) AUV validate the resulting controllers against a nominal MPCC and a field proven Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller.

</details>


### [277] [SIGMO: Sigmoid Gated Interaction-Aware Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14414)
*Avinash Singh,Manish Saroya,Fatemeh Cheraghi Pouria,Faizan Tariq,Jovin D'sa,David Isele,Yosuke Sakamoto,Sangjae Bae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGMO——一种基于sigmoid门控的交互感知运动规划器，利用多项式sigmoid函数解析估计周围智能体的让行评分，并嵌入滚动时域规划器中，在交互密集仿真与硬件实验中实现主动性与防御性的平衡，且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在拥挤环境中的可靠导航仍很困难，因为需要交互感知策略，例如多智能体窄通道通行、密集车流换道、无信号交叉口穿越。此类场景要求高度主动的策略，同时兼顾可解释性与快速适应性，这对端到端和数据驱动方法构成挑战。有效策略需满足：可扩展至多智能体、适应其他智能体行为变化、与周围交通和谐而不僵死或过度保守。

Method: SIGMO包含两项主要贡献：(1) 构建多项式sigmoid函数，基于具有物理依据、能刻画驾驶直觉的交互特征，解析地估计周围智能体的让行评分（yield scores）；(2) 将这些多项式sigmoid函数集成到滚动时域（receding-horizon）规划器中，从而支持可搭配任意轨迹预测的交互感知规划。

Result: 在交互密集的仿真场景与硬件实验中进行验证，结果表明SIGMO在主动与防御行为之间取得平衡，同时保持较低的计算负担，可作为出行（mobility）技术栈中的强基线方法。

Conclusion: SIGMO以可解释的多项式sigmoid让行评分建模交互，并与滚动时域规划结合，兼具多智能体可扩展性、行为适应性与交通和谐性，且计算轻量，适合作为移动堆栈中的基线规划器。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of autonomous vehicle technologies, reliable navigation within cluttered environments remains arduous due to the necessity of interaction-aware strategies; example scenarios include: narrow passages with multiple agents, lane changing in dense traffic, and crossing unsignalized intersections. These scenarios often require highly proactive strategies, simultaneously demanding interpretability and quick adaptability, which challenge end-to-end and data-driven methods. An effective strategy requires: scalability to multiple agents, adaptability to changes in other agents' behavior, and harmony with surrounding traffic without freezing or becoming overly conservative. To meet these requirements, we present SIGMO, a sigmoid-gated interaction-aware motion planner, with two main contributions. First, we formulate polynomial sigmoid functions that analytically estimate yield scores of surrounding agents using physics-grounded interaction features that capture driving intuitions. Second, we integrate the polynomial sigmoid functions into a receding-horizon planner, which facilitates interaction-aware planning with arbitrary trajectory predictions. We validate SIGMO in interaction-intensive simulations and hardware experiments. The results show that SIGMO balances proactive and defensive behavior while remaining computationally lightweight, serving as a strong baseline in a mobility stack. The simulation and hardware experiment videos are available here: https://sites.google.com/view/sigmoid-polygate.

</details>


### [278] [One Policy for 100 Household Tasks: Our Solution for the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14432)
*Ilia Larchenko,Gleb Zarin,Atindra Nair,Keerti Manney,Vitaly Kleban,Mirko Kiefer,Anjana Yodaiken*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向2026 BEHAVIOR挑战赛（OmniGibson中100项长程家务任务）的解决方案，以单一VLA模型覆盖全部任务，并对14项任务额外路由到大模型编码智能体，最终公开提交获0.446 Q与27.3%全任务成功率，位列第9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年经验表明模型训练不足是性能瓶颈，故2026年工作主要围绕"训练更久、更高效、更稳定"展开；同时探索将部分任务交由具备工具调用能力的编码智能体处理以弥补学习策略的短板。

Method: 在基于π_{0.5}的2025冠军方案上扩展：1) 从50迁移到100任务——由示范的技能标注构建语义阶段、新任务的任务/阶段嵌入从相似旧任务热启动、21维动作空间对齐遥操作控制；2) 方差缩减的多样本流匹配，并用以噪声-计算量模型选择每次观测的动作专家采样数（无消融）；3) 20万步训练、约遍历2.11亿帧示范一遍；4) 云端评测设施维护带溯源、回合诊断与可恢复仿真状态的共享记录，单GPU上6个仿真器并行且快于实时；5) 14项任务路由至GPT-6 Astra编码智能体，通过观测与运动工具操控机器人，每回合从训练实例上的练习会话冻结副本开始。

Result: 与2025共享的50项任务Q由0.260升至0.371；全部100项任务得0.392。Astra在14项路由任务上平均Q为0.639，学习策略仅0.108。公开提交仅含部分Astra结果，得0.446 Q、27.3%全任务成功率，最终排名第9，为前十中唯一独立团队。

Conclusion: 延长训练与降低梯度噪声对VLA长程任务性能提升有效；混合式架构（学习策略＋编码智能体）在难任务上显著优于纯学习策略，但公开提交未完全释放该增益，暗示进一步提升空间。

Abstract: We describe our solution for the 2026 BEHAVIOR Challenge: 100 long-horizon household tasks in the OmniGibson simulator. We placed 9th on the final leaderboard, the only independent team in the top 10. The core of the solution is a single vision-language-action (VLA) model, one checkpoint for all 100 tasks, built on our 1st-place 2025 solution, which is based on $π_{0.5}$. On the 50 tasks shared with 2025, its Q rises from 0.260 to 0.371; across all 100 tasks it scores 0.392. Our main conclusion from 2025 was that the model was undertrained, so most of the 2026 work went into training the model for longer, optimizing some training parameters, making each training step slightly faster and reducing the gradient noise: a migration from 50 to 100 tasks (semantic stages built from the demonstrations' skill annotations, task and stage embeddings of new tasks warm-started from similar old ones, and a 21-dimensional action space matching the teleoperation controls); variance-reduced multi-sample flow matching with a simple noise-vs-compute model that picks the number of action-expert samples per observation without ablations; and a 200k-step training run, about one pass over the 211M frames of the demonstrations. A cloud evaluation infrastructure keeps a shared record with provenance, episode diagnostics and restorable simulator states, and runs six simulators on one GPU together faster than real time. On top of the learned policy, 14 tasks are routed to a GPT-6 Astra coding agent that controls the robot through observation and motion tools, starting every episode from a frozen copy of its own practice session on training instances. Across these 14 tasks, Astra's mean Q is 0.639, against 0.108 for the learned policy. The public submission included only part of the Astra results and scored 0.446 Q with 27.3% full-task success.

</details>


### [279] [TanCRAFT: Context-Reasoned Anticipatory Feedback for Assembly Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14470)
*Sassan Mokhtar,Lars Doorenbos,Marius Bock,Sina Mokhtar,Katharina Beckh,Dominik Bach,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TanCRAFT四阶段实时预测式辅助系统，在自由装配顺序场景下预测并阻止错误发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理装配中防止人为错误需在错误发生前干预；当用户可自由选择装配顺序、正确性依赖不断演化的装配状态而非固定下一步时，这一挑战尤为突出。

Method: TanCRAFT为四阶段实时预测式辅助框架：跟踪场景状态、对当前部分装配进行推理、预测被操作部件的近期运动，并在预测放置与目标不兼容时进行干预。以受控七巧板装配任务为实例，通过离线基准测试与被试内用户研究验证。

Result: 预测式反馈相比无引导与反应式条件显著减少错误，同时平均感知工作负荷最低、平均完成时间最短。

Conclusion: 结果证明预测式反馈在错误预防方面的价值，并为自动化与人机协作工作单元中具备成本感知的辅助系统迈出一步。

Abstract: Preventing human error in physical assembly tasks requires assistance systems that can intervene before mistakes are made. This is especially challenging in settings where users are free to choose the construction order and correctness depends on the evolving assembly rather than a fixed next step. We present TanCRAFT, a four-stage real-time predictive assistance system that tracks the scene state, reasons about the current partial assembly, forecasts the near-future motion of manipulated pieces, and intervenes when the predicted placement is incompatible with the target. We instantiate our framework and confirm its effectiveness in a controlled tangram assembly task through offline benchmarks and a within-subject user study. Our findings show that the predictive feedback significantly reduces mistakes relative to unguided and reactive sessions, while yielding the lowest mean perceived workload and shortest mean completion time. Overall, these results demonstrate the value of predictive feedback for error prevention and provide a step toward cost-aware assistance for automation and human-robot workcells.

</details>


### [280] [Is Your Robot's Memory Just a Clock? Temporal Shortcuts in Memory-Augmented VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14488)
*Chubin Zhang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示记忆增强VLA策略存在"幻影进展"失败：机器人在空事件（暂停/手腕微动）期间被误判为任务有进展而提前终止；根因是时间戳索引带来的时序捷径，改为事件索引可在不重训情况下恢复一半以上损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA按时间均匀采样帧并打时间戳来构建记忆，但"时间流逝"不等于"任务有进展"。作者希望验证这种按时间索引的记忆是否会混淆"等待"与"工作"，并弄清失败根源与可行修复方向。

Method: 在RoboMME的计数任务上做受控干预实验：让机器人短暂暂停或抖动腕部（不改变任务相关状态的空事件），观测成功率变化；将相同帧从记忆中剔除以定位因果来源；单独缩放时间戳以检验时序捷径；去除时间编码以检验帧占位影响；比较不同记忆索引方式（时间索引 vs 事件索引）以及注入暂停的再训练方案。

Result: 空事件导致已发布记忆VLA把间隔计为已完成工作并提前停止：两个任务成功率分别从47/50降至16/50、从44/50降至10/50；剔除相同帧后成功率恢复，说明问题源于记忆而非中断本身。仅重缩放时间戳即可复现提前停止（时钟快）或重复动作（时钟慢）；移除时间编码无效，因空事件帧仍占用时间采样记忆槽。脆弱性与记忆索引方式相关；注入暂停训练未能消除失败且损害计数能力，而事件索引记忆无需重训即可挽回一半以上损失。

Conclusion: 记忆应按"发生了什么事件"而非"时钟走了多少"来索引；时间索引会引入进度预测的捷径偏差，产生幻影进展，事件索引是更稳健且低成本的修复方向。

Abstract: Memory-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) policies are meant to remember what happened. Many of them, however, fill memory by time: they sample frames uniformly across the episode and stamp each frame with its time step. Because time passes even when nothing happens, such a memory may not tell waiting from working. We show that it does not. On the counting tasks of RoboMME, we make the robot briefly pause or fidget its wrist. Such a null event changes nothing the task depends on, yet a released memory VLA counts the interval as work done and stops early. We call this failure phantom progress. It cuts success from 47 to 16 of 50 on one task and from 44 to 10 on the other. When the same frames are withheld from memory, success returns, so the cause lies in memory rather than in the interruption itself. We trace the failure to a temporal shortcut: because demonstrations proceed at a steady pace, elapsed time predicts progress. Indeed, rescaling the time stamps alone makes the policy stop early when the clock runs fast and repeat when it runs slow. Surprisingly, removing the time encoding does not cure the failure, because the frames of a null event still occupy slots of the time-sampled memory. Across RoboMME's released designs, vulnerability follows how memory is indexed. Of the fixes the diagnosis suggests, training with injected pauses does not remove the failure and impairs counting, whereas indexing memory by events recovers half or more of the loss without retraining. A memory meant to remember what happened should count events rather than clock ticks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [An ensemble of graph generative models for assisted history matching of the Brugge field case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12927)
*Gleb Shishaev,Anton Shirokov,Shadfar Davoodi,Mohammed Al-Shargabi,Vasily Demyanov,Daniel Arnold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于图自编码器（graph autoencoder）的分层带（zone-based）辅助历史拟合方法：为Brugge基准场的每个沉积带建立专用生成模型，并在160维联合潜空间中以CMA-ES进行优化，结合拓扑数据分析的潜空间诊断，在保持地质合理性的同时获得与EnKF、PSO-PCA、MKL-PSO等方法相当的井级拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助历史拟合存在非唯一性：多个地质实现可产生等效的产量响应，但单井拟合差异显著；当油田包含多种沉积环境与建模策略时，这种模糊性进一步加剧。传统方法（如基于PCA的参数化）难以处理角点网格的不规则几何，且易产生脱离地质流形的不合理解。

Method: 1) 图自编码器生成器直接作用于角点网格活动网格单元，无需转换为规则格架即可处理不规则几何；2) 按地层带分别构建生成模型，并构造160维联合潜空间，用于历史拟合优化循环；3) 采用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）在联合潜空间搜索；4) 多分量目标函数包含静态井数据失配、动态生产失配及基于解码器诱导潜度量的地质合理性惩罚项；5) 历史拟合前用拓扑数据分析进行潜空间诊断，判断单一生成器是否充分或需多生成器形式。

Result: 基于分带的建模形式给出连贯的潜空间，保留了各带间差异化的主要孔隙度—渗透率关系，并在历史拟合中使解保持在所学地质流形内；拟合模型在先验不确定性范围内再现了油田产油与产水，井级拟合效果与已发表的Brugge研究（EnKF、PSO-PCA、MKL-PSO）相当。

Conclusion: 分带图自编码器联合潜空间优化为多沉积环境下非规则网格的历史拟合提供了有效框架，兼顾了拟合质量与地质合理性；拓扑数据分析的诊断流程可指导生成器结构与建模策略的选择。

Abstract: Assisted history matching remains non-unique because many geological realisations can reproduce equivalent production responses with substantial differences between individual well matches. This ambiguity increases when a field comprises several depositional settings and modelling strategies. The paper applied a graph autoencoder approach to history matching of a benchmark Brugge field that comprises different depositional zones. Each stratigraphic zone is modelled with its dedicated generative model that comprises a joint latent space for optimisation history matching loops. Graph-based generators operate directly on the active cells of the corner-point grid, so its irregular geometry is handled without conversion to a regular lattice. The optimisation search in the 160-dimensional joint latent space is done with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy. The multi-component objective function includes static well data mismatch, dynamic production mismatch, and a geological realism penalty based on the Decoder induced latent metric. Latent space diagnostics with topological data analysis are applied prior to history matching to test whether one generator is sufficient or whether a multiple generator formulation is required. The results show that the zone-based formulation gives coherent latent spaces, preserves the main porosity and permeability relations that vary from one zone to another, and supports history matching while keeping the solutions within the learned geological manifold. The matched models reproduce field oil and water production within the prior uncertainty range and provide a comparable well-level match to previously published Brugge studies based on Ensemble Kalman Filter, Particle Swarm Optimisation with principal component analysis of parameterisation, and Multiple Kernel Learning with Particle Swarm Optimisation.

</details>


### [282] [Predictive Surprise as Self-Grounding Concept Bottleneck for Interpretable Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12955)
*Sachith Abeywickrama,Emadeldeen Eldele,Min Wu,Xiaoli Li,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTime 用冻结概率预测器的"预测惊奇度"这一自监督信号，同时完成时序分段与概念表征，从而一次性满足准确、可解释、可弃权三项要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感领域的时序分类器必须同时做到准确、可解释、在证据薄弱时能弃权；现有方法难以三者兼得。其根源是结构性的循环依赖：无分段边界则无法定义有意义的时间模式，而无预期模式则无法确定边界，现有方法用固定窗口或专家标注词表打破循环，因而牺牲了至少一项性质。

Method: 引入冻结的概率预测器，在每个时间步预测下一观测的分布，将预测与真实信号的失配定义为"预测惊奇度"（predictive surprise）。该单一自监督信号既用于定位分段边界，又通过预测器刻画每个分段；分段的语义由"相对于预期的行为"而非"外观形态"来确定。这些分段摘要聚类成"动力学机制"词表（即概念）。随后用一个轻量分类头基于概念序列进行分类，并以到最近概念的距离天然给出可靠性度量。

Result: 在 17 个公开基准上，ConceptTime 的准确率可与 SOTA 黑盒模型媲美，优于所有可解释基线；在 Epilepsy 数据集上仅用 1% 标签仍保持约 98% 准确率。在 OOD 检测与删除保真度上，与 softmax、原型距离以及 SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++ 相比仍具竞争力，且无需任何标注、语言模型调用或领域专家。

Conclusion: 以预测惊奇度这一自监督信号闭合"分段—模式"的循环依赖，可在不依赖标注与专家知识的前提下，统一实现准确、可解释与可弃权，并具备良好的标签效率与 OOD/忠实性表现。

Abstract: Time-series classifiers deployed in risk-sensitive domains must be (1) accurate, (2) interpretable, and (3) able to abstain when evidence is weak. Existing methods do not deliver all 3 at once, and the obstacle is structural, rather than logistical. Meaningful temporal patterns cannot be defined without knowing segment boundaries, yet meaningful boundaries cannot be placed without knowing which patterns to expect. Existing approaches break this circularity by using fixed windows or expert-annotated vocabularies, thereby sacrificing at least one of the 3 properties. We introduce ConceptTime, which closes the loop through a single self-supervised signal. A frozen probabilistic forecaster predicts a distribution over the next observation at every timestep of the sequence. We call the mismatch between its prediction and the realized signal the predictive surprise. This signal both locates segment boundaries and characterizes each resulting segment through the forecaster. Segments are thus grounded by how they behaved relative to expectation, not by what they look like. These summaries cluster into a vocabulary of dynamical regimes that we call concepts. A lightweight head classifies from the concept sequence, and the distance to the nearest concept yields reliability by design. Across 17 public benchmarks, ConceptTime is competitive with state-of-the-art black-box accuracy, beats every interpretable baseline, and retains near-98% accuracy on Epilepsy with 1% of labels. It further remains competitive with softmax, prototype-distance, and SHAP/TimeX/TimeX++ on OOD detection and deletion faithfulness, without a single annotation, language model call, or domain expert. Code is available at: https://github.com/Sachithx/ConceptTime.git.

</details>


### [283] [ADIndex: Layer-Adaptive Sparse Attention Indexing via Group-Level Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12956)
*Jin-Fei Qi,Yiduo Li,Jiang-Xin Shi,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Hanlin Tang,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ADIndex，一种自适应、蒸馏驱动的索引器方法，消除 DeepSeek 稀疏注意力中索引器对整个因果前缀打分造成的延迟瓶颈，在几乎不损失精度的前提下实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA 通过 token 级选择降低注意力计算开销，但其索引器仍需为每个 query 对完整因果前缀打分，成为长上下文推理延迟的主要来源（长上下文下可占 prefill GPU 时间的 50% 以上）。

Method: ADIndex 包含三部分：(i) 逐层校准策略，基于观察到的显著层间异质性，自适应地利用跨 query 的检索重叠；(ii) 组级蒸馏方案，利用该异质性高效训练自适应的逐层组索引器，从单个 query 中捕获显著检索信号；(iii) 低延迟推理策略，通过 query 组内的 token bank 共享减少重复的全前缀扫描，同时保留 query 级细化。

Result: 在 DeepSeek-V3.2 与 GLM-5.1 上，LongBench、LongBench v2 与 RULER 任务性能接近无损；相比标准 DSA，在逐层复用配置下 256K 上下文时 prefill 与 decoding kernel 分别最高加速 6.22x 和 3.40x，128K 上下文端到端服务加速 1.54x。

Conclusion: 通过层间异质性驱动的自适应分组索引与蒸馏训练，可在保持稀疏注意力效果的前提下大幅降低索引开销，为长上下文推理提供实用的效率提升方案。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) reduces the computational cost of attention through token-level selection. However, its indexer still scores the entire causal prefix for each query, making indexing a dominant source of latency in long-context inference. Notably, the indexer alone can account for more than 50% of total prefill GPU time at long context lengths. To address this bottleneck, we propose ADIndex, an Adaptive and Distillation-driven Indexing method for token-level sparse attention. ADIndex integrates three key components: (i) a layer-wise calibration strategy that adaptively accommodates cross-query retrieval overlap, motivated by our observation of pronounced inter-layer heterogeneity; (ii) a group-level distillation scheme that leverages this heterogeneity to efficiently train adaptive layer-wise group indexers, capturing salient retrieval signals from individual queries; and (iii) a low-latency inference strategy that reduces repeated full-prefix scans through token bank sharing within query groups while retaining query-specific refinement. Experiments on DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5.1 demonstrate that ADIndex preserves near-lossless task performance on LongBench, LongBench v2, and RULER while delivering substantial efficiency gains over standard DSA, including prefill and decoding kernel speedups of up to 6.22x and 3.40x, respectively, at a context length of 256K across the layer-wise reuse configurations, as well as an end-to-end serving speedup of 1.54x at a context length of 128K.

</details>


### [284] [Exploration via Exploitation: The Blessing of Reward Diversity in Personalized Federated RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12964)
*Jiin Woo,Gauri Joshi,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将联邦强化学习中的奖励异质性从"共识障碍"重新诠释为"探索资源"，提出PF-UCBVI（解耦共享动力学与个性化目标，实现最优线性加速）与无需显式探索的PF-EFVI（纯贪婪算法，利用奖励多样性保证覆盖），并在充分奖励多样性下证明PF-EFVI达到对数级遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦RL在同质环境中效率高，但当智能体目标不一致（奖励异质）时协作受阻。作者反转这一视角，把奖励异质性视为促进探索的结构性资产，而非达成共识的障碍。

Method: 1) PF-UCBVI：将共享的环境动力学与个性化的奖励目标解耦，按个性化目标进行置信上界值迭代；2) PF-EFVI：去除显式探索奖励，采用纯贪婪策略，借助智能体间奖励差异自然实现状态-动作空间的覆盖。

Result: PF-UCBVI获得最优的线性加速（相对单智能体）；PF-EFVI在奖励多样性充分时，无需显式探索奖励即可达到对数级遗憾（logarithmic regret）。

Conclusion: 智能体间的目标分歧将探索负担从时间维度的复杂度转移到空间维度的多样性上，从而在无显式探索的情况下实现高效的集体学习。

Abstract: While federated reinforcement learning (RL) has traditionally guaranteed high efficiency in homogeneous environments, collaboration is often hindered when agents possess distinct goals. In this paper, we flip this paradigm, framing reward heterogeneity not as a hurdle to consensus, but as a structural asset that catalyzes exploration. We first propose Personalized Federated Upper Confidence Bound Value Iteration (PF-UCBVI), which achieves optimal linear speedup by decoupling shared dynamics from personalized objectives. To eliminate the risks of explicit exploration, we then introduce Personalized Federated Exploration-Free Value Iteration (PF-EFVI), a purely greedy algorithm that leverages reward diversity to ensure state-action coverage. We prove that PF-EFVI attains logarithmic regret without explicit exploration bonuses under sufficient reward diversity. Our results reveal that agent disagreement shifts the exploration burden from temporal complexity to spatial diversity, enabling efficient collective learning without explicit exploration.

</details>


### [285] [StatPrompt-LLM: A Lightweight Statistical Refinement for Foundation-Style Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12974)
*Aniket Sinha,Veronica W. Tsou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 StatPrompt-LLM：在 Time-LLM 基础上做轻量级统计增强，引入数据集级统计信息提示和零初始化自回归旁路，作为线性预测补充路径；在10%缩减前缀设定下显著优于 Time-LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大型预训练语言架构迁移到时间序列预测时，如何向模型有效暴露趋势、尺度、时间依赖等基础时序结构仍是开放问题，在观测数据有限（如短前缀）时尤为突出。

Method: 跨不同代际预测架构开展探索性实证研究；提出 StatPrompt-LLM——对 Time-LLM 的提示机制做统计精炼，加入数据集级统计信息提示（statistical prompt），并以零初始化自回归旁路（zero-initialised autoregressive bypass）作为并行线性预测通道；在 ETTh1、ETTh2、Weather 上以全前缀与缩减前缀两种配置评估，并进行消融与计算开销剖析。

Result: 10% 缩减前缀下样本内预测误差低于 Time-LLM，跨数据集未加权平均相对降幅为 MSE 40.2%、MAE 21.4%；全前缀结果对数据集依赖较强；掩蔽部分统计描述子组的消融显示性能变化较小且依赖数据集；计算剖析显示相对 Time-LLM 增加参数量与运行时间开销。

Conclusion: 数据集级统计提示与线性旁路在数据受限场景下能有效弥补 LLM 主干对时序结构的感知不足，但在数据充足时收益不稳定，且以额外参数和运行时开销为代价；统计描述子的增量贡献有限，未来需更精细的结构注入方式与效率优化。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting has recently seen a paradigm shift with the introduction of foundation-style models that adapt large pretrained language architectures to sequential data. However, effectively exposing fundamental temporal structures, such as trend, scale, and temporal dependence, to these models remains an open challenge, particularly when the available observations are limited. In this paper, we conduct an exploratory empirical study across different generations of forecasting architectures and propose StatPrompt-LLM, a lightweight statistical refinement of Time-LLM. StatPrompt-LLM augments the existing Time-LLM prompting mechanism with dataset-level statistical information and introduces a zero-initialised autoregressive bypass as a complementary linear forecasting pathway. We evaluate our framework on electricity and meteorological time-series benchmarks, including ETTh1, ETTh2, and Weather, under full-prefix and reduced-prefix configurations. Under the 10% reduced-prefix configuration, StatPrompt-LLM achieved lower in-sample prediction errors than Time-LLM, with unweighted average relative reductions across datasets of 40.2% in MSE and 21.4% in MAE. Full-prefix results were more dataset-dependent. Ablation analyses revealed relatively modest and dataset-dependent changes when selected additional statistical descriptor groups were masked within the combined architecture. Computational profiling indicated additional model parameters and runtime overhead relative to Time-LLM.

</details>


### [286] [MADSNet: A Multi-Scale Axial-Attentive Dense Spiking Network for Spike Camera Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12980)
*Sirui Li,Rui Zhao,Siqi Cai,Haizhou Li,Tiejun Huang,Yajing Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADSNet——一种多尺度轴向注意力密集脉冲网络，用分层脉冲编码器-解码器从脉冲相机信号重建连续强度图像，在SREDS上PSNR超过SSIR 4.3 dB、超过Spk2ImgNet 1.94 dB，同时延迟约低15倍、估计能耗约低至1/40。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲相机将动态场景编码为高时间分辨率的二值脉冲流，SNN可凭借稀疏、仅累加的计算高效处理；但现有SNN重建方法结构偏浅，重建保真度仍落后于ANN方法。作者希望证明经精心设计的SNN架构能同时兼顾重建质量与计算效率。

Method: MADSNet采用分层脉冲编码器-解码器架构，包含四个关键模块：多尺度脉冲stem提取早期特征；轴向脉冲注意力（axial spike attention）捕获长程依赖；密集脉冲瓶颈（dense spiking bottleneck）增强特征复用；脉冲到连续解码器将脉冲域表示重建为连续强度图像。在灰度与彩色脉冲重建任务上评估。

Result: 在SREDS数据集上，MADSNet较SSIR提升4.3 dB PSNR，较Spk2ImgNet提升1.94 dB；与Spk2ImgNet相比，延迟约降低15倍，估计能耗仅约为其1/40。

Conclusion: 精心设计的SNN架构可在保持计算效率（低延迟、低能耗）的同时提升脉冲相机图像重建质量，为SNN替代ANN进行高速视觉重建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Spike cameras encode dynamic scenes into high-temporal-resolution binary spike streams for efficient high-speed vision. Although spiking neural networks (SNNs) can process such signals with sparse and accumulate-only computation, existing SNN-based reconstruction methods are typically shallow and still lag behind ANN-based models in reconstruction fidelity. This paper proposes MADSNet, a Multi-scale Axial-attentive Dense Spiking Network for spike camera image reconstruction. MADSNet adopts a hierarchical spiking encoder--decoder, where a multi-scale spike stem extracts early features, axial spike attention captures long-range dependencies, a dense spiking bottleneck enhances feature reuse, and a spike-to-continuous decoder reconstructs continuous intensity images from spike-domain representations. Experiments on gray-scale and color spike reconstruction show that MADSNet outperforms SSIR by 4.3 dB PSNR and Spk2ImgNet by 1.94 dB on SREDS. Compared with Spk2ImgNet, it achieves about 15$\times$ lower latency and uses only about 1/40 of the estimated energy, demonstrating that carefully designed SNN architectures can improve reconstruction quality while preserving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [287] [Third Eye: Beyond Immediate Gains in Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12988)
*Sujato Dutta,Padala Indira Sneha,Kypu Mahitha,Shashank Vanga,Neeraj Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文"Third Eye"提出将自我更新的"即时性能"与其后再次原生更新的结果分开评估，发现在5个3–8B骨干模型上，经选出的自我更新在后续更新后效用进一步下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常以自我更新后的即时性能衡量其价值，但模型还会继续更新；需回答"有用的自我更新在再次更新后是否仍然有用"，并揭示即时指标可能被选择效应掩盖。

Method: 在数学与代码任务上评估五个3–8B骨干；共64次独立运行，每次运行3个候选、每候选3次延续更新；选择集与最终评分集互不重叠（独立评分），并设无更新基线。以平衡的Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B对比为核心，考察即时选中效用与延续后效用差；辅以候选平均即时效用分析、SmolLM3-3B与Llama-3.1-8B扩展、以及匹配配方与"接收者–供体"对照实验。

Result: 即时选中效用接近零，延续使其再降1.78个百分点（同时95%区间[0.68, 2.88]）；候选平均的即时效用已为负，说明选择过程部分解释了首步"看似中性"的表现。延续同样降低SmolLM3-3B与Llama-3.1-8B的效用，Qwen3-8B结论不定。目标损失与分布外损失主导了保留性变化；匹配配方与接收者–供体对照未能识别可靠的缓解手段或单一机制。

Conclusion: 应将持续性自我更新作为"适应转移"来评估，采用独立评分与无更新基线，而不能把即时性能当作其全部价值。

Abstract: Does a useful self-update remain useful after the model updates again? Third Eye separates immediate performance from the outcome of a further native update. We evaluate five 3-8B backbones on math and code: 64 fresh runs, three candidates per run, and three continuations per candidate. Selection and final scoring use disjoint items. In the balanced Qwen3-4B/Llama-3.2-3B comparison, immediate-selected utility is near zero, but continuation reduces it by 1.78 percentage points (simultaneous 95% interval [0.68, 2.88]). Candidate-averaged immediate utility is already negative: selection partly accounts for the apparently neutral first step. Continuation also lowers utility for SmolLM3-3B and Llama-3.1-8B; Qwen3-8B remains inconclusive. Target and out-of-distribution losses dominate retention changes. Matched recipe and recipient-donor controls identify neither a reliable mitigation nor a single mechanism. The results support evaluating persistent self-updates as adaptation transitions, with independent scoring and a no-update baseline, rather than interpreting immediate performance as their full value.

</details>


### [288] [Multimodal Graph Learning: A Survey of Models, Benchmarks, and Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12990)
*Rui Xue,Tianfu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述以算法为中心并以评测为落脚点，系统梳理多模态属性图（MAG）学习：提出统一消息传递表述并按五个融合层级（特征、传播、拓扑、学习、基础模型）组织方法，并系统整理数据集、基准与评测协议，进而展望与AI智能体的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAG（节点携带文本、图像等模态、边编码实体关系）广泛存在于电商、社交媒体、内容平台与创意应用中，但方法沿"图中心"与"基础模型中心"两条路线各自发展，基准的数据集与评测协议也各不相同，缺乏统一的建模视角与可比较的评测框架，因此需要一篇算法中心、评测导向的系统综述。

Method: (1) 建模：给出统一的消息传递公式，将方法划分为五个整合层级——特征级、传播级、拓扑级、学习级、基础模型级，覆盖判别式与图条件生成式模型；在该框架下把代表性方法推导为特例，比较其假设、表达力与计算开销，并指出跨模态敏感性是区分相似架构的关键属性。(2) 数据集与评测：回顾近期基准，汇总数据集与指标，定义模型无关的描述量（标签同质性、平均度、聚类系数、边的来源/溯源），并将每种评测协议映射到其所用基线。(3) 前沿：连接多模态图学习与AI智能体，涵盖智能体所感知与记忆的多模态图以及"图工程"（将智能体工作流组织为显式图）。

Result: 形成统一的五层级分类体系与可推导的特例对照；建立跨基准的数据集/指标汇总与模型无关描述量体系，实现评测协议与基线的对应；明确跨模态敏感性作为架构区分属性；并给出智能体场景下的研究方向与开放问题（如强多模态基础模型存在时图结构何时有效）。

Conclusion: 论文以统一公式与五级融合分类统一了MAG学习的方法版图，并以模型无关描述量和协议—基线映射规范了评测；同时把MAG扩展到智能体的感知/记忆与图工程，提出开放问题与社区可推进的具体工作，尤其关注在多模态基础模型强大背景下图结构的价值边界。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), in which nodes carry text, images, and other modalities while edges encode relations among entities, underlie e-commerce, social media, content platforms, and creative applications. Learning on MAGs has developed along two complementary lines: graph-centric methods redesign how modalities are fused, propagated, and connected within message passing, while foundation-model-centric methods pretrain transferable multimodal graph encoders or bring graph context into multimodal large language models for prediction and generation. In parallel, several benchmarks have appeared with different datasets and evaluation protocols. This survey takes an algorithm-centric and evaluation-grounded view of the field and makes three contributions. Modeling: we introduce a unified message-passing formulation and organize methods into five integration levels (feature, propagation, topology, learning, and foundation model), covering discriminative and graph-conditioned generative models; within this formulation we derive representative methods as special cases, compare their assumptions, expressiveness, and cost, and identify cross-modal sensitivity as a property that separates otherwise similar architectures. Datasets and evaluation: we review recent benchmarks, summarize their datasets and metrics, define model-independent descriptors (label homophily, average degree, clustering, and edge provenance), and map each evaluation protocol to the baselines it uses. Frontiers: we connect multimodal graph learning to AI agents, covering the multimodal graphs that agents perceive and remember and graph engineering, the practice of organizing agent workflows as explicit graphs; we then discuss open problems, concrete efforts for the community, and research directions, including when graph structure helps in the presence of strong multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [289] [Linear Probes to Bayesian Manifolds: Geometry of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12998)
*Anshul Kumar,Neha Prasad,Manoj Kumar,Barnabás Póczos,Deeksha Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出四阶段贝叶斯流程，将线性探针结论拆分为可解码性、形状、归属与因果使用四项独立断言，并在 GPT-J 6B、Llama 3.1 8B、Pythia 6.9B 的两位数加减乘任务上验证：概念几乎均可解码但方向高度重叠；高置信形状为 ribbon 或 torus，其中 81% 为继承而非专有；形状干预约保留全探针效应的 61%，且仅需≤5 维。作者主张以四项断言报告探针结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针常被用于从 LLM 激活中提取 helix、circle 等几何结构，并据此推断模型以该结构进行计算。但探针精度混杂了四种不同断言，单独精度无法确立"模型使用该结构"的结论，因此需要将各断言分离检验。

Method: 提出四阶段贝叶斯流程：Stage 2 在预设阈值下比较候选形状并择优；Stage 3 预先指定并剔除一组相关概念，判别形状为专有（owned）还是继承（inherited）；Stage 4 对形状施加干预，检验输出是否依赖该形状。应用于三个模型的两位数加法与乘法任务。

Result: 几乎全部概念可解码，但解码方向在概念间显著重叠。所有强支持比较均选出 ribbon（半径随数值变化的 helix）或 torus。在 1,330 个具备归属判定的单元中，19% 为专有形状、81% 为继承形状；两类形状对输出的影响频率相近。形状干预最多用 5 维即保留约 61% 的全探针干预效应，而全探针维度中位数为 9。

Conclusion: 单一探针精度高估了模型实际计算所依赖的结构；探针结果应以四项断言的判定形式报告，而非单一准确率。

Abstract: Linear probes are a standard method to study geometric structures, such as helices and circles, from the activations of large language models (LLMs), and such structure is often taken as evidence that the model computes with it. Probe accuracy alone cannot establish this, because it conflates four separate claims. These are (1) that the concept is decodable from the activations, (2) that they form a specific shape, (3) that the shape belongs to the probed concept rather than to a related concept the model also represents, and (4) that the output of the model depends on the shape. We call these claims decodability, shape, ownership, and causal use, and we test them separately with a four-stage Bayesian pipeline. Stage 2 compares candidate shapes and selects the best one only when its evidence passes a predefined threshold. Stage 3 removes a set of related concepts, specified in advance, and tests whether the shape is owned or inherited. Stage 4 intervenes on the shape to test whether the output of the model depends on it. We apply the pipeline to two-digit addition and multiplication in GPT-J 6B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Pythia 6.9B. Nearly every concept is decodable, but the decoded directions overlap substantially across concepts. Every strongly supported comparison selects a ribbon, which is a helix whose radius changes with the value, or a torus. Of 1,330 cells with an ownership verdict, 19% of shapes are owned and 81% are inherited. Owned and inherited shapes move the output about equally often. Intervening on the shape retains approximately 61% of the effect of intervening on the full probe while using at most five dimensions, against a median of nine for the probe. Our results indicate that a probe verdict alone overstates what a model computes with, and we propose that probe results be reported as verdicts on all four claims rather than as a single accuracy.

</details>


### [290] [One Table, Many Queries: Benchmarking Generalist Tabular Models Across Generation, Imputation, and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13001)
*Yuanrui Wang,Kaifang Li,Xuanyue Li,Ziyun Li,Yan Lu,Xingxuan Zhang,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出One Table Benchmark（OTB），在56个数据集、46种方法、20个能力轴上统一评估表格数据的生成、插补与预测，发现三者共享部分条件信息但保真度排序不同，含自身目标函数的联合训练收益最大，通用模型在效用上可超越专家组合，而保真度仍偏向专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成、插补与预测实际刻画同一表格分布的不同侧面，却被分别评估，导致三者共享哪些能力、训练目标如何相互影响、单一模型能否同时胜任三者均不清楚，缺少对齐数据划分与明确任务契约的统一基准。

Method: 构建One Table Benchmark：56个数据集、46种已注册方法、20个能力轴，统一数据划分并明确任务契约。具体包括：在对齐支持集上比较任务排名；在5个数据集、3个随机种子上，以全部7种非空目标组合训练5个骨干模型；将固定模型画像与专家组合（specialist portfolio）对比；并结合冻结的表格基础模型进行评估。

Result: 排名比较显示插补与预测通过数值恢复和依赖关系保持相联系，生成通过预测效用与二者相连，而分布保真度的排序不同甚至相反。目标干预中，纳入接收任务自身目标带来的汇总增益最大；生成具有最广的正向辅助效应覆盖，预测则有望提升插补与合成数据效用且随骨干变化。交叉叠加效应表明目标收益不可简单相加。固定模型比较显示效用—保真度权衡：部分通用模型在效用上超过专家组合，保真度则仍倾向专用化。冻结基础模型在预测与数值点插补上表现强，合成数据效用亦有潜力。

Conclusion: 三项任务共享有用的条件信息，但保真度与效用存在结构性权衡；结论支持发展统一表格模型，在共享条件信息的同时改进类别变量重建与忠实的联合采样。

Abstract: Generation, imputation, and prediction query different aspects of the same tabular distribution, but are usually evaluated separately. This leaves unclear which capabilities they share, how their training objectives interact, and whether one model can perform all three well. We introduce the One Table Benchmark (\ours{}), spanning 56 datasets, 46 registered methods, and 20 capability axes under aligned data splits and explicit task contracts. We compare task ranks on aligned support, train five backbones under all seven non-empty objective combinations on five datasets with three seeds, and contrast fixed-model profiles with specialist portfolios. Rank comparisons connect imputation and prediction through numerical recovery and dependence preservation, and link generation to both through predictive utility; distributional fidelity follows different, sometimes opposing, rankings. Objective interventions show the largest pooled gains from including the recipient's own objective. Generation has the broadest coverage of positive pooled auxiliary effects, while prediction shows potential to improve imputation and synthetic-data utility, with responses that vary across backbones. Cross-addition effects reveal that objective benefits need not add and depend on the objectives already present. Fixed-model comparisons expose corresponding utility--fidelity trade-offs: some generalists surpass specialist portfolios on utility, while fidelity continues to favor specialization. Frozen foundation models show strong prediction and numerical point imputation, together with promising synthetic-data utility, highlighting their potential across all three tasks. These findings motivate unified tabular models that share useful conditional information while improving categorical reconstruction and faithful joint sampling.

</details>


### [291] [Branch-and-Commit for Frozen RL Agents: When and Where to Spend Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13003)
*Sahar Admoni,Assaf Hallak,Yftah Ziser,Ofra Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究"分支-提交"(branch-and-commit)这一部署期推理技巧：从当前可复制的状态采样多条后续轨迹、评估后沿选中分支端点继续。研究表明其收益取决于"离散度"(最佳候选回报与平均候选回报之差)，且主要来自在同一动作的不同随机实现间择优，而非选出更好的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略常在部署后无法再训练或修改，但数字环境的状态可复制、可恢复，这提供了一种额外手段：从当前状态采样多条延续、评估并选定一条分支的终点继续执行。作者希望厘清该做法何时能提升回报、提升来自何处，以及计算预算应如何分配，从而为实际部署提供依据。

Method: 在4个环境、2个冻结策略、5种延续验证器以及一种无模型选择规则构成的实验网格上系统评估branch-and-commit；核心分析变量为候选回报的"离散度"(best minus mean candidate return)，并检验不同类型的验证器质量、以及基于预测离散度的"门控"分配机制(仅在可能产生有用离散度的状态分支)。

Result: 收益取决于离散度：低离散度场景下分支无增益；在主要实验设置Craftax-Medium中回报提升12.8%。增益主体并非来自"选到更好的动作"，而是来自"在同一动作的不同随机实现之间择优"。多种能力较强的验证器取得相近的episode回报，说明在本文的预算水平下验证器质量并非主要瓶颈。基于预测离散度进行门控，在留出种子上保留了均匀分支提升量的69%，而分支调用次数仅为后者的48%。

Conclusion: branch-and-commit是一种条件性有效的部署期改进手段，其适用性由候选回报离散度决定；计算应优先投向可能产生有用离散度的状态。该结果同时厘清了这类方法收益的实质内涵，并为预算受限下的分支调度提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents are often deployed with policies that cannot be retrained or modified. In digital environments whose states can be copied and resumed, an operator has another option: sample several continuations from the current state, evaluate them, and continue from the endpoint of the selected branch. We call this procedure branch-and-commit. We study when it improves return, what produces the improvement, and where its computational budget should be spent. Across four environments, two frozen policies, five continuation verifiers, and a model-free selection rule, we find that the benefit of branching depends on dispersion: the difference between the best candidate return and the mean candidate return. Branching provides no benefit in low-dispersion settings, while increasing return by 12.8% in our main Craftax-Medium setting. Most of this gain comes not from selecting better actions, but from selecting among different stochastic realizations of the same action. Several competent verifiers achieve similar episode returns, suggesting that verifier quality is not the main constraint at our operating budget. Instead, computation is better allocated to states where useful dispersion is likely. On held-out seeds, gating branching using predicted dispersion retains 69% of the improvement from uniform branching while using 48% as many branching calls. These results characterize when branch-and-commit is useful and clarify what its gains represent.

</details>


### [292] [The Shape of a Program: Path Signatures for Trace-to-Program Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13005)
*Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将执行轨迹视为高维粗糙路径并用路径签名变换编码，提出log-signature轨迹编码器，在Karel、List、RASP三个程序归纳域上优于LSTM、Transformer与Fourier基线，且可利用trace-to-program管线的收缩性实现无oracle的冗余监督自举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从执行轨迹合成程序是算法可解释性与复杂系统逆向工程的核心难题；既有序列编码器（LSTM、Transformer、Fourier）未利用轨迹的路径结构性质，且在数据稀缺时表现不佳，同时缺乏可解释的隐空间几何。

Method: 采用粗糙路径理论视角，把执行轨迹建模为高维粗糙路径，并用随机分析中的路径签名变换（path signature）及log-signature进行编码——签名的期望可证明地刻画程序所诱导轨迹的分布；基于此构建log-signature轨迹编码器，并利用trace-to-program管线的天然收缩性（常输出比真值更短的有效程序）设计无外部oracle的自举（bootstrapping）流程以暴露真值监督中的冗余。

Result: 在Karel、List、RASP三个域上几乎全部设置中优于LSTM、Transformer与Fourier基线，数据稀缺时优势最大；在注入冗余的数据集上成功演示了自举流程可发现真值监督冗余；log-signature编码器诱导的潜空间几何更具可解释性，每个状态的最邻近点均位于其自身执行轨迹上。

Conclusion: 路径签名/log-signature为执行轨迹提供了兼顾表达力与可解释性的编码方式，在程序归纳中提升性能与数据效率，并带来可解释的潜几何及无需oracle的监督冗余检测能力。

Abstract: Synthesizing programs from execution traces is a fundamental challenge in algorithmic interpretability and the reverse engineering of complex systems. We bring a rough-path-theoretic perspective to program induction, treating execution traces as high-dimensional rough paths and encoding them with the path signature transform, a tool from stochastic analysis whose expectation provably characterizes the distribution of traces a program induces. On three program-induction domains, Karel, List, and RASP, our log-signature trace encoder outperforms LSTM, Transformer, and Fourier baselines in almost every setting, with its largest margins when training data is scarce. We further observe that the trace-to-program pipeline is naturally contractive: it often produces valid programs that are shorter than the ground truth. We turn this into a bootstrapping procedure that exposes redundancy in the ground-truth supervision without any external oracle, which we demonstrate on redundancy-injected versions of our datasets. Finally, the log-signature encoder induces a markedly more interpretable latent geometry than the baselines, in which the nearest neighbors of every state lie on its own execution trace.

</details>


### [293] [FaST: Factored Synthesis of Transitions for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13013)
*Daniel Campa,Spandan Das,Srinjoy Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FaST 将扩散生成器的转移元组按链式法则分解为 state、action、dynamics、reward、terminal 五个阶段分别建模，在十二个 D4RL 任务上与 SynthER 效果相当但生成速度快 90 倍以上，并可通过按状态重采样动作实现生成期的"择优合成"，在 medium/medium-replay 设置中 best-of-10 平均提升 +8.9 D4RL 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的合成数据增广把转移元组 (s, a, s', r, terminal) 当作整体建模，无法单独重抽某个因子，因而缺乏可控性与可查询性，同时生成开销大。作者希望既能高效生成转移，又能利用条件结构在生成阶段主动合成更优行为。

Method: 提出 FaST（Factored Synthesis of Transitions）：用链式法则 p(s,a,s',r,d)=p(s)p(a|s)p(s'|s,a)p(r|s,a,s')p(d|s,a,s') 将转移分解为五个阶段，每个阶段用与其统计性质匹配的估计器（如动作给定状态的条件分布可单独查询）。对生成的每个状态采样 N 个候选动作，用同一数据上拟合的价值函数选出优势最高者，实现"生成时择优"而非事后过滤。在 TD3+BC 与 IQL 上、12 个 D4RL locomotion 与 maze 基准上评测。

Result: 1) 与 SynthER 扩散基线性能匹配，生成速度提升 90 倍以上；2) best-of-10 合成在 12 个 medium / medium-replay 设置中的 11 个上超过真实数据训练，平均 +8.9 D4RL 点，最高 +28.4；3) 与 advantage-filtered SynthER 竞争力相当，但生成成本仅为其一小部分；4) 在 expert 混合数据上两种机制退化方式一致。

Conclusion: 因子化分解在几乎不损失保真度的前提下大幅降低生成成本，并解锁了按状态重查询动作的条件接口，使增广从"事后筛选"转为"生成期优化"。但 expert 混合数据上两种 targeting 机制同步失效，说明瓶颈在于优势估计本身而非某一种机制。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation promises to scale offline reinforcement learning beyond fixed logged datasets, but leading diffusion-based generators model the transition tuple (state, action, next state, reward, terminal) as a monolith: no single factor can be natively redrawn in isolation. We present FaST (Factored Synthesis of Transitions), which factors transitions via the chain rule into state, action, dynamics, reward, and terminal stages, fitting each with an estimator matched to its statistical properties. Across twelve D4RL locomotion and maze benchmarks, TD3+BC and IQL agents trained on FaST transitions match the SynthER diffusion baseline while generating transitions over 90x faster. The explicit action-given-state conditional can also be re-queried for a fixed state, enabling targeted action synthesis: drawing N candidate actions per generated state and keeping the highest-advantage one under a value function fit to the same data, thereby synthesizing improved behavior during generation rather than filtering for it post-hoc. Best-of-10 synthesis beats training on real data on 11 of 12 medium and medium-replay settings (+8.9 D4RL points on average, up to +28.4), and is competitive with advantage-filtered SynthER at a fraction of its generation cost. On expert mixtures both mechanisms degrade identically, tracing the failure to the advantage estimate itself rather than to either targeting mechanism.

</details>


### [294] [Permutation-Based Pre-trained Model Merging via Reciprocal Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13016)
*Akira Ito,Daiki Chijiwa,Atsutoshi Kumagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过搜索使两模型权重与对方表示"互正交(RO)"的置换，在较小宽度下也能实现线性模式连通性(LMC)，并支持跨数据分布的模型融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明足够宽的独立训练网络经softmax温度校准后可无需置换即呈现LMC，其机制为逐层指数加权连通(LEWC)；但此前观察到的LEWC需要大幅加宽（MLP每隐层8192单元、VGG-11需16倍宽度），这些宽度是否必要尚不清楚。

Method: 将置换搜索建模为独立的逐层二次分配问题(QAP)，对其实数松弛用Frank–Wolfe近似求解，目标是鼓励互正交性(RO)，从而抑制一个模型的权重与另一模型表示的干扰，使双方计算在合并网络中并存。

Result: 实验表明该方法在更小的宽度扩张下即可促成LMC；且能融合在不同数据分布上训练的模型，而最大化权重相似度的weight matching在此情形下表现不佳。合并模型的预测近似于原模型logit集成的预测。

Conclusion: LEWC并不需要极端加宽，通过RO导向的置换搜索可在较小宽度下获得带置换的LMC，并拓展了模型融合到异构数据分布场景。

Abstract: Ito et al. (2026) showed that sufficiently wide, independently trained neural networks can exhibit linear mode connectivity (LMC) with softmax temperature calibration, even without permutation. LMC means that a low-loss linear path connects the models' parameters. This behavior is explained by layerwise exponentially weighted connectivity (LEWC), whereby each merged layer outputs an exponentially weighted sum of the original models' corresponding representations, yielding ensemble-like predictions. However, prior work observed LEWC with substantial widening, such as 8192 units per hidden layer for MLPs and $16\times$ width for VGG-11, raising the question of whether such widths are necessary. We therefore search for permutations that encourage reciprocal orthogonality (RO), which suppresses interference between each model's weights and the other model's representations. This allows both models' computations to coexist in the merged network, promoting LEWC at smaller widths. We formulate the search as independent layerwise quadratic assignment problems and approximately solve their continuous relaxations using Frank--Wolfe. Experiments show that our method facilitates LMC with less width expansion. It also merges models trained on different data distributions, where weight matching, which maximizes weight similarity through permutations, struggles. The merged predictions approximate those of a logit ensemble of the original models.

</details>


### [295] [Explanation-Aware Fairness Beyond Output Parity: Joint Output-Attribution Regularization for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13052)
*Gökhan Özbulak,Oscar Jimenez-del-Toro,Lilian Berton,André Anjos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 输出层公平性无法约束不同人口群体在表示空间中是否依赖相似的预测贡献。作者证明：对"非线性编码器+线性头"，输出分数差异可分解为各特征归因差异的符号和（允许小预测差距通过"抵消效应"产生），并检验"归因差异惩罚"能否补充输出层均衡几率公平代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性研究多聚焦输出层指标（如均衡几率），但这些指标不能保证不同子群在表示空间中使用相似的特征贡献；存在通过特征贡献相互抵消而"掩盖"归因差异的隐患，因此需要一种解释感知的补充约束。

Method: 理论部分：对非线性编码器+线性头的模型，推导输出得分差异等于子群间特征归因差异的带符号求和，并刻画"抵消效应"（cancellation effect）。实验部分：在HAM10000与Harvard-GF数据集上，针对性别与种族公平，训练960个DINOv2-LoRA模型；在输出层均衡几率公平替代损失基础上加入归因差异惩罚，比较超体积（hypervolume）效用-公平权衡，并报告配对95%置信区间。

Result: 归因增强在6种设置下均提升超体积权衡表现，相对增益1.49%–10.57%（配对95% CI）；在预设效用约束下，增强模型族的均衡几率差异降低9.0%–38.7%，归因差异与抵消比同时下降，且预测效用基本保持。

Conclusion: 归因公平性可作为输出层公平性的一种互补的、解释感知的约束；输出层指标相等并不蕴含归因一致，二者结合能更有效地改善效用-公平权衡。

Abstract: Output-level fairness does not constrain whether demographic groups rely on similar predictive contributions in representation space. For a nonlinear encoder with a linear head, we show that outcome-conditioned output score disparity equals the signed sum of feature attribution differences across subgroups by allowing small prediction gaps to arise through cancellation effect. We test whether an attribution disparity penalty complements output-level equalized odds based fairness surrogates. Across 960 DINOv2-LoRA models on HAM10000 and Harvard-GF datasets for gender and race fairness, attribution augmentation increases utility-fairness trade-off performance for hypervolume in six different settings with paired 95% confidence intervals and relative gains of 1.49-10.57%. Under a predefined utility constraint, augmented model families also achieve 9.0-38.7% lower equalized odds difference, lower attribution disparity, and reduced cancellation ratio while preserving predictive utility. These results support attribution fairness as a complementary explanation-aware constraint on output fairness.

</details>


### [296] [Continuous-Time Set-Transformers for Asynchronous WiFi-IMU Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13057)
*Mohamed Bachar,Ilyass Abouelaziz,Yuehua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种连续时间Transformer，将WiFi RSSI与惯性观测表示为无序、带时间戳的token集合，用单一注意力块联合融合跨模态与跨时间信息，无需重采样和模态专用分支，并在随机掩码训练下实现缺失/陈旧观测的鲁棒室内定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内定位常融合WiFi RSSI指纹与惯性测量，但两类传感器异步、采样率不同，观测在时间上鲜有对齐且可能缺失或陈旧。现有方法多将数据重采样到统一时钟并为每个模态设置独立分支，导致重采样误差与分支割裂，难以自然处理缺失和不新鲜数据。

Method: 构造连续时间Transformer：把观测表示为带时间戳token的无序集合，每个token编码测量值、模态标识及自采集以来的经过时间；用单个注意力块在模态与时间维度联合融合，保持token顺序不变性，从而避免重采样与模态专用分支。训练时随机掩蔽整模态与单个观测，使模型可在缺失或过期输入下工作。模态专用编码器分别在各自基准上验证，融合模型在仿真与真实数据集（含跨会话/未见日期采集）上评估。

Result: 在跨会话漂移下优于开放WiFi指纹基线，与学习式融合及航位推算基线相比具竞争力；随观测缺失或陈旧程度增加，性能平缓退化（graceful degradation）。

Conclusion: 单一连续时间Transformer可作为异步传感器室内定位的实用统一框架，兼顾多模态异步融合、缺失鲁棒性与跨会话泛化。

Abstract: Indoor localization commonly combines WiFi received signal strength indicator (RSSI) fingerprints with inertial measurements. However, these sensor streams are sampled asynchronously at different rates; therefore, observations rarely align in time and may be missing or stale. Most existing methods resample the data onto a shared clock and process each modality using a separate branch. We instead present a continuous-time transformer that represents observations as an unordered set of timestamped tokens. Each token encodes its measurement, modality, and elapsed time since acquisition. A single attention block jointly fuses information across modalities and time, avoiding both resampling and modality-specific branches while remaining invariant to token order. During training, we randomly mask entire modalities and individual observations, enabling the model to operate under missing or outdated inputs. We validate the modality-specific encoders on their respective benchmarks and evaluate the fused model on both simulated and real datasets, including a crosssession dataset collected on unseen days. The proposed approach improves upon an open WiFi fingerprinting baseline under cross-session drift, remains competitive with learned fusion and dead-reckoning baselines, and degrades gracefully as observations become missing or stale. These results suggest that a single continuoustime transformer provides a practical framework for indoor localization from asynchronous sensors.

</details>


### [297] [INTENT: Out-of-Sample, Out-of-Time, and Cold-Start Evaluation on User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13069)
*Mattias Kvist,Erik Lidman Hillbom,Edoardo de Cal,Davis Siemens,Adam Fredriksson,Rickard Brüel-Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出公开基准 INTENT，用"用户/时段/目标物品是否在训练中出现"三个维度划出七个评估格子，审计59个公开数据集族后保留5个，按统一规则各自设定时间切点，并给出参考模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行等行业在客户事件序列上训练基础模型并做前向预测，测试对象常是训练中未见的客户与商户；但各家评测都在私有数据上进行、彼此不可比。现有公开序列基准很少真正测试模型未见过的时段，推荐研究通常只留出每个用户的最后若干事件。

Method: 构建公开基准 INTENT（INTEraction, Novelty, and Transfer evaluation for next-action prediction）：以用户、时段、目标物品是否出现在训练集中定义七个评估单元；对59个公开数据集族做审计，保留5个（最常见缺陷是缺少可排序事件的日历时间戳）；因不存在同时落在5个数据集时间跨度内的日期，每个数据集按事先固定的一条规则在本数据集切点处划分；汇总后每个单元至少含111,769名用户；并给出参考模型结果。

Result: 参考结果中"哪个模型最好"取决于所在单元：Transformer 在切点后的已见物品上领先，但在新物品上得分为零；嵌入物品元数据的变体主要在 MicroLens 上有帮助；重复用户最后一个物品的参数无关对照基线在5个数据集中的4个上于新物品单元领先。该对照还表明，在3个数据集上，切点后大部分性能损失源于行为变化而非模型本身。

Conclusion: 评测应显式区分交互、新颖性与迁移等条件，单一平均指标会掩盖模型差异；缺乏时间戳的数据集不适用于此类时间外推评测；流水线、切点规则与各单元用户数均公开，便于可复现比较。

Abstract: Banks now train foundation models on ordered sequences of customer events such as transactions, app sessions, and authorizations. Deployed, such a model predicts forward in time, often for customers and merchants absent from training, yet each is evaluated on private data, so no two can be compared. Public benchmarks for such sequences rarely test a period the model has not seen; recommendation studies usually hold out each user's last events instead. We present INTENT (INTEraction, Novelty, and Transfer evaluation for next-action prediction), a public benchmark that scores a model in seven cells defined by whether the user, the period, and the target item appeared in training. An audit of 59 public dataset families retains five datasets; the most common defect is the lack of a calendar timestamp that orders events. No date lies within the time span of all five datasets, so each is split at its own cutoff, chosen by one rule fixed before any model is evaluated. Summed over the datasets, every cell holds at least 111,769 users. In reference results the cell determines the best model: a transformer leads on seen items after the cutoff but scores zero on new items, a variant that embeds item metadata helps mainly on MicroLens, and repeating the user's last item leads on new items in four of five datasets. This parameter-free control also shows that on three datasets most of the score lost after the cutoff reflects changed behavior rather than the model. The pipeline, the cutoff rule, and the per-cell user counts are publicly available.

</details>


### [298] [IC-GNN: Modeling Individualized Brain Community Organization for Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13070)
*Yang Zhang,Xiao Zhou,Jonathan Warrell,Avram Holmes,Xuan Zhang,Mark Gerstein,for the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IC-GNN，一种以脑图谱为锚点的模型，直接在图谱定义的脑区上构建个体化、多尺度离散功能社区，兼顾个体化建模与跨被试比较，在ABIDE、ADNI和UK Biobank共3万余人上优于15个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑网络具有模块化组织，社区结构在个体与疾病间差异显著，但常用的群体水平预定义脑图谱忽略了这种异质性；个体化功能分区虽能刻画被试特异的社区边界变异，却需较长扫描时间才能达到细粒度分辨率，且难以进行跨被试比较。因此需要在个体化与可比性之间取得平衡。

Method: IC-GNN以脑图谱为锚点，从静息态fMRI数据中推导离散的、个体化的多尺度功能社区；对社区引导的连接进行细化，并将共享的区域权重根据每个被试的社区上下文进行自适应调整，从而实现个体化建模与群体水平比较的统一。

Result: 在来自ABIDE（自闭症谱系障碍）、ADNI（阿尔茨海默病）和UK Biobank（抑郁症）的3万余名被试上，IC-GNN的性能优于15种基线方法；个体化社区在单次扫描内和跨session间可复现，且自身具有预测能力；模型识别出的疾病相关社区组织偏移与既有文献一致。

Conclusion: IC-GNN为个体化社区结构建模提供了可行途径，有望用于疾病预测与生物标志物发现。

Abstract: Brain networks exhibit modular organization, where community structures vary substantially across individuals and diseases. Understanding these individual differences is crucial for advancing both basic neuroscience research and precision medicine. However, widely used predefined group-level brain atlases often overlook this heterogeneity. While individualized functional parcellations capture subject-specific variability in community boundaries, they often require lengthy scan times to achieve fine-grained resolution and complicate cross-subject comparisons. To address this trade-off and investigate how individualized community organization captures disease-associated changes across individuals, we propose IC-GNN, an atlas-anchored model that forms distinct, individualized functional communities directly over atlas-defined brain regions. IC-GNN derives discrete, individualized multiscale communities from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, refines community-guided connections, and adapts shared regional weights to each subject's community context to enable both individualized modeling and group-level comparisons. Across more than 30,000 participants from ABIDE (autism spectrum disorder), ADNI (Alzheimer's disease), and the UK Biobank (depression), IC-GNN achieves the highest performance compared with 15 baseline methods. We further demonstrate that these individualized communities are reproducible within scans and across sessions, and carry predictive power on their own. Furthermore, IC-GNN identifies disease-related shifts in community organization that align with established literature, highlighting a promising approach for modeling individualized community structure for disease prediction and biomarker discovery.

</details>


### [299] [One Temperature to Rule Them All?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13077)
*Aviv Orly,Ori Shem-Ur,Yaron Oz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统分析pass@K场景下的温度多样性：证明多温度分配相对单温度（按K调优）收益通常有限，并用"dulling effects"解释其稳健性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明逐样本变化温度可提升多次采样推理，但实践中仍普遍使用固定温度。作者希望在标准pass@K设定下厘清这一矛盾：何时温度多样性真正有用，何时单一调优温度已足够。

Method: 在多个模型与数据集上统计逐题成功概率随采样温度变化的profile，归纳其一致性质（如越难的问题越难共享高效温度，且该趋势仅随难度对数增长）；推导可高效评估理论最优多温度分配方案的算法，并与单温度基线对比。

Result: 理论最优多温度方案确实优于单温度基线，但当温度作为采样预算K的函数被调优时，增益通常较为温和；该现象由所称"dulling effects"的数学机制导致，并保证了温度选择在不同问题分布间的稳健性。

Conclusion: 多温度采样的实用价值有限且条件性强；结果明确了多温度何时有用、以及温度应如何按预算K理想选取，为实践中固定温度策略提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Inference-time sampling is a central mechanism for improving large language models, with temperature controlling the tradeoff between reliability and diversity. While recent work suggests that varying temperature across samples can improve multi-sample inference, simple fixed-temperature schemes remain the standard in practice. We investigate this discrepancy in the canonical pass@K setting, asking when temperature diversity is useful, and when a single tuned temperature suffices. To this end, we analyze per-question success probabilities across multiple models and datasets, tracking how they vary with sampling temperature. We identify several consistent properties of these profiles, including that harder questions are less likely to share efficient sampling temperatures, although this trend is only logarithmic in hardness. We then derive an efficient method for evaluating the theoretically optimal multi-temperature allocation scheme, and show that although such a scheme improves performance, its gains over single-temperature baselines are typically modest when temperature is tuned as a function of the sampling budget K. We explain this phenomenon through mathematical mechanisms that we call dulling effects, and show how they ensure the robustness of temperature choices across different question distributions. Our results clarify when multi-temperature sampling is useful, and how temperatures should ideally be chosen.

</details>


### [300] [Which Successes Make Good References? Action-Sequence Alignment for Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13135)
*Mufei Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Daniel Edmiston,Pan Li,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将多轮工具使用智能体的on-policy蒸馏（OPD）中"成功参考轨迹选择"建模为自由目标最优传输问题，并用动作序列的LCS作为轨迹兼容性的可观测代理，实验证明LCS是唯一在ALFWorld与τ³-bench airline域上均优于无参考OPD的选择器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD通过在学生自身轨迹上监督来缓解训练-推理分布不匹配；对多轮工具使用智能体，若将教师条件化于一条经过验证的同任务成功轨迹作为参考，可提供有效的事后视角（hindsight）。但不同成功轨迹可能到达互不兼容的交互状态，带来误导性监督，因此如何挑选合适的成功参考是关键问题。

Method: 将成功参考选择转化为自由目标最优传输（free-target optimal transport）问题，即把"无法预设目标的参考分配"退化为为每条失败轨迹分配其最兼容的成功参考。由于潜在状态路径不可见，采用动作序列对齐作为兼容性的可观测代理，并以最长公共子序列（LCS）实例化：识别动作的有序共享主干，同时允许可恢复的绕行（detour）。由此确立"轨迹兼容性"作为多轮OPD参考选择的核心准则。

Result: 在ALFWorld与τ³-bench的airline域上，LCS是所评估的参考选择器中唯一在ALFWorld总体得分及airline全部三项指标上均优于无参考OPD的方法；此外，在airline上训练的模型无需额外优化即可迁移至retail域，且LCS在该域同样提升全部三项指标。

Conclusion: 轨迹兼容性是多轮工具使用智能体OPD中参考选择的核心标准；以LCS为代表的动作级对齐代理能有效实现最优传输式的参考分配，并在跨域迁移中展现稳健收益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-inference distribution mismatch by supervising a student on its own trajectories. For multi-turn tool-use agents, conditioning the teacher on a validated same-task successful trajectory as a reference can provide useful hindsight, but different successes may reach incompatible interaction states and offer misleading supervision. We cast successful-reference selection as a free-target optimal transport problem, which reduces to assigning each failed trajectory its most compatible successful reference. Because latent state paths are hidden, we use action-sequence alignment as an observable proxy for compatibility and instantiate it with longest common subsequence (LCS), which identifies a shared ordered backbone of actions while allowing recoverable detours. This perspective makes trajectory compatibility the central criterion for reference selection in multi-turn OPD. Experiments on ALFWorld and the airline domain of $τ^3$-bench show that LCS is the only evaluated reference selector to improve over reference-free OPD on both ALFWorld overall scores and all three airline metrics. Without further optimization, the airline-trained models also transfer to the retail domain, where LCS improves all three metrics.

</details>


### [301] [Byzantine-Robust Muon: Resilient Momentum Orthogonalization for Distributed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13137)
*Grigory Malinovsky,Yury Demidovich,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在统一的一阶信赖域（trust-region）框架下研究基于LMO与动量正交化的Muon优化器，给出其在拜占庭鲁棒分布式/联邦学习中的非凸与星凸收敛保证（不依赖i.i.d.假设），并用以解释实验中的鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon类优化器在大规模与分布式学习中经验表现优异，但其对对抗性行为（拜占庭攻击）与数据异构性的鲁棒性缺乏理论理解；现有收敛分析多依赖i.i.d.与欧氏几何假设。

Method: 将Muon类更新纳入统一的一阶信赖域框架，结合鲁棒聚合（robust aggregation）分析拜占庭鲁棒的分布式与联邦学习；分析不假设i.i.d.数据，并引入与信赖域几何相匹配的非欧氏异构性度量；在显式聚合假设下推导非凸与星凸目标的收敛界，并进一步在显式加性oracle误差条件下覆盖非精确正交化。实验方面：CIFAR-10上ResNet-18与ViT-Tiny的模拟联邦训练、额外图像基准、TinyStories上的38M参数字节级语言模型；并设置fixed-honest控制实验（有界RFA-query攻击下的RFA vs. 平均、Muon-NS vs. 逐张量欧氏归一化、与调优AdamW的困惑度对比）。

Result: 理论上得到含加性异构性误差地板（与已知不可能性结果一致）的收敛保证；非精确正交化时非凸拜占庭项的分子不变、仅进度分母变小。实验中测试的Muon配置在被攻击准确率（及语言模型困惑度）上优于SGD、归一化SGD，并在CIFAR-10上优于sign更新；fixed-honest控制显示RFA相对平均聚合在受限RFA-query攻击下有实质优势，Muon-NS优于逐张量欧氏归一化，且在held-out TinyStories上困惑度低于调优AdamW。

Conclusion: 该文为拜占庭鲁棒的Muon类优化提供了首个较为系统的非欧氏、非i.i.d.理论刻画，理论与仿真一致地表明Muon在对抗与异构场景下具有可解释的鲁棒性优势；误差地板的存在说明在无额外假设下鲁棒性存在固有极限。

Abstract: Optimization methods, including the Muon optimizer based on the Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO) and momentum orthogonalization, have shown strong empirical performance in large-scale and distributed learning, yet their robustness to adversarial behavior and data heterogeneity is not well understood. We study these methods within a unified first-order trust-region framework and analyze Muon-based updates with robust aggregation for Byzantine-robust distributed and federated learning. Our analysis does not rely on the i.i.d. assumption and introduces a non-Euclidean notion of heterogeneity consistent with the trust-region geometry. Under this framework, we establish convergence guarantees for both general non-convex and star-convex objectives.
