Our results provide a theoretical characterization of Byzantine-robust Muon-based optimization, with an additive heterogeneity error floor consistent with known impossibility results, under explicit aggregation assumptions. Under an explicit additive oracle-error condition, the guarantees also cover inexact orthogonalization, with the non-convex Byzantine terms in the bound's numerator unchanged but a smaller progress denominator. In simulated federated training with ResNet-18 and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10, additional image benchmarks, and a 38M-parameter byte-level language model on TinyStories, the tested Muon configuration attains higher attacked accuracy (lower perplexity) than the tested SGD and normalized-SGD configurations, and than sign updates on CIFAR-10. Fixed-honest controls further show a substantial RFA advantage over averaging under a bounded RFA-query attack, higher attacked accuracy for Muon-NS than tensorwise Euclidean normalization from shared checkpoints, and lower held-out TinyStories perplexity than a tuned AdamW baseline.

</details>


### [302] [High Probability Risk Control for Online Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13163)
*Yihong Guo,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线策略学习中的风险控制问题，提出基于排列（permutation）的加权风险评估器（权重用 Sinkhorn 高效近似），并用高斯 bootstrap 构造风险 UCB 进行策略选择，实现高概率的同时风险上界与渐近有效的风险控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略学习中，agent 需自适应收集数据并学习风险低于阈值的新策略。策略更新与数据收集会引发反馈协变量偏移（FCS）：观测数据依赖历史策略与数据收集过程。这带来两个难题：(1) 对每个候选策略进行高效风险评估；(2) 在 FCS 下进行有效的策略选择并保证高概率风险控制。传统重要性加权评估在 FCS 下方差大、可靠性不足。

Method: 1) 提出基于排列（permutation）的加权风险评估器，权重通过 Sinkhorn 算法高效近似求解；2) 仅选择估计风险 UCB 低于目标阈值的策略，UCB 由所提出的高斯 bootstrap 方法得到；3) 理论上分析评估器方差上界与同时风险界的渐近有效性。

Result: 理论上证明所提风险评估器的方差上界不大于标准重要性加权评估器；策略控制方法给出渐近有效的同时风险界，并为所选策略提供渐近高概率风险控制。实验在在线 contextual bandit、安全问答基准与 Medical Claim Filtering 任务上进行：方法能维持目标风险水平，风险低于基线，风险估计方差更低，并实现可靠的高概率受控策略选择；消融实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: 基于排列加权评估与高斯 bootstrap UCB 的风险控制框架可有效应对反馈协变量偏移，兼顾评估效率、方差降低与高概率风险保证，在多个在线学习与安全对齐任务中验证有效。

Abstract: We study risk control for online policy learning, where an agent adaptively collects data and aims to learn a new policy whose risk stays below a tolerance threshold at every round with high probability. Such policy updates and data collection induce feedback covariate shift (FCS): observed data depend on history through evolving policies and the data-collection process. FCS brings two substantive problems for risk control: 1) efficient risk evaluation for each candidate policy and 2) valid policy selection with high-probability risk control. To address these, we propose a permutation-based weighted risk evaluator with weights efficiently approximated by the Sinkhorn algorithm and select policies whose estimated risk upper confidence bounds (UCB) fall below the target threshold, with the UCB obtained through a proposed Gaussian bootstrap method. Theoretically, we prove that our risk evaluator has a no-larger variance upper bound than that of the standard importance-weighted evaluator. Our policy control method also yields an asymptotically valid simultaneous risk bound and provides asymptotic high-probability risk control for the selected policy. Empirically, we evaluate our method on the online contextual bandit problem, safe question answering benchmark, and Medical Claim Filtering tasks. Our method maintains the target risk level, achieves lower risk than baseline methods, has lower variance in risk estimation, and achieves reliable policy selection with high-probability risk control. Further ablation studies support our theoretical claims.

</details>


### [303] [RadRef: A Reference-Free Metric for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13168)
*Shelly Schwartz,Alan Ropp,Michael B. Sneider,Nur Yildirim,Cree M. Gaskin,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无参考指标 RadRef，基于图像衍生的临床发现信念并结合临床指征相关性加权来给放射报告打分，在 ReXVal 上与放射科医生排序的一致率达 73.1%，在遗漏与伪发现维度上与主流指标相当，可在无参考报告存在时进行图像落地的质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成放射报告通常依赖与放射科医生撰写的参考报告进行比较来评估，当不存在参考报告时无法评分，限制了部署前/无标注场景下的质量监控。

Method: 构建 RadRef 无参考指标：将报告中的断言（claims）与基于图像推断的、在临床发现词表上的信念进行比对打分，并按与临床指征的相关性加权；使用同一图像落地表征派生伪发现读数 RadRef-FF；在 ReXVal 的同影像非并列报告对及 100 例 MIMIC-CXR 研究（100 份模型生成 + 100 份放射科医生撰写，由两位认证放射科医生独立标注）上评估，并用受控扰动检验对图像、报告与临床指征的依赖性。

Result: ReXVal 上同影像非并列报告对与放射科医生排序一致率 73.1%，整体与领先的参考型指标相当；在伪发现上具竞争力，在遗漏（漏检发现）上相对优势最明显。读者研究中遗漏维度 Kendall's tau-b = 0.295（两位读者之间为 0.386），且无对比指标在该目标上与 RadRef 存在显著差异；RadRef-FF 在 ReXVal 上 tau-b = 0.32、读者研究中 0.29，且无需参考报告。受控扰动证实 RadRef 依赖放射图像、报告与临床指征三要素。

Conclusion: RadRef 可在放射科医生撰写参考报告尚不存在时实现图像落地的评估，支持临床场景下报告生成系统的质量监控，并在有参考报告时作为参考型指标的有效补充。

Abstract: AI-generated radiology reports are usually evaluated by comparison with a radiologist-authored reference, leaving no way to score them when no reference exists. We introduce RadRef, a reference-free metric that scores report claims against image-derived beliefs over a clinical finding vocabulary, weighted by relevance to the clinical indication. On ReXVal, RadRef achieves 73.1% agreement on non-tied same-radiograph report pairs with radiologist rankings, comparable to leading reference-based metrics overall. It is also competitive on false findings and has its clearest relative result on omissions (missed findings). We further evaluate 100 model-generated and 100 radiologist-authored reports from 100 MIMIC-CXR studies, all independently annotated by two board-certified radiologists. On omissions, RadRef attains Kendall's tau-b = 0.295, against 0.386 between the two readers themselves, and no compared metric differs significantly from RadRef on this target. The same image-grounded representation provides a false-finding readout, RadRef-FF, with tau-b = 0.32 on ReXVal and 0.29 in the reader study, without requiring a reference. Controlled perturbations confirm that RadRef depends on the radiograph, report, and clinical indication. RadRef enables image-grounded evaluation before a radiologist-authored reference exists, supporting quality monitoring of report-generation systems in clinical settings, and complements reference-based metrics when one is available.

</details>


### [304] [Making Strides in Symbolic Imitation Learning: Offline Demonstrations, Online Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13182)
*Bryce MacInnis,Malcolm I. Heywood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Symbolic Imitation Learning框架，将oracle策略离线蒸馏为"决策表+线性模型运动基元"的可解释策略，并用在线ARS微调常数与基元，在20个基准RL任务上逼近甚至超越oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续控制任务中DRL因函数逼近能力强而占主导，但其策略不可解释；进化式（可解释）方法因相反原因长期落后。作者希望弥合"性能"与"可解释性"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 离线符号模仿学习——利用离线数据集将oracle策略蒸馏为决策表（decision list），每个分支为线性模型的运动基元（motion primitives）；(2) 在线Augmented-Random Search (ARS) 对常数与运动基元进行调优，以恢复逼近精度。

Result: 在20个基准RL任务上，整体性能接近oracle，并在其中11个任务上超过oracle；同时生成的策略简洁（parsimonious）且可解释，便于领域专家理解。

Conclusion: 符号化蒸馏+在线随机搜索调优能够在保持高连续控制性能的同时产出可解释、简洁的策略，说明可解释的进化/符号方法在连续控制中并非必然落后于DRL。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods have been the dominant paradigm in continuous control tasks due to their efficacy in function approximation, while interpretable evolutionary methods have lagged largely behind for the opposite reason. In this work, we introduce Symbolic Imitation Learning, a framework for distilling an oracle policy through an offline dataset into a decision list with linear model motion primitives. We show that this, in conjunction, with online Augmented-Random Search (ARS) tuning of the constants and motion primitives approximates the performance of the oracle across 20 benchmark RL tasks and exceeds the oracle in 11 of those 20 tasks while simultaneously producing parsimonious, interpretable policies that domain experts can better understand.

</details>


### [305] [Prediction-Powered LLM Ranking with Adaptively Collected Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13186)
*Gabriel Sargent,Will Wei Sun,Zhengwu Zhang,Yufeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PP-Rank 框架，将 LLM 评委预测与人类反馈结合，在自适应采样下实现有效推断，从而以更低成本获得更精确的大模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类评估成本高，平台采用自适应采样以优先收集信息量大的模型对比；同时存在大量未评分回答与廉价 LLM 评委可用，但评委偏差与自适应依赖破坏了标准 i.i.d. 推断框架，难以获得有效精度提升。

Method: PP-Rank 将 LLM 评委预测与人类反馈融合，采用数据驱动的预测权重，在给定类内最小化渐近协方差迹；通过联合鞅分析处理自适应人类反馈与辅助数据间的依赖，建立一般评估目标及同时排名推断的渐近正态性；并推导减少探索的分配规则，在匹配保守方差界的前提下优先使用低成本比较，刻画成对均值实现常规 √T 速率估计的分配极限。

Result: 在 UltraFeedback-Extended 上的半合成实验显示，相比仅用人类反馈的基线，PP-Rank 在得分估计、置信区间宽度与排名认证方面均有改进；合成实验表明其对评委质量与自适应分配具有稳健性，并能在不损失精度的前提下显著降低评估成本。

Conclusion: PP-Rank 为自适应人类评估提供了带有效推断的预测增强排名方法，兼顾精度与成本，适用于实际 LLM 评估平台的决策场景。

Abstract: Ranking large language models (LLMs) through human evaluations is essential for downstream decision-making. Because human feedback is expensive, evaluation platforms often collect it adaptively, prioritizing informative model comparisons using previously observed feedback. To further improve precision with limited human feedback, evaluators can draw on abundant unrated responses and relatively inexpensive LLM judges; however, judge bias and adaptive dependence challenge standard i.i.d. inference frameworks. We propose Prediction-Powered Ranking (PP-Rank), a framework that combines LLM-judge predictions with human feedback to improve precision while providing valid inference under adaptive sampling. Data-driven prediction weights minimize the asymptotic covariance trace within the proposed class. In theory, we establish asymptotic normality for general evaluation targets and simultaneous ranking inference. A joint martingale analysis handles dependence between adaptive human feedback and auxiliary data. We further derive a rule for reducing exploration that prioritizes lower-cost comparisons while matching a conservative variance bound, and characterize allocation limits for regular $\sqrt{T}$-rate estimation of pairwise means. Semi-synthetic experiments on UltraFeedback-Extended show improvements over human-only baselines in score estimation, confidence interval width, and ranking certification. Synthetic experiments demonstrate robustness to judge quality and adaptive allocation, while showing that PP-Rank can substantially reduce evaluation costs without sacrificing precision.

</details>


### [306] [Structured Kernel Surrogate Models for Multimodal Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13198)
*Ziniu Zhang,Zhenshuo Zhang,Jianglin Lu,Ruoxuan Xiong,Yun Fu,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构化核代理模型与SKS算法，用于度量跨样本-跨模态的数据归因，在真实预测结果相关性上较现有方法提升57.8%，下游数据筛选准确率提升1.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统中同一模态对不同样本的价值差异很大：对某样本（如自解释图像）可能冗余，对另一样本却可能唯一有效或需与其他模态组合才有效。现有工作只做样本级影响估计，无法刻画"样本×模态"的联合交互，该问题尚属开放。

Method: 提出结构化核代理模型：将"哪些样本、哪些模态被选中参与训练"的离散选择状态映射到预测结果。设计SKS算法，在选择状态上做核岭回归并采用结构化组合核——核函数由两部分相乘：单样本内任意两模态间的核相似度，以及两样本之间的核相似度。并设计基于梯度的估计算法高效求解该代理模型。

Result: 在两个多模态基准（涵盖卫星影像、路网、文本、音频、表格数据）上，SKS与真实预测结果的相关性比现有方法高57.8%；下游数据选择任务中平均准确率提升1.93%；所选数据训练出的模型Hessian迹更低，损失面更平坦；用于卫星影像筛选时能定位事故常发的路段而非背景图像。

Conclusion: 通过结构化组合核代理建模，可有效刻画跨样本、跨模态的联合影响，既提升归因准确性，又带来更平坦的损失面与更合理的数据选择行为。

Abstract: We study the problem of cross-sample modality attribution, which involves measuring how one modality in one sample affects the learning outcome in other multimodal samples. This problem is crucial in a multimodal system because a data modality (such as a text description) may be redundant for one sample (a self-explanatory image), but the same data modality would otherwise be uniquely informative for another sample, or informative when combined with other modalities. Prior work focuses on influence estimation at the level of one sample, and it remains an open question how one should capture the interactions across both samples and modalities. To capture scenarios like the above, we propose structured kernel surrogate models, which are surrogate mappings from both samples and modalities to the prediction outcome when they are trained together. We design an algorithm, SKS, which applies kernel ridge regression with a structured, compositional kernel on the selection states of individual samples and modalities. Specifically, the kernel function multiplies kernel-based similarity scores between every pair of data modalities for one sample and a pair of two samples. Additionally, we design a gradient-based estimation algorithm to efficiently estimate the structured kernel surrogate model. We evaluate our approach on two multimodal benchmarks, spanning a range of data modalities including satellite images, road networks, text, audio, and tabular data. We find that SKS achieves $57.8$% higher correlation with the true prediction outcomes than existing methods. In downstream data selection, SKS improves the average accuracy by $1.93$% over baselines. Models with data selected by SKS have lower Hessian traces, indicating a flatter loss surface. Using SKS to select satellite images identifies road segments, where accident scenes commonly occur, as opposed to background images.

</details>


### [307] [XAI-Driven Confidence Estimation for OOD-Resilient Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13199)
*Mahmoud Sanad,Mohammed Bakr Sikal,Osama Abboud,Heba Khdr,Jörg Henkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEX——首个面向ML资源管理的事后XAI框架，用GMM对训练输入嵌入聚类并分配簇级误差上界，实现无需重训的输入级运行时置信度估计；在Intel Alder Lake上以LSTM IPS预测模型、34个基准应用验证，未见负载包含率达~94%，置信界比全局基线紧约17%，开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML资源管理模型在运行时遭遇分布外（OOD）输入时性能退化，而现有方法仅依赖单一全局误差裕度，既过于保守，又无法区分可靠预测与灾难性失效，缺乏输入级、可解释的运行时置信信号。

Method: LEX为事后（post-hoc）可解释AI框架：对训练输入空间的嵌入向量做高斯混合模型（GMM）聚类，将各簇的预测误差上界关联至具体输入样本，从而在运行时输出细粒度置信区间，且无需重新训练模型。评估对象为Intel Alder Lake上基于LSTM的IPS预测模型，覆盖34个基准应用。

Result: 在未见负载上达到约94%的包含率（containment），逐输入置信界较全局基线紧约17%，运行时开销可忽略。

Conclusion: LEX可为ML预测提供可解释、可靠的输入级置信信号，使ML资源管理在运行时对OOD输入更具鲁棒性，并将簇级误差归因引入在线资源管理场景。

Abstract: Machine learning-based resource management (RM) models degrade under out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs at runtime, yet existing approaches in the literature rely on a single global error margin that is overly conservative and fails to distinguish reliablepredictions from catastrophic failures. We propose Lightweight Runtime EXplainability (LEX), the first post-hoc XAI framework for input-specific runtime confidence estimation in ML-based RM. LEX clusters the embeddings of the training input space using a Gaussian Mixture Model and associates per-cluster prediction error bounds to input samples, enabling fine-grained runtime confidence signals without retraining. Evaluated on a state-of-the-art LSTM-based IPS forecasting model on Intel Alder Lake, across 34 benchmark applications, LEX achieves ~94% containment on unseen workloads and produces ~17% tighter per-input confidence bounds than global baselines. With its negligible runtime overhead, LEX advances the field by providing explainable and reliable input-specific confidence signals of ML predictions, thereby making ML-based resource management resilient to OOD at runtime.

</details>


### [308] [Audited Gradients, Anchored Policies: Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13203)
*Ruige Kong,Stefania Fresca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"动作梯度审计"+低维反馈律锚定的策略优化流程：用高保真求解器有限差分核验代理模型的动作梯度，通过则以代理梯度拟合低维反馈律、不通过则用求解器估计，再以该律初始化神经策略并用二次惩罚约束优化过程，在四个测试案例上以3–156倍更少的求解器交互达到或超过无模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流动反馈控制器设计昂贵，每个候选控制律都需高保真求解器评估；可微代理模型虽降本，却存在"预测误差无法察觉"的失效模式——代理能紧密跟随流动轨迹却错误估计目标对动作的导数（动作梯度），使基于代理的策略优化失效。

Method: 1) 在留出状态上，将代理的动作梯度与高保真求解器的中心有限差分估计对比，验收准则事先固定，称为"审计"；2) 审计通过则将低维反馈律拟合到代理动作梯度，不通过则拟合到求解器估计；3) 用该反馈律初始化神经策略，并在优化过程中以二次惩罚（锚定项）将策略约束在其附近。

Result: 四个测试案例中控制器优于或持平无模型基线，高保真求解器交互减少3–156倍。反例一：某代理对目标预测相关系数0.9996，但动作梯度相关仅0.459，说明预测精度不能识别可用代理。反例二：另一代理动作梯度与求解器估计相关0.996，但若无锚定惩罚而直接经其优化，目标反而变差0.52%，说明梯度准确不等于可用控制器。反例三：在某一固定动作上通过审计的代理，在部署控制器实际施加的动作附近未通过，说明审计通过具有局部性。

Conclusion: 代理模型的可微性必须经动作梯度审计验证，且审计结论仅局部有效；引入低维反馈律锚定与二次惩罚可稳健利用代理梯度、显著降低求解器开销，同时避免"预测准但梯度错"的隐性失效。

Abstract: Feedback controllers for flows are expensive to design, since each candidate must be evaluated with a high-fidelity solver. Differentiable surrogates reduce that cost, but they also introduce a failure mode that prediction error cannot detect. Policy optimization uses the derivative of the objective with respect to the action, which we call the action gradient and which can be computed either through the surrogate or through the high-fidelity solver. Optimization through the surrogate uses only its action gradient, and a surrogate can follow the flow trajectory closely while misestimating this derivative. The surrogate action gradient is compared against a central finite-difference estimate from the high-fidelity solver on held-out states, under acceptance criteria fixed before the comparison, and we call that comparison an audit. A low-dimensional feedback law is then fitted to the surrogate action gradient when the surrogate passes the audit, and to the solver estimate when it does not. This law initializes a neural policy, and a quadratic penalty holds the policy near it during optimization. The proposed approach is validated on four test cases. The resulting controllers outperform or match a model-free baseline, while using 3 to 156 times fewer high-fidelity solver interactions across the four test cases. One surrogate predicts the objective at correlation 0.9996 but its action gradient at only 0.459, so prediction accuracy does not identify a usable surrogate. Another surrogate, whose action gradient agrees with the solver estimate at correlation 0.996, makes the objective 0.52% worse when the policy is optimized through it without the anchor penalty, so an accurate gradient alone does not provide a working controller. Finally, a surrogate that passes the audit at one fixed action fails it around the actions the deployed controller applies, so a pass is local.

</details>


### [309] [Wrong the Same Way: Cross-Prompt Sharing Both Suppresses and Amplifies Reward Hacking in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13218)
*Michael Li,Rohan Pandey,Ray Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可精确计算全响应分布的小规模受控环境中，RLVR的KL正则最优解等价于对接受集重加权的"过滤器"（不改变组成，故hack率完全继承自基座模型）；以该零模型为基准的漂移可分解为各prompt上奖励与经验切核协方差之和，参数共享使"多数prompt接受的错误回答"被放大、少数接受的被压低，最终hacking率方向取决于核加权概率落在接受prompt上的比例，而非验证器错误率或普适性单独决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于RLVR与不完美验证器交互存在两种对立观点：一种认为reward hacking继承自基座模型，另一种认为RL会放大它，已有实证研究结论互相矛盾。作者希望在可精确求解的受控环境中厘清机制，并给出解释早期研究分歧的可检验假设。

Method: 构建小规模环境以精确计算完整响应分布；操纵泄漏的普适性（接受同一错误回答的prompt比例）与验证器静态假阳性率；以KL正则最优解作为零模型，用将漂移写成"奖励与经验切核（tangent kernel）协方差按prompt求和"的已知分解进行分析；对比无参数共享（独立per-prompt副本）与共享参数策略；在Adam、SGD与采样GRPO下扫描收敛学习率。

Result: KL正则最优时hack率被精确继承；无参数共享时跨prompt协方差项消失，共享参数下每个接受错误答案的prompt通过baseline将其概率推高、每个拒绝的prompt将其压低，幅度与模型在该处的概率成正比。因此在所有收敛学习率下（Adam/SGD/采样GRPO），高普适性下的hacking终值都高于低普适性，但是否越过基座率取决于步长；独立per-prompt副本可消除该步长依赖。推送方向取决于核加权错误概率中落在接受prompt上的份额（受冻结基座模型控制），而非验证器错误率或普适性本身。验证器修补的效果差异显著：移除同等数量错误的两个补丁，真实准确率相差可达38个点。

Conclusion: RLVR本身并不必然放大hacking，其最优解仅是接受集的重加权；放大/抑制效应源于参数共享下跨prompt的核加权耦合与步长。验证器修复应针对模型跨prompt重复犯的错误，而非单纯减少错误数量。作者将该对齐视角作为解释既有研究结论分歧的假设。

Abstract: Two accounts of how reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) interacts with an imperfect verifier coexist: in one, reward hacking is inherited from the base model; in the other, RL amplifies it. We study both in a controlled environment small enough to compute the entire response distribution exactly. We vary the universality of a leak, or the fraction of prompts that accept the same wrong response, and the verifier's static false-positive rate. At its KL-regularized optimum, RLVR acts as a filter that reweights the accepted set without altering its composition, so the hack rate is inherited exactly. Taking that as a null model, a known decomposition writes the drift from it as a sum over prompts of covariances between reward and the empirical tangent kernel. The terms from other prompts vanish without parameter sharing. With sharing, every prompt that accepts a wrong answer pushes it up and every prompt that rejects it pushes it down through the baseline, in proportion to the model's probability of producing it there. A shared-parameter policy thus drives a leak that few prompts accept below the base rate and amplifies one that most prompts accept. At every convergent learning rate under Adam, SGD, and sampled GRPO, hacking ends higher at high universality than at low, but whether either end crosses the base rate depends on the step size. Independent per-prompt copies remove the dependence. The direction of this push depends on neither the verifier's error rate nor universality alone, but on how much of the model's kernel-weighted probability of a wrong answer falls on prompts that accept it, which frozen base model controls support. Verifier fixes should target the mistakes the model repeats across prompts: two patches that remove equally many errors differ by up to 38 points of true accuracy. We propose this alignment as a hypothesis for why earlier studies disagree.

</details>


### [310] [GigaEdge Spench: An SDK for Practical Streaming Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13243)
*Artem Sokolov,Artem Khaidarov,Yana Lysova,Alexander Gladkikh,Vitaliy Yulis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开源SDK「GigaEdge Spench」，支持在CPU服务器、移动端和浏览器上进行流式语音增强，并提供跨平台统一部署与系统性实验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式语音增强模型虽多，但缺乏可配置、跨平台（服务器/移动端/浏览器）的统一部署系统，也缺少关于模型选择、块大小与执行环境对实际使用影响的实验研究。

Method: 构建共享C++核心，通过原生接口支持ONNX Runtime与TensorFlow Lite，并适配Android/iOS SDK及WebAssembly；应用可由神经降噪、归一化、动态处理、重采样与回声消除等模块组合成增强通道；发布8 kHz INT8降噪器及判别式与混合48 kHz FP32模型；支持流上下文共享模型资源及多帧单次推理（时间帧分组）；在EARS-WHAM v2上与RNNoise、DPDFNet2对比质量与计算开销，并测量单模型在CPU上可并行处理的流数。

Result: 所有测试的原生与Web配置均快于实时处理，但较大块尺寸需要更长时间收集输入；给出了不同模型、块大小与执行环境下质量与处理成本的对比结果，以及共享模型可承载的并发流数量。SDK计时均基于ONNX Runtime。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于一个可配置的跨平台部署系统，以及关于模型选择、块大小和执行环境如何影响流式语音增强应用的实验性研究。

Abstract: We present GigaEdge Spench, an open-source SDK for streaming speech enhancement on CPU servers, mobile devices, and in browsers. Applications can build enhancement channels from neural denoising, normalization and dynamics blocks, sample-rate conversion, and acoustic echo cancellation where supported. These stages can be combined, while normalization can also run without a neural model. The release includes an 8 kHz INT8 denoiser and discriminative and hybrid 48 kHz FP32 models. A shared C++ core supports ONNX Runtime and TensorFlow Lite through native interfaces, Android and iOS SDKs, and WebAssembly integration. Independent stream contexts share model resources, and consecutive frames from one stream can be processed in a single inference call. We evaluate the released models on EARS-WHAM v2 and compare their quality and processing costs with RNNoise and DPDFNet2. All tested native and Web configurations process files faster than real time, although larger chunks require more time to collect input. We also measure how many streams a shared model can process on a CPU. Our contribution is a configurable deployment system and an experimental study of how model choice, chunk size, and execution environment affect its use. All reported SDK timing uses ONNX Runtime. Index Terms: speech enhancement, streaming inference, cross-platform deployment, temporal frame grouping, computational efficiency

</details>


### [311] [Domain-Routed Delta Optimization for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13249)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Domain-Routed Delta Optimization (DRD-MOPD)，在域路由的多教师 on-policy 蒸馏中显式指定各域样本更新哪些参数分量；学生模型为冻结基座与可训练分量的和，并通过角度、投影系数与能量分析原始专家几何的保留情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师 on-policy 蒸馏虽为每个样本分配域教师，却统一更新共享学生参数，导致不同域知识在同一参数空间内互相干扰。已有工作（如 Open-MOPD）直接从基座模型蒸馏，其发布检查点的增量能量绝大部分落在原始专家方向张成空间之外，说明专家几何被破坏。

Method: 学生 = 冻结基座 + 可训练分量之和，分量由"专家减基座"权重初始化；所有分量参与每次前向；定义四种更新模式，建立样本域与参数分量之间的对应关系，各分量拥有独立优化器状态；随后分析更新规则，并用角度、投影系数、能量指标度量对原始专家几何的保留。

Result: 经 20 次更新后，Own 检查点沿各分量原始方向的系数接近 1，分量两两夹角为 88.04°–92.41°；导出 BF16 学生的增量仅 4.90% 能量位于张成空间外，而保存的 FP32 分量之和为 10.52%；对比 Open-MOPD 最终检查点该比例为 97.02%。六项基准宏平均得分 32.72%，初始 task-arithmetic 合并为 32.44%。

Conclusion: 域路由的增量参数更新可在有限训练阶段下较好保持专家方向几何并带来小幅能力提升；但结论仅适用于所评估的训练阶段与设置，且 BF16 导出与 FP32 分量求和在能量分布上存在差异。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation assigns each sample a domain teacher but typically updates shared student parameters. We introduce Domain-Routed Delta Optimization (DRD-MOPD), which specifies which parameter components each domain's samples update. The student is the sum of a frozen base and trainable components initialized from expert-minus-base weights. All components participate in every forward pass; four update modes define the correspondence between sample domains and components, each with independent optimizer states. We analyze these update rules and measure retention of the original expert geometry through angles, projection coefficients, and energy. Prior work such as Open-MOPD distills directly from the base model; 97.02% of its released final checkpoint's increment energy lies outside the span of the original expert directions. In comparison, our Own checkpoint after 20 updates retains coefficients near one along each component's original direction and pairwise angles ranging from 88.04 to 92.41 degrees; its exported BF16 student's increment has 4.90% outside energy, versus 10.52% for the sum of saved FP32 components. The same checkpoint achieves a 32.72% macro score across six benchmarks, compared with 32.44% for its initial task-arithmetic merge. These measurements describe geometry and capability at the evaluated training stages and settings.

</details>


### [312] [Off-policy Learning with Excursion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13275)
*Jiamin He,Mark Rowland,Zhaohan Daniel Guo,Hado van Hasselt,Diana Borsa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"excursion policies"（短程偏移策略）：智能体先执行目标策略若干步再回到行为策略，并给出其动态规划理论与可靠（sound）的off-policy TD估计算法，在连续控制基准上获得与混合策略相当的高效性—稳定性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Off-policy RL中数据来自行为策略而需评估目标策略，常在偏差与方差/稳定性之间权衡。常见的做法是在行为策略与目标策略间做线性插值（混合策略），但这只覆盖了更广插值机制空间中的一个点，缺乏结构化的替代方案。

Method: 1) 定义excursion policies：智能体先按目标策略连续执行多步"excursion"，随后回退到行为策略，从而形成一类结构化插值机制；2) 推导该类策略的动态规划结果，建立其在RL中使用的理论基础；3) 设计可证明可靠（provably sound）的off-policy时序差分（TD）算法用于excursion价值估计；4) 在连续控制基准上与混合策略（mixture policies）进行对比，并分析二者行为差异。

Result: 实验表明excursion策略在连续控制基准上提供的高效性—稳定性权衡与混合策略相当；理论分析揭示了两类插值机制在概念与行为上的差异。

Conclusion: Excursion policies构成一类有理论支撑的结构化策略插值方案，可作为混合策略之外的可行选择，在保持可比权衡效果的同时提供不同的行为特性。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning (RL) tries to learn the value of a target policy while data comes from a different behavior policy. This often requires trading off bias for variance or stability. One way to trade these off is to linearly interpolate between the behavior and target policies, but this represents only one point in a broader space of interpolation mechanisms. We introduce excursion policies--a structured alternative where the agent follows the target policy for a multi-step "excursion" before reverting to the behavior policy. We derive dynamic programming results for these policies, establishing the theoretical basis for their use in RL. We further develop provably sound off-policy temporal-difference (TD) algorithms for excursion value estimation. We find that excursion policies provide an efficiency-stability trade-off comparable to mixture policies in continuous control benchmarks, and our analysis illustrates conceptual differences in their behavior.

</details>


### [313] [An Analytical Model of Compute-limited Multistage Training Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13276)
*Nishil Patel,Jin Hwa Lee,Basile Confavreux,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构建一个可解析的高维模型，统一刻画无监督预训练（线性自编码器）、带噪逐步标签的SFT与稀疏情节奖励的RL三阶段训练，用随机矩阵理论与算子投影闭合推导低维学习动力学，揭示表示质量、反馈结构与计算分配之间的耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基础模型普遍采用"预训练—SFT—RL"多阶段训练范式，但各阶段之间如何相互作用、计算量应如何在三阶段间分配，尚缺乏理论理解。现有工作多孤立分析单一阶段，缺少能同时刻画三阶段耦合的可解析框架。

Method: 提出一个解析可处理的高维模型：预训练为线性自编码器（无监督），SFT使用含噪的逐步标签，RL使用稀疏情节奖励。借助随机矩阵理论与算子投影闭合（operator-projection closure），推导出逼近实际模拟的低维学习动力学方程；并在OLMo-2 1B的GSM8K实验上验证SFT初始化对RL平台期的影响；另构建互补的组合式模型分析RL对预训练技能的保留与强化。

Result: (1) 有限预训练数据造成依赖表示的泛化上限，贝叶斯分类误差约按预训练样本比的平方根反比缩放，且该指数在渐近SFT与RL误差中同样出现；(2) 预训练质量越高，对含噪监督的敏感性越低；(3) SFT初始化缩短稀疏奖励导致的RL初期平台期，该现象在OLMo-2 1B/GSM8K上得到观测；更长的SFT暖启动还提升渐近RL性能；(4) 三阶段联合计算优化显示最优分配依赖监督质量与总预算，RL计算占比对预算呈非单调依赖；(5) 组合式模型表明RL选择性地强化任务相关的预训练技能，而基本保留未被使用的技能。

Conclusion: 研究识别出连接表示质量、反馈结构与多阶段计算分配的关键机制：预训练数据量通过表示质量设定性能标度上限，SFT暖启动缓解RL稀疏奖励瓶颈，最优计算分配需随监督质量与预算自适应调整，RL则在技能层面呈选择性强化。

Abstract: Modern foundation models are trained through multiple stages, yet how these stages interact and how compute should be allocated across them remain poorly understood. We introduce an analytically tractable high-dimensional model combining unsupervised pretraining with a linear autoencoder, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with noisy stepwise labels, and reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse episodic rewards. Using random matrix theory and an operator-projection closure, we derive approximate low-dimensional learning dynamics that closely match simulations. We show that finite pretraining data imposes a representation-dependent performance limit, with Bayes classification error scaling as the inverse square root of the pretraining sample ratio, and observe the same exponent in asymptotic SFT and RL errors. Better pretraining also reduces sensitivity to noisy supervision. SFT initialization shortens the initial RL plateau induced by sparse rewards, an effect we also observe in OLMo-2 1B on GSM8K. Longer SFT warm starts additionally improve asymptotic RL performance. Optimizing compute across all three stages reveals that the optimal allocation depends on supervision quality and total budget, with a non-monotonic dependence of the RL compute share on budget. Finally, a complementary compositional model shows how RL selectively strengthens task-relevant pretrained skills while largely preserving unused skills. Together, these results identify mechanisms linking representation quality, feedback structure, and compute allocation in multistage training.

</details>


### [314] [Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN for Efficient Multipath Parameter Estimation in MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13298)
*Chaofan Deng,Linyu Sun,Jaeho Lee,Zishen Wan,Arijit Raychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NN-CLEAN：用多头分类器替代CLEAN算法每步的多维搜索，保留LS复增益估计、参数化重构与残差消除，在宽带MIMO信道多径估计中实现约27倍加速与52倍GPU显存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带MIMO信道的高分辨率多径估计需在到达角、离开角与传播距离上反复做多维搜索，计算与存储开销大，难以满足实际链路中的实时性需求。

Method: 将CLEAN每次提取步骤的多维网格搜索替换为多头神经网络分类器（同一分类器迭代作用于后续残差，输出结构不随提取深度变化），同时保留最小二乘复路径增益估计、参数化重构与残差抵消；按参考MPC数设定迭代次数，在离网格合成数据上训练，并在Sionna射线追踪LOS/NLOS信道（编码MIMO链路）上零重训直接应用。

Result: 在参考MPC数确定的迭代次数下，离网格合成测试中1~2条MPC的路径精度略优于GS-CLEAN，但路径数增大时精度下降更快；批量8、完整提取3条MPC时相对GS-CLEAN约27倍加速、峰值GPU显存降低约52倍；零重训应用于二径LOS/NLOS Sionna信道，相较插值LS信道估计显著降低信道重构MSE与BER，但GS-CLEAN仍保持精度优势。

Conclusion: NN-CLEAN以可接受的精度代价换取显著的计算与存储效率，CNN分类器替代搜索具有可行性，适合低时延、低资源的宽带MIMO多径参数估计；精度—开销折中及多径数增大时的性能退化仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: High-resolution multipath estimation in wideband multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channels requires repeated searches over angle of arrival, angle of departure, and propagation distance. This paper presents neural network-assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), which replaces the multidimensional search at each extraction step with a multi-head classifier while retaining least-squares (LS) estimation of the complex path gain, parametric reconstruction, and residual cancellation. The same classifier is applied to successive residuals, allowing the extraction depth to vary without changing its output structure. With the number of iterations prescribed by the reference multipath-component (MPC) count, off-grid synthetic tests show that NN-CLEAN slightly exceeds grid-search CLEAN (GS-CLEAN) in path accuracy for one and two MPCs, although its accuracy declines more rapidly at larger path counts. For complete three-MPC extraction at a batch size of eight, NN-CLEAN achieves an approximately $27\times$ speedup and a $52$-fold reduction in peak GPU memory relative to GS-CLEAN. Applied without retraining to two-MPC line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) Sionna ray-tracing channels within a coded MIMO link, NN-CLEAN substantially reduces channel-reconstruction mean-squared error (MSE) and bit error rate (BER) relative to interpolated LS channel estimation. GS-CLEAN retains an accuracy advantage on these channels, indicating the estimation cost associated with the computational savings.

</details>


### [315] [Train High, Ship Low: Efficient LoRA Synchronization for Distributed On-Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13306)
*Shenghui Li,Li Ju,Thiemo Voigt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiLo 将 LoRA 适配器参数传输替换为「压缩梯度分发 + 本地优化器重放」，并配套预算感知梯度压缩器 Spar，在带宽受限下以约 1.6% 的字节量保持接近全同步的学习质量，端到端加速最高 2.2×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式 on-policy 后训练中，向 rollout worker 传输 LoRA 适配器会成为带宽瓶颈；降低训练秩会牺牲模型容量，而自适应优化器状态/更新本身难以压缩，因此需要一种不依赖降低训练秩、又能大幅削减通信量的同步机制。

Method: 提出 HiLo：与压缩器无关的框架，worker 端维护副本优化器状态，接收压缩梯度后本地重放优化器更新，从而用梯度分发替代参数传输；在可复现执行下，learner 与 worker 应用相同解码梯度，保证同一版本下策略状态一致。进一步提出 Spar：预算感知梯度压缩器，结合谱截断、自适应秩分配与误差反馈，将训练秩与通信成本解耦。

Result: 在 on-policy 蒸馏与奖励引导后训练实验中，HiLo+Spar 的学习质量接近全同步，传输字节最少仅为适配器字节的 1.6%；在带宽受限条件下端到端更新加速最高达 2.2×。

Conclusion: 通过梯度压缩分发与本地优化器重放，可在不降低训练秩的前提下显著降低 LoRA 后训练通信开销，并保持策略一致性与学习质量，为带宽受限的分布式 on-policy 后训练提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Transmitting LoRA adapters to rollout workers can bottleneck distributed on-policy post-training over bandwidth-constrained networks. Lowering the training rank may limit the capacity a workload needs, while adaptive optimizer updates are difficult to compress. Rollout workers can instead reconstruct updates from gradients by maintaining replicated optimizer states. We introduce HiLo, a compressor-agnostic framework that replaces parameter transmission with compressed gradient dissemination and local optimizer replay. By applying the same decoded gradients, the learner and workers maintain consistent policy states at the same version under reproducible execution. We further develop Spar, a budget-aware gradient compressor that combines spectral truncation, adaptive rank allocation and error feedback to decouple training rank from communication cost. Experiments across on-policy distillation and reward-guided post-training show that HiLo with Spar maintains learning quality close to full synchronization while transmitting as little as 1.6% of the adapter bytes, and accelerates end-to-end updates by up to $2.2\times$ under bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [316] [One Trajectory, Two Requirements: Accuracy and Local Stability in Euler Sampling for Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13311)
*Mahindra Rautela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文质疑"用离线精度扫描确定固定Euler步数"的实践，区分轨迹精度需求与局部Euler稳定性需求，并在精确模型与受控增强的六个流模型中验证二者可给出不同步数要求；在冻结的π0.5-LIBERO策略上，虽存在强收缩方向，但采样未违反Euler非扩张条件，5个配对样本表现为精度受限而非稳定性受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching机器人策略通常离线扫描选一个固定Euler步数，但推理时每个输入（视觉观测、机器人状态、语言指令）加噪声采样都对应不同的ODE轨迹与局部Jacobian序列，因此"按名义噪声-动作轨迹的精度足够"未必意味着"强收缩方向附近也足够"。作者由此追问：轨迹精度与局部欧拉稳定性是否会给出不同的步数要求。

Method: (1) 构造精确的两坐标系解析系统，给出可解算的对照；(2) 对六个已训练流模型做受控增强，保持原有学习传输不变，仅改变局部几何；(3) 在冻结的π0.5-LIBERO检查点（不改权重与动作动力学）上，对10个LIBERO-10任务的30个策略输入计算Jacobian，检验Euler非扩张条件，并对其中5个输入做精度扫描（5%与1%终端误差下的步数）。

Result: 解析与受控实验显示：即使学习到的传输不变，轨迹精度与局部Euler稳定性仍可施加不同的步数要求。π0.5-LIBERO上，Jacobian在动作端点附近存在强收缩方向，但30个采样模态均未违反Euler非扩张条件；5个输入的精度扫描在5%终端误差下需4–9步，1%下需87–112步。这些配对样本与"精度受限而非稳定性受限"的判定一致。

Conclusion: 终端精度本身不能排除独立的稳定性约束：受控实验说明二者是可分离的需求。但在所考察的冻结π0.5-LIBERO检查点与局部判据下，实际瓶颈是精度而非稳定性，说明对这类策略做自适应/输入相关步数调度时应以精度为驱动，同时保留稳定性判据作为潜在风险的筛查工具。

Abstract: Flow matching robot policies commonly use a fixed Euler step count selected from offline accuracy sweeps. At inference time, however, each policy input (visual observation, robot state, and language instruction) together with a source-noise draw defines a different ODE trajectory and sequence of local Jacobians. We ask whether a step count that accurately follows the nominal noise-to-action trajectory can still be too coarse for strongly contracting directions around it. An exact two-coordinate system and a controlled augmentation of six trained flow models show that trajectory accuracy and local Euler stability can impose different step requirements even when the original learned transport is unchanged. We then study a frozen $π_{0.5}$-LIBERO checkpoint without modifying its weights or action dynamics. Across 30 policy inputs from 10 LIBERO-10 tasks, the Jacobian exhibits strong contracting directions near the action endpoint, yet no sampled mode violates the Euler non-expansion condition. On five of those inputs, accuracy sweeps require 4--9 Euler steps at 5% terminal error and 87--112 at 1%. These five paired cases are therefore consistent with accuracy-limited rather than stability-limited sampling under this local criterion, while the controlled experiments show why endpoint accuracy alone cannot rule out a separate stability requirement.

</details>


### [317] [MicroLens: Read, Trace, and Rewire the Residual Stream with Two Lenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13322)
*Nika Zahedi,Fangyi Zhu,Anna Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个交互式可视化工具，在残差流的"层×token"网格上同时支持解码、溯源与编辑；用logit lens与Jacobian lens（J-lens）双视角在Gemma-3-4B-it上揭示：两种lens对应不同的可编辑方向，两跳事实在早期层即可读出并可被改写，且思维链写作前答案往往已位于残差流顶层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有把隐藏状态解码为词的热力图只能显示某向量"读起来像什么"，无法区分模型是否、以及在多大程度上真正使用了该信息；缺少能把"读取—溯源—干预"统一在同一视图上的分析工具，因而难以判断某层某token的表示是因果有效还是仅仅可读。

Method: 构建交互式viewer，在残差流的layers-by-tokens网格上对每个单元支持三种操作：(1)用两种lens解码——logit lens与Jacobian lens（J-lens）；(2)将单元trace回其信息来源token；(3)对单元进行编辑并即时重跑模型后续计算。基于Gemma-3-4B-it，用数百条prompt的sweep支撑三个使用场景的实证分析。

Result: (1)两lens给出不同的"写入方向"：logit lens的词方向决定当前token的输出，J-lens的方向影响后续token的读取；(2)两跳知识问答（含给定城市的州/国家首都）中，州/国家信息最早在第7层即可于城市token处读出，在此处替换可92–98%地改变答案，但在答案位置该信息未被使用；(3)在分步推理prompt下，正确答案常在写任何推理之前已是残差流top词——检索类问题89–92%，多跳与串行类39–63%，远高于干扰项（≤5%）；排序类谜题例外，需写出最后推理步骤才算出答案；(4)对可见答案，J-lens比logit lens提前约1–2层读出。

Conclusion: "可读性"≠"被使用"：需借助多lens解码、溯源与因果编辑三者结合来判定信息是否真正参与计算。J-lens在早期层更灵敏，适合作为干预与探测方向；logit lens更贴近输出行为。该工具为机制可解释性提供了统一、可交互的分析界面。

Abstract: Lenses that decode hidden states into words are a popular way to look inside language models, but a heatmap of decoded words only shows what a vector reads as and cannot distinguish whether, or how much, the model uses the information. We present an interactive viewer that puts reading, tracing and editing on the same layers-by-tokens grid of the residual stream. In the viewer, every cell can be decoded with two lenses, the logit lens and the Jacobian lens (J-lens), traced back to the tokens it drew from, and edited, with the rest of the model rerun on the spot. We demonstrate three usage scenarios on Gemma-3-4B-it, backed by sweeps over a few hundred prompts, to show what this tool can discover. First, the two lenses give different directions to write with, each suiting a different edit: the logit lens's word direction sets what a token outputs, while the J-lens's direction changes what later tokens read. Second, in two-hop trivia questions (the capital of the state or country containing a given city), the state or country is readable at the city token from as early as layer 7, and swapping it there can redirect the answer 92-98% of the time; however, at the answer position, the state or country information is not used. Third, when a chat model answers questions with a step-by-step reasoning prompt, the correct answer is often already the top word in the residual stream before any reasoning is written: for 89-92% of retrieval questions and 39-63% of multi-hop and serial ones, far above their respective distractors (at most 5%). However, ordering puzzles are an exception, as the model works them out only once it writes its final reasoning steps. Furthermore, for answers that are visible in the residual stream, the J-lens reads them a layer or two earlier than the logit lens across the different types of question.

</details>


### [318] [Robust Flow Matching under Target Corruption and Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13324)
*Mert Can Kurucu,Erik Englesson,Hossein Azizpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将流匹配（Flow Matching, FM）训练形式化为条件向量场上的时间相关回归问题，分析目标数据损坏与标签噪声两类污染对训练目标的影响，并提出将污染特异性残差分数映射为样本权重，以提升 FM 的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FM 具有训练稳定、采样高效的优点，但其前提是目标数据与标签干净；实际场景中该假设常被违反。作者希望揭示两类污染（目标损坏、标签噪声）分别如何影响 FM 目标函数，并据此设计针对性的鲁棒训练机制，而非采用通用的鲁棒训练手段。

Method: 1）将 FM 训练建模为条件向量场上的时间相关回归问题，据此分析污染影响：目标损坏会引入随 FM 时间增长的时间相关残差项；标签噪声则导致更新指向错误的类别条件向量场。2）基于该分析，把污染特异性残差分数映射为样本权重。3）针对目标损坏提出 Temporal Residual Score（TRS），在 FM 的中后期时间步强调残差幅度，因为此时干净样本与损坏样本更易区分。4）针对标签噪声提出 Comparative Conditional Score（CCS），在同一轨迹与速度目标下，将观测类别条件向量场的残差与替代类别条件向量场的残差进行比较以判定权重。

Result: 在受控污染设置与真实含噪标签的实验上，所提方法相较标准 FM 及鲁棒训练基线均提升了鲁棒性，验证了污染特异性残差结构作为鲁棒 FM 实用依据的有效性。

Conclusion: 两类污染在 FM 目标中表现出不同的残差结构，可根据其特异性残差分数构造样本权重并以较低改动实现鲁棒训练；该分析框架为设计鲁棒流匹配提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Flow Matching (FM) is a strong framework for generative modeling due to its stable training and efficient sampling. However, FM assumes clean target data and labels, an assumption often violated in practice. We formulate FM training as a time-dependent regression problem over conditional vector fields and analyze how the two corruption types affect the objective: i) target corruption adds a time-dependent residual term that grows with time, ii) label noise causes updates to the wrong class-conditional vector field. Based on this analysis, we propose to improve FM robustness by mapping corruption-specific residual scores to sample weights. For target corruption, we propose the Temporal Residual Score (TRS), which emphasizes residual magnitudes at intermediate-to-late FM times, where clean and corrupted samples are more separable. For label noise, we propose the Comparative Conditional Score (CCS), which compares the residual of the observed class-conditional vector field against alternative class-conditional vector fields for the same trajectory and velocity target. Experiments across controlled corruption settings and real-world noisy labels show that the proposed methods improve robustness over standard FM and robust-training baselines, supporting corruption-specific residual structure as a practical basis for robust FM.

</details>


### [319] [Stochastic Projections and Rank-Aware Learning Rates for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13331)
*Saber Malekmohammadi,Kevin Kuo,Virginia Smith,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPRA-LoRA：客户端冻结并周期性重采样LoRA的A矩阵，并将学习率与适应秩r绑定，在联邦学习中把计算与上行通信开销降低50%，同时收敛性有理论保证且性能优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基础模型在资源受限的分布式客户端上微调面临计算与通信瓶颈；LoRA+FL虽提供秩级灵活性，但A、B矩阵相互耦合使优化困难。

Method: 理论证明：高斯初始化且冻结A的LoRA微调是全量微调的随机近似，批次梯度带有与秩相关的随机扰动。据此在客户端冻结并周期性重采样A，解耦A/B梯度、削弱低秩约束、降低50%开销；进一步证明冻结A机制下适应秩r与梯度扰动SNR线性正相关，故按r动态缩放客户端学习率，构成SPRA-LoRA，并给出异构资源下的收敛证明。

Result: 基准数据集实验显示SPRA-LoRA在客户端开销仅为一半的情况下稳定优于现有最优基线；理论给出SNR与r线性关系及异构资源下的收敛性结论。

Conclusion: 冻结-重采样A是解耦LoRA优化、释放秩资源的有效手段；秩感知学习率是实现该收益的关键配套设计，SPRA-LoRA为通信受限联邦微调提供了理论完备的高效方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large-scale foundation models across resource-constrained, distributed clients presents significant computational and communication challenges. While merging Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with Federated Learning (FL) enables rank-based flexibility, the interdependence of the adaptation matrices ($\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$) often complicates optimization. In this work, we theoretically demonstrate that LoRA fine-tuning with Gaussian initialization and a frozen $\mathbf{A}$ matrix acts as a stochastic approximation of full fine-tuning, where batch gradients are subject to a random, rank-dependent perturbation. Motivated by this insight, we propose freezing and periodically resampling $\mathbf{A}$ matrices at the client level. This strategy decouples the $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ gradients, diminishes LoRA low-rank constraints and reduces both computational and uplink communication overheads by 50\%, allowing for the reallocation of resources toward higher adaptation ranks. Furthermore, we show that in this frozen-$\mathbf{A}$ regime, the adaptation rank $r$ directly governs the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of the perturbed gradients. Specifically, we find that the SNR increases linearly with $r$, implying that clients' learning rates must be dynamically scaled with their adaptation ranks for higher utility. Building on these foundations, we introduce SPRA-LoRA (Stochastic Projection Rank-Aware LoRA), a federated rank-aware fine-tuning strategy that utilizes frozen, resampled $\mathbf{A}$ matrices and rank-dependent learning rates for clients. We also prove its convergence with heterogeneous client resources. Our experiments across benchmark datasets demonstrate that SPRA-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with half the overhead on clients.

</details>


### [320] [Advancing Mass-Conserving Deep Learning for Distributed Hydrologic Modeling: A Case Study in a Snow-Dominated Watershed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13346)
*Yihan Wang,Lujun Zhang,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yang Hong,Tiantian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出质量守恒卷积LSTM框架PECAN（含严格PECAN-MC与松弛PECAN-MCR两版本），用于分布式水文建模并诊断物理约束引导的深度学习模型的结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有质量守恒神经网络多为集总式（lumped）表述，只能提供零散且间接的证据来说明物理约束何时、为何会导致不真实的水文行为，缺乏分布式框架下的直接诊断能力。

Method: 构建基于卷积LSTM的分布式质量守恒框架PECAN，设置两种变体：PECAN-MC采用严格基于降水的守恒约束，PECAN-MCR采用允许降水之外额外水分输入的松弛约束；仅以站点径流（gauge streamflow）训练，在科罗拉多州一个融雪主导流域评估，并与集总DL基线和分布式过程模型VIC对比。

Result: PECAN在出口流量模拟上达到与基线有竞争力的精度，并输出可解释的空间显式隐状态；其单元状态与参考土壤湿度相比呈现合理的时间动态；但分析发现PECAN对积雪过程刻画错误，过早将冬季雪水储量移出系统而非留待春季释放。

Conclusion: PECAN在站点径流模拟上表现准确，并具备暴露水文过程表征结构性缺陷的诊断潜力；研究强调仅依赖径流训练的守恒约束可能"以正确的方式做错的事"，为未来水文物理感知神经网络设计提供指导。

Abstract: Recent progress in physics-aware deep learning (DL) has embedded mass conservation within Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) architecture for hydrologic modeling, improving physical consistency and enabling diagnostics of internal states. However, existing mass-conserving neural networks are primarily developed in lumped formulations. Consequently, previous studies provide only fragmented and often indirect evidence of when and why imposed physical constraints may lead to unrealistic hydrologic behavior. To address this limitation, we propose Physics-Enhanced Conservation-Augmented Net (PECAN), a mass-conserving convolutional LSTM-based framework for distributed hydrologic modeling and diagnosing structural limitations of physics-aware DL models. PECAN is implemented in two variants: (1) PECAN-MC with a strict precipitation-based conservation constraint and (2) PECAN-MCR with a relaxed conservation constraint allowing additional water inputs beyond precipitation. Both variants are trained solely on gauge streamflow and evaluated in a snow-dominated watershed in Colorado. We benchmark PECAN against lumped DL baselines and the distributed process-based Variable Infiltration Capacity (VIC) model. Results show that PECAN achieves competitive outlet streamflow simulation accuracy while producing spatially explicit latent states for interpretation. Comparisons with reference soil moisture indicate that PECAN cell states resemble meaningful temporal soil moisture dynamics. However, analyses also reveal that PECAN misrepresents snow processes by prematurely removing winter snow water storage from the system rather than retaining it for spring release. PECAN demonstrates accurate streamflow simulation at gauges and shows diagnostic potential to expose structural limitations in hydrologic process representation, thereby informing future physics-aware neural network development for hydrology.

</details>


### [321] [When Are Predictions Enough? An Evaluation Protocol for Frozen Expert Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13356)
*Alessandro Pereira,Keith J Ransom,Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可复用的评估协议，在DF40的21个冻结深度伪造检测器上对比"特征融合"与"预测融合"，发现留一生成器评估下特征级逻辑回归比最强预测融合宏AUROC高0.081，且增益主要来自信息可及性而非组合器容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常将独立训练的专家模型冻结后通过最终预测进行集成，但专家内部特征可能保留预测所丢失的有用信息。作者希望厘清：特征融合何时优于预测融合，以及如何做公平比较（现有基准的评估选择可能扭曲结论）。

Method: 提出可复用评估协议：采用强预测基线；比较简单（线性）与更大容量特征组合器；分别变化架构与训练数据；以留一生成器（LOGO）划分报告结果并控制训练/测试重叠。应用于DF40的21个冻结深度伪造检测器，并审计评估选择（同步弱化预测基线、重叠污染的标准划分）对结论的影响。

Result: LOGO评估下，对6个架构多样专家的特征做逻辑回归，每折宏AUROC比最佳测试预测组合器高0.081，8个生成器中有7个增益；更大网络（约3.5M参数 vs. 10K）无明确提升。在受重叠影响的标准划分上弱化预测基线，可使特征优势虚高至0.172 AUROC；重叠感知评估也会改变估计增益，但该对比不能单独分离重叠效应。按生成器与专家池的结果显示优势存在保持、减弱甚至反转的情况。

Conclusion: 当特征保留了预测所遗漏且可迁移到新生成器的信息时，特征融合有益；若最强预测基线已表现良好，预测融合可能足够；两者均无法可靠弥补专家迁移能力差的问题。评估控制缺失时，同一基准可支持不同结论，故需采用重叠感知、强基线、LOGO的报告规范。

Abstract: Practitioners often combine independently trained models while keeping their weights frozen. These experts are commonly combined through their final predictions, although their internal features may retain useful information that those predictions omit. We ask when combining features improves on combining predictions, and how to make that comparison fair. We introduce a reusable evaluation protocol, apply it to 21 frozen deepfake detectors from DF40 (Yan et al., 2024), and audit how evaluation choices change the conclusions. The protocol uses strong prediction baselines, compares simple and larger feature combiners, varies architecture and training data separately, and reports results by held-out generator with train/test overlap controls. Under leave-one-generator-out evaluation, logistic regression on the features of six architecture-diverse experts improves macro AUROC by 0.081 over the best tested prediction combiner in each fold, with gains on seven of eight generators. The larger network shows no clear improvement over the linear model (approximately 3.5M versus 10K parameters), suggesting that access to richer information matters more than additional combiner capacity on this pool. Features may help when they retain information that the predictions omit and that transfers to new generators. Predictions may be enough when the strongest prediction baseline already performs well, while neither approach reliably compensates for poor expert transfer. Weakening prediction baselines on the overlap-affected standard split inflates the apparent feature advantage by up to 0.172 AUROC. Overlap-aware evaluation also changes the estimated gain, though the contrast does not isolate overlap. Results by generator and expert pool show where the advantage holds, weakens, or reverses. Without these controls, the same benchmark can support different conclusions.

</details>


### [322] [Reconstructing Boundary Layer Wind Profiles with Machine Learning: A Comparative Study on IGRA Radiosonde Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13359)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov,Egor Bugaev,Vladimir Gordin,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模IGRA探空数据上系统基准测试了现代表格学习模型与解析参数化方案对大气边界层风廓线的插值能力，并证明精度瓶颈在于预测因子而非模型架构，最后用一维FNO作为后处理校正器进一步降低近地面误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界层风廓线对短临预报与风能选址至关重要，但探空观测稀疏，实践中只能用少量物理预测因子进行插值；需要在现代表格学习模型与传统解析参数化之间建立可比较的基准，并厘清误差究竟来自模型架构还是预测因子信息量。

Method: 基于158,097条IGRA探空样本，对比CatBoost、TabM、FT-Transformer与幂律、Monin-Obukhov解析拟合；通过固定预测因子集做架构消融，并用分箱内廓线离散度的队列分析估计逐点预测的经验误差下限；再将一维Fourier Neural Operator(FNO)作为冻结表格预测的事后校正器。

Result: CatBoost、TabM、FT-Transformer将RMSE降至1.3–1.6 m/s，而幂律与Monin-Obukhov拟合约为3 m/s；固定预测因子后三种架构差异小于0.05 m/s。队列分析给出约1.77 m/s的经验下限，与既有涡黏度反演结果一致。一维FNO事后校正在美国站点将近地面RMSE从1.5降至1.2 m/s，高层误差不变。

Conclusion: 表格学习模型显著优于解析参数化，但收益主要来自预测因子而非架构；约1.77 m/s的经验下限表明逐点预测已接近信息饱和，进一步提升应依赖新的预测因子（如更丰富的廓线/遥感先验），而非更大模型；FNO式结构先验可作为有效的事后校正手段，但增益局限于近地面。

Abstract: Wind profiles in the atmospheric boundary layer (ABL) drive short-range forecasting and wind-energy siting, yet sparse radiosonde coverage forces practitioners to interpolate them from a handful of physical predictors. We benchmark modern tabular learners against analytical parameterizations on 158,097 IGRA soundings. CatBoost, TabM, and FT-Transformer reduce RMSE to 1.3-1.6 m/s, against approximately 3 m/s for power-law and Monin-Obukhov fits; once the predictor set is fixed the three architectures differ by less than 0.05 m/s, so accuracy is driven by predictors rather than architecture. A cohort analysis of within-bin profile dispersion places an empirical floor of approximately 1.77 m/s on any point-wise predictor that uses standard radiosonde features, consistent with prior eddy-viscosity inversions. A one-dimensional Fourier Neural Operator applied as a post-hoc corrector to frozen tabular predictions reduces near-surface RMSE on U.S. stations from 1.5 to 1.2 m/s, while leaving upper-layer error unchanged. Further gains should come from new predictors rather than larger models.

</details>


### [323] [Looping Deeper Without Growing the KV Cache: Recurrent Depth for Causal Encoder-Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13378)
*Jiaqi Leng,Minshen Zhang,Zhaofeng Sun,Yu-Yang Qian,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Looped CED（循环因果编码器-解码器），把循环深度放在编码器侧、只让稀疏编码层产生全局 KV，实现零轮次 KV 增长，在保持长上下文与内存不变的前提下获得循环语言模型的收益，并在 552B 模型上显著提升可服务批量与吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环（looped）语言模型不需增加参数即可加深计算，但跨层、跨轮缓存完整上下文的 KV 状态会严重限制服务时的批大小，导致大模型长请求难以装入单节点显存。

Method: 设计非对称堆栈的循环因果编码器-解码器：仅稀疏编码层生成全局 KV；将循环置于编码器以迭代精炼全局 KV 表示，而非重复读取静态解码器投影；在随机循环深度下训练编码器循环，使其收敛到不动点均衡，从而每轮可无损共享最后一轮的 KV 缓存；并叠加跨层与跨轮的 KV 共享。

Result: Looped CED 达到与无循环 CED 相同的最小内存占用，以 1/6 全局 KV 恢复循环 Transformer 86–93% 的收益；在 4xGB200 单节点上循环 552B 的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 8 轮：逐层逐轮 KV 无法装入单个 128K 请求，而 Looped CED 每 token KV 少 36.8 倍，可容纳 38 个此类请求（与无循环模型相同），8K 提示吞吐提升 3.6 倍。

Conclusion: 循环深度应施加于编码器侧全局 KV 的精炼过程，配合随机深度训练产生的不动点性质可实现无损跨轮 KV 共享，从而在零额外 KV 增长下兼得循环模型的建模收益与生产级服务效率。

Abstract: Looped language models deepen computation without adding parameters, yet caching full-context key-value (KV) states across layers and rounds severely bounds serving batch size. We design a looped causal encoder-decoder (CED) that achieves recurrent depth with zero round-wise KV growth, addressing where and how to loop an asymmetric stack in which only sparse encoder layers generate global KV. We find that recurrence must be placed in the encoder to iteratively refine global KV representations rather than re-reading static decoder projections, which both drives language-modeling gains and preserves long-context scaling. Furthermore, training the encoder loop under stochastic recurrence depth induces a fixed-point equilibrium where every round losslessly shares the last round's KV cache. Compounding cross-layer and cross-round KV sharing, the resulting Looped CED matches an unlooped CED's minimal memory footprint and recovers 86-93% of a looped Transformer's gain with 1/6 of its global KV. To test serving at production scale, we loop the 552B-parameter DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash for 8 rounds on one 4xGB200 node: with per-layer and per-round KV the loop cannot fit a single 128K-token request, whereas Looped CED holds 36.8x less KV per token, fits 38 such requests (as many as the unlooped model), and serves 3.6x higher throughput on 8K-token prompts.

</details>


### [324] [Settling the Burn-in Gap: Sharp Risk Bounds for Tail-Averaged SGD in Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13383)
*Qingyue Zhao,Xuheng Li,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在高维线性回归中研究常数步长、尾平均在线SGD的总超额风险，给出初始化显式的上下界，并证明Zou等(2021)的上下界均不紧：头部初始化的快模态方差在burn-in期指数衰减，但第四矩能量散射到慢模态后无衰减进入平均窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析在有效方差上存在根本性缺口：Zou等(2021)的上界保留了头部特征空间初始误差的无衰减贡献，而下界却完全忽略头部初始化引入的方差，导致上下界无法匹配，亟需刻画初始误差在谱空间中的真实传播机制。

Method: 沿用Zou等的实例相关谱框架，在轨迹谱反馈条件下建立尾平均SGD总超额风险的初始化显式上、下界（以总体协方差谱H和初始误差w0-w*表示）；并证明该谱反馈条件在两类情形下成立：(i)任意协方差谱、任意有限时域，步长依时域显式预先选取；(ii)多项式衰减谱λ_i≍i^{-a}(a>1)，单一与时域无关的正步长；另构造一维与二维分离实例。

Result: 证明现有上下界均宽松：单跳快模态方差在burn-in期指数衰减，而第四矩能量从头部散射至慢模态后在平均窗口内无衰减存留；上下界因此可匹配。分离构造给出Zou等下界中指数级与多项式级之间的差距，并说明仅靠第四矩假设无法确定渐近指数衰减速率，需额外结构条件。

Conclusion: 尾平均SGD的初始化影响需通过跨谱模态的第四矩能量散射而非单跳快模态方差来刻画；所提谱反馈条件在依时域步长与多项式谱固定步长两类设定下可验证，为常数步长SGD的高维精细化风险分析提供了紧致的实例相关刻画。

Abstract: We study the excess risk of constant-stepsize online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with tail-averaging for high-dimensional linear regression. Building on the instance-dependent spectral framework of Zou et al. (2021), we establish matching initialization-explicit upper and lower bounds on the total excess risk of tail-averaged SGD in terms of the population covariance spectrum $\mathbf{H}$ and initialization $\mathbf{w}_0-\mathbf{w}^*$ whenever a trajectory spectral feedback condition holds. Existing analyses exhibit a fundamental gap in the effective variance: the upper bound of Zou et al. (2021) retains an undamped contribution from the initial error in the head eigenspace $\mathbf{I}_{0:k^\dagger}$, whereas their lower bound omits the variance induced by the head initialization altogether. We show that both the existing upper and lower bounds are loose: the single-hop fast-mode variance contribution from the head initialization is exponentially damped during the burn-in phase, whereas fourth-moment energy scattered from the head initialization into slow spectral modes during the early burn-in steps survives undamped into the averaging window. We prove that our spectral feedback condition is satisfied in two concrete constant-stepsize regimes: (i) for arbitrary covariance spectra $\mathbf{H}$ and any finite horizon, under an explicit horizon-dependent stepsize chosen prior to execution; and (ii) for polynomially decaying spectra $λ_i\asymp i^{-a}$ ($a>1$), under a single horizon-independent positive stepsize. Finally, we complement these positive results with explicit one- and two-dimensional separation constructions proving an exponential-versus-polynomial gap in the lower bound of Zou et al. (2021) and showing why fourth-moment assumptions alone cannot determine the asymptotic exponential decay rate without additional structural conditions.

</details>


### [325] [AdjointFlow: Accelerating Scientific Inverse Problems with Physics-Based Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13389)
*Ethan Hanold,Nicholas M. Boffi,Qin Li,Leonardo Zepeda-Núñez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AdjointFlow：基于随机插值的生成框架，将已知物理（伴随）注入生成输运过程，配合数据相关的伴随初始化、正则化速度场与初始化内嵌的网络参数化，在逆散射、地震成像和黑洞成像上比条件扩散模型更准，推理比 PnP 快至多五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典逆问题求解器面临非凸性与昂贵物理模型重复求值的困难；数据驱动的条件扩散模型虽可离线摊销计算，却未充分利用已知物理，且当目标分布集中时输运映射病态；PnP 方法虽借助物理模型保证数据一致性，但重新引入昂贵的迭代计算。

Method: 基于随机插值（stochastic interpolants）构建生成式输运，将物理模型直接注入输运过程：(1) 设计数据依赖的、基于伴随（adjoint）的初始化，缩短到目标的输运距离；(2) 修正输运速度场，使其在端点分布趋于集中时仍保持正则性；(3) 重新参数化网络，把初始化内建到学习到的速度场中。另提供可选的推理期细化，通过查询物理模型进一步提升数据一致性。

Result: 在逆散射、地震成像与黑洞成像实验中，AdjointFlow 的重建精度持续优于条件扩散模型，同时推理速度比 PnP 方法快至多五个数量级；可选的推理期细化可匹配甚至超过 PnP 的精度，且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 将伴随物理先验嵌入生成输运，可在保持/提升重建精度的同时大幅降低推理成本，为物理引导的摊销式逆问题求解提供了一条兼顾数据一致性与效率的路径。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous across scientific applications ranging from seismic imaging to astrophysics to nondestructive testing, yet classical solvers often struggle with nonconvexity and repeated evaluations of expensive physical models. Diffusion models can amortize this computation offline, but largely data-driven conditional models underutilize known physics and suffer from ill-conditioned transport maps when the target distribution is concentrated. Plug-and-play (PnP) methods explicitly leverage the physical model to guide a learned prior, improving data consistency but reintroducing costly iterative computations. We instead propose AdjointFlow, a framework built on stochastic interpolants that injects the known physics into the generative transport itself. We design a data-dependent, adjoint-based initialization to reduce transport distance to the target, modify the transport velocity to remain regular as the endpoint distributions concentrate, and parameterize the network to build the initialization directly into the learned velocity. Together, these enable accurate reconstruction at substantially lower cost than generative models starting from Gaussian noise. Experiments on inverse scattering, seismic imaging, and black hole imaging show that AdjointFlow consistently produces more accurate reconstructions than conditional diffusion models while being up to five orders of magnitude faster at inference than PnP methods. Optional inference-time refinement that queries the physical model can further improve data consistency, matching or exceeding the accuracy of PnP methods without retraining.

</details>


### [326] [Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13414)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj,Shahab Rahimirad,Aliasghar Pourghani,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Abolfazl Hashemi,Joseph Campbell,Vijay Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出利用SMC幂采样解码过程中已估计的配分函数，零边际成本地读出序列级R'enyi熵（SRE），并通过R'enyi链式法则扩展到任意阶的逐步熵族；其中1/2阶成员为最优零成本选择性预测信号，并可指导自适应粒子预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对整条LLM补全的不确定性量化对选择性预测与自适应计算很重要，但现有信号存在局限：均值token熵与对数概率是局部（token级或单条补全级）的，而自一致性需要大量额外采样，成本高。因此需要一个零额外成本的序列级不确定性度量。

Method: 观察到用于幂采样的SMC解码本就在估计幂分布的配分函数，据此在任意粒子预算下都有保证地零边际成本获得模型补全分布的序列级R'enyi熵（SRE）；用R'enyi链式法则把该读数扩展为任意阶的逐步熵族；并证明一阶以上阶数与每步最可能token相关（因此与对数概率同向），而1/2阶成员累积的是每步搜索努力的log。

Result: 1/2阶SRE在13个设置中的10个上是最佳零成本选择性预测信号；在MATH500上，四个模型及长度四分位内AUROC比对数概率高0.022–0.044，且逐设置留一验证下仍选中同一阶数。8粒子探针可识别受益于更多粒子的题目，两遍策略在MATH500上以三随机种子匹配64粒子准确率且粒子用量减少34.2%。

Conclusion: SMC幂采样中的配分函数估计可作为零成本的序列级不确定性读出，1/2阶R'enyi熵是稳健优越的选择性预测信号，并可用于自适应粒子预算分配，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算量。

Abstract: Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R'enyi entropy (SRE) of the model's completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R'enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

</details>


### [327] [You Don't Need Feature Learning to Do In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13431)
*Joseph Turnbull,Jamie Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在旋转不变核机的谱框架下研究ICL线性回归任务中记忆与泛化的关系，发现泛化解本质上是记忆解的"低阶截断"，即欠拟合；模型随训练先泛化后劣化为记忆，且特征学习对ICL并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作观察到ICL模型可能记忆任务（memorization）而非学习通用算法（generalization），但二者在ICL中的界定、发生时机与转换机制缺乏清晰的理论刻画，作者希望给出可分析的谱理论解释。

Method: 沿用Raventos等(2023)提出的ICL线性回归任务，研究旋转不变核机（rotation-invariant kernel machines）在该任务上的学习行为；将任务在核特征基（kernel eigenbasis）中分解，得到学习过程的谱表示，并按阶数分析各分量随样本量增长的演化，最后用实验训练Transformer进行对照验证。

Result: 谱分析显示：随样本量增大，核机依次学习记忆解中越来越高阶的分量，这些分量依赖训练任务集的高阶统计量；泛化解约等于记忆解的低阶截断，因此模型在训练早期先泛化，随后逐步劣化为记忆。实验上，经ICL训练的Transformer确实通过核理论所预测的训练任务高阶统计量来记忆任务。

Conclusion: 三点结论：(1) 若该情形具有代表性，ICL任务上的"泛化解"实质只是对记忆解的欠拟合；(2) 核机能在该任务上泛化，说明特征学习对ICL并非必要；(3) 具备特征学习能力的Transformer也呈现核理论预测的记忆模式，提示该理论具更广适用性。

Abstract: How should we understand memorization vs. generalization in in-context learning (ICL) tasks, and when does one happen vs. the other? To investigate, we study how rotation-invariant kernel machines learn the ICL linear regression task proposed by Raventos et al. (2023). By decomposing the task in the kernel eigenbasis, we obtain a spectral picture of learning in this ICL setting. This picture reveals that as sample size grows, kernel machines learn successively-higher-degree components of the memorizing solution, which depend upon successively-higher-degree statistics of the training task set. The generalizing solution is approximately a low-degree truncation of the memorizing solution, and so the model first generalizes, then degrades into memorization as training proceeds.
  The takeaways are threefold. First, if this case is indicative, learning a "generalizing" solution on an ICL task is really just underfitting the memorizing solution! Second, the fact that kernel machines can generalize on this task means that feature learning is inessential to ICL. Third, transformers empirically trained to do ICL are shown to memorize tasks through the higher-order statistics of the training tasks that the kernel-based theory predicts, suggesting it applies even to models capable of feature learning.

</details>


### [328] [Fermat ProbCover: Coverage-Based Active Learning on Fermat Distance Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13448)
*Gokul Bhusal,Delanna Do,Kevin Miller,Ekaterina Merkurjev,Stephan Wojtowytsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PC-Fermat：用数据自适应的Fermat距离替代欧氏距离定义覆盖邻域，并借助加权欧氏邻域图+截断最短路高效计算，在极低预算下优于现有主动学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低预算场景下基于不确定性的主动学习方法常失效，基于覆盖的方法（如ProbCover）更有效；但ProbCover依赖欧氏距离，难以刻画数据的潜在几何结构与分布。

Method: 以Fermat距离（沿数据分布的路径型数据自适应距离）替代欧氏距离定义覆盖邻域；构建加权欧氏邻域图，用截断最短路算法计算Fermat覆盖图；并给出该图构造与完全图上Fermat覆盖计算等价的充分条件。

Result: 在8个分类数据集、不同标注预算（尤其极低预算）下评估，PC-Fermat在多数数据集上优于若干现有主动学习方法。

Conclusion: Fermat距离能为覆盖式主动学习提供更贴合数据几何的覆盖度量，且可通过邻域图上的截断最短路近似实现，兼具效果与可计算性。

Abstract: Active learning aims to reduce annotation cost by selecting the most informative examples for labeling. In the low-budget regime, where uncertainty-based methods often fail, coverage-based active learning methods such as ProbCover have been shown to be effective. However, these methods typically rely on Euclidean distance, which may not adequately capture the underlying geometry of data. In this article, we propose PC-Fermat, a coverage-based active learning method that replaces Euclidean coverage neighborhoods with neighborhoods defined by a Fermat distance, i.e., a data-adaptive distance that reflects the underlying distribution of the data points. To facilitate computation, we construct a weighted Euclidean neighborhood graph and use a truncated shortest-path algorithm to compute the Fermat coverage graph. We also establish sufficient conditions under which this construction exactly preserves the Fermat coverage computed on the complete graph. Finally, we evaluate PC-Fermat on eight classification datasets across varying labeling budgets, with particular emphasis on the very low-budget regime. Our experimental results demonstrate that PC-Fermat outperforms several existing active learning methods on the majority of the datasets.

</details>


### [329] [SLIC: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning Small LMs for Multi-Turn Analog Circuit Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13452)
*Yan Xu,Zhengqi Gao,Ching-Yun Ko,Zhang-Wei Hong,Wenjie Lu,Tao Yu,Xin Zhang,Duane S. Boning,Ruonan Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLIC 是一个强化微调框架，训练 4B 规模可本地部署的小语言模型完成闭环模拟电路优化；在开源 65nm PTM 上训练即达 87.1% 分布内 Pass@1，并可在未见拓扑与五个未见工艺节点及商用 TSMC 65nm PDK 上迁移，无需外传专有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟 IC 设计需在耦合的性能折衷上进行多轮、以仿真器为依据的迭代推理，而专有 PDK（工艺设计套件）使得依赖云端托管大模型的工作流难以在实际设计中部署。作者希望用可本地推理的小模型替代托管大模型，同时解决该领域反馈稀疏、约束强的优化难题。

Method: 提出 SLIC（Small LM Iterating on Circuits）强化微调框架，核心包含：结构化电路表示 + 离散化相对更新动作；领域与成功双重门控的多目标奖励；奖励引导的 rollout 引导机制（RGRS），将每步仿真器反馈同时用于策略学习与在线 rollout 构造。并发布含 41 个放大器拓扑的目标条件基准，含分布内验证、留出拓扑评估、跨工艺节点迁移三套标准协议。

Result: 仅用开源 65nm CMOS 预测技术模型（PTM）训练：SLIC-Random 在八轮内取得 87.1% 的四指标 Pass@1，超过 763B 的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think 达 48.5 个百分点；SLIC-Softmax 在留出拓扑上 Pass@1 达 72.6%；策略无需额外微调即可迁移到五个未见工艺节点，并延伸至商用 TSMC 65nm PDK，留出拓扑 Pass@1 为 49.9%；测试时增加仿真交互可将留出拓扑 Pass@1 提升至 83.2%（32 轮预算）。

Conclusion: 小型、可本地部署的语言模型经强化微调后可在闭环模拟电路优化中显著优于超大托管模型，并具备跨拓扑、跨工艺节点的泛化与向商用 PDK 的实用迁移能力，实现不在外部 LLM 服务上传专有数据的安全部署；性能随测试时仿真器交互预算增加而进一步提升。

Abstract: Analog integrated circuit (IC) design is a multi-turn, simulator-grounded engineering task that demands iterative reasoning over coupled performance tradeoffs, yet proprietary process design kits (PDKs) make hosted large language model workflows difficult to deploy in practical design settings. We present SLIC (Small LM Iterating on Circuits), a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that trains a 4B-scale, locally deployable language model for closed-loop analog circuit optimization. SLIC pairs structured circuit representations with discretized relative-update actions, domain- and success-gated multiobjective rewards, and Reward-Guided Rollout Steering (RGRS), which turns per-step simulator feedback into signals for both policy learning and online rollout construction in this sparse, highly constrained domain. We further release an open, goal-conditioned benchmark of 41 amplifier topologies with standardized protocols for in-distribution validation, held-out topology evaluation, and cross-technology-node transfer. Trained solely with open source 65 nm CMOS predictive technology models (PTM), SLIC-Random achieves 87.1% in-distribution four-specification Pass@1 within eight turns, surpassing the 763B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash Think by 48.5 percentage points. Beyond the training distribution, SLIC-Softmax reaches 72.6% Pass@1 on held-out topologies, and the trained policies consistently transfer across five unseen technology nodes without additional fine-tuning. This transfer extends to a commercial TSMC 65 nm CMOS PDK, where SLIC-Random achieves 49.9% held-out-topology Pass@1, demonstrating practical deployment through local inference without transmitting proprietary data to external LLM services. Performance further scales with test-time simulator interactions, reaching up to 83.2% held-out-topology Pass@1 at a 32-turn budget.

</details>


### [330] [Deep Personalization: Continuous Context Observation for Local Proactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13457)
*Yijia Shao,Song Bian,Junxian Guo,Jindong Jiang,Zhen Dong,Huizi Mao,Diyi Yang,Song Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 "深度个性化"(deep personalization) 闭环框架,使主动式智能体能通过持续观察用户活动、选择性保留长期上下文并从隐式反馈中学习干预时机;在 Coco 桌面智能体中以独立的 Observer Agent 实现并本地化运行,OSWorld-Persona 基准上 macro F1 由 0.12–0.19 提升至 0.35–0.48(2.4–3.0 倍),一周用户研究中由 0.199 提升至 0.515。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户往往难以判断何时、如何求助,导致大量可被支持的机会未被兑现。主动式智能体有望弥合这一缺口,但有效主动性依赖对用户实时活动的理解与干预时机的个性化——需在"有帮助"与"造成干扰"之间取得平衡。现有方法缺乏将连续上下文观察与智能体持续自适应相连接的闭环机制,也缺乏大规模、保护隐私的评测手段。

Method: 1) 提出 deep personalization 闭环:智能体观察用户活动→选择性保留长期上下文→从隐式反馈中持续调整干预决策。2) 在主动式桌面智能体 Coco 中引入独立的 Observer Agent,监控桌面活动、维护紧凑的长期用户建模上下文,并基于参与度、忽略/关闭、显式请求、重复工作模式等隐式信号学习"何时帮助是可取的"。3) 设计高效的端侧(on-device)策略,使 Observer Agent 可运行于本地 AI 算力平台,原始活动数据不出设备。4) 构建数据合成管线,将已有的 computer-use 轨迹转化为带 persona 条件干预标签的基准 OSWorld-Persona,以支持无需采集真实敏感用户数据的大规模评测。

Result: OSWorld-Persona 上,三个模型骨干的 macro F1 从直接提示的 0.12–0.19 提升至自适应后的 0.35–0.48,相对提升 2.4–3.0 倍。为期一周的用户研究中,Coco 的宏平均 F1 从 0.199 提升至 0.515。

Conclusion: 端侧深度个性化可使主动式智能体的干预更及时,并逐步与每位用户的日常工作方式对齐;结果同时表明隐私保护的本地运行与可扩展的合成基准是可行路径。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, users must still recognize when and how to ask for help, leaving many opportunities for useful support unrealized. Proactive agents hold the promise to close this gap, but effective proactivity requires understanding users' real-time activities and personalizing intervention timing so that assistance is offered when it is likely to be helpful rather than disruptive. We introduce deep personalization, a closed-loop process that connects continuous context observation with continual agent adaptation: an agent observes user activity, selectively retains long-term context, and adapts intervention decisions from implicit feedback. We initiate deep personalization in Coco, a proactive desktop agent, by having a separate Observer Agent that monitors desktop activity, maintains compact long-term user modeling context, and learns when assistance is desirable from implicit signals such as engagement, dismissal, explicit requests, and repeated work patterns. We further develop efficient on-device strategies to enable the Observer Agent to run on local AI compute platforms without streaming raw activity data off-device. For evaluation at scale without collecting sensitive real-user activity, we introduce a data-synthesis pipeline that transforms existing computer-use trajectories into OSWorld-Persona, a benchmark with persona-conditioned intervention labels. On OSWorld-Persona, deep personalization improves macro F1 across three model backbones from 0.12-0.19 under direct prompting to 0.35-0.48 after adaptation, a 2.4-3.0x relative gain. In a week-long user study, we observe that Coco improves its macro-averaged F1 score from 0.199 to 0.515. These results suggest that on-device deep personalization can make proactive agents more timely and increasingly aligned with each user's everyday work.

</details>


### [331] [Leveraging Psychophysical Attentional Distribution for Gaze-Augmented Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13477)
*Chaeho Lee,Junyup Kim,Wansoo Kim,Young Min Jung,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在RLHF奖励模型中引入认知心理学关于字母识别的结论，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视点为中心的空间梯度分布，并用于奖励模型训练，取得更高的偏好预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作将眼动（ET）数据作为额外监督信号训练奖励模型，使奖励学习扎根于人类阅读行为；但先前模型多采用"注视点中心"式的离散建模，未充分利用人类字母识别中注意分配的空间分布特性，因此需要更贴合认知机制的表征。

Method: 扩展以注视点为中心的模型：依据认知心理学中人类字母识别的既有发现，将视觉注意建模为以当前注视位置为中心的空间梯度分布（spatially graded distribution），并把该分布表征引入奖励模型训练。

Result: 使用所提出的注视分布训练的奖励模型，其偏好预测准确率高于基线模型；部分实验定性结果与阅读中人类注意的既有发现一致，提示该表征可能捕捉到注意分配中具有认知意义的成分。

Conclusion: 以空间梯度分布刻画注视注意可有效提升基于眼动监督的奖励模型偏好预测性能，并具有与人类阅读认知相吻合的可解释性，为将认知心理学先验融入RLHF对齐提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Integrating human feedback into language models has gained increasing attention as a means to align model outputs with human preferences, with Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) serving as a prominent framework for preference-based alignment. Recent studies have incorporated eye-tracking (ET) data as an additional supervisory signal for reward model training, grounding reward learning in human reading behavior. Building on this approach, we extend prior fixation-centric models by leveraging established findings from cognitive psychology on human letter recognition. Specifically, we model visual attention as a spatially graded distribution centered on the current gaze location and incorporate this representation into reward model training. Our results show that the reward model trained with the proposed gaze distribution achieves higher preference prediction accuracy than a baseline model. Moreover, some results from our experiments are qualitatively consistent with established findings on human attention during reading, suggesting that they potentially capture cognitively meaningful aspects of human attention allocation.

</details>


### [332] [Sharp Sample Complexity of KL-Regularized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13480)
*Andrew Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究KL正则化强化学习（最大熵RL与RLHF的目标）在“控制即推断”图模型下的后验学习样本复杂度，给出折扣MDP中plug-in估计的样本上界与匹配下界，并刻画了渐近常数与最优采样分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化RL的最优策略可视为轨迹上的后验，策略次优性等于路径测度间KL散度乘以β，但即使有生成模型，学习该后验所需样本数未知；此前1/ε型保证在有效时域H上依赖H^5，且MDP下界均不依赖温度β，温度与样本复杂度关系不清。

Method: 针对含K个状态-动作对的折扣MDP，分析plug-in估计器的误差；构造下界（包括可自适应选择采样位置的算法）；推导plug-in估计的精确渐近常数（估计软Q函数的Fisher加权方差），并研究等量采样方法的局部渐近minimax性与自适应设计下的最优样本分配。

Result: plug-in估计器能以Õ(KH^3/(ε·max{ε,β}) + KH)样本从每个初始状态找到ε最优策略；当ε≲min{H, H^2/β}时任何算法（含自适应采样）都无法更少。ε<β时为1/ε速率，且时域依赖与非正则化RL相同。给出精确渐近常数，证明等量采样下plug-in估计局部渐近minimax，并给出自适应最优分配；小规模MDP仿真与速率和常数吻合。

Conclusion: KL正则化RL后验学习的样本复杂度被精确刻画：上界与下界匹配，温度β通过max{ε,β}进入速率，ε<β时回到标准1/ε速率与非正则化时域依赖；并提供了渐近最优的估计器性质与采样设计指导。

Abstract: KL-regularized reinforcement learning, the objective behind maximum-entropy RL and RL from human feedback, is variational inference in the graphical model of "control as inference": the regularized optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories, and the suboptimality of a policy is $β$ times a KL divergence between path measures. However, it is not known how many samples it takes to learn this posterior, even with a generative model. Earlier $1/ε$ guarantees scale with $H^5$ in the effective horizon $H$, and no lower bound for MDPs depends on the temperature $β$. In this work, we show that for a discounted MDP with $K$ state-action pairs, the plug-in estimator finds a policy that is $ε$-optimal from every initial state with $\widetilde{O}(KH^3/(ε\max\{ε,β\}) + KH)$ samples, and that no algorithm, even one that chooses where to sample, can do with fewer when $ε\lesssim \min\{H, H^2/β\}$. For $ε< β$ this is a $1/ε$ rate with the same horizon dependence as unregularized RL. We also find the exact asymptotic constant of the plug-in estimator, a Fisher-weighted variance of the estimated soft Q-function. The estimator is locally asymptotically minimax among methods that sample every pair equally often, and we give the allocation of samples that is optimal among adaptive designs. Simulations on small MDPs match the rates and the constants.

</details>


### [333] [Evaluating Compositional Generalization in Transformers: The Role of Composition Equivalence and Module Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13498)
*Purva Pruthi,Andrew Yuan,Alexander D'Amour,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统性评估 Transformer 的组合泛化能力，发现"组合等价性"与"模块覆盖"是决定性能的关键分布因素，现有基准因含组合等价性而高估直接模型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合泛化（在部分模块组合上训练、泛化到未见组合）是重要的分布外泛化形式，但已有针对 Transformer 的实证结论不一致，原因在于评估仅依赖少量、临时指定的训练-测试划分，缺乏系统性的基准设计原则。

Method: 通过系统性评估识别训练-测试分布中两个关键维度：（1）组合等价性，即不同模块序列归约为相同端到端函数；（2）模块覆盖，即同一模块在训练与测试中出现于不同上下文。设计相应基准变体，比较仅用最终输出训练的"直接模型"与使用中间及最终输出训练的"逐步模型"，并以 Llama3-8B 在 LiveCodeBench 代码输出预测任务上作为案例研究。

Result: 直接模型的表面性能主要来自利用组合等价性；当基准消除等价性后性能显著下降，表明其无法泛化到"由已知模块构成但产生全新端到端函数"的组合。性能随模块覆盖偏移增大而单调下降，直接模型与逐步模型均受影响。

Conclusion: 对基准设计具有明确启示：含组合等价性的基准会显著高估直接模型的泛化能力；同时含模块覆盖违背与组合等价性的基准会加剧捷径学习。案例研究证实组合等价性影响预训练模型 Llama3-8B 的泛化表现。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is an important form of out-of-distribution generalization, defined as the ability to train on some combinations of modules (e.g., functions such as sort, filter) and then generalize to unseen combinations. Despite substantial empirical work evaluating compositional generalization in transformers, findings have been inconsistent because evaluations were often conducted on a limited set of train-test splits, defined in an ad hoc manner. Through a systematic evaluation, we identify two key aspects of the train-test distribution that strongly affect performance: composition equivalence and module coverage. We show that the apparent performance of direct models (trained only on final outputs) results primarily from exploiting composition equivalences, meaning different sequences of modules that reduce to identical end-to-end functions. When benchmarks eliminate these equivalences, performance drops significantly, showing their inability to generalize to compositions of known modules that produce novel end-to-end functions. We further demonstrate that performance degrades monotonically as module coverage shifts increase (when modules appear in different contexts between train and test), affecting both direct and step-by-step models (trained on intermediate and final outputs). These findings have concrete implications for benchmark design: benchmarks that include compositional equivalences can lead to significant overestimation of the generalization abilities of direct models, and benchmarks that include both module coverage violations and compositional equivalences can exacerbate shortcut learning.
