As a case study, we demonstrate the role of compositional equivalence on the generalization performance of the pre-trained model Llama3-8B on the code-output prediction task in the coding benchmark LiveCodeBench.

</details>


### [334] [Parallel Broyden Methods for Efficiently Evaluating Nonlinear State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13523)
*Ian Christopher Tanoh,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿方法，用"对角+低秩"分块近似Jacobian以捕捉状态维度间的耦合，通过轨迹割线构造近似、避免自动微分并借助秩压缩兼容并行扫描，理论上证明有限步精确恢复与误差刻画收敛性，实验中收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性状态空间模型的顺序求值方式无法利用现代并行硬件。已有工作将求值重构为求根问题并用并行Newton法求解，但精确Newton需要昂贵的稠密Jacobian矩阵乘法；基于对角近似的拟牛顿法虽可扩展，却因忽略状态维度间交互而收敛缓慢。

Method: 引入基于Broyden方法的拟牛顿方法，对Jacobian的分块采用"对角+低秩"（diagonal-plus-low-rank）近似以刻画耦合项；由轨迹割线（trajectory secants）构造近似，避免自动微分，同时保持对角近似良好的扩展性，并通过秩压缩（rank compression）与并行扫描兼容。

Result: 理论上证明在压缩扫描下可有限步恢复精确轨迹，并说明收敛性由压缩误差与沿当前轨迹误差方向的块近似误差追踪，而非谱范数。实验表明：在低秩RNN、并行MCMC算法与去噪扩散模型上，迭代次数少于其他拟牛顿方法；当Jacobian求值昂贵或状态维度较大时，是并行方法中最快的一种。其优势场景为交互近似低秩或Jacobian求值昂贵。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性状态空间模型的并行求值提供了兼顾可扩展性与收敛速度的拟牛顿框架，"对角+低秩"分块近似在秩压缩下保持有限步精确性，并在低秩交互或高代价Jacobian场景中展现显著优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear state space models are typically evaluated sequentially, limiting their ability to exploit modern parallel hardware. Recent work has shown that state space models can be evaluated in parallel by reformulating evaluation as a root-finding problem, which can be solved with a parallel form of Newton's method. However, exact Newton methods require expensive multiplication of dense Jacobian matrices, and quasi-Newton methods based on diagonal approximations, while more scalable, are often slow to converge because they neglect interactions across state dimensions. We introduce a novel quasi-Newton approach based on Broyden's method, which captures coupling terms with diagonal-plus-low-rank approximations to blocks of the Jacobian. We construct the approximations from trajectory secants, avoiding automatic differentiation while retaining the favorable scaling of diagonal approximations and remaining compatible with parallel scan through rank compression. We prove finite-step recovery of the exact trajectory under the compressed scan, and show that convergence is tracked by the compression error and by the block approximation error along the current trajectory error, rather than in spectral norm. Empirically, we show that our method is advantageous when interactions are approximately low rank or when Jacobian evaluation is expensive: on low-rank RNNs, parallel MCMC algorithms, and denoising diffusion models, it converges in fewer iterations than other quasi-Newton methods and is the fastest parallel method when Jacobian evaluation is expensive or state dimensions are large.

</details>


### [335] [The RSI Tax: When Recursive Self-Improvement Is Not Worth the Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13524)
*Yitong Zhang,Ang Li,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"RSI Tax"（递归自我改进的token成本）指标，在连续任务流上对六种方法与两个语言模型进行端到端评测，发现轻量的bounded-note基线在准确率上不逊于Meta-Harness、EvoSkill、RRSI，且总token节省65.6–71.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）用自身任务经验提升智能体，但其实际价值取决于性能收益能否覆盖"自我改进+任务执行"的全部成本。现有评测通常只关注能力提升而忽略改进过程本身的代价，缺乏对成本-收益权衡的量化。

Method: 在连续任务流上评测，每个任务在反馈被用于后续更新之前先行打分，避免信息泄漏。定义RSI Tax为自我改进过程的token成本，与任务执行成本一并计入端到端评测。跨6种方法、2个语言模型对比；将RSI Tax分解为验证（verification）、探索（exploration）与写入（writing）三类成本，并追踪所生成状态/工件在后续任务中的实际使用情况。

Result: bounded-note基线在观察到的准确率上达到或超过Meta-Harness、EvoSkill与RRSI，同时平均总token节省65.6–71.0%。对三种RSI方法，验证成本占聚合RSI Tax的74.8–93.6%。相当比例的RSI Tax花费在后续任务流中并未被使用的状态或工件上。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了昂贵RSI流程的实际局限：部署智能体的性能提升未必能证明改进过程开销的合理性。作者据此呼吁设计同时优化RSI Tax与期望任务执行成本、并保证智能体能力在任务流上持续增长的RSI目标。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) seeks to improve an agent using its own task experience. Its practical value depends on whether performance gains justify the full cost of self-improvement and task execution. We evaluate this trade-off on continual task streams, scoring each task before its feedback is used for subsequent updates. We define the \emph{RSI Tax} as the token cost of the self-improvement process and include it alongside task-execution costs in end-to-end evaluation. Across six methods and two language models, a bounded-note baseline matches or exceeds the observed accuracy of Meta-Harness, EvoSkill, and RRSI, with mean total-token savings of 65.6-71.0\%. We decompose the RSI Tax into verification, exploration, and writing costs, and track subsequent use of the associated state or artifact. For these three RSI methods, verification accounts for 74.8--93.6% of the aggregate RSI Tax. A substantial share of the RSI Tax is also associated with states or artifacts that are not used later in the evaluated streams. These results expose a practical limitation of costly RSI procedures: improvements in the deployed agent need not justify the expense of the improvement process. They motivate RSI objectives that jointly optimize the RSI Tax and expected task-execution cost while ensuring continued growth in agent capabilities across the task stream.

</details>


### [336] [Beyond the Log-Linear Frontier: Pre-Asymptotic Analysis of Finite-Budget Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13534)
*Tianyu Zhao,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究有限预算下神经缩放律偏离经典可分幂律的原因，从模型容量与数据规模两个轴分别用Mellin–Barnes协方差预解分析、Aitken差分初始化、KKT对偶水填充与Schur补分解，给出ISO-compute前沿上最优分配指数随指数方差漂移的理论刻画，并在45个算子谱、Pythia、Chinchilla与RegMix上验证估计精度与分配收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典缩放律将超额风险建模为关于模型容量N与数据量D的可分幂律，但在有限预算下，经验学习曲线与算力最优分配明显偏离这一对数线性前沿。现有做法在窄试点网格上拟合多个幂律存在病态问题，且缺乏对跨域数据混合、有限预算瓶颈及等算力前沿上分配指数漂移的统一理论解释，因此需要刻画谱异质性与域异质性如何导致有限尺度偏离。

Method: 容量轴：对协方差预解进行Mellin–Barnes分析，得到三项展开，其中竞争性的谱模式与正则化模式产生有限尺度曲率与对数型转变；针对窄试点网格上多幂律拟合病态的问题，用一阶Aitken差分消去损失下限，获得良态的闭式初始化。数据轴：通过KKT对偶将K域混合化为标量水填充，导出有限预算瓶颈界、在线采样课程，以及用于跨域迁移的单纯形Hessian度量。在等算力前沿C=6ND上，证明最优分配指数漂移正比于容量与数据指数方差，并用Schur补约化将内在漂移与冻结超参偏差分离。

Result: 在45个算子谱、Pythia（70M–12B）、Chinchilla（245次运行）与RegMix（17个域）上，所提估计器达到5.89%的留出集MAPE，将高算力分配遗憾最多降低76.4%，跨尺度混合RMSE降低59.4%，且选择遗憾为零。

Conclusion: 有限尺度下缩放律的偏离可由谱异质性与域异质性定量解释：容量轴上的多模式竞争与数据轴上的水填充结构共同决定有限预算行为，等算力前沿的最优分配指数漂移由指数方差主导。所提差分初始化与Schur补分解使估计在窄网格上良态，实验证明其在预测精度、分配效率与跨域混合外推上均显著优于基线。

Abstract: Classical neural scaling laws model excess risk as a separable power law in model capacity $N$ and dataset size $D$. At finite budgets, however, empirical learning curves and compute-optimal allocations depart from this log-linear frontier. This paper analyzes how spectral and domain heterogeneity govern these finite-scale departures. Along the capacity axis, Mellin--Barnes analysis of covariance resolvents yields a three-term expansion where competing spectral and regularization modes produce finite-scale curvature and logarithmic transitions. Because fitting multiple power laws on narrow pilot grids is ill-conditioned, we eliminate the loss floor via first-order Aitken differencing to obtain a well-conditioned closed-form initializer. Along the data axis, KKT duality reduces $K$-domain mixing to scalar water-filling, yielding finite-budget bottleneck bounds, an online sampling curriculum, and a simplex Hessian metric for cross-domain transfer. Along the iso-compute frontier $C=6ND$, we prove that the optimal allocation exponent drifts in proportion to capacity and data exponent variances, while Schur-complement reduction separates intrinsic drift from frozen-hyperparameter bias. Across 45 operator spectra, Pythia (70M--12B), Chinchilla (245 runs), and RegMix (17 domains), our estimators achieve $5.89\%$ holdout MAPE, cut high-compute allocation regret by up to $76.4\%$, and reduce cross-scale mixture RMSE by $59.4\%$ with zero selection regret.

</details>


### [337] [Adaptive catalyst discovery emerges from composable scientific intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13536)
*Wentao Li,Qilong Cai,Qi Lei,Jinxing Chen,Ruixuan Chen,Jiangjie Qiu,Botian Wang,Kai Zhao,Honghao Chen,Leyi Zhao,Zemeng Wang,Yijun Li,Jun Yin,Fangfang Li,Hongxuan Liu,Yingdong Lu,Yawen Ouyang,Manu Suvarna,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Kostya S. Novoselov,Hao Zhou,Jiong Lu,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理基础驱动的模块化AI框架CatAI，从180万条构效关系学习并组织为30个物理化学编码器，形成可复用的中间层，支撑面向目标反应与目标的催化剂发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催化剂设计知识分散于实验观测、计算数据与催化各分支学科之间，缺乏可跨体系复用的统一表征与知识组织方式，制约了数据驱动的催化剂发现效率。

Method: 构建Composable Catalytic Intelligence（CatAI）：以物理规律为基础，学习180万条结构-性质关系，聚类/组织为30个物理化学编码器（physicochemical encoders）；该可复用中间层可组合成任务专用模型，用于计算筛选与自主闭环优化的模型-实验迭代反馈。

Result: 基准任务上任务平均预测误差比次优模型低7.8%；实验验证中发现一种可通过受限空间口袋的新型催化剂，用于工业均相聚合物合成，活性为商业基准的9倍；并指导Cu基孪生原子光催化剂（C-O偶联）的自主闭环优化，性能超越传统Ni基体系。

Conclusion: CatAI将性质级数据与催化目标相连接，实现跨机理不同体系的物理化学知识复用，并通过计算筛选与迭代实验反馈支持催化剂发现与优化。

Abstract: Catalysis underpins chemical manufacturing, yet knowledge for catalyst design remains dispersed across experimental observations, computational data and catalysis subdisciplines. To overcome this challenge, we present Composable Catalytic Intelligence (CatAI), a physics-grounded modular AI framework that learns 1.8 million structure-property relations and organizes them into 30 physicochemical encoders. This reusable intermediate layer enables task-specific models to guide catalyst discovery for targeted reactions and objectives. Across benchmark tasks, CatAI achieves a 7.8% lower task-averaged prediction error than the next-best models. In experimental validation, CatAI identified a new confined-passable pocket catalyst applicable in industrial homogeneous polymer synthesis with 9 times the activity of the commercial benchmark. In parallel, it guided autonomous closed-loop optimization of a highly active Cu-based geminal-atom photocatalyst for C-O coupling reactions surpassing the traditional Ni-based systems. This approach connects property-level data to catalytic objectives, supporting physicochemical knowledge reuse across mechanistically distinct systems through computational screening and iterative model-experiment feedback.

</details>


### [338] [Sanity Checks for Attention Surgery: Null Models and Matched Interventions for Representation-Motivated Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13543)
*Basavaraj R M,Aryan Bharat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出三项可复用检验（是否超过噪声下限、是否通过几何零假设、训练增益是否依赖被移除的特定方向），并用于检验自注意力移除（删除注意力头输出中与其自身value向量对齐的分量）：在9个多头预训练模型（5,408个头，124M–6.9B）中，前期位置因果零假设可解释48%–63%的头-value余弦对齐；GQA下该对齐具有组内特异性；仅当移除自身value方向（而非固定任意rank-one方向）时才获得训练增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构改动的常见范式是"测量某内部统计量→发现其很大→架构上移除它→报告性能提升"。这种推断链条存在三个未受检验的隐含前提：统计量是否显著高于噪声下限、是否可由几何结构（如位置因果性）平凡解释、以及训练收益是否真的依赖于所移除的具体方向。作者希望将这一诊断流程形式化为可复用的检验，并以自注意力移除为案例进行严格验证。

Method: 提出三项检验：(1) 噪声下限检验；(2) 几何零假设检验——构造早期位置因果零模型（earlier-position causal null）；(3) 方向特异性检验。研究对象为"自注意力移除"：删除注意力头输出中与其自身value向量对齐的分量。实验涵盖9个多头预训练模型（5,408个头，124M–6.9B参数），分析GQA（分组查询注意力）下组内/组间对齐差异，并用分半信度（split-half reliability）比较各统计量与其干预效应的可分辨性。训练实验采用预注册的3臂×8种子配对因子设计，每run 4.0×10⁸ tokens（合计399,900,672 tokens）。

Result: 因果零假设解释了48%–63%的头-value余弦对齐，且无单调的规模趋势。GQA下对齐具组内特异性并跨组反转（组内+0.24~+0.27，组间−0.11~−0.19）。分半信度：逐头干预效应在GPT-2可分辨（r_Δ=0.80），在Pythia-160M（0.42）与Pythia-410M（0.53）被衰减，而结构性自余弦统计量在三者中近乎完美（r_stat>0.99），说明"哪个统计量能预测自身干预效应"依赖于模型。训练实验中，固定任意rank-one方向与基线无差异（+0.0011 nats, p=0.197）；自定向移除降低验证损失（−0.0029 nats, Holm校正p=0.0023），并显著优于固定任意对照（−0.0040 nats, p=0.0018）。

Conclusion: 在50.9M规模上，训练收益确实依赖自身value方向而非固定任意方向，说明方向特异性成立；但大的余弦对齐本身并不能证明冻结模型中的冗余性——对齐在很大程度上可由几何零假设解释，且高可靠性的统计量未必能预测其干预效应。因此，基于"统计量大→移除→性能提升"的架构改动结论必须经过噪声下限、几何零假设与方向特异性三重检验。

Abstract: Transformer architectural modifications frequently measure a statistic on internal representations, observe that it is large, remove it architecturally, and report an improvement. Interpreting that sequence requires evaluating whether the statistic exceeds its noise floor, whether it survives a geometric null, and whether the training gain requires the specific direction removed. We formulate these requirements as three reusable checks and apply them to self-attention removal, which deletes the component of an attention head's output aligned with its own value vector. Across nine multi-head pretrained models (5,408 heads, 124M to 6.9B parameters), an earlier-position causal null accounts for 48% to 63% of the head-value cosine alignment, with no monotone scale trend. Under grouped-query attention, this alignment is specific to each head's own key-value group and reverses across groups (+0.24 to +0.27 within-group, -0.11 to -0.19 across-group). Split-half reliability demonstrates that the per-head intervention effect is resolvable in GPT-2 ($r_Δ= 0.80$) but attenuated in Pythia-160M (0.42) and Pythia-410M (0.53), whereas structural self-cosine reliability is near-perfect in all three ($r_{\text{stat}} > 0.99$); which statistic predicts its own intervention is model-dependent. In a pre-registered 3-arm by 8-seed paired factorial at $4.0 \times 10^8$ tokens per run (399,900,672 tokens), a fixed arbitrary rank-one direction does not differ from baseline (+0.0011 nats, $p = 0.197$), while self-directed removal lowers validation loss (-0.0029 nats, Holm-corrected $p = 0.0023$) and outperforms the fixed arbitrary control directly (-0.0040 nats, $p = 0.0018$). At 50.9M scale the training benefit requires the own-value direction rather than a fixed arbitrary one, but a large cosine alignment alone does not establish redundancy in frozen models.

</details>


### [339] [Closing the BF16-FP16 Convergence Speed Gap in RL Post-Training with Stochastic Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13545)
*Mahdi Nazemi,Oleg Rybakov,Tijmen Blankevoort,Asit Mishra,Carlo del Mundo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中，学习率偏低时 BF16 混合精度训练收敛慢于 FP16，原因是"权重粘滞"（weight sticking）；改用随机舍入（stochastic rounding）将 FP32 影子权重量化到 BF16，可在不同模型与数据集上追平 FP16 的收敛速度，同时降低相对参考模型的 KL 散度，并保留 BF16 的动态范围优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BF16 因与 FP32 动态范围一致、收敛行为接近 FP32 而被广泛用于大模型预训练。但在 RLVR 场景中学习率通常很低，作者观察到同学习率下标准 BF16 混合精度训练明显慢于 FP16，需要解释该现象并给出低成本的修复方案，同时避免为提速而放大学习率带来的 KL 散度上升。

Method: 将性能差距归因于权重粘滞：FP32 影子权重经 round-to-nearest 量化为 BF16 时，小幅更新常不足以改变用于 rollout 生成与训练的 BF16 权重，导致多个优化器步后更新才生效。对比方案包括：(1) 提高 BF16 学习率；(2) 在 FP32→BF16 量化中使用随机舍入，使微小更新以概率方式被体现。

Result: 提高学习率可缓解权重粘滞并使 BF16 收敛速度接近 FP16，但会增大相对参考模型的 KL 散度；随机舍入则在多模型、多数据集上达到与 FP16 相当的收敛速度，且 KL 散度低于"放大学习率的 BF16"基线。

Conclusion: 随机舍入是一种简单有效的低精度 RLVR 训练改进：兼具 FP16 级收敛速度、更低的策略漂移，并保留 BF16 更宽动态范围、无需 loss scaling 的工程优势。

Abstract: BF16 is widely used to pretrain large language models because it has the same dynamic range as FP32 and converges similarly to FP32 training. However, in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where learning rates are typically low, we find that standard mixed-precision BF16 training converges more slowly than FP16 training at the same learning rate. We attribute this gap to weight sticking: when FP32 shadow weights are quantized to BF16 using round-to-nearest, small updates often leave the BF16 weights used for rollout generation and training unchanged. Consequently, several optimizer steps may be needed before updates to the shadow weights are reflected in the BF16 weights. Raising the BF16 learning rate reduces weight sticking and matches FP16's convergence speed, but it also increases the KL divergence from the reference model. We instead apply stochastic rounding when quantizing the FP32 shadow weights to BF16. This approach matches FP16's convergence speed across different models and datasets while reducing the KL divergence relative to BF16 training with a larger learning rate. Unlike FP16, it keeps BF16's wider dynamic range and needs no loss scaling.

</details>


### [340] [MouldIt: Data Efficiency of Multi-Task Temporal Representation Learning in Injection Moulding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13646)
*Abdullah Ayad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向注塑成型过程的时序卷积+注意力变分自编码器MouldIt，在合成数据集上验证其数据效率与多模态优势，单模态4000周期、多模态2000周期起优于kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林与PCA等经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工业方案需同时完成多任务，要求模型理解底层工艺过程；作者以注塑成型为案例研究表示学习，考察其相比经典机器学习的数据效率与多模态收益。

Method: 基于Erlanger Träger零件的真实参考周期合成工艺感知数据集，构建MouldIt——结合时间卷积与注意力的变分自编码器，重建模内信号并将零件分类为合格或某类合成失效模式；在单模态/多模态、9种数据规模下评测已知类分类与未知类检测，并与accept-all基线、kNN、逻辑回归、随机森林、PCA对比。

Result: MouldIt在单模态（≥4000周期）与多模态（≥2000周期）下两项任务均超越经典方法；分拣损失分别为6.0±0.7%（单模态）和1.0±0.3%（多模态），在所有基线及PCA+随机森林组合中排名第一。

Conclusion: 工艺感知的时序表示学习能以更高数据效率、借助多模态信息显著提升注塑过程的多任务建模性能，验证了该方法相对经典机器学习的优势。

Abstract: Smart industrial solutions that can address multiple tasks require models that understand the underlying processes. We examine representation learning for modeling the injection moulding process as an example of such solutions. We consider its data efficiency and multi-modal benefit compared to classical machine learning alternatives. To this end, we synthesize a process-informed dataset based on a real reference cycle of an Erlanger Träger part. We then introduce MouldIt, a temporal convolution and attention based variational autoencoder that reconstructs in-mould signals, while classifying parts as acceptable or as one of our synthetic failure modes. We evaluate MouldIt on two tasks, known-class classification and unknown-class detection, across nine dataset sizes in single-modal and multi-modal settings. We compare it with an accept-all baseline and the classical methods k-nearest neighbors, logistic regression, random forests, and principal component analysis, which we adapt to support both tasks. MouldIt outperforms classical methods across both tasks from 4000 cycles in the single-modal setting and from 2000 cycles in the multi-modal setting. It achieves part sorting losses of 6.0 $\pm$ 0.7% and 1.0 $\pm$ 0.3% in the single-modal and multi-modal settings respectively, ranking first across all baselines and a combination of principal component analysis and random forests.

</details>


### [341] [Massive Activations Confound Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13548)
*Nathan Delisle,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出大规模激活（massive activations）沿第一主成分（PC1）主导方差，使稀疏自编码器（SAE）的"方差解释率"和"消融损伤"两种评估均失真，并提出先移除PC1再训练SAE、最后把PC1加回重构的修正方法，显著提升特征稳定性与重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE常用于解释模型激活，但其评估依赖方差解释率与消融损伤两项指标。作者发现这两项指标会被大规模激活污染：PC1方向集中了高达99.9%的激活方差，而该方向不存在唯一的稀疏分解，导致不同训练运行用不同特征集重建同一PC1方向，进而使特征重要性排名与评估结果不可比、不可信。

Method: 将激活中的PC1分量剔除后在残差上训练SAE，再在重构阶段把PC1加回，从而绕过PC1不可辨识的分解问题。评估手段包括：两次独立运行间Top-50重要特征的重合率、PC1之外的方差解释率，以及在相同TopK稀疏度下的模型消融损伤与内容重建误差。

Result: 诊断方面：Top-50重要特征中平均47个在另一次运行中无法匹配（随机50个仅约27个）；字典可报告99.96%方差解释率，但PC1之外仍有33%的方差未被解释。修正方面：在受影响模型中，Top-50特征共享率由4–39.5%提升到73–99.5%；同等TopK稀疏度下消融损伤最多降低44%，内容重建误差最多降低33.9%。

Conclusion: PC1主导的大规模激活是SAE评估失真的关键来源，方差解释率与消融损伤在未处理PC1时会高估字典质量。移除PC1训练再回加的简单修正能大幅提升特征可辨识性与可复现性，并改善损坏度与重建精度，应作为受影响模型的默认评估与训练处理。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are commonly evaluated by variance explained and ablation damage. We show how massive activations undermine both evaluations, and propose a correction. Massive activations cause up to 99.9% of activation variance to lie along the first principal component (PC1). Because the PC1 component has no unique sparse decomposition, each SAE run rebuilds it from a different set of features. These features dominate importance rankings: an average of 47 of the 50 most important features are unmatched in a separate run, compared with about 27 of a random 50. A dictionary can also report 99.96% of variance explained while leaving 33% of the variance outside PC1 unexplained. We bypass PC1 by training the SAE on activations with PC1 removed, then adding PC1 to the reconstruction. In affected models, the top-50 feature sharing rate rises from 4-39.5% to 73-99.5%. At the same TopK sparsity, the resulting SAE degrades the model up to 44% less and reduces content reconstruction error by up to 33.9%.

</details>


### [342] [Tabular Foundation Models in Financial Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13550)
*Ed deHaan,Allen H. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将表格基础模型（TFMs）引入会计研究，在"预测企业下一年盈余变动"这一基准任务上评估其表现；结果显示 TabFM 精度优于微调大语言模型与多种树模型，且比大语言模型更快、算力更低，但与树模型存在精度—速度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会计与金融预测亟需易用、高效且无前视偏差的预测工具。TFMs 具备上下文学习能力，无需发布后训练或调参即可输出数值预测，且因预训练于合成表格数据而避免记忆企业结果所导致的前视偏差，因此值得系统评估其在会计基准任务上的表现。

Method: 在一项常用基准任务（预测企业下一年盈余变动）上，将 Google Research 的 TabFM 与近期会计研究中微调的开源权重大语言模型（LLM）以及多种树模型（tree-based models）进行比较，评估指标包括预测精度、运行速度与计算开销。

Result: TabFM 的预测精度高于所有对比模型；相较微调大语言模型，其速度更快、计算资源消耗更低；部分树模型规格的预测速度超过 TabFM，故二者之间存在精度与速度的权衡。

Conclusion: TFMs 有望成为会计与金融预测的新工具；但本评估仅基于单一基准任务，且不太可能有某一类模型在所有情境下占优，模型选择需结合具体任务场景。

Abstract: This technical note introduces tabular foundation models (TFMs) to accounting research and evaluates their performance in one common benchmark task: predicting firms' next-year earnings changes. TFMs produce numeric predictions and learn in context, without post-release training or tuning, making them natural and easy to use for forecasting. The TFMs we study are pretrained on synthetic tabular data, so they avoid look-ahead bias from memorizing firms' outcomes. We compare Google Research's TabFM with a fine-tuned open-weight large language model developed in recent accounting research and with several tree-based models. TabFM is more accurate than all comparison models. It is also faster and less computationally intensive than the large language model. Several tree-based specifications produce predictions more quickly than TabFM, so choosing between them involves a trade-off between accuracy and speed. We conclude that TFMs have promise as a new tool for forecasting in accounting and finance. However, our evaluation is based on a single benchmark task, and no class of models is likely to dominate in all settings.

</details>


### [343] [Discovering Structurally Plausible and Interpretable Cognitive Models with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13561)
*Boyoung Kwon,Jiyoon Seo,Sang Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DisCo：一个 LLM 引导、结合进化搜索的符号认知模型发现框架，通过 Validator（过滤结构性无效模型）与 Cleaner（去除冗余参数以提升参数可识别性）两个模块，在保证预测竞争力的同时获得可解释、心理上合理的认知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算认知建模依赖人工构建，耗时且需大量领域专业知识；现有 LLM 自动生成方法过度追求预测精度，牺牲了结构合理性与参数可识别性。

Method: DisCo 采用 LLM 引导的进化搜索来发现可解释的符号认知模型，包含两个结构组件：Validator 用于过滤结构上无效的模型，Cleaner 通过删除冗余参数提升参数可识别性；并在三个行为任务上与任务特定基线进行对比，辅以消融分析。

Result: 在三个行为任务上，DisCo 发现的模型性能与任务特定基线相当；消融实验显示 Validator 对恢复具有心理学意义的结构至关重要——缺失该组件时模型易违反行为约束，且与外部认知测量的关联一致性更差。

Conclusion: LLM 驱动的模型发现若配合恰当的结构约束，可生成超越单纯预测拟合的、认知上合理的模型。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling has traditionally relied on manual model construction, which is a time-intensive process that requires substantial domain expertise. Although recent LLM-based approaches have begun to automate model generation, they often prioritize predictive accuracy at the expense of structural plausibility and parameter identifiability. We introduce DisCo, an LLM-guided framework that discovers interpretable symbolic cognitive models through evolutionary search with two structural components: a Validator that filters structurally invalid models, and a Cleaner that promotes parameter identifiability by removing redundant parameters. Across three behavioral tasks, DisCo consistently discovers models that achieve performance competitive with task-specific baselines. Ablation analyses show that the Validator is important for recovering psychologically meaningful structure. Without this component, models tend to violate behavioral constraints and show less consistent associations with external cognitive measures. These results suggest that LLM-driven model discovery, when coupled with appropriate structural constraints, can yield cognitively plausible models that go beyond predictive fit.

</details>


### [344] [Investigating Composite Modeling in Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Scientific Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13565)
*Ricky Ye,Sahas Goli,Muhammad Ali,Poompol Buathong,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）中"复合建模"（直接学习中间响应而非仅以目标黑箱观测为条件）的收益及其驱动因素，提出当复合映射非双射且非仿射时复合建模更有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目标BO中已证明利用中间响应可显著提升采样效率，但复合建模在MOBO中的收益仍缺乏研究，且其增益差异来源不明。

Method: 先推导单目标下中间观测不提供额外预测信息的条件（复合方法与黑箱方法共享同一warped GP先验）；再在8个合成与科学设计启发的基准上，用4种MOBO方法做受控数值实验，其中黑箱法用标准多输出GP建模目标。

Result: 两类研究均支持：当中间响应到最终目标的映射为非双射、非仿射时，复合建模的优化增益更明显。

Conclusion: 复合建模在MOBO中的收益条件性依赖复合函数结构，非双射、非仿射映射是增益的主要驱动因素。

Abstract: Scientific design often requires balancing competing objectives that are expensive to evaluate through experiments or simulations. Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) addresses this challenge by using probabilistic models to guide the selection of promising designs. In many applications, tunable inputs map to observable intermediate responses, which are then combined through known functions to compute the final objectives. Prior work on Bayesian optimization of composite functions shows that learning from these intermediate responses, which we call Composite modeling, can substantially improve sampling efficiency in single-objective optimization, compared to methods that condition only on black-box observations of the objective function. The benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO, however, remain understudied. In this work, we study the benefits of Composite modeling in MOBO and investigate the factors driving variation in its gains. We first establish single-objective conditions under which intermediate observations provide no additional predictive information beyond objective values, where composite and black box methods use the same warped Gaussian process prior. Controlled numerical experiments with versions of four MOBO methods across eight synthetic and application-inspired scientific-design benchmarks examine how these conditions relate to optimization gains in the multi-objective setting, where the black box approach models the objective functions with a standard multi-output Gaussian process. Both investigations support the claim that composite modeling tends to help more when the function mapping intermediate responses to final objectives is non-bijective and non-affine.

</details>


### [345] [Metamorphic testing of learned AC optimal power flow surrogates reveals representation dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13566)
*Stephen Peng,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用13条蜕变关系（metamorphic relations）审计电网不同序列化编码下AC-OPF学习代理模型的一致性，覆盖17种模型与流水线配置；所有配置均违反至少6条关系，并定位到GridSFM-Open v1.1的索引bug及三处非规范/数值不稳定预处理；augmentation实验显示收益依赖训练协议且所有预测仍AC不可行。建议在报告精度、可行性与成本之外同时报告表示一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅在单一电网序列化上评估学习型AC-OPF代理模型，无法判断其精度是否依赖该编码方式；缺乏对表示不变性/一致性的系统审计手段。

Method: 设计13条蜕变关系，每条明确变换的适用条件与输出比较方式；审计17种配置（2个已发布训练检查点+15个随机权重架构/机制对照）；采用源码检查与中间张量干预定位问题；另开展case500训练实验考察数据增强对变换视图误差、中位差异与约束违反P95的影响。

Result: 所有配置至少违反6条关系，即便在放宽或收紧10倍的报告容差下亦然；GridSFM-Open v1.1存在索引bug与三处非规范或数值不稳定预处理（修复后检查点尚未测试）；case500增强实验在4个模型中3个降低变换视图误差，4个全部降低中位差异与约束违反P95，但部分关系变差且所有预测仍AC不可行；原始拉普拉斯基线的大幅提升主要源于GPU/CPU特征值求解器不匹配。

Conclusion: 建议在精度、可行性与成本之外同时报告表示一致性；结果既不证明可部署性，也不证明普遍不变性。

Abstract: Evaluating a learned AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) surrogate on one grid serialization cannot show whether its accuracy depends on that encoding. We test this dependence with thirteen metamorphic relations, each specifying when a transformation is valid and how to compare its outputs. Our audit covers 17 model and pipeline configurations: two released trained checkpoints and 15 random-weight architecture or mechanism controls. Every configuration violates at least six relations, even at reporting tolerances ten times tighter or looser. For GridSFM-Open v1.1, source inspection and intermediate-tensor interventions point to an indexing bug and three noncanonical or numerically unstable preprocessing choices; a repaired checkpoint remains untested. In a separate case500 training experiment, augmentation lowers transformed-view error in three of four models and median discrepancy and constraint-violation P95 in all four. These benefits depend on the training protocol: some relations worsen, and every evaluated prediction remains AC infeasible. A GPU/CPU eigensolver mismatch accounts for most of the large raw-Laplacian-baseline gain. We recommend reporting representation consistency alongside accuracy, feasibility, and cost. These results establish neither deployment readiness nor universal invariance.

</details>


### [346] [Market-Conditioned JEPA: Learning from Unpredictable Market Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13572)
*Yuqi Wang,Fengyuan Liu,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Lei Li,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Abstract 提出 Market-Conditioned JEPA (MC-JEPA)，将“已实现市场因子”作为条件注入联合嵌入预测架构，使资产的市场暴露成为最优预测的必要信息并降低特质成分的学习噪声；在中国 A 股 11 年分钟级数据上，于收益、市场暴露、风险、流动性等九类目标上全面优于逐资产 JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列的大部分变异来自无法由单个资产自身历史预测的市场级冲击，而逐资产的自监督学习（per-asset SSL）把这类变异当作不可约噪声丢弃，导致预测与表示学习效率低。

Method: 在潜在因子模型下理论证明：以已实现市场因子为条件时，资产的市场暴露（因子载荷）成为最优预测的必要量，同时降低特质成分学习所面对的噪声。据此构建 MC-JEPA：训练时用其他资产未来目标嵌入构造逐资产的 leave-one-out 市场条件，并通过由资产自身表示导出的因子载荷将该条件注入预测器；归一化去除的波动尺度在评估时恢复，以检验因子载荷隐含的 beta 排序能力。

Result: 在 11 年分钟级中国 A 股数据上，MC-JEPA 在收益、市场暴露、风险、流动性共九类目标上均优于 per-asset JEPA，其中暴露、风险、流动性提升最强且统计显著性最高；在所有流动性目标及 2 小时至 5 天收益期限上取得最佳 RankIC，超过时间序列基础模型与自监督基线，并在历史回测中取得最佳组合表现。消融显示收益与暴露的增益要求条件与已实现市场走势对齐；恢复波动尺度后，预测器的因子载荷无需标签即可将股票按市场 beta 排序，接近有监督探针水平。已知参数的合成因子模型实验表明，条件化同时改善市场暴露与资产特质成分的恢复。

Conclusion: 将市场因子作为条件而非噪声，是提升金融时序自监督表征与预测的关键机制；MC-JEPA 以无标签方式同时获得更好的预测精度、因子暴露刻画与组合表现，为金融 JEPA 类架构提供了理论与实证支撑。

Abstract: Much of the variation in financial time series is driven by market-wide shocks that cannot be predicted from an asset's own history. Per-asset self-supervised learning treats this variation as irreducible noise. Under a latent factor model, we show that conditioning prediction on the realized market factor makes the asset's market exposure necessary for optimal prediction, while reducing the noise against which the asset-specific component is learned. Building on this insight, we introduce Market-Conditioned JEPA (MC-JEPA), a joint-embedding predictive architecture for financial time series. During training, we construct a leave-one-out market condition for each asset from the future target embeddings of other assets and incorporate it through factor loadings derived from the asset's own representation. On 11 years of minute-level Chinese A-share data, MC-JEPA improves over per-asset JEPA across nine targets spanning returns, market exposure, risk, and liquidity, with the strongest and most statistically significant gains in exposure, risk, and liquidity. It achieves the best RankIC on all liquidity targets and on return horizons from two hours to five days, outperforming time-series foundation models and self-supervised baselines, and delivers the best portfolio performance in a historical backtest. Ablations show that the return and exposure gains require the condition to be aligned with the realized market movement. After restoring the volatility scale removed by normalization, the predictor's factor loadings rank stocks by market beta nearly as well as a supervised probe, without labels. A synthetic factor-model study with known parameters shows that conditioning improves the recovery of both market exposure and the asset-specific component.

</details>


### [347] [Unified Optimization of Traffic Signal and Lane Channelization for an Isolated Intersection Using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13599)
*Jiwon Kang,Jiyoung Kim,Hyunseok Jun,Keemin Sohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的实时联合优化方法，同时决定交叉口信号配时与车道渠化（标线）设计。通过深度Q网络处理信号与车道标线的联合动作，并利用人类反馈训练的规则过滤危险/不可行动作转换，实验表明可提升交叉口通行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通信号控制是实时优化问题，而车道渠化（road design for lane channelization）是静态设计问题，二者难以联合求解。已有MILP联合优化模型在交通流模式变化时需反复求解，且问题规模增大时计算时间不可接受，缺乏实时性。

Method: 1) 采用强化学习（RL）实时求解信号与车道渠化的联合优化问题，替代反复求解MILP；2) 设计可容纳信号相位与车道标线联合动作的深度Q网络（DQN）；3) 车道标线突变若与信号相位不兼容会危及驾驶员安全，故采用带人类反馈（human feedback）训练模型，以基于规则的方式过滤不可行的动作转换；4) 实施前提是交叉口各停车线处设置可变车道标线指示牌。

Result: 实验结果证实所提方法通过提高通行量（throughput）改善了交叉口运行性能。

Conclusion: 基于RL的实时联合优化可有效替代MILP重复求解，在保证动作转换安全可行的前提下提升交叉口通行能力；但落地需依赖停车线处的可变车道标线标志设施。

Abstract: Optimizing traffic signal control has been a long-lasting critical issue in traffic engineering, and a number of robust methodologies have emerged to solve the problem so far. On the other hand, the road design for lane channelization is a static problem that is difficult to solve jointly with the real-time traffic signal control. If traffic volume does not vary, it is possible that both problems can be solved in an integrated manner as a single static optimization problem. Recently, researchers have devised a mixed integer linear problem (MILP) to jointly determine the traffic signal and road width designs. However, this joint optimization problem must be solved repeatedly whenever the traffic flow pattern varies. Solving the MILP may also take impractical computing time as the problem size gets larger. We present a robust methodology for solving the joint optimization problem using reinforcement learning (RL) on a real-time basis. Abrupt changes in lane markings with an incompatible signal phase can jeopardize drivers. To sidestep such a dangerous action change, we train a model with human feedback to filter infeasible action transitions in a rule-based manner. We devised a deep Q-network that can accommodate joint actions for traffic signal and lane marking plans. To ensure the implementation of the proposed operation, a variable sign for lane markings is necessary at each stop line of an intersection. The experimental results confirm that the proposed methodology enhances the intersection performance by increasing throughput.

</details>


### [348] [Multifidelity Operator Inference for Non-intrusive Model Reduction under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13624)
*Vivian Zhang,Tomoki Koike,Atticus Rex,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多保真算子推断（MF-OpInf），利用大量低保真数据补充稀缺高保真快照，通过多保真线性回归估计降维算子，在相同计算预算下显著降低方差并提升PDE解场预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计与不确定性量化中的多查询分析依赖反复求解高保真PDE模型，成本极高。代理建模以廉价近似替代仿真，其中机器学习类方法因非侵入性而受青睐，算子推断（OpInf）通过拟合降维算子尤为突出。但受计算预算限制，高保真训练数据稀缺，仅用高保真数据训练的OpInf模型方差大，在高风险工程设计场景中是严重隐患。

Method: 提出MF-OpInf：以同一问题的粗网格离散产生的大量低保真快照数据，补充稀缺的高保真快照；采用多保真线性回归框架，统一处理不同保真度的状态与时间导数数据，估计降维算子。

Result: 在若干模型PDE问题上的数值实验表明，在相同计算预算下，MF-OpInf预测PDE解场的精度高于仅用高保真数据的OpInf。

Conclusion: 多保真数据融合可有效缓解OpInf在数据稀缺下的高方差问题，为高保真昂贵仿真场景下的代理建模提供了一种非侵入、数据高效且易实现的方案。

Abstract: Many-query analyses, in which a computational model is repeatedly evaluated, play an important role in uncertainty quantification and optimization for engineering design. However, many-query analyses can be prohibitively expensive when considering high-fidelity numerical models, especially those governed by partial differential equations (PDEs). Surrogate modeling replaces costly simulations with cheap approximations, enabling practical uncertainty quantification and design workflows. Machine learning (ML) approaches to surrogate modeling have gained significant traction in the development of surrogate models due to their non-intrusive nature; they do not require access to the underlying simulation code. The Operator Inference (OpInf) approach, in particular, fits reduced operators to state and corresponding time derivative data. However, high-fidelity training data is scarce due to limited computational budgets, and OpInf models trained on such scarce data often exhibit high variance, a critical liability in high-risk engineering design. We propose Multifidelity Operator Inference (MF-OpInf), which reduces this variance by supplementing scarce high-fidelity snapshots with abundant low-fidelity data drawn from coarser discretizations of the same problem. MF-OpInf handles data across varying fidelity levels by utilizing a multifidelity linear regression framework to estimate the reduced operators. Numerical experiments on model PDE problems demonstrate that MF-OpInf predicts PDE solution fields with higher accuracy compared to the high-fidelity-only OpInf with the same computational budget.

</details>


### [349] [CP-MLPs: A Tensor-Rank Theory of Tied and Untied MLP Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13647)
*Md Rifat Arefin,Farzaneh Heidari,Irina Rish,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CP-MLPs 张量框架，将乘法单元分解为"检测-读取-写入"三种角色，证明绑定/解绑门控块在张量秩上存在精确的按次数分离，并指出宽度优势取决于目标函数结构，而非普遍成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 前馈块叠加了平方激活、门控、参数绑定/解绑等多种设计，但各设计对表达能力的独立贡献缺乏理论刻画，尤其"绑定 vs 解绑"的作用机制不清楚。

Method: 构建 CP-MLPs 张量分解框架，每个乘法单元视作贡献秩-1 交互，承担检测输入特征、读取值、写入输出三种角色；对比绑定门控（如 ReLU²，检测与读取共享方向）与解绑门控（ReGLU、SwiGLU，方向可不同），并按次数推导精确的张量秩分离结果，辅以损失平台与 border rank 分析及训练实验。

Result: 给出精确的按次数张量秩分离：对不定二次交互与角色非对称单项式，解绑单元严格更宽度高效；对对称纯幂目标，绑定单元结构上更匹配。精确表示宽度以下出现损失平台，但该平台不能证明正近似间隙，任意精度逼近由 border rank 决定。训练后的门控 MLP 在匹配输入投影预算的选定比较中，解绑块仍具优势。

Conclusion: 解绑带来的宽度优势依赖于目标结构而非普适；近似能力须用 border rank 而非秩刻画，损失平台不代表本质近似间隙。为门控 MLP 的架构选择提供了基于张量秩的理论判据。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward blocks combine several design choices: squared activations, gating, and tied versus untied parameter sharing, whose separate contributions to expressivity remain poorly understood. We introduce CP-MLPs, a tensor framework in which each multiplicative unit contributes a rank-1 interaction with three roles: detecting an input feature, reading a value, and writing to the output. Tied gated blocks such as ReLU$^2$ force the detector and value roles to share the same direction; untied gated blocks such as ReGLU and SwiGLU let them differ. We show that this distinction yields exact, degree-wise tensor-rank separations: untied detector-value units are strictly more width-efficient for indefinite quadratic interactions and role-asymmetric monomials, while tied units are structurally matched to symmetric pure-power targets. The width advantage of untying therefore depends on the target structure. Experiments show loss plateaus below the exact-representation width, but these plateaus alone do not establish a positive approximation gap: arbitrarily accurate approximation is governed by border rank. In trained gated MLPs, untied blocks retain an advantage in selected comparisons at matched input-projection budget. Code is available at https://github.com/rarefin/CP-MLPs.

</details>


### [350] [SepsisFlow: an event-stream foundation model for continuous risk warning and trajectory modelling in ICU sepsis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13661)
*Yawen Zhang,Wanzhe Xu,Zhenhua Zhang,Jinyu Wu,Yizhou Zhuang,Yitao Yang,Kenta Nakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SepsisFlow 是一个基于小时级时钟的脓毒症全程分析框架：发病前预测 6/12/24 小时风险，发病时通过门控网络融合 Mamba 与表格编码器生成患者状态以界定发病情境，发病后利用注意力双向循环网络编码 72 小时器官支持与生理轨迹刻画早期病程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脓毒症是 ICU 主要死因，起病与病程异质性大；现有预警模型多只预测"是否会发生脓毒症"，报警后临床医生无法获知发病情境与早期演变轨迹。

Method: 以 ICU 入科为起点、小时级统一时钟，分三阶段构建：(1) 发病前：在 257 万份 EHR 上预训练的 Mamba 模型结合约 1,700 个每小时特征，输入梯度提升树预测 6/12/24 小时风险；(2) 发病时：门控网络融合 Mamba 与表格编码器，得到连续患者状态以定义发病情境；(3) 发病后：注意力双向循环网络编码 72 小时器官支持与生理轨迹，形成早期病程分型。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 的发病前预警中，相同报警量下检出病例数多于 NEWS2、qSOFA 和 SIRS，小时级更新带来约每 100 例增加 8 例的增益，并优于 16 个重训练算法，且在 eICU 上保持风险排序能力；发病时，各情境的 28 天死亡率从心血管 ICU 的 2% 到其他情境的 15%–17%；发病后各情境内，持续性器官支持者死亡率约为降阶梯者的五倍。

Conclusion: SepsisFlow 为重症监护中的连续风险评估与轨迹分层建立了基础模型范式。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of ICU death with heterogeneous onset and course, yet most warning models predict only whether sepsis will occur, leaving clinicians uncertain of its onset context and early trajectory once an alarm sounds. To answer these questions on one hourly clock from ICU admission, we built SepsisFlow: before onset, it combines a Mamba model pretrained on 2.57 million EHRs with about 1,700 hourly features in gradient-boosted trees to predict 6/12/24-h risk; at onset, it fuses Mamba and tabular encoders via a gated network into continuous patient states defining onset contexts; after onset, it encodes 72-h organ-support and physiological trajectories via an attention bidirectional recurrent network into early courses. For pre-onset warning in MIMIC-IV, SepsisFlow detected more cases than NEWS2, qSOFA, and SIRS at equal alarms, gaining about 8 cases per 100 from hourly updating, while outperforming 16 retrained algorithms and maintaining risk ranking in eICU. At onset, its contexts separated 2% cardiovascular-ICU from 15%-17% other 28-day mortality, and within each context after onset, persistent support carried fivefold higher mortality than de-escalation. SepsisFlow establishes a foundation-model paradigm for continuous risk and trajectory stratification in critical care.

</details>


### [351] [Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13680)
*Yue Wu,Xueyu Zhou,Yangrong Hu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文发现on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）在持续训练下会因特权教师与无参考学生之间的分布失配而退化，据此提出两阶段的Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation（BOPD）：先用OPSD把参考条件下的监督内化到中间模型，再将该无参考中间模型蒸馏到初始模型的新副本中，在数学推理与代码生成上优于初始模型和OPSD教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD免除了外部教师，早期提升显著，但作者观察到其持续训练后效果不再稳定甚至显著下降。分析表明，问题源于不断演化的无参考学生与冻结的特权教师之间的分布失配：训练早期两者对齐良好，指导有效；随训练推进，失配扩大，教师给出误导性监督，导致学生性能退化。

Method: 提出BOPD两阶段框架。第一阶段：用OPSD将参考条件（privileged reference）监督中的任务相关信息内化到一个中间模型，即获得一个任务适配的、无需参考条件的模型；第二阶段：将该无参考中间模型作为教师，蒸馏到一个由初始模型复制而来的全新学生中，从而避免特权教师与学生之间的分布失配。

Result: 在多个模型上的实验显示，BOPD在数学推理和代码生成任务上均超过初始模型以及所构建的OPSD教师模型，说明"先内化、再蒸馏"的方式比直接使用参考条件监督更有效。

Conclusion: 特权参考监督的价值应通过"先将任务相关信号迁移到任务适配模型，再从该模型无参考地蒸馏"的方式来更充分地利用；这同时规避了OPSD长期训练中的教师—学生失配问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language models by distilling teacher predictions on student-generated trajectories, while on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes the need for an external teacher by using a reference-conditioned copy of the model as the teacher. Despite its great early improvements, we find that OPSD does not remain consistently effective under continued training and can eventually suffer substantial performance degradation. We study this training behavior and link the degradation to a growing mismatch between the evolving reference-free student and the frozen privileged teacher. In the early stage of OPSD, the privileged teacher remains closely aligned with the student and provides useful guidance for improving the student's generation trajectories. However, as training progresses, the growing distributional mismatch between the privileged teacher and reference-free student can cause the teacher to provide misleading supervision, ultimately degrading student performance. This phenomenon motivates us to first leverage privileged reference supervision to acquire useful knowledge and then transfer it to a fresh student. We introduce Bootstrapped On-Policy Distillation (BOPD), a two-stage framework that first uses OPSD to internalize task-relevant information from reference-conditioned supervision into an intermediate model, and then distills this reference-free intermediate model into a fresh copy of the initial model. Experiments across models show that BOPD improves mathematical reasoning and code generation beyond both the initial models and the constructed OPSD teachers. These results suggest that privileged reference supervision can be more effectively exploited by first transferring its task-relevant signal into a task-adapted model and then distilling from that model without reference conditioning.

</details>


### [352] [FedVP: Federated Learning across Heterogeneous Models by Pooling Locally Trained Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13708)
*Yifan Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Federated Verifier Pool（FEDVP）联邦学习新范式，通过共享"验证器"而非聚合模型参数，实现异构LLM客户端间的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖共享模型骨干来聚合客户端更新，这限制了那些部署来自不同家族、不同规模LLM的机构之间的协作。

Method: 每个客户端用本地数据及其所选LLM骨干生成的回答训练一个验证器；服务器汇聚这些独立训练的验证器，组合其判断来选择答案，而不合并模型参数。LLM骨干保持冻结，骨干与验证器均可在架构和规模上不同，协作收益来自改进的答案选择。

Result: 在五个公开医学问答数据集（视为联邦客户端）上，使用3B–14B的Qwen与LLaMA骨干评估，FEDVP在全部五个客户端上均稳定提升，平均准确率比最强传统联邦基线高1.46–5.79个百分点。

Conclusion: FEDVP证明了以"共享验证"替代"参数聚合"是支持异构LLM联邦协作的可行且有效路径，为跨机构LLM协作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on the shared model backbone to aggregate client updates, limiting collaboration among institutions that deploy large language models (LLMs) from different families and at different scales. We propose Federated Verifier Pool (FEDVP), a new federated learning paradigm that enables heterogeneous clients to collaborate through shared verification rather than parameter aggregation. Each client trains a verifier using local data and responses generated by its chosen LLM backbone. The server pools these independently trained verifiers and combines their judgments to select answers without merging model parameters. This allows both LLM backbones and verifiers to differ in architecture and size, while keeping the LLM backbones frozen and deriving collaborative gains from improved answer selection. We evaluate FEDVP across five public medical question-answering datasets treated as federated clients, using Qwen and LLaMA backbones ranging from 3B to 14B parameters. FEDVP consistently improves performance across all five clients and outperforms the strongest conventional federated baselines by 1.46-5.79 percentage points in mean accuracy.

</details>


### [353] [TRUER: Test-Time Reward Correction Using an Error-Aware Rectifier for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13714)
*Mingxiang Shen,Jinhao Li,Jiaming Xu,Yibo Han,Guohao Dai,Yijia Zhang,Ningyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRUER框架，用有限标注数据训练奖励纠正器以区分"正确性"与"共识性"，再冻结该纠正器用于测试时自适应阶段的奖励校正，显著优于朴素TTRL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习依赖模型自身答案导出的奖励，但多数投票机制即使在存在正确答案时也可能强化错误解。训练审计显示，奖励错误中以假阳性（错误回答获得正奖励）为主。在真值奖励难以大规模获取、仅能获得有限标注源集的现实设定下，需要一种机制来提升奖励准确性。

Method: TRUER框架分两阶段：先用有限的监督数据训练一个奖励纠正器（reward rectifier），训练时重点覆盖"错误多数"与"正确少数"这两类共识与正确性相矛盾的样本，使纠正器学会区分正确性与共识；随后冻结该纠正器，在后续测试时自适应过程中用它校正奖励信号。

Result: 通过提升奖励准确率进而改善模型性能，TRUER在所有评估配置下于奖励准确率与模型性能两项指标上均稳定优于朴素TTRL。

Conclusion: 有限监督足以矫正自生成奖励中的共识偏差，且冻结式奖励纠正器可即插即用地嵌入测试时强化学习流程，为缺乏大规模真值奖励的场景提供了实用改进路径。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning enables language models to adapt using rewards derived from their own answers. However, majority voting can reinforce incorrect solutions even when correct alternatives are available. Our training audit shows that false positives (incorrect responses receiving positive rewards) dominate reward errors. We focus on a practical regime where ground-truth rewards are difficult to obtain at scale, while a limited labeled source set is available. We introduce TRUER, a framework that uses limited supervision to train a reward rectifier that distinguishes correctness from consensus. To achieve this goal, we train the rectifier with an emphasis on incorrect majorities and correct minorities, targeting cases where consensus and correctness disagree. After that, we freeze the rectifier and use it to correct rewards during subsequent test-time adaptation. By improving reward accuracy and thereby model performance, TRUER consistently outperforms naive TTRL in both metrics across all evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [354] [Weighted-AoI-Driven UAV Trajectory and Reporting for Dynamic CKM Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13726)
*Zeyu Zhao,Yueling Che,Sheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 面向无人机集群在二维空域上对动态信道知识图（CKM）的连续在线跟踪，提出以加权信息年龄（W-AoI）为核心的调度与轨迹方案，仿真中稳态误差较 AoI-only 与 FIFO 基线分别降低 3.8 dB² 和 4.8 dB²，接近理想 oracle 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 CKM 构建普遍假设准静态环境，一旦信道快速去相关便失效；因此需要研究在无人机移动与上报约束下的连续在线动态 CKM 跟踪问题。

Method: 将问题建模为 UAV 移动性与上报约束下的长期误差最小化问题；由于未测小区的误差不可观测导致问题难解，提出度量驱动的调度方法 W-AoI，将精确时间陈旧度与由 2D U-Net 预测的空间不确定性相结合；证明 Top-K W-AoI 选择可最大化单时隙误差下降上界，并据此设计含沿路径连续感知的轻量贪心轨迹策略。

Result: 仿真表明所提方法相对 AoI-only 与 FIFO 基线将稳态误差分别降低 3.8 dB² 与 4.8 dB²，且性能接近理想化的 oracle 方案。

Conclusion: 以 W-AoI 作为统一度量可将时间陈旧度与空间不确定性耦合，并具备理论最优性保证，配合轻量贪心轨迹规划即可有效支撑动态 CKM 的连续在线跟踪。

Abstract: Existing channel knowledge map (CKM) construction assumes quasi-static environments, failing when channels rapidly decorrelate. This paper investigates continuous online dynamic CKM tracking over an airborne 2D plane via a UAV fleet. We formulate a long-term error minimization problem under UAV mobility and reporting constraints. This problem, however, is intractable with unobservable errors for unmeasured cells. To tackle this, we propose a metric-driven scheduling approach featuring the Weighted-Age of Information (W-AoI). W-AoI evaluates cells by combining exact temporal staleness with spatial uncertainty predicted by a 2D U-Net. We prove that top-$K$ W-AoI selection maximizes the one-slot error reduction bound. Guided by this guarantee, we develop a lightweight greedy trajectory policy incorporating continuous along-path sensing. Simulations demonstrate our method reduces steady-state error by 3.8 dB$^2$ and 4.8 dB$^2$ over AoI-only and FIFO baselines, closely approaching an idealized oracle.

</details>


### [355] [Deterministic, Reproducible Inference in Exact Integer Arithmetic, and Verifiable Distributed Inference as Its Consequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13730)
*Sayed Amir Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: tritify 是一个开源运行时，将真实模型（解码器 LLM、MoE、SAM 2.1）的前向传播全程用精确整数算术执行，浮点仅保留在离线量化与验证参考中，从而在跨硬件/跨指令集/跨 CPU-GPU/跨机器条件下实现输出比特级可复现，并给出输出 SHA-256 复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化后的浮点推理不可复现：同一模型在不同硬件、编译器或线程数下会产生不同比特，导致结果无法审计与跨环境校验。作者希望获得可验证、可复算的推理路径。

Method: 把权重/激活量化为整数码，前向路径全部使用精确整数算术，浮点仅用于离线量化与浮点参考校验；在 OLMoE-1B-7B、106B 参数 MoE（GLM-4.5-Air，427 GB 整数码）及 SAM 2.1 上分别验证：(1) 跨指令集与 CPU/GPU 的比特一致性；(2) 与浮点参考的决策保真度（含逐层保真度、例外量化）；(3) 计算切分到多设备/多机时的输出比特无关性。

Result: 三项主张均获验证：跨 ISA、跨 CPU/GPU、多次运行输出比特完全相同（SHA-256 复现）；整数路径在测试模型与输入上复现浮点参考的决策，逐层检查至 16 层未出现保真度断崖；GLM-4.5-Air 借助三台均无法在内存中容纳其整数码的机器完成推理，结果与单机参考比特一致。

Conclusion: 整数化运行时支持“可验证的分布式推理”：不可信服务器或设备集群执行模型，开放标量 oracle 可重算精确字节；但不声称单设备加速、浮点中间值比特相等、机密性，且防不诚实服务器的保证尚属未来工作。

Abstract: Optimized floating-point inference is not reproducible across environments: the same model on different hardware, compilers or thread counts can produce different bits. We present tritify, an open-source runtime that executes the forward pass of real models -- decoder-only language models, Mixture-of-Experts decoders, and the Segment Anything 2.1 image/video stack -- with the entire run-time path in exact integer arithmetic; floating point is confined to offline quantization and the float reference used for verification. We make three separate claims and verify each. (1) Cross-hardware reproducibility: the integer path produces bit-identical output run to run, across CPU instruction-set architectures, and across CPU/GPU, carried by a reproducing SHA-256 of the output. (2) Decision fidelity: the integer path reproduces the float reference's decision on the models and inputs tested, with the numeric exceptions measured; per-layer fidelity through sixteen layers of OLMoE-1B-7B and of a 106-billion-parameter MoE shows no fidelity cliff. (3) Location independence: a model's computation can be split across instruction sets, CPU and GPU, and networked machines with zero effect on the output bits. GLM-4.5-Air (106,852,251,264 parameters, 427 GB of integer codes) generated its answer across three machines, none of which can hold its integer codes in RAM, bit-identically to a single-host reference. The consequence is verifiable distributed inference: an untrusted server or a fleet of devices runs the model while an open scalar oracle re-derives the exact bytes; a guarantee against a dishonest server is future work. We do not claim a per-device speed-up, bit-equality of floating-valued intermediates, or confidentiality. Code: https://github.com/pelloran-inc/tritify

</details>


### [356] [Plug-and-Play ADMM for Inverse Problems with Flow Matching Denoiser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13742)
*Dibyanshu Kumar,Magda Gregorova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于ADMM的PnP框架以集成流匹配先验，并通过轨迹校正机制增强数据保真与先验一致性，实现高质量且低计算成本的逆问题重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PnP方法虽在逆问题中表现优异，且流匹配先验支持快速可扩展重建，但数据保真更新会使迭代点偏离流轨迹，导致数据保真步骤与学习先验之间的一致性难以维持，这是核心挑战。

Method: 构建ADMM式PnP框架：引入辅助变量与对偶更新，强化测量模型与先验之间的一致性；并提出轨迹校正机制，通过确定性投影与随机投影的组合使迭代重新回到流轨迹上。

Result: 相比现有方法，所提方法在重建质量上达到高水平，同时降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 将流先验更"有原则地"融入PnP框架是可行且有效的，ADMM结构加轨迹校正为一致性保持提供了原则化方案。

Abstract: Plug-and-Play (PnP) methods with generative priors have achieved strong performance in inverse problems, with recent flow matching models enabling fast and scalable reconstruction. However, a key challenge in PnP methods is maintaining consistency between data fidelity updates and the learned generative prior, as data-consistency steps can move iterates away from the flow trajectory. We propose an ADMM-based PnP framework for flow matching priors that enforces stronger consistency between the measurement model and the prior through auxiliary variables and dual updates. In addition, we introduce a trajectory correction mechanism that restores consistency with the flow via a combination of deterministic and stochastic projections. Our method provides a more principled integration of flow priors within PnP frameworks, achieving high-quality reconstructions with reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [357] [When Covariance Is Not Enough: Kernel-Based Regularization for Single-Cell Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13748)
*Korbinian Traeuble,Edward de Brouwer,Archit Verma,Doron Haviv,Kipper Fletez-Brant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出概念瓶颈生成模型中常用的线性互协方差惩罚无法在非线性交互下充分分离已知/未知因子，提出引入归一化Hilbert-Schmidt独立性准则（nHSIC）作为非线性核判据，从而更忠实地实现单细胞反事实编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞反事实编辑（预测某因素改变后细胞的样子）可用于不可行实验的in-silico筛选，但其价值取决于编辑对个体是否忠实，而非仅对条件忠实。概念瓶颈模型通过将细胞表示分解为已知与未知因子使干预效应可解释，但既有方法仅用线性协方差惩罚进行解耦，作者认为在存在非线性交互时一阶判据不足以完全分离信息。

Method: 1）用带有受控编辑和已知非线性协变量效应的模拟实验，证明线性交叉因子惩罚的不足；2）在概念瓶颈模型中额外加入非线性核判据——归一化Hilbert-Schmidt独立性准则（nHSIC）以惩罚已知与未知因子间的非线性依赖；3）将该非线性概念瓶颈模型应用于复杂单细胞RNA-seq数据集。

Result: 模拟实验表明线性交叉协方差惩罚在非线性交互下残留相关信息；加入nHSIC后，模型能够在模拟数据上恢复真实的干预效应。在复杂scRNA-seq数据上，非线性概念瓶颈模型提升了在不同组织环境下生成细胞反事实表达谱的能力。

Conclusion: 仅用一阶（线性）解耦判据不足以支撑忠实的单细胞反事实编辑，引入非线性核独立性判据可改善因子解耦与干预效应恢复，并在真实单细胞数据上提升反事实表达谱生成质量。

Abstract: Counterfactual editing of single cells, predicting how an observed cell would look had one factor been different, enables in-silico screening where experiments are infeasible, but is useful only if the edit is faithful to the individual and not merely to the condition. Concept bottleneck generative models make the effects of interventions interpretable by decomposing a cell's representation into known and unknown factors. Previous work uses a linear cross-covariance penalty to disentangle known and unknown dimensions. We demonstrate that this first-order criterion does not fully separate information when non-linear interactions are present. We first demonstrate the inadequacy of linear cross-factor penalization with simulation experiments with controlled edits and known non-linear covariate effects. We then demonstrate that adding a nonlinear kernel criterion, the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, is able to recover the true intervention effects on simulation data. Finally, we apply the non-linear concept bottleneck model to a complex single-cell RNA-seq dataset and find that it improves the ability to generate counterfactual expression profiles of cells under different tissue environments.

</details>


### [358] [Mixed Signals: Weight-Space Programming for EEG and ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13758)
*Dara Varam,Taraneh Aminosharieh Najafi,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将"权重空间编程"（由语言模型直接读取并编辑神经网络权重）迁移到EEG/ECG等生物信号模型，证明前沿模型可无监督定位并修复植入故障、甚至仅凭架构/任务/少量被试从零写出全部权重，且性能可比梯度下降训练与经典手工EEG流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已在博弈网络、语言模型特定失败修复乃至扩散模型上验证了权重空间编程的功能与潜力，但尚未考察其在生物信号（EEG、ECG）模型上的适用性；若成立，可为边缘设备、可穿戴设备上的小型模型提供"以推理替代梯度下降"的建模途径。

Method: 以EEG/ECG数据训练的模型为对象，让前沿大语言模型直接访问网络权重：（1）在不被告知故障类型的前提下调试植入的特定故障；（2）仅给定网络架构、任务描述和极少量训练被试，直接写出网络全部权重，全程不使用任何梯度下降步骤；并与标准梯度下降训练、经典手工EEG流程（alpha频段功率、CSP+LDA）进行对比。

Result: 语言模型可在无提示情况下定位并修复植入故障；在部分实验中，仅靠推理即用少6倍的训练被试达到与标准梯度下降训练相当的性能；Claude Opus 5.5写出的网络在相同5名被试条件下优于alpha频段功率与CSP+LDA等经典手工EEG流程。局限包括：无法改进已训练良好的分类模型，且难以仅凭权重可靠识别网络所训练的任务。

Conclusion: 权重空间编程对部署于边缘设备与可穿戴设备的小型网络具有实用价值，但其能力边界（不能提升成熟模型、任务识别不可靠）表明该概念仍需深入研究，尚不足以投入实际应用。

Abstract: Weight-space programming allows language models to read a trained neural network's weights and edit them. This opens the door to ``training'' by reasoning rather than through gradient descent. Prior work has demonstrated its functionality and promise for game-playing networks and fixing specific failures in language models, even extending to diffusion models. In this work, we present its applicability to models trained on biosignal data, specifically EEG and ECG data. We show that frontier models are not only able to debug specific faults planted in biosignal-based models without being told what these faults are, but are even capable of writing all the weights of a network from scratch, given only the architecture, the task, and a minimal set of training subjects. In particular, in one set of experiments, a language model was able to match standard gradient-descent training using $6\times$ fewer training subjects, through reasoning alone and without any gradient descent step. Networks written by Claude Opus 5.5 also scored higher than classic hand-crafted EEG pipelines (alpha band power, and common spatial patterns with linear discriminant analysis) when built from the same 5 subjects. We also highlight some of the limitations, which include the inability of language models to improve already well-trained classification models, and their incapacity to reliably recognize, from the weights alone, the task a network was trained on. Overall, our results indicate that weight-space programming can be useful for small networks, such as those deployed on edge devices and wearables, but the concept still requires further investigation before it can be used in practice.

</details>


### [359] [Beyond Interaction Scores: Selecting Feature Interactions via Independent Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13765)
*Jincheng Gao,Shuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LENS 提出一种与模型无关的特征交互选择框架：用同一学习算法在不相交数据上训练两个模型，仅通过预测访问模型，先剥离主效应得到交互强度，再要求第二个模型在显著强度上复现该交互，并用一次多重检验校正确定保留的交互对，仅需两次模型拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征交互对预测精度和可解释性建模至关重要，但高交互分数可能只是拟合噪声而非可复现模式。现有方法存在缺陷：排序类方法只给特征对打分而把截断阈值留给用户；检验类方法能给出阈值，但依赖反复重拟合模型或需要联合特征分布模型。因此需要一个模型无关、计算开销小且具有统计保证的交互筛选方法。

Method: LENS 在同一学习算法下于不相交数据上训练两个模型，只通过预测值访问模型。对每个特征对，从联合效应曲面中移除主效应以分离出交互模式，按交互强度排序，并在强度下限约束下检验第二个模型是否以有意义的强度复现该交互，最终用单次多重检验校正决定保留哪些特征对；全程仅需两次模型拟合，与特征数量无关。理论上证明了仅依赖复现比例会保留两个拟合共享的任意弱模式，而加入强度下限可恢复选择一致性，并刻画了 Friedman H² 中归一化导致交互排序反转的条件。

Result: 在16个合成设计上，LENS 与领先的模型特定排序方法（如 TreeSHAP）相当，恢复91%的实质交互，而基于 knockoff 的 DIAMOND 仅45%，且不选出虚假特征对；在1,225个候选对且无真实交互时，假发现显著更少。在大规模法国汽车保险数据上，识别出精算文献已记录的交互，并取得使用全部特征对所得偏差增益的90%。在两个信用数据集上，LENS 仅选取少量特征对，同时保持或略微提升相对加性基线的预测性能。

Conclusion: LENS 提供了一个模型无关、仅需两次拟合、具多重检验校正的交互选择框架，兼顾筛选精度、统计一致性与计算效率，能在真实数据中发现可解释且有文献支持的交互，并避免过度选择。

Abstract: Learning feature interactions is essential for accurate prediction and interpretable modeling, yet a high interaction score may reflect fitting noise rather than a reproducible pattern. Ranking methods score feature pairs but leave the cutoff to the user; testing methods set the cutoff but rely on repeated model refits or a model of the joint feature distribution. We present LENS, a model-agnostic framework for selecting feature interactions through independent replication. LENS trains two models with the same learning algorithm on disjoint data and accesses them only through predictions. For each feature pair, it removes main effects from the joint-effect surface to isolate an interaction pattern, ranks pairs by its strength, and tests whether the second model reproduces it at a meaningful strength. A single multiple-testing correction determines which pairs to retain, using only two model fits regardless of the number of features. We prove that a replication ratio alone can retain arbitrarily weak patterns shared by both fits, whereas a strength floor restores selection consistency, and we characterize when normalization in Friedman's $H^2$ reverses interaction rankings. On 16 synthetic designs, LENS matches leading model-specific ranking methods (e.g., TreeSHAP) and recovers 91% of material interactions, against 45% for the knockoff-based DIAMOND, without selecting false pairs; with 1,225 candidate pairs and no interactions, it makes substantially fewer false discoveries. On large-scale French motor insurance data, it identifies interactions documented in the actuarial literature and recovers 90% of the deviance gain obtained using all pairs. On two credit datasets, LENS selects few pairs while maintaining or slightly improving predictive performance over the additive baseline.

</details>


### [360] [Omni-JEV: Text and Dual-Stream Speech Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13777)
*Shengzhou Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将全双工对话中的"何时响应/保持/打断/恢复"建模为五类交互动作预测任务，提出 Omni-JEV 模型，用对齐的语音-语音理解骨干分别编码用户与助手参考语音的角色信息，并与文本输入共享候选读出；采用双语弱监督微调加 RLCD 强化学习，对比高斯 logit 与分类式 Choice RLCD，音频测试准确率 84.08%，文本 96.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单句用户话语无法唯一决定助手下一步动作，其结果依赖助手的响应状态与交互历史；语音活动、话语完成度与打断意图只是相关证据，需显式的动作策略才能转化为响应决策。因此需要一个统一的动作接口来判断何时响应、保持说话、打断或恢复被打断的响应。

Method: 任务构建于采集片段端点处，输出五类交互动作的概率分布。模型 Omni-JEV 以对齐的 speech-to-speech 理解骨干网络，按角色分别编码用户语音与助手参考语音，并与文本输入共享同一候选读出层。训练流程为：双语弱监督 → 交叉熵微调 → 针对概率敏感决策的 RLCD 强化学习；在相同监督初始化与匹配训练暴露下比较高斯 logit 与分类式 Choice RLCD。

Result: 高斯 Omni-JEV 在测试集上取得音频 84.08%、文本 96.55% 的准确率；两类 RLCD 变体在分类质量与概率质量之间呈现不同权衡。

Conclusion: 将全双工对话决策统一表述为五类交互动作的概率预测是可行的，且语音与文本可共享同一动作读出接口；该接口支持全双工语音助手对响应、保持语音、打断与恢复响应的判断，RL 阶段的选择需在分类准确率与概率校准质量之间权衡。

Abstract: A user utterance does not uniquely determine the next action in full-duplex dialogue: its consequences depend on the assistant's response state and interaction history. Speech activity, utterance completion, and interruption intent provide related evidence, whose implications for a response require an explicit action policy. We formulate this task at collected-segment endpoints and introduce Omni-JEV, which maps text or role-indexed user and assistant-reference speech to probabilities over five interaction actions. An aligned speech-to-speech understanding backbone encodes the acoustic roles separately and shares its candidate readout with text inputs. Bilingual weak supervision supports cross-entropy fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning for probability-sensitive decisions (RLCD). We compare Gaussian-logit and categorical Choice RLCD from the same supervised initialization under matched training exposure, observing different trade-offs between classification and probability quality. Gaussian Omni-JEV achieves test accuracies of 84.08% for audio and 96.55% for text. This common action interface supports decisions about when a full-duplex voice assistant should respond, preserve ongoing speech, interrupt, or resume an interrupted response.

</details>


### [361] [AnchorMTS: Multi-View Signal Representation and Forecast-Anchored Fusion for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13794)
*Gao Wu,Tingting Xu,Changning Wu,Tengfei Long,Yong Liu,Jiabin Jin,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorMTS从每个观测窗口自对齐生成视觉与文本视图，仅用于精化以数值锚点为基础的完整数值预测，在六个基准上取得最优宏观MSE/MAE（0.336/0.367）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序预测器通常只优化单一数值表示，忽略了同一历史序列中蕴含的互补的几何、周期（循环）与语义结构信息。

Method: 构建自对齐的视觉与文本视图；设计可训练的证据路径生成候选预测，并通过无参数的输出投影对其相对数值锚点的偏移施加逐变量的精确界约束，使辅助多视图证据仅用于精化数值预测。

Result: 在6个基准、4个预测跨度上，于7个有监督基线中取得最优宏观MSE/MAE（0.336/0.367），在24个MSE设置中有18个优于cellwise基线包络；去除辅助路径后宏观MSE升至0.340；投影使95.8%的候选精化保持不变且零界违反。

Conclusion: 互补的多视图证据可提升预测性能，同时其最终影响通过无参数投影被显式控制，兼顾了精度与可控性。

Abstract: Long-term time-series forecasters typically optimize a single numerical representation, even though the same history exposes complementary geometry, recurrence, and semantic structure. We propose AnchorMTS, which derives self-aligned visual and textual views from each observed window and uses them only to refine a complete numerical forecast. A trainable evidence path produces a candidate forecast, while a parameter-free output projection enforces an exact per-variable bound on its displacement from the numerical anchor. Across six benchmarks and four horizons, AnchorMTS achieves the best macro MSE/MAE (0.336/0.367) among seven supervised baselines and wins 18 of 24 MSE settings against the cellwise baseline envelope; removing the auxiliary path raises macro MSE to 0.340. The projection leaves 95.8% of candidate refinements unchanged while yielding zero bound violations, showing that complementary multi-view evidence can improve forecasting while its final influence remains explicitly controlled.

</details>


### [362] [JAX-WAVE: A Differentiable Physics-Supervised Framework for Inverse Wave Design in Heterogeneous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13826)
*Honghui Du,Binyao Guo,Yushu Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JAX-WAVE，一种摊销式（amortized）逆波设计方法：用共享权重神经网络将目标终端波场与已知波速图直接映射为有界初始压力，并通过对可微FDTD求解器反向传播物理设计目标进行训练，无需最优控制标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非均匀介质中设计指定波场，需要能补偿介质传播响应的初始压力分布；传统数值反演需对每个目标和环境重复求解，计算代价高、不可复用。

Method: 构建共享神经网络作为摊销式逆映射；训练采用可微时域有限差分（FDTD）求解器，直接优化物理设计目标。利用线性传播与对称压力界推出正则化最优解的精确符号反演关系，并用以共享权重投影强制执行该关系、保持可行性、满足配对符号目标的恒等式；同时刻画离散传播算子及其加权伴随，将学习、数值反演与最优性评估统一起来。

Result: 独立确认集上，三个训练模型对未见材料取得误差25.24%（几何型目标）与4.77%（可达目标）；同容量对比显示符号参数化提升重建效果；在5 ms中位在线预算下，学习映射相较加速数值反演将异构基准平均误差降低45%，而更长数值求解可获得更高精度。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一套可复用的逆设计流程，并表明其精度与计算价值依赖于目标结构与查询负载——摊销学习适合低延迟、批量查询场景，而高精度需求仍可由更长数值求解补充。

Abstract: Designing a prescribed wave field in a heterogeneous medium requires an initial pressure distribution that compensates for the medium's propagation response. Repeated numerical inversion solves this task afresh for each target and environment. We introduce JAX-WAVE for amortized inverse wave design: a shared neural network maps desired terminal fields and known wave-speed maps directly to bounded initial pressures. Training through a differentiable finite-difference time-domain solver uses the physical design objective without requiring optimized control labels. Linear propagation and symmetric pressure bounds imply an exact sign-reversal relation for the regularized optimum. A shared-weight projection enforces this relation, preserves feasibility, and satisfies an exact identity for the paired-sign objective. We characterize the discrete propagator and its weighted adjoint to connect learning, numerical inversion, and optimality assessment. On an independent confirmation set, three trained models achieve unseen-material errors of 25.24% for geometric targets and 4.77% for reachable targets. Matched-capacity comparisons show improved reconstruction with the sign parameterization. Within a 5 ms median online budget, the learned map reduces mean error by 45% relative to accelerated numerical inversion on the heterogeneous benchmark; longer numerical solves recover greater accuracy. The results establish a reusable inverse-design procedure and show how its accuracy and computational value depend on target structure and query workload.

</details>


### [363] [Where to Approximate in Neurosymbolic Inference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13862)
*Samy Badreddine,Emile van Krieken,Luciano Serafini,Antonio Vergari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将现有概率神经符号方法统一为三步自底向上近似编译流水线，并用张量列分解实例化其中两步，显著提升加权模型计数（WMC）估计精度与下游学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确 WMC 是 #P-难的、可扩展性差，现有方法普遍转向近似；但近似预算应如何在不同步骤间分配缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出统一的三步自底向上近似编译流水线框架；受张量网络启发，用张量列（tensor train）分解实现其中两步，并推导出对近似因子做精确相乘、再经 SVD 或插值方案对乘积重新压缩的新流水线。

Result: 在数独、算术推理与场景理解三类共六个任务、且问题规模递增的设置下，WMC 估计精度比以往方法高出数个数量级；在依赖符号因子间紧密交互的任务上带来更强的下游学习效果，而粗糙近似会忽略这些依赖。

Conclusion: 该流水线为困难概率推理任务的有效近似编译开辟了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Probabilistic neurosymbolic methods rely on weighted model counting (WMC) to combine neural predictors with symbolic constraints. Exact WMC is #P-hard and typically scales poorly, so many methods resort to approximations. By unifying existing approaches under a three-step bottom-up approximate compilation pipeline, we study where approximation budget is best allocated. Inspired by tensor networks, we instantiate two steps with tensor train decompositions and derive new pipelines that exactly multiply approximated factors and recompress their products via SVD- or interpolation-based schemes. Across six tasks in Sudoku, arithmetic reasoning, and scene understanding, and across increasing problem sizes, our pipelines produce WMC estimates orders-of-magnitude more accurate than prior methods. This translates into stronger downstream learning on tasks where reasoning relies on tight interactions between symbolic factors, where cruder approximations ignore these dependencies. Our pipelines open novel design space for effective approximate compilation of challenging probabilistic reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [364] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Deep Neural Networks: Methods, Frameworks, and Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13868)
*Lorenzo Cirillo,Irene Amerini,Andrea Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，梳理面向深度神经网络因果推断分析的"机制可解释性"研究进展，核心是识别激活对特定行为具有因果责任的网络组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络广泛应用于图像、音频、语言等信号领域的基础任务，但内部机制难以解释：层间传递的激活是高结构化信号，且单个神经元常编码多重概念，导致概念间存在大量相互依赖关系，需要系统梳理机制可解释性方法以揭示其内部机理。

Method: 采用综述性方法：界定机制可解释性的研究目标——通过定位其激活对特定行为具有因果责任的组件来揭示网络内部机制；并围绕该框架组织、归类深度神经网络因果推断分析方面的既有进展。

Result: 摘要为概述性质，未报告具体实验结果；其成果在于给出机制可解释性在因果推断分析方向上的研究图景与进展归纳。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为理解深度网络内部机制提供了以因果责任为核心的组件级分析框架，但受神经元多义性与概念间相互依赖所限，该领域仍是开放问题，值得持续深入。

Abstract: Deep neural networks underpin fundamental tasks in a wide range of signal domains, such as image, audio, and language processing. However, their internal mechanisms are notably difficult to interpret. Internal activations are structured signals transformed across layers that may encode a multitude of interdependent relationships, as individual neurons frequently represent multiple underlying concepts. Mechanistic interpretability aims to uncover the internal mechanisms of neural networks by identifying components whose activations are causally responsible for specific behaviors. In such a context, this article provides an overview of advances in mechanistic interpretability for the analysis of causal inference in deep neural networks.

</details>


### [365] [Are Causal Neuron Circuits Unique and Transferable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13885)
*Ömer Tekin Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过穷举4096个子集的因果干预，考察"改变模型答案的神经元组"中究竟多少是必要的，发现完整组很少必要：少数神经元即可翻转答案，且常存在多个互不相同的充分子集；组的效应可迁移到同链改写，但在其他链条上明显减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性演示常以"干预某组神经元改变模型回答"作为论证，如移除Qwen3-32B中18个神经元使模型对《狮子王》两跳谜题回答"Simba"而非"Scar"。但这类演示只说明该组"有影响"，未回答该组中多少是必要的、是否存在替代组、效应能否迁移到其他输入，因此需要定量刻画组内必要性与冗余性。

Method: 对每个选定的12个MLP神经元组，穷举其全部4096个子集进行神经元级因果干预。任务包括：(1) Qwen3-0.6B上的两选项首都任务，对比零消融与跨问题激活替换（patching）；(2) 两跳关系链任务，将12个神经元用第一跳问题的激活进行替换，并在Qwen3-1.7B、Llama-3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B上统计答案是否转向桥接实体（Scar→Simba），以随机神经元组为对照。此外分析子集充分性、不相交子集独立翻转答案的现象及其与模型置信度的关系，并检验效应在改写与其他链条上的迁移。作者明确限定为神经元层因果干预，不恢复完整电路连通性。

Result: 首都任务中，零消融12个选定神经元使答案margin下降27%，随机神经元几乎无影响；但用其他问题的激活替换同一批神经元也几乎无变化——其激活跨问题近乎恒定，无证据表明携带问题特异性内容。两跳链中，激活替换使贪心答案以桥接实体起始：Qwen3-1.7B为9/11，Llama-3.2-1B为9/11，Gemma-3-1B为8/10，随机神经元从未出现该现象。完整组很少必要：少数神经元往往即足够，许多链上存在多个互不相交、各自即可单独翻转答案的子集，探索性分析显示这多发生在模型置信度较低时。组效应可迁移到同一链条的改写版本，但在其他测试链条上显著减弱。

Conclusion: "干预某组神经元改变答案"不足以支撑该组是必要机制的结论：组内存在高度冗余，少数神经元或多种不相交子集均可产生相同因果效应，且效应特异于具体链条、跨链迁移有限。评估神经元级可解释性主张应报告子集层面的必要性与充分性而非仅整组效应。

Abstract: Interpretability demonstrations often show that intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer. A recent example removes an 18-neuron group in Qwen3-32B and the model answers "Simba" instead of "Scar" to a two-hop Lion King riddle. Such a demonstration establishes that the group matters, but not how much of it is necessary, whether other groups could do the same, or whether the effect carries over to other inputs. We ask: when intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer, how much of that group is actually necessary? For every selected group we measure all 4,096 subsets of 12 MLP neurons. On a two-alternative capital task (Qwen3-0.6B), zero-ablating 12 selected neurons lowers the answer margin by 27% while random neurons barely change it, yet replacing the same neurons with their values from other questions also barely changes it: their activations are nearly constant across questions, and we find no evidence that they carry question-specific content. On two-hop relation chains, patching 12 neurons with activations from the first-hop question shifts the answer to the bridge entity (from Scar to Simba): the greedy answer starts with the bridge in 9/11 chains for Qwen3-1.7B, 9/11 for Llama-3.2-1B and 8/10 for Gemma-3-1B; random neurons never do. The full group is rarely necessary: a few neurons often suffice, and in many chains disjoint subsets each flip the answer on their own, in an exploratory analysis most often when the model is less confident. Group effects transfer to rephrasings of the same chain but are much weaker on other tested chains. We study neuron-level causal interventions and alternative subsets; we do not recover the full connectivity of circuits.

</details>


### [366] [Conformal Risk Control for QoT-Aware Lightpath Feasibility Under Technology-Driven Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13893)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种后验保形风险控制（CRC）框架，在固定预训练AI/ML回归器的前提下，对不确定带误覆盖率提供有限样本保证，并将其映射为光网络运维决策规则；在SDM QoT数据集的光路可行性评估中，90%目标覆盖率在全部170次试验中均达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI/ML的回归模型虽可支撑光网络运维决策，但通常只输出点预测，缺乏对其可靠性的统计受控信息；尤其在光纤、放大器与交换升级引起域偏移时，预测不确定性无法量化，难以据此做可靠决策。

Method: 构建后验保形风险控制框架：保持预训练回归器不变，用保形方法构建不确定带并给出误覆盖率的有限样本保证；通过策略规则将带宽映射为运维决策。在QoT感知光路可行性评估中实例化：回归器预测OSNR，用特征偏移贡献评分识别跨技术代际关系变化最大的光路特征，并设计两种策略（偏保守拒绝与偏激进接受）以调节权衡；决策错误率通过经验评估。

Result: 在CONUS拓扑的公开SDM QoT数据集上，目标覆盖率90%时，跨17种域偏移与10种校准-测试划分共170次试验中不确定带全部满足目标，而仅用源域分位数回归（QR）基线无一次满足；严重偏移下，效率导向（EO）策略将零样本可行性判定准确率从约85%提升至99%以上，接近迁移学习（TL）策略且无需重训练。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于回归的光网络决策支持提供了不确定带误覆盖率的有限样本控制，并具备推广至QoT感知光路可行性评估之外场景的潜力。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML)-based regression models support operational decision-making in optical networks but typically expose only point predictions, which carry no statistically controlled information about their reliability. This paper proposes a post-hoc conformal risk control (CRC) framework that keeps a pretrained AI/ML regressor fixed, provides a finite-sample guarantee on uncertainty band miscoverage, and maps the band to operational decisions through policy rules. The guarantee applies to interval miscoverage, while decision-error rates are evaluated empirically. We instantiate the framework for quality of transmission (QoT)-aware lightpath (LP) feasibility assessment, in which a regressor predicts optical signal-to-noise ratio (OSNR) under domain shifts caused by fiber, amplifier, and switching upgrades. Feature shift contribution scores identify the LP features whose relation to QoT changes the most across a technology transition, and two policies translate the bands into feasibility decisions, allowing operators to tune the trade-off between conservatively rejecting or aggressively accepting LP realizations. Experiments on open space-division multiplexing (SDM) QoT datasets over the CONUS topology show that, for a target coverage of 90%, the uncertainty bands meet the target in all 170 trials spanning 17 domain shifts and ten calibration-test partitions, whereas a source-only quantile regression (QR) baseline meets it in none. Under severe shifts, the efficiency-oriented (EO) policy empirically improves zero-shot (ZS) feasibility-decision accuracy from about 85% to above 99%, approaching a transfer learning (TL) policy while avoiding retraining. The framework provides finite-sample control of uncertainty band miscoverage for regression-based optical network decision support, with potential applications beyond QoT-aware LP feasibility assessment.

</details>


### [367] [Target Selection with a Disappearing-Stimulus Mixed-Reality P300 Interface: Feasibility and Object-Distinguishable Versus Uniform Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13906)
*Runqi Pan,Takuto Niinuma,Yunshan Huang,Masato Sugino,Kenta Shimba,Kiyoshi Kotani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MR P300接口在提示消失条件下可实现试次级目标选择（18/48，AUC≈0.6），但目标物体身份线索（稳定颜色-形状）相比统一球体无可检测增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合现实P300接口需在用户真实环境中标记可选目标（如轮椅目的地），但闪烁间隙提示消失，用户必须保持一个已不可见的目标意图；同时需检验赋予每个提示稳定颜色-形状身份是否优于视觉上均一的球体。

Method: 六名健康被试在头戴式MR显示上进行5×5行/列任务，每种提示条件下完成4个试次，采集Fz、Cz、Pz脑电；采用收缩线性判别分析（sLDA），并配合嵌套验证（完整试次留出，不参与类别平衡、超参数选择与拟合）。

Result: 两种条件均诱发顶区目标相关正电位；留出试次中解码正确18/48（随机期望1.9，二项检验p<1e-12），其中三名被试单独亦显著；提示形式无差异：完整目标正确率均为9/24（配对差0.0个百分点，95%CI [-37,37]），留出AUC球体0.609、物体线索0.585。

Conclusion: 消失刺激的MR P300选择在严格试次级评估下可行，但物体身份线索的潜在收益即使存在，也小到该规模预实验无法分辨。

Abstract: Mixed-reality (MR) P300 interfaces can register selectable destinations, such as wheelchair goals, directly in the user's surroundings. When the cues disappear between flashes, however, the user must retain an intended target that is no longer visible. We tested whether such an interface supports target selection and whether giving each cue a stable colour-shape identity improves it compared with visually uniform spheres. Six healthy adults completed four trials in each cue condition of a 5x5 row/column task on a head-mounted MR display, with EEG from Fz, Cz, and Pz. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis was evaluated with nested validation in which complete trials were held out from class balancing, hyperparameter selection, and fitting. Both conditions evoked a parietal target-related positivity. The decoded cell was correct in 18 of 48 held-out trials, against 1.9 expected by chance (binomial p < 1e-12), and in three participants individually. Cue format made no detectable difference: complete-target accuracy was 9/24 in both conditions (paired difference 0.0 percentage points, 95% CI [-37, 37]), and held-out AUC was 0.609 for spheres and 0.585 for object cues. Disappearing-stimulus MR P300 selection is therefore feasible under strict trial-level evaluation, but the benefit of object identity, if any, is too small to resolve in a pilot of this size.

</details>


### [368] [Learning from First Discrepancies with Joint Reset and Pilot Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13913)
*Michal Čertík*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究“首次错误即停止”的短运行（仅显示到首次错误判定为止的查询与正确标签）作为学习资源时的统计代价，给出在VC维≤d的二分假设类上的主动学习算法及其紧确的样本/运行/飞行员数量上界，并证明其各阶数在有限类上均为必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动学习假设查询有标签即可获得完整信息；本文考察更受限的反馈：一次运行中只有当飞行员（预先固定的假设）不出现任何不一致时才能看到记录终端信息，一旦出现首个错误决策运行即终止，其后信息全部缺失。作者希望量化“需要多少次这样的停止运行”才能完成学习，从而刻画这类弱反馈模型下的统计资源需求。

Method: 1）模型：每次运行从固定二分类（VC维≤d）中预先选取一个“飞行员”假设，在一段最多D个查询的新记录上测试，查询来自与飞行员无关的同一未知分布；只有飞行员全程无差异时才显示该记录的终端信息；对假设停止的位置附加事先固定的有界损失。2）算法核心：依据停止显示中可计算的“增益”、指数权重法（exponential weights）在假设间更新，结合稀疏的depth-robust图与调和平均（harmonic-mean）界来控制采样与运行分配。3）学习器按显示查询上的标签模式枚举类中模式（可能指数时间），输出类中一个成员及宽度≤ε的风险区间；并处理未知上界（ceiling）与期望代价情形。4）在标准可测性条件下，结论仅关于统计资源（运行数、不同飞行员数、查询数），不涉及计算复杂度。

Result: 设类内成员的差异概率下确界≤f、风险下确界≤r，则存在学习器在任意历史上使用 Õ((d+1)[D(f+ε)+r]/ε²) 次运行与 Õ(D(1+f/ε)) 个不同飞行员，输出类中一个假设及宽度≤ε的区间，使其风险与最优风险均以至少1−δ的概率落入该区间。有限类的下界表明：在正维数、小精度与固定置信度下，上述各阶数在最坏情形下（可能在不同实例上）均为必要。

Conclusion: 在“首次错误即停止”的弱反馈模型中，学习仍可在运行数近似 Õ((d+1)[D(f+ε)+r]/ε²)、飞行员数近似 Õ(D(1+f/ε)) 的规模内以高概率得到近似最优风险区间，且这些阶数在有限类上是紧的；所提分析框架（停止显示增益＋指数权重＋depth-robust稀疏图＋调和平均界）为主动学习的统计资源刻画提供了新工具，结果限于统计层面，计算可行性不保证。

Abstract: A run that stops at its first wrong decision shows the queries and their correct labels up to and including that decision, and nothing after it. We study how many such runs are needed for learning. Each run tests a hypothesis, the pilot, chosen beforehand from a fixed binary class of VC dimension at most $d$, on a fresh record of at most $D$ queries drawn from one unknown distribution that does not depend on the pilot.
