The terminal information of a record is shown only if the pilot makes no discrepancy, and a bounded loss, fixed in advance, is attached to the position where a hypothesis stops. If the discrepancy probabilities of members of the class have infimum at most $f$ and their risks have infimum at most $r$, one learner uses $\widetilde O((d+1)[D(f+\varepsilon)+r]/\varepsilon^2)$ runs and $\widetilde O(D(1+f/\varepsilon))$ distinct pilots on every history. It returns a member of the class and an interval of width at most $\varepsilon$ containing, with probability at least $1-δ$, both the output's risk and the optimal risk. We also treat unknown ceilings and expected costs. Lower bounds for finite classes show that each order is necessary in the worst case, possibly on different instances, at positive dimension, small accuracy and fixed confidence. The learner enumerates label patterns of the class on displayed queries, possibly in exponential time; the results, under standard measurability conditions, concern statistical resources only. The proof combines a gain computable from stopped displays, exponential weights, a sparse depth-robust graph and a harmonic-mean bound.

</details>


### [369] [Dual-Core Tensor Wheel Decomposition with Structure-Cached Acceleration for Tensor Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13938)
*Wenchao Xie,Qingsong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双核张量轮分解（DCTW），将高阶中心核分解为两个由共享潜维连接的低阶核，并提出"结构缓存"加速策略，避免大规模矩阵的显式构造与重复张量网络收缩计算，从而提升优化效率并控制峰值内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量轮分解通过外环因子与高阶中心核的交互刻画高维数据的复杂相关性，但更新组件需显式构造和操作大规模矩阵，中心核更新的计算与内存开销尤其高昂，限制了计算效率。

Method: 1) 提出双核张量轮分解（DCTW）：把中心核因式分解为两个较低阶核，二者由共享的潜维（latent bond）连接，各自耦合不同的外环因子组；2) 提出结构缓存加速策略：避免显式大规模矩阵乘法，并缓存张量网络收缩过程中的中间张量，在各块更新之间复用，减少冗余计算；3) 给出复杂度分析。

Result: 复杂度分析与实验表明：与显式更新、隐式更新方案相比，基于结构缓存的策略加速了优化过程，同时抑制了随秩增长带来的峰值内存上升；在彩色视频补全和鲁棒去噪任务上，DCTW 在重建质量、计算效率和可扩展性方面达到或超过代表性张量网络方法。

Conclusion: 将高阶中心核拆分为共享潜维连接的双核，并配合结构缓存的中间张量复用机制，可在不显式构造大规模矩阵的前提下高效优化张量轮结构，在视频补全与鲁棒去噪中兼顾精度、效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Tensor wheel decomposition captures complex correlations in high-dimensional data through interactions between outer-ring factors and a high-order central core. However, updating these components requires the explicit construction and manipulation of large-scale matrices, which limits computational efficiency and incurs particularly high computational and memory costs for the central-core update. To address these limitations, we propose dual-core tensor wheel decomposition (DCTW), which factorizes the central core into two lower-order cores linked by a shared latent bond. Each core is coupled to a different group of outer-ring factors. We further propose a structure-cached acceleration strategy that avoids explicit large-scale matrix multi- plications and caches intermediate tensors generated during tensor network contractions for reuse across block updates. This reuse reduces redundant computation and avoids the explicit construction of large matrices. Complexity analysis and experiments show that the strategy based on structural caching accelerates optimization compared with explicit and implicit up- date schemes while limiting the increase in peak memory as the ranks grow. Experiments on color video completion and robust denoising show that DCTW matches or outperforms representative tensor network methods in reconstruction quality, computational efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [370] [PARDIG: Global Precipitation Estimates Learned from Sparse Satellite Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13941)
*Guy Shalev,Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Andrew El-Kadi,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Samier Merchant,Fred Zyda,Dominic Masters,Ferran Alet,Salva Rühling Cachay,Ilan Price,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PARDIG——基于球面图网格Transformer的AI框架，利用大气状态分析与静止卫星多光谱影像等稠密多模态输入，预测星载雷达稀疏高精度降水，生成0.1°、15分钟、近实时（2016至今）及历史回推至1999年的全球完整降水场，在阈值与区域上均优于IMERG V07、MSWEP V3、ERA5与HRES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度降水估计对地球系统科学与灾害响应至关重要，但观测覆盖不足、测量精度受限；现有产品在技巧、时延、地理覆盖、时间覆盖及时空分辨率之间存在权衡，难以兼顾。

Method: 构建PARDIG人工智能框架，采用球面图网格（spherical graph-mesh）Transformer架构；训练输入为稠密多模态数据（大气状态分析场与多光谱静止卫星影像），监督目标为星载雷达的稀疏高精度降水观测；推理时在全球范围稠密应用模型，空间分辨率0.1°、时间分辨率15分钟。

Result: 在美国、欧洲、日本三个独立雷达网及全球超36,000个地面站的评估中，PARDIG在各类降水阈值与区域上稳定优于IMERG V07（Final与Early）、MSWEP V3、ERA5与HRES，极端强度下改进最大；极端降水事件案例中能准确刻画其空间结构与发生时间；下游应用中，以其为训练目标可提升全球天气模型WeatherNext 3的降水预报技巧。

Conclusion: PARDIG以单一AI框架同时兼顾技巧、时效、覆盖与分辨率，产出近实时（2016年至今）与历史（回溯至1999年）两套全球降水数据集，并以CC BY 4.0协议开放获取，兼具科研与业务应用价值。

Abstract: Estimating precipitation at the global scale is vital for Earth system science and disaster response, yet observational coverage and measurement precision remain constrained. Existing solutions provide options that trade off desirable traits such as skill, latency, geographical coverage, temporal coverage, and spatial and temporal resolution. We present PARDIG, an artificial intelligence framework based on a spherical graph-mesh transformer architecture that produces near-real-time global precipitation estimates. PARDIG is trained on dense multi-modal inputs, including atmospheric state analyses and multi-spectral geostationary satellite imagery, to predict sparse, high-precision precipitation measurements from spaceborne radar. At inference, the trained model is applied densely across the globe at $0.1^\circ$ spatial and 15-minute temporal resolution, producing complete near-real-time precipitation fields from 2016 to the present, alongside a historical variant extending the record back to 1999. Evaluation against three independent radar networks (over the US, Europe, and Japan) and against over 36,000 surface stations worldwide shows that PARDIG consistently outperforms IMERG V07 (Final and Early), MSWEP V3, ERA5, and HRES across precipitation thresholds and regions, with the largest improvements at extreme intensities. Case studies of extreme precipitation events demonstrate that PARDIG successfully captures their spatial structure and timing. The improvements also extend to forecasting: WeatherNext 3, a global weather model, produces more skillful precipitation forecasts when trained to predict PARDIG. Both the historical and near-real-time PARDIG datasets are openly available under a CC BY 4.0 license.

</details>


### [371] [Chem World: A Large-Scale Benchmark and Physics-Aware Framework for Trustworthy Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13945)
*Tianyou Bai,Huan Wang,Mingchen Gao,Tongrui Yang,Fangyue Lin,Pinze Ren,Zhenlin Zhao,Siming Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出大规模分子性质预测基准 Chem World（17个数据集、80万+实例、10类性质、涵盖单分子与多组分体系），并基于此提出物理感知组合建模方法 PACM，实验表明分子语言模型具有强可迁移表征，PACM 在多种化学体系上稳定提升预测性能，并衍生出药物性质预测 Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测基准在数据集、性质类别与评测协议上高度碎片化，缺少统一测试平台，难以系统评估模型的泛化能力；同时多组分（配方）体系的组合建模与物理约束利用不足。

Method: 1) 构建 Chem World 基准：整合17个公开数据集，覆盖10条性质赛道，包含单分子体系与多组分化学体系（如药物溶解度），提供统一评测协议；2) 提出 PACM（Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling）：将分子表征与组成感知建模相结合，并引入领域特定的物理约束；3) 在基准上对分子表征与组合建模进行系统评测，并构建由 PACM 驱动的药物性质预测 Agent。

Result: 实验显示分子语言模型（molecular language models）可提供强且可迁移的分子表征；PACM 在多样化化学体系上持续提升预测性能，优于基线组合建模方式。

Conclusion: Chem World 与 PACM 驱动的药物性质预测 Agent 为可泛化的化学建模提供统一基础，可支撑 AI 辅助的生物医学研究与药物制剂开发。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is fundamental to computational chemistry and drug formulation research, yet existing benchmarks remain fragmented across datasets, properties, and evaluation protocols, limiting systematic evaluation of model generalization. We introduce Chem World, a large-scale benchmark integrating 17 public datasets with over 800,000 molecular and chemical-system instances across 10 property tracks, covering single-molecule and multi-component systems, including drug solubility. Chem World provides a unified testbed for evaluating molecular representations and compositional modeling across diverse chemical properties. Building on the benchmark, we propose PACM (Physics-Aware Compositional Modeling), which combines molecular representations with composition-aware modeling and domain-specific physical constraints. Extensive experiments show that molecular language models provide strong and transferable representations, while PACM consistently improves prediction across diverse chemical systems. Chem World and our PACM-powered drug-property prediction Agent support generalizable chemical modeling and offer a foundation for AI-assisted biomedical research and drug formulation development.

</details>


### [372] [Geometry-Dependent Approximation and Stability Properties of Shallow ReLU$^k$ Networks on Bounded Euclidean Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13952)
*Yuqing Liu,Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究有界欧氏域上隐藏参数几何（用填充距离 h 与对径分离距离 ḫ 刻画）如何影响浅层 ReLU^k 网络的逼近速率、饱和型下界、Gram/刚度矩阵谱、Bernstein 与 Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund 不等式、离散最小二乘恢复及逆逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浅层 ReLU^k 网络的实际表现依赖参数（神经元位置）的几何分布，但既有逼近理论多忽略这一因素，缺少对参数几何如何同时支配逼近精度、表示稳定性与正则性可辨识性的系统刻画。

Method: 以光滑局部化与二进 Fourier 估计为分析工具，用填充距离 h 和对径分离距离 ḫ 量化参数几何，在给定网络空间 L_n^k 上证明逼近上界与饱和型下界，并推导依赖几何的谱估计、范数离散化、正则化离散最小二乘恢复与逆逼近结论。

Result: 逼近上界为 inf‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω')} ≲ h^r‖f‖_{H^r(Ω)}，利用到临界阶 (d+2k+1)/2 的目标正则性，且仅在内部子域 Ω' 上成立；下界为 inf‖f−g_n‖_{L²(Ω)} ≳ ḫ^{(d+2k+1)/2}‖f‖_{L²(Ω')}，对更光滑的固定目标渐近成立且涉及更大区域 Ω。另得几何相关的 Gram 与刚度矩阵谱估计（刻画条件数与系数稳定性）、Bernstein 与 Marcinkiewicz–Zygmund 型不等式、函数值采样下的范数离散化与正则化恢复，以及在系数或分离条件下由逼近速率反推目标正则性。

Conclusion: 参数几何统一支配浅层 ReLU^k 网络的逼近精度、系数表示的稳定性、离散恢复性能，以及收敛速率中所蕴含的正则性信息；临界阶 (d+2k+1)/2 区分了上界可达与下界饱和两种机制。

Abstract: On bounded Euclidean domains, we study how hidden-parameter geometry affects shallow ReLU$^k$ networks through results on approximation rates, saturation-type lower bounds, Gram and stiffness matrix spectra, Bernstein and Marcinkiewicz--Zygmund inequalities, discrete least-squares recovery, and inverse approximation.
  Our analysis uses smooth localization and dyadic Fourier estimates. We quantify parameter geometry by the fill distance $h$ and antipodal separation distance $\underline h$. For the prescribed network space $L_n^k$, representative approximation estimates take the form \begin{equation*} \begin{aligned} \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}&\lesssim h^r\|f\|_{\mathcal H^r(Ω)},\\ \inf_{g_n\in L_n^k}\|f-g_n\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω)}&\gtrsim \underline h^{\frac{d+2k+1}{2}}\|f\|_{\mathcal L^2(Ω')}. \end{aligned} \end{equation*} The upper bound exploits target regularity up to the critical order $\frac{d+2k+1}{2}$, while the lower bound applies asymptotically to fixed targets smoother than this order. The upper estimate holds on an interior subdomain $Ω'$, whereas the lower estimate concerns the larger domain $Ω$.
  We also obtain geometry-dependent spectral estimates that quantify conditioning and coefficient stability. Further results establish norm discretization and regularized recovery from function values, and infer target regularity from approximation rates under coefficient or separation conditions. Altogether, these results describe how parameter geometry governs approximation accuracy, the stability of coefficient representations, discrete recovery, and the regularity information contained in convergence rates.

</details>


### [373] [RB-LDC: Redundancy-Balanced Latent Coding for Noise-Robust Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13955)
*Hyunseok Jeong,Jaeho Jeon,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究编码器与扩散过程之间的可逆线性白化层，通过按解码器敏感度重新分配各方向的log-SNR，最小化解码敏感度加权的LMMSE风险，提出RB-LDC方法并在ImageNet 256×256上验证其从噪声潜变量重建与采样抗扰性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型中解码器对潜变量扰动敏感，而白化后的前向扩散对每个方向以相同SNR加噪，忽略了解码器敏感度的方向差异，导致潜编码在噪声下重建质量次优。

Method: 在编码器与扩散过程之间引入可逆线性层，平移各方向的log-SNR；对任意白化潜分布给出LMMSE恢复风险的精确谱表达式；在能量预算与可逆下界约束下，最小化沿扩散调度积分后的解码敏感度加权风险，得到唯一最优分配——该层对齐解码器的期望拉回度量并均衡各方向的边际风险下降，由确定性谱求解器计算；大预算下分配服从幂律，指数由低SNR尾部损失权重决定（噪声预测1/2，数据预测2/3，截断调度存在交叉）。以基于雅可比的敏感度估计实例化为RB-LDC。

Result: ImageNet 256×256、SD-VAE与VA-VAE上，RB-LDC在全部四项指标、五种编码配置下（15个SNR、3个随机种子平均）优于同等预算的各向同性编码；VA-VAE块局部编码下扰动FID平均降低17.1且保持干净重建；报告了扰动FID变差的中间SNR区间；冻结扩散模型下，采样受扰时12种采样进度×通道SNR组合的FID点估计均更低。

Conclusion: 按解码器敏感度分配各方向SNR的线性可逆层具有闭式最优解与可解释的幂律规律，能有效提升潜表示在噪声下的重建与生成鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models decode approximately correct latents, making decoder sensitivity to perturbations relevant to image quality. After whitening, forward diffusion corrupts every direction at the same signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), despite unequal decoder sensitivities. We study an invertible linear layer between the encoder and diffusion process that shifts each direction's log-SNR. Its linear minimum mean-square-error (LMMSE) recovery risk has an exact spectral expression for any whitened latent distribution. Under an energy budget and a floor ensuring invertibility, minimizing decoder-sensitivity-weighted LMMSE risk integrated over the diffusion schedule yields a unique optimal allocation. The layer aligns with the decoder's expected pullback metric and equalizes marginal risk reduction across directions, computed by a deterministic spectral solver. At large budgets, the allocation follows a power law in sensitivity whose exponent is determined by the low-SNR tail of the loss weighting: one half for noise prediction and two thirds for data prediction under the cosine schedule, with a crossover on truncated schedules. We instantiate this principle as RB-LDC using Jacobian-based sensitivity estimates. On ImageNet at 256 x 256 resolution with SD-VAE and VA-VAE, trained RB-LDC improves reconstruction from noisy latents over budget-matched isotropic coding in all four metrics and five coding configurations, averaged over 15 SNRs and three pipeline seeds. It reduces average perturbed FID by 17.1 for VA-VAE block-local coding while preserving clean reconstruction. We also report intermediate SNRs where perturbed FID worsens. With a frozen diffusion model, RB-LDC yields lower FID point estimates in all 12 tested combinations of sampling progress and channel SNR under corruption during sampling.

</details>


### [374] [Universal Approximation Theorem for Metaplectic Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13991)
*Ahmed Abdeljawad,Malek Yacine Rezoug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了metaplectic神经算子在非空有界Lipschitz区域上的通用逼近性：单隐层、单非局部分支即可在C^s→C^r与H^s→H^r上紧一致逼近连续算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fourier神经算子及其分数傅里叶推广依赖特定乘法子构造，其通用逼近性缺乏对一般辛结构（metaplectic变换）的系统刻画；作者希望建立以任意固定自由辛矩阵为核的多重乘子型神经算子的逼近理论。

Method: 非局部分支取整空间metaplectic乘子（零延拓+限制），对应任意固定自由辛矩阵；激活为光滑、全局Lipschitz且非多项式的函数。证明结合平均神经算子理论与精确的chirp共轭平均恒等式及导数估计，并借助单个标量符号原子与有限个位置特征。

Result: 对任意非负整数s,r及有限通道维数，得到C^s→C^r和H^s→H^r的紧一致逼近；Sobolev情形要求紧输入集额外在L^∞中一致有界。一个隐层、一个非局部分支即足够；符号原子与位置特征由变换和区域预设，与目标算子、紧集和精度无关。结论涵盖Fourier与固定非退化分数傅里叶乘子构造。

Conclusion: metaplectic神经算子构成一类具有通用逼近保证的算子学习架构，将Fourier/分数Fourier神经算子纳入统一框架，且参数化与目标无关，体现出结构预设的逼近效率。

Abstract: We prove universal approximation for metaplectic neural operators on nonempty bounded Lipschitz domains. The nonlocal branches are whole-space metaplectic multipliers with zero extension and restriction, associated with any fixed free symplectic matrix. Assuming a smooth, globally Lipschitz, nonpolynomial activation, we obtain compact-uniform approximation of continuous operators from $C^s$ to $C^r$ and from $H^s$ to $H^r$ in the respective output norms, for arbitrary nonnegative integers $s,r$ and finite input and output channel dimensions. In the Sobolev setting, compact input sets must additionally be uniformly bounded in $L^\infty$. One hidden operator layer with one nonlocal branch suffices. A single scalar symbol atom and finitely many positional features are prescribed by the transform and domain independently of the target operator, compact set and accuracy. The proof combines averaging neural operator theory with an exact chirp-conjugated averaging identity and derivative estimates. The result includes Fourier and fixed nondegenerate fractional Fourier multiplier constructions.

</details>


### [375] [Temporal Behavior Trees for Reinforcement Learning: Specifications as Rewards, Curricula, and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13993)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TBTCL框架，以时序行为树（TBT）作为规范引导强化学习的统一接口，同一棵TBT同时充当任务规范、稠密奖励、课程与策略诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规范引导RL将任务定义、奖励塑形、课程设计与策略诊断割裂为不同数学对象，需额外转换步骤，并丢弃规范中已有的子任务边界、时序约束与定量度量等结构信息。

Method: 改造TBT语法与语义以适配规范引导RL，使其表达力强于LTL且天然层次化；提出从TBT语义自动提取课程DAG、直接推导基于鲁棒性的稠密奖励的算法；利用TBT轨迹分段构建可理解的能力画像，并自动将奖励自适应指向瓶颈子任务。

Result: 在离散与连续域的六个规范上评估TBTCL，验证时序行为树可同时作为规范、奖励、课程与诊断工具。

Conclusion: TBT可作为规范引导RL的统一形式化接口，消除多数学对象间的转换开销并保留规范结构，为规范、奖励、课程与诊断的一体化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from formal specifications often separates task definition, reward shaping, curriculum design, and policy diagnostics into different mathematical objects. This fragmentation requires additional conversion steps and discards structure already present in the specification, such as subtask boundaries, ordering constraints, and quantitative measures. We introduce TBTCL (Temporal Behavior Tree Centric Learning), a framework that uses Temporal Behavior Trees (TBTs) as a unified interface for specification-guided reinforcement learning. A single TBT specifies the task, induces dense robustness-based rewards, defines a curriculum, and supports better understanding of learned policies. By adapting TBT syntax and semantics for specification-guided reinforcement learning, we provide a formalism that is more expressive than popular specification languages such as LTL and inherently hierarchical. We present algorithms to automatically extract a curriculum DAG and derive dense, robustness-based rewards directly from TBT semantics. Furthermore, we utilize TBT trace segmentation to build understandable competence profiles and automatically adapt the reward toward bottlenecks. We evaluate TBTCL on six specifications across discrete and continuous domains and show that Temporal Behavior Trees can serve as a specification, a reward, a curriculum, and a diagnostic.

</details>


### [376] [BioXArena-Pro: a research-grade benchmark for autonomous biomedical machine learning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13994)
*Loka Li,Duzhen Zhang,Xingbo Du,Leonard Song,Kun Zhang,Le Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BioXArena-Pro基准，含8个生物医学领域64项研究级建模任务，多数采用物种/队列等整单位留出以防信息泄露；22种agent配置最高仅55.1/100，表明编码agent作为生物医学模型自主构建者仍远未成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码agent用于生物医学建模的基准规模小、趋于饱和或存在信息泄露风险，缺乏可靠的研究级评测手段，无法真实衡量agent的自主建模能力。

Method: 从已发表研究重建64个研究级建模任务，覆盖8个生物医学领域，多数任务留出完整生物单元（如物种或队列）；横向评测22种agent配置；对每个任务重跑已发表方法作为参照；消融预训练模型（含生物基础模型）的影响；所有任务经多轮AI辅助审计与最终人工复核。

Result: 最佳配置得分55.1/100；脚手架（scaffold）影响几乎与模型本身相当；在已发表流程可直接套用的任务上agent与方法打平，而在重建任务上方法落后8.2分；禁用预训练模型会使最强agent在其依赖任务上下降9分。

Conclusion: 现有编码agent尚不能可靠自主构建生物医学模型，性能受脚手架与预训练模型显著影响；agent的优势体现在对新数据的适应性上，而BioXArena-Pro提供了防泄露、经过严格审计的研究级评测平台。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly proposed as autonomous builders of biomedical models, but existing benchmarks are small, near saturation or open to leaked information. We present BioXArena-Pro, 64 research-level modelling tasks from eight biomedical domains, each rebuilt from a published study; most hold out whole biological units such as species or cohorts. Across 22 agent configurations, the best scores 55.1 of 100, and the scaffold matters almost as much as the model. We re-ran a published method for every task: where the published recipe applies unchanged, the best agent and the methods tie; on rebuilt tasks the methods trail by 8.2 points, suggesting that agents gain by adapting to new data. Pretrained models (including biological foundation models) matter where the strongest agent uses them: forbidding them decreases its score on those tasks by 9 points. Every task was audited in several AI-assisted rounds and a final human review.

</details>


### [377] [Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II: Unlocking the Memory Potential of Pre-trained File Operations for Long-Horizon Tasks via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14009)
*Lirui Luo,Kelong Mao,Xinwei Yang,Rongqing Li,Luyu Chen,Kieran Wong,Yudong Guo,Xinrui Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Gu Simiu,Sulong Xu,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CAMG-II 长程智能体强化学习环境套件与 CAMP-II 异步在线策略蒸馏（OPD）训练方案，让策略通过普通 Bash 与持久工作区以文件形式自主管理记忆，在五类环境中联合训练，性能增益为最强 OPD 基线的 2.97 倍，并泛化至 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-Bench Pro V2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务的交互历史超出模型活跃上下文，智能体需自主决定“写什么、读什么、何时读写”。已有工作用 on-policy distillation 将记忆控制纳入策略，但依赖模型预训练中从未见过的记忆工具与压缩协议，导致师生两侧都受损：教师无法对该陌生接口给出有效指导，学生采样的有效记忆动作过少，蒸馏信号难以生效。

Method: 构建 CAMG-II：覆盖 Shop、Coding、AutoResearch、General、Visual 五类长程智能体 RL 环境，除原生任务接口外统一提供普通 Bash 访问与跨情节持久的工作区，使策略能在上下文替换之间创建、修改、检索和复用文件作为记忆。提出 CAMP-II：在全部五个环境中以全异步 OPD 联合训练单一策略，教师为具备文件操作先验的冻结模型，从而绕开对外部专有记忆工具与压缩协议的依赖。

Result: 在 CAMG-II 上，CAMP-II 取得的性能增益为最强 OPD 基线的 2.97 倍；所学得的文件式记忆行为可迁移至 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与 SWE-Bench Pro V2 两个外部基准，成功率均有提升。

Conclusion: 以“原生文件系统即记忆接口”替代外挂记忆工具，可有效缓解 OPD 中教师指导失效与学生探索不足的双侧退化问题；全异步 OPD 支持跨异构环境联合训练单策略，且学到的记忆行为具备跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks whose interaction histories exceed the model's active context. They must therefore decide what to write down and what to read back, and when to do either. Recent work has begun to use on-policy distillation (OPD) to make memory control part of the policy, relying on memory tools and compaction protocols that lie outside either model's pre-training. However, this weakens the distillation on both sides: the teacher cannot give useful guidance on an interface it has never used, and the student samples too few meaningful memory actions for that guidance to take effect. To study this problem, we introduce Coding Agent Memory Gym II (CAMG-II), a suite of long-horizon agentic-RL environments spanning Shop, Coding, AutoResearch, General, and Visual. Alongside each environment's native task interface, CAMG-II provides ordinary Bash access and an episode-persistent workspace, enabling the policy to create, revise, search, and reuse files as memory across context replacements. We also introduce Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II (CAMP-II), which trains a single policy jointly across all five environments with fully asynchronous OPD to learn this file-based memory behavior directly from a frozen teacher with a prior for file operations. On CAMG-II, CAMP-II obtains 2.97 times the gain of the strongest OPD baseline. The memory behavior CAMP-II learns generalizes to two external benchmarks, Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-Bench Pro V2, with improved success rates.

</details>


### [378] [Margin-Guided Token Credit for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14015)
*Chi Liu,Xin Chen,Yuchuan Weng,Srinivasan Manoharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCPO（Margin-Credit Policy Optimization），利用策略下一token分布的top-2 margin衡量模型"犹豫"程度，将解的优势按token级重新分配且保持均值不变，在无额外评估开销下优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO将可验证奖励等量分配给解答的每个token，忽略了不同token的贡献差异；作者认为应把信用放在模型犹豫（不确定性高）的位置上。

Method: 用top-2 margin M=p1-p2衡量犹豫，通过两个固定阈值将其映射为分数，再把每个解的优势按该分数分散到各token上并保持均值不变；margin直接取自训练器已有的前向传播，无需额外模型评估或批次统计。实验上以LoRA微调MiniCPM5-2B-Base 200步（2,048道DAPO-Math题），在六个竞赛数学基准评测，并用Qwen3-4B-Base复现。

Result: 五个数据种子全部优于未修改的GRPO基线，macro avg@32平均提升6.55分（95% CI [4.1, 9.0]）；Qwen3-4B-Base三个种子全部提升（+4.75）。增益集中于GRPO只能部分解出的题目，并随pass@k的k增大而增加，pass@32处达13.8分。MCPO模型答案长度增加2–3倍，且答案更长的run得分更高，但实验未能分离这两个效应。

Conclusion: 基于margin的token级信用分配是提升可验证奖励强化学习的有效且低开销手段；长度增长与性能提升的因果关系尚待厘清，是后续工作需要解决的混淆因素。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, a language model is rewarded for a correct final answer, and GRPO passes that reward to every token of the solution in equal measure. We instead place the credit where the model hesitated, in a method we call MCPO (Margin-Credit Policy Optimization). We measure hesitation by the top-2 margin $M=p_1-p_2$ of the policy's next-token distribution, map it to a score with two fixed thresholds, and spread each solution's advantage over its tokens so that the mean stays unchanged. The margin is read from a forward pass the trainer already performs, so the method needs no extra model evaluation and no batch statistics. We train MiniCPM5-2B-Base with LoRA for 200 steps on 2,048 DAPO-Math problems and evaluate on six competition-math benchmarks. MCPO beats an unmodified GRPO baseline for all five data seeds, by 6.55 points of macro avg@32 on average (95\% CI $[4.1, 9.0]$), and a Qwen3-4B-Base replication improves for all three seeds ($+4.75$). The gain is largest on problems that GRPO solves only some of the time and grows with $k$ in pass@$k$, reaching 13.8 points at pass@32. MCPO models also write two to three times longer answers, and runs that write longer score higher; our experiments do not separate the two effects.

</details>


### [379] [ScaleBrain: A Scale-Consistent Framework for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14035)
*Minyu Zhou,Yajing Wu,Yongqiang Tang,Wang Jia,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleBrain，一个尺度一致（scale-consistent）的EEG基础模型预训练框架，从模型输入与优化目标两侧显式对齐多尺度表征，在六个公开BCI基准上以仅5.0M参数取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通过固定的定长patch获得多尺度表征，但多尺度构建多在特征层面完成，导致学到的特征与原始EEG信号中尺度特有特性的对应关系是隐含的；此外，当自监督目标与多尺度输入表征不对齐时，部分时间尺度在预训练中受到监督不足。

Method: 1) 多尺度EEG分词（tokenization）：用不同时间跨度、共享时间锚点的分析窗表征同一EEG片段；2) 尺度特定重建（scale-specific reconstruction）自监督目标，与多尺度输入结构对齐，在不同时间分辨率上提供监督；3) 轴向时空注意力（axial spatio-temporal attention），沿空间与时间维度对融合后的多尺度表征进行上下文建模。

Result: 在六个公开BCI基准、对比12个代表性基线的评测中，ScaleBrain在五个数据集上取得最高平均平衡准确率（Balanced Accuracy），在剩余一个上排名第三；模型仅5.0M参数。

Conclusion: 输入端与目标端的尺度一致性设计可有效提升EEG多尺度表征学习，并在参数效率上具有优势（5.0M参数即达到领先性能）。

Abstract: Foundation models pre-trained on large unlabeled electroencephalography (EEG) corpora have emerged as a transformative paradigm for Brain-Computer Interface (BCI) applications. Recent EEG foundation models have shown that multi-scale representations derived from fixed-length patches can improve EEG representation learning. Existing frameworks commonly construct temporal scales through feature-level operations. However, these operations leave the correspondence between learned features and scale-specific characteristics of the original EEG signals implicit. Moreover, when the self-supervised objective is not aligned with the multi-scale input representations, information at some temporal scales may receive insufficient supervision during pre-training. In light of this, we propose ScaleBrain, a scale-consistent pre-training framework that explicitly coordinates multi-scale representations of EEG from both the model input and optimization objective sides. Specifically, ScaleBrain introduces multi-scale EEG tokenization to represent the same EEG segment through analysis windows with different temporal spans and shared temporal anchors, and pairs these representations with scale-specific reconstruction to align with the multi-scale input structure and provide supervision across temporal resolutions during self-supervised learning. We further develop an axial spatio-temporal attention mechanism to contextualize the fused multi-scale representation along spatial and temporal dimensions. Extensive evaluations on six public BCI benchmarks against 12 representative baselines show that ScaleBrain achieves the highest mean Balanced Accuracy on five datasets and ranks third on the remaining one, with only 5.0M parameters, demonstrating strong performance and favorable parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [380] [Structured Confidence Perturbation via Fisher Geometry for Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14046)
*Chenlong Wei,Hanling Wang,Chong Zhang,Qinkai Yu,Fangyu Wu,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出对抗训练中存在“伪攻击”（提升了损失但未真正改变模型置信结构）问题，提出从损失视角与几何视角两条互补路径，直接攻击并防御置信结构形变，从而提升对抗鲁棒性与鲁棒迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练常受“伪攻击”困扰：这类扰动显著抬高损失值，却未能真正改变模型的置信度结构，导致训练信号质量不佳、鲁棒性提升受限。作者因此希望直接针对“置信结构形变”进行攻击与防御。

Method: 两条互补路线：其一，在传统损失最大化框架下引入交叉熵与KL散度的混合目标，使对抗样本同时破坏预测正确性与分布一致性；其二，对对抗训练做几何重构，生成在置信流形上最大化分布距离的扰动。理论分析表明最优扰动方向与Fisher信息矩阵的主特征向量对齐，并提出高效算法以避免高维矩阵运算。

Result: 在多个数据集与多种模型架构上的实验表明，该方法在对抗鲁棒性与鲁棒迁移性上优于现有方法，尤其在高置信度区域内的结构性扰动上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过直接攻击与防御置信结构形变，可有效缓解伪攻击问题；损失混合目标与基于Fisher信息几何的扰动生成相结合，为对抗训练提供了兼顾精度破坏与分布一致性的新范式。

Abstract: Adversarial training is often plagued by "pseudo-attacks", perturbations that significantly increase the loss value but fail to truly alter the model's confidence structure. To address this, we propose two complementary approaches to directly attack and defend against confidence structure deformation. First, building upon the traditional loss maximization perspective, we introduce a hybrid objective that integrates cross-entropy and KL divergence, encouraging adversarial examples to simultaneously disrupt prediction accuracy and distribution consistency. Second, we geometrically reconstruct adversarial training by generating perturbations that maximize the distribution distance on the confidence manifold. Theoretical analysis shows that the optimal perturbation direction aligns with the principal eigenvectors of the Fisher information matrix. We also propose an efficient algorithm to avoid high-dimensional matrix computations. Experiments on multiple datasets and model architectures demonstrate that our method outperforms existing methods in adversarial robustness and robust transferability, particularly excelling in structural perturbations within high-confidence regions.

</details>


### [381] [Generalizing Causal Decision-Making: Markov Equivalence, Transportability and Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14066)
*Min Woo Park,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将结构因果bandit扩展到部分已知因果结构、跨环境信息迁移与可实现的“反事实”动作，给出最小干预集与可能最优性的图准则、基于部分可迁移性的奖励界、以及动作约简算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结构因果bandit假设因果结构完全已知且仅考虑干预动作；现实中因果结构往往只部分已知（如仅知Markov等价类或PAG），并且存在来自其他环境的数据以及无法操纵变量，同时实验能力可能支持反事实型动作。因此需明确因果知识与实验能力如何决定哪些动作值得探索。

Method: (1) 在Markov等价类下给出最小干预集的图准则，针对最大祖先图（MAG）刻画“可能最优的最小干预集”，并设计在部分祖先图（PAG）所表示至少一个图中可能最优的集合搜索算法；(2) 通过部分可迁移性从源分布导出目标奖励界，处理不可操纵变量；利用支配关系进一步给出界用于动作剪枝与受限探索，并在有效先验界下给出regret保证；(3) 将反事实结构因果bandit形式化为受指定实验能力支撑的动作集，给出最小性与可能最优性的图刻画，并据此删除等价或受支配动作而保留最优动作。

Result: 在指定结构因果模型上的实验考察了这些约简与迁移界对学习的影响；理论结果显示所提准则与算法能在保留至少一个最优动作的前提下显著缩小动作空间，且迁移界与支配关系可加速学习并提供regret保证。

Conclusion: 因果知识与实验能力共同决定了哪些动作值得探索；通过图准则、部分可迁移界与支配约简可安全地压缩动作空间并在保留空间内引导学习。

Abstract: Structural causal bandits use causal knowledge to guide exploration when reward mechanisms are unknown. This paper extends the approach to partially known causal structure, information from other environments, and realizable counterfactual actions. First, we develop graphical criteria for minimal intervention sets under Markov equivalence and characterize possibly-optimal minimal intervention sets for maximal ancestral graphs. A search algorithm finds sets that are possibly optimal in at least one graph represented by a partial ancestral graph. Second, we derive target reward bounds from source distributions through partial transportability, accounting for non-manipulable variables. Dominance relations provide further bounds for action pruning and restricted exploration, with a regret guarantee under valid prior bounds. Third, we formulate counterfactual structural causal bandits over actions supported by specified experimental capabilities. Graphical characterizations of minimality and possible optimality yield algorithms that remove equivalent or dominated choices while preserving an optimal action. Experiments with specified structural causal models examine how these reductions and transferred bounds affect learning. Together, these results clarify how causal knowledge and experimental capabilities determine which actions merit exploration and guide learning within the retained action space.

</details>


### [382] [Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic: Adaptive Switching Costs for Robust Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14099)
*Khaled Ammoura,Aya Kayal,Naseem Daher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机会成本选项评论家（OC²）框架，用"当前选项与其最优替代选项之间的正价值差"定义状态依赖的深思成本，使选项持续性取决于放弃当前行为的机会成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRL 中常用深思成本防止选项退化为原子动作，但现有方法依赖固定惩罚或基于时长的自适应，二者都只是选项效用的间接代理，无法反映"何时切换才划算"。

Method: 以活跃选项与最佳替代选项之间的正价值差构造状态依赖深思成本；指出直接学习该成本会退化为零成本解，故引入辅助目标，使学习到的成本与价值差目标对齐。

Result: 在 Four Rooms 与 Pinball 环境（平稳、目标漂移、几何漂移条件）中评估；Pinball 目标漂移任务中 OC² 前十回合成功率达 52%，固定成本基线为 18%。

Conclusion: 基于机会成本的持续性机制可提升非平稳环境下的适应能力。

Abstract: Deliberation costs are commonly used in hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) to prevent options from collapsing to primitive actions. Existing approaches rely on fixed penalties or duration-based adaptations, both of which are indirect proxies for an option's utility. This paper argues that option persistence should instead depend on the opportunity cost of abandoning the current behavior. We propose the Opportunity-Cost Option-Critic ($OC^2$) framework, which defines a state-dependent deliberation cost using the positive value gap between the active option and its best alternative. This encourages persistence when the current option is clearly preferable and allows rapid switching when a better option emerges. We show that directly learning such costs leads to a degenerate zero-cost solution and introduces an auxiliary objective that aligns the learned cost with the value-gap target. The approach is evaluated in Four Rooms and Pinball domains under stationary, goal-shift, and geometry-shift conditions. In the Pinball goal-shift task, the $OC^2$ framework achieves a 52\% first-ten-episode success rate compared with 18\% for a fixed-cost baseline. The results suggest that opportunity-cost-based persistence improves adaptation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [383] [Causal Time Series Modeling of Supraglacial Lake Evolution in Greenland under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15265)
*Emam Hossain,Muhammad Hasan Ferdous,Devon Dunmire,Aneesh Subramanian,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIC-TSC框架，将滞后感知的因果发现（J-PCMCI+）嵌入序列建模，用多模态卫星与再分析数据识别格陵兰冰上湖演化的区域特定与不变预测因子；在1000个湖泊的分布外评估中比相关性基线准确率最高提升12.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空地球观测领域模型多依赖纯相关性特征，在异质域间迁移能力差；因果建模能刻画稳定不变关系、提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性与泛化性，但其潜力在该领域尚未被充分利用。

Method: RIC-TSC为区域信息引导的因果时间序列分类框架，把lag-aware因果发现直接嵌入序列建模。数据包括Sentinel-1微波后向散射、Sentinel-2与Landsat-8光学反射率及CARRA气象变量；用Joint PCMCI+（J-PCMCI+）在全局与分流域两个层级估计因果图，识别格陵兰冰上湖演化的区域特定及不变预测因子及其时滞，再将验证过的预测因子送入轻量级分类器。

Result: 在来自两个对比性消融季（2018–2019）、人工标注的1000个湖泊平衡基准上，因果模型在分布外评估中准确率最高比相关性基线提升12.59%。

Conclusion: 因果发现不仅是特征选择手段，更是构建可泛化、具有机制解释性的动态地表过程模型的途径，可兼顾预测精度与科学可解释性。

Abstract: Causal modeling offers a principled foundation for uncovering stable, invariant relationships in time-series data, thereby improving robustness and generalization under distribution shifts. Yet its potential is underutilized in spatiotemporal Earth observation, where models often depend on purely correlational features that fail to transfer across heterogeneous domains. We propose RIC-TSC, a regionally-informed causal time-series classification framework that embeds lag-aware causal discovery directly into sequence modeling, enabling both predictive accuracy and scientific interpretability. Using multi-modal satellite and reanalysis data-including Sentinel-1 microwave backscatter, Sentinel-2 and Landsat-8 optical reflectance, and CARRA meteorological variables-we leverage Joint PCMCI+ (J-PCMCI+) to identify region-specific and invariant predictors of supraglacial lake evolution in Greenland. Causal graphs are estimated globally and per basin, with validated predictors and their time lags supplied to lightweight classifiers. On a balanced benchmark of 1000 manually labeled lakes from two contrasting melt seasons (2018-2019), causal models achieve up to 12.59% higher accuracy than correlation-based baselines under out-of-distribution evaluation. These results show that causal discovery is not only a means of feature selection but also a pathway to generalizable and mechanistically grounded models of dynamic Earth surface processes.

</details>


### [384] [Hierarchical Graph Representation Learning with Pooling-Induced Substructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14126)
*Luca Sbicego,Xiaowen Dong,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNN（PSA-GNN），在结构式池化中显式保留并处理池化所诱导的子结构，从WL测试角度证明其增强表达力，并引入两个可控层级结构的合成数据集与层级模式普及度指标用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在局部邻域之外推理子结构的层级图级任务对标准GNN构成挑战。图池化通过粗化为层级表示来应对，但现有方法往往不能显式保留池化所诱导的子结构，导致实验表现不稳定；同时缺乏具有显式层级结构的基准，评估困难。

Method: 提出PSA-GNN：显式保留并处理由基于结构的池化方案所揭示的子结构；理论上以Weisfeiler-Lehman测试为框架分析其表达力提升。评估方面，构建两个层级结构可控的合成数据集，并提出一个刻画真实图级基准中层级模式普及度的指标。

Result: 在具有相关层级结构的任务上，PSA-GNN优于参数匹配的池化基线；在结构更简单的基准上保持竞争力。理论分析表明该设计提升了基于池化GNN在WL意义下的表达力。代码与数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 显式保留池化诱导子结构是提升层级图级推理的有效设计，并需配套具有可控层级结构的基准与层级模式度量以进行原理化评估。

Abstract: Hierarchical graph-level tasks that require reasoning over substructures that extend beyond local neighborhoods are posing challenges for standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). While graph pooling aims to address this by coarsening graphs into hierarchical representations, existing methods often fail to explicitly preserve the substructures they induce, leading to inconsistent empirical performance. Moreover, evaluating such approaches remains challenging due to the lack of benchmarks with explicit hierarchical structure. We propose Pooled-Substructure-Aware GNNs, an approach to graph pooling that explicitly preserves and processes substructures uncovered by structure-based pooling schemes. We show that this design enhances the expressive power of pooling-based GNNs in the sense of the Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) test. To enable principled evaluation, we introduce two new synthetic datasets with controllable hierarchical structure and propose a metric to characterize the prevalence of hierarchical patterns in real-world graph-level benchmarks. On tasks with relevant hierarchical structure, our approach outperforms matched pooling baselines, while remaining competitive on structurally simpler benchmarks. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/lsbicego/PSA-GNN/.

</details>


### [385] [Right for the Wrong Reasons? Evaluating Plausibility and Behavioral Change in Explanation-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14134)
*David Dembinsky,Adriano Lucieri,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了不同解释引导学习（EGL）目标在受控捷径数据集上的表现，发现EGL效果高度依赖具体目标：基于原理的数据增强最有效提升鲁棒性，归因约束主要提升解释合理性，但两者都不必然带来更高的扰动保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGL声称能训练出既准确又依赖任务相关证据的模型，但"解释与人类原理更一致"是否意味着模型决策策略真正改变仍不清楚。作者希望在可控条件下厘清解释一致性提升与行为改变之间的关系。

Method: 构建受控合成捷径数据集，引入局部化、背景型与全局型多种捷径线索，模拟真实应用场景；在此基准上比较不同EGL目标（如基于原理的数据增强、归因约束目标）所产生的行为变化与解释层面变化，并从准确率、前景依赖度、重要性质量及扰动保真度等多维度评估。

Result: EGL效果强烈依赖具体目标。基于原理的数据增强在捷径证据可与任务相关前景分离时鲁棒性提升最明显，平均准确率最高提升+4.8个百分点，并增加前景依赖；归因约束目标主要提升解释合理性，平均Importance Mass最高提升+14个百分点，但行为纠正较弱且不稳定，准确率增益至多+1.6个百分点。此外，鲁棒性或合理性的提升都不能可靠地预示更高的扰动保真度。

Conclusion: EGL可以有用，但其成功不能仅凭原理一致性推断，而应沿行为层面与解释层面等互补维度综合评价；作者公开全部实验结果以支持后续分析。

Abstract: Explanation-Guided Learning (EGL) promises models that are not only accurate but also rely on task-relevant evidence. However, whether improved agreement between model explanations and human-provided rationales also implies a changed decision strategy remains unclear. We study this tension under controlled synthetic shortcut introduction using a constructed shortcut dataset. Inspired by real-world applications, our benchmark covers shortcuts of different kinds, including localized, background-based, and global cues. This allows us to evaluate whether different EGL objectives produce behavioral changesand explanatory changes. Across these axes, we find that the effects of EGL are strongly objective-specific. Rationale-based data augmentation provides the clearest robustness gains when shortcut evidence can be separated from task-relevant foreground information, reaching average accuracy improvements of up to +4.8 percentage points (pp) together with increased foreground reliance. Attribution-constraining objectives primarily improve explanation plausibility, increasing Importance Mass by up to +14 pp on average, but provide weaker and less consistent behavioral correction, with average accuracy gains of at most +1.6 pp. Moreover, neither improved robustness nor improved plausibility reliably implies higher perturbation-based fidelity. Overall, our results show that EGL can be useful, but its success cannot be inferred from rationale agreement alone and should instead be evaluated along complementary behavioral and explanation-level axes. We publicly release all experimental results to enable further analysis (https://right-for-wrong-reasons.dfki.de/).

</details>


### [386] [HaiRuo: Forecasting Ocean Eddies as Fields and Objects with a Satellite-Altimetry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14136)
*Xukun Qin,Haochen Sun,Yuyang Tian,Ge Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HaiRuo 是一个同时以"场"和"对象"两种方式预报中尺度涡的模型：输入30天卫星海平面高度图，一次前向推理即输出15天的海平面高度与地转流速场、逐日涡旋掩膜，以及当日所有存活涡旋的轨迹。它由两个分工明确的部分组成——在24年无标签全球高度计数据上预训练的1.05B参数基础模型 HaiRuo-1B，以及拉格朗日对象解码器 XuanJi。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中尺度涡携带海洋大部分动能，并跨海盆输运热量、盐度与营养盐，但其两周后的位置目前仍只能靠海洋模式输出再配合事后追踪启发式来推断，缺乏原生、对象级的涡旋预报能力。

Method: (1) HaiRuo-1B：开放权重的1.05B参数基础模型，在24年全球卫星高度计观测上无标签预训练，提供15天物理场与涡旋掩膜；(2) XuanJi：拉格朗日对象解码器，在每个涡的随动极坐标系中采样演变中的预报场，经三轮前瞻迭代精修15天路径，并配备对象注意力机制。二者联合时采用冻结预训练与联合微调两种设置评估。

Result: 同一解码器下，HaiRuo-1B 带来 +0.14 场技巧与 +1.1 mIoU；15天涡心误差在冻结设置下降低1.2 km（从头训练降低1.35 km）。XuanJi 在 HaiRuo-1B 上再降低2.5 km（联合微调2.9 km），并在六个已发表骨干上通过对象注意力降低2.5–3.3 km。全球预注册评估中，HaiRuo 在全部指标上优于十四种方法：15天涡心误差25.2 km vs. 30.6 km（-18%），且在标注区域之外及 ENSO 两个相位下均保持优势，并复现西向漂移、50–60°S 东向逆转与气旋-反气旋向极偏转。经固定追踪器的十天流程中，其预报场使 WenHai 与 XiHe 的涡心误差下降37–43%（-2.8 km，而二者退化 +4.3–8.7 km），对象轨迹使流水线误差全球下降77–78%。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的涡旋基础模型与显式的拉格朗日对象解码器耦合，可同时提升涡旋场预报与涡旋身份追踪的精度，并在全球范围、跨区域与跨ENSO相位的统一评估下稳定超越现有方法，为涡旋预报提供可直接替代事后追踪启发式的端到端方案。

Abstract: Mesoscale eddies carry most of the ocean's kinetic energy and move heat, salt and nutrients across basins, yet where an eddy will be two weeks ahead is still read off ocean models with post-hoc tracking heuristics. We introduce HaiRuo, which forecasts eddies as fields and as objects at once: from 30 days of satellite sea-level maps, one forward pass returns 15 days of sea level and geostrophic velocity, daily eddy masks and the trajectory of every eddy alive today. HaiRuo pairs two components with a clear division of labour: (1) HaiRuo-1B, an open-weight 1.05-billion-parameter foundation model pretrained without labels on 24 years of global satellite-altimetry observations, which supplies the 15-day physical fields and masks (+0.14 field skill and +1.1 mIoU points under the same decoder) and further lowers 15-day centre error by 1.2 km frozen (1.35 km over training from scratch); and (2) XuanJi, a Lagrangian object decoder that samples the evolving forecast in each eddy's co-moving polar frame and refines the 15-day path across three look-ahead rounds, removing 2.5 km on HaiRuo-1B (2.9 km with joint fine-tuning) and 2.5-3.3 km via its object attention across six published backbones. Under one pre-registered global evaluation over the whole ocean, HaiRuo improves on fourteen methods on every endpoint (25.2 vs. 30.6 km 15-day centre error, -18%), holding its margin outside the labelled regions and through both phases of the El Nino-Southern Oscillation (ENSO), while reproducing westward drift, 50-60 deg S eastward reversal and poleward cyclone-anticyclone deflection. Over ten days through one fixed tracker, HaiRuo's forecast field cuts the eddy-centre error of WenHai and XiHe by 37-43% (-2.8 km vs. day 0 while they degrade +4.3-8.7 km), and its object trajectories cut pipeline error by 77-78% globally. Open-weight checkpoints, code and project page: https://github.com/xkqin/HaiRuo

</details>


### [387] [Deviation-Aware Pretraining for Cardiovascular Disease Detection from Electrocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14141)
*Siyuan Yan,Haibo Zheng,Feng Lu,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督ECG特征提取框架，含偏差定位嵌入（DLE）与关联重建嵌入（ARE）两模块，从近百万条无标注12导联ECG中学习，在多公开数据集上AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1提升12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习ECG模型多依赖有监督、任务特定训练，难以在疾病类型、人群和采集条件间泛化；且标注数据稀缺成本高。

Method: 基于近百万条无标注12导联ECG的自监督学习框架，包含两个模块：偏差定位嵌入（DLE）检测波形级相对于正常模式的偏差；关联重建嵌入（ARE）通过偏差引导的重建建模跨导联关联，从而捕捉多导联共同表达的异常模式，融合细微偏差模式及其跨导联上下文。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，即使不对预训练特征提取器额外微调，AUROC最高提升6.5%、F1最高提升12.8%；嵌入可视化显示学到的特征支持有意义的疾病分组与异常识别。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了对标注数据的依赖，可在多样医疗场景中辅助临床医生，有望促进心血管疾病的早期检测与监测，推动更可及、更公平的心脏健康照护。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) signals are critical for cardiovascular disease (CVD) detection. Most current deep learning models rely on supervised, task-specific training, which limits their ability to generalize across diseases, patient populations, and acquisition settings. This paper presents a self-supervised ECG feature extraction framework that learns from nearly one million unlabeled 12-lead ECG recordings. The framework includes two modules: Deviation Localization Embedding (DLE), which detects waveform-level deviations from normal patterns, and Association Reconstruction Embedding (ARE), which models cross-lead associations through deviation-guided reconstruction to capture abnormal patterns expressed across multiple leads. These modules enable the framework to capture subtle deviation-related patterns and their cross-lead context. The framework achieves up to 6.5% AUROC and 12.8% F1 improvements over prior methods on multiple public datasets, even without additional fine-tuning the pretrained feature extractor. Embedding visualizations show that the learned features support meaningful disease grouping and anomaly identification. The approach reduces the need for labeled data and can support clinicians in diverse healthcare settings. The framework has potential to improve early detection and monitoring of cardiovascular diseases, contributing to more accessible and equitable heart care.

</details>


### [388] [Unipolar Neuron Networks Trained by Recirculation for Reward-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14153)
*Florin Leon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将章鱼等无脊椎动物的单极神经元抽象为被动线段树模型，提出"再循环（recirculation）"纯奖励学习规则与网络，并用任务编码、巩固机制缓解连续学习中的灾难遗忘，在MNIST与Iris序列任务上实现高精度低遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发动机：章鱼及多数无脊椎动物的主导神经细胞为单极神经元，其单一神经突分支成树，同时承载输入与输出突触，既向邻近细胞发送分级局部输出，又通过主突触某位点向远端目标发送全局输出。这种"输入输出共位"的结构与人工神经网络中单向传播的模型差异巨大。作者希望基于该生物学特性构建网络与学习规则，探索在无反向传播、仅有奖励信号条件下可学习的网络，并解决序列学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 1) 神经元建模：每个神经元表示为一棵被动（passive）线段树，各线段激活值通过线性系统稳态精确求解得到。2) 学习规则：提出recirculation规则，纯靠奖励学习；前向传播后，第二次传播将输出所需改变量沿同一连接反向传回，每个突触仅用其自身位置可获的局部量调整权重；由于神经元系统对称，该更新近似于预测奖励误差的梯度。3) 抗遗忘机制：任务编码在每项任务中抑制不同子集的分支；巩固机制降低近期变化较大突触的学习率。4) 评估：在MNIST与Iris任务序列上测试。

Result: 网络在MNIST与Iris任务序列上保持高准确率且遗忘程度低；作为对照，对同一网络采用基于梯度的训练方式时，早期任务几乎被完全遗忘。

Conclusion: 基于单极神经元被动树结构与recirculation奖励学习规则的网络，可在不需要全局反向传播、仅依赖局部信息的条件下有效学习，并通过任务编码与巩固两种机制显著抑制灾难性遗忘，为生物可信的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Unipolar neurons are the predominant type of nerve cell in the octopus and in most other invertebrates. A single neurite (a fiber extending from the cell body) branches into a tree that carries both input and output synapses. The branches send graded local outputs to nearby cells, and one site on the main neurite sends a global output to distant targets. This paper proposes a network of such neurons and a learning rule for it. Each neuron is modeled as a tree of passive segments, whose activations are computed exactly as the steady state of a linear system. The network learns from reward alone, with a rule called recirculation. After each forward pass, a second pass sends requested changes of the outputs back through the same connections. Each synapse then adjusts its weight using only quantities available at its own location. Because the system of each neuron is symmetric, this update closely follows the gradient of the error in the predicted reward. Two mechanisms limit forgetting when tasks are learned in sequence: a task code inhibits a different subset of branches in each task, while consolidation lowers the learning rate of synapses that changed strongly in the recent past. The model is evaluated on a sequence of MNIST and Iris tasks. The network maintains high accuracy with low forgetting, whereas gradient-based training of the same network forgets earlier tasks almost completely.

</details>


### [389] [Tree-Ising: Repairing Language Models with Bit-Level Ising Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14210)
*Hadi Salloum,Ammar Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tree-Ising将LLM权重修复表述为BF16权重上的稀疏离散优化问题，按输出行精确分解为独立QUBO(Ising)问题并用GPU退火/模拟分岔求解，性能超过智能体编辑且成本极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期MIT的weight-space programming让前沿LLM智能体直接读取网络权重并结合前向统计写出修复，但代价极高（158轮交互、8970万token、50次前向请求）。本文旨在用算法化的稀疏离散优化替代昂贵的智能体搜索，实现同等或更优的权重修复。

Method: 1) 用前向统计构造目标函数：削弱循环方向、强化结论方向，同时在有效token上保持该层输出不变；2) 在状态树表示中，将可行编辑限定为符号翻转与指数平移（保持共享组指数与尾数不变），无损BF16存储作为特例；3) 将该目标精确分解为每个输出行独立的QUBO(Ising)子问题；4) 在GPU上用模拟退火或量子启发的模拟分岔求解；5) 另在IBM Heron处理器上用QAOA执行8变量子问题作为概念验证。

Result: 在智能体修改的两层上，Tree-Ising在MATH-500达54.6%，优于智能体编辑的51.7%（同评估协议），基座模型仅7.9%；模拟分岔解为55.1%，统计上无显著差异。两种求解器在全部五个数学基准上均优于智能体编辑，校正多重比较后MATH-500与GSM8K显著。修改权重少13%–17%，BF16位翻转减少2.2–2.3倍；仅需4次前向请求、零LLM token，退火方案GPU耗时约$0.90。在Qwen3.5-2B上（层与剂量仅用基座模型选择），贪婪GSM8K准确率由21.6%升至69.4%。

Conclusion: 将智能体式的weight-space编程转化为可精确分解的稀疏QUBO问题，可在显著更低成本下获得更优且更稀疏的权重修复，并具备向量子硬件迁移的可行性，为无梯度的权重级模型纠错提供了高效范式。

Abstract: Weight-space programming, introduced in recent MIT work by Takehi et al., enables a frontier LLM agent to read a network's weights and write a repair. Their GPT-6 Astra agent corrected the reasoning loops of Qwen3.5-0.8B without gradients, using 158 agent turns, 89.7 million tokens, and 50 forward-pass requests. Our method, Tree-Ising, casts this repair as a sparse discrete optimization problem over the model's own BF16 weights. Forward statistics define an objective that weakens a loop direction, strengthens a concluding direction, and preserves the layer's output on productive tokens. Within a state-tree representation, where lossless BF16 storage acts as a special case, the admissible edits are sign flips and exponent shifts that preserve all shared group exponents and mantissas. We decompose this objective exactly into independent QUBO (Ising) problems, one per output row, which we solve on a GPU using simulated annealing or quantum-inspired simulated bifurcation. On the agent's two layers, Tree-Ising achieves 54.6% on MATH-500, outperforming the agent's edit (51.7%) under the same evaluation protocol, while the base model scores 7.9% as reported. The simulated bifurcation solver yields a statistically indistinguishable repair at 55.1%. Both solvers outperform the agent edit across all five math benchmarks, with statistically significant improvements on MATH-500 and GSM8K after correcting for multiple comparisons. Our approach modifies 13% to 17% fewer weights and reduces BF16 bit flips by a factor of 2.2 to 2.3. It requires only four forward-pass requests and zero LLM tokens, with the annealing recipe costing about $0.90 in GPU time. On Qwen3.5-2B, with layers and dose selected using only the base model, the same recipe increases greedy GSM8K accuracy from 21.6% to 69.4%. As a proof of concept, we execute an eight-variable repair subproblem using QAOA on an IBM Heron processor.

</details>


### [390] [Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class: A Policed Quality-of-Service Mechanism for Agentic AI Traffic, Evaluated from Simulation to Real-Kernel Adversarial Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14211)
*Naveen Babu Narayanan Nair,Sajay Kurup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VATC（可验证智能体流量类别）：用保留DSCP标记声明智能体流量，路由器侧分类器将违背声明的流降级为低于best-effort的类别，并配限速优先队列；在两种仿真器和真实Linux内核上与静态DiffServ及fq_codel对比，验证了限速防伪造与低时延收益，但分类器存在漏检与时延环境下特征失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的软件智能体产生的流量与人类Web流量特征不同，但现有路由器既没有为其预留的服务类别，也无法核验发送方"我是智能体"的声明，导致无法对智能体流量做差异化调度与防伪。

Method: VATC由三部分组成：保留DSCP值声明智能体流量；路由器侧分类器检测与声明矛盾的流并将其降级到best-effort之后的服务等级；限速优先队列。对照方案为静态DiffServ（信任标记的专用类）与fq_codel（逐流公平调度）。评测在两种仿真器与真实Linux内核上完成，并加入伪造洪泛、四特征实时分类器、仿真网络时延以及真实智能体harness实验。

Result: 稳态负载下VATC与DiffServ持平。构造突发场景中（假设即时判定），平均智能体排队时延较DiffServ降低98%，人类时延增加12%，收益主要来自独立类别。内核伪造洪泛下，限速使人类时延比DiffServ低11倍。无验证时DiffServ与紧配置的fq_codel给合法智能体的时延优于VATC；实时四特征分类器将全部20个批量型伪造流降级，智能体时延0.30 ms，但漏掉6个复制智能体模式的伪造流。加入仿真网络时延后，分类器的时序特征失去区分能力。真实harness在拥塞链路上，路由器排队占任务时间13%–63%（依harness与假设模型时延而变）。

Conclusion: VATC在带验证前提下能以极小的人类时延代价换取智能体流量的显著时延收益，并通过限速有效抑制伪造洪泛；但其分类器可被模仿智能体模式绕过，且在广域时延下时序特征失效。作者亦指出流量为合成或沙箱规模，结论外推需谨慎。

Abstract: Software agents built on large language models generate traffic unlike human web traffic, yet routers have no class for it and cannot verify a sender's claim to be an agent. This paper proposes the Verifiable Agentic Traffic Class (VATC), which combines a reserved Differentiated Services Code Point (DSCP) value declaring agent traffic, a router-side classifier that demotes flows contradicting the declaration to a class served after best-effort traffic, and a rate-limited priority queue. VATC was compared with static Differentiated Services (DiffServ), a dedicated class that trusts the marking, and fq_codel, a per-flow fair-queuing scheduler, in two simulators and on a real Linux kernel. VATC matched DiffServ under steady load. In a constructed burst it cut mean agent queuing delay by 98% relative to DiffServ in simulation (assuming an immediate verdict), at a 12% increase in human delay; most of the gain came from the separate class. Under a spoofed flood in the kernel, the rate limit held human latency 11 times below DiffServ's. Without verification, DiffServ and a tightly configured fq_codel gave legitimate agents lower latency than VATC; a live four-feature classifier then demoted all 20 bulk-shaped spoofers and gave agents 0.30 ms, but missed six spoofers that copied the agent pattern. With emulated network delay, a timing feature of the classifier stopped separating agent from human flows. In real agent harnesses on a congested link, router queueing took 13% to 63% of task time, depending on harness and assumed model latency. The traffic was synthetic or sandbox-scale.

</details>


### [391] [SinkState: On-the-Fly SVD of Recurrent States for Efficient Hybrid LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14246)
*Haolin Jiang,Weihang Li,Zhongzhu Zhou,Jinwen Pan,Santosh Pandey,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinkState：利用线性注意力状态的稳定低秩特性（linear attention sink），在推理全流程中以自适应低秩形式存储循环状态，降低显存占用与解码访存开销，在4B–120B混合模型上实现最高2.6×显存压缩与1.58×解码加速且精度无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型用线性注意力将历史压缩为固定大小的循环状态，以兼顾质量与效率，但现有服务系统仍以稠密形式保存这些状态，每步解码都要完整读写，访存开销随batch size增长并成为解码瓶颈。作者发现线性层的Q/K/V集中于稳定的低维子空间（linear attention sink），使每个状态实际为低秩，且秩随状态变化，因此稠密存储存在冗余。

Method: SinkState在推理期间以自适应低秩形式存储每个循环状态：采用带缓冲的更新机制，并将局部SVD限制在顶层子空间内完成以控制开销；其余组件（剩余部分）异步卸载到CPU上整合（consolidation），避免打断GPU解码关键路径。

Result: 在四个含Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet（GDN）层的混合模型上（4B至120B参数），相比Mamba-SSM与FLA，循环状态峰值显存最高降低2.6×（平均1.9×），解码速度最高提升1.58×（平均1.25×），同时保持精度。

Conclusion: 线性注意力的attention sink现象可被系统级利用：将循环状态以自适应低秩形态存储并配合局部SVD与CPU异步整合，能在不损失精度的前提下显著缓解解码阶段的访存瓶颈，为混合模型的高效服务提供可行方案。

Abstract: Hybrid models combine softmax attention with linear attention, which compresses history into fixed-size recurrent states to balance model quality and inference efficiency. However, linear attention has not pushed this compression to its limit: we find that the queries, keys, and values of linear layers concentrate in a stable, low-dimensional subspace, a phenomenon we call the linear attention sink, so each state is effectively low-rank, with a rank that varies across states. Yet existing serving systems keep these redundant states dense, reading and rewriting them in full at every decoding step, so their memory traffic grows with the batch size and dominates the decoding cost. Thus SinkState stores each recurrent state in an adaptive low-rank form throughout inference with buffered updates and a local SVD restricted to the top subspace. SinkState further consolidates the remaining components asynchronously on the CPU. On four hybrid models with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet (GDN) layers, from 4B to 120B parameters, SinkState reduces the peak memory of recurrent states by up to 2.6x (1.9x on average) and speeds up decoding by up to 1.58x (1.25x on average) over Mamba-SSM and FLA, while maintaining accuracy.

</details>


### [392] [Chain of Physics: Supporting Fault Diagnosis with Engineering-Inspired Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14263)
*Wenqi Zhou,Leonardo Baldo,Kai Goebel,Manuel Arias Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain of Physics (CoP)框架，利用由资产专属参考文档自动生成的约束知识图谱(CKG)，将故障模式、物理量变化与监测传感器关联，并通过图路径检索+物理链式推理提升LLM故障诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统故障诊断需要把观测到的物理量变化与故障机理联系起来，而LLM的诊断推理高度依赖资产专属工程知识的可得性及其表示与使用方式。现有直接提示与常规RAG难以有效利用文本中的工程故障传播知识，尤其是故障-传感器关联问题。

Method: 从资产专属参考文档自动构建约束知识图谱(CKG)，存储"故障模式—物理量变化—监测传感器"诊断链；推理时对每个问题检索语义相似的图路径，同时作为信息与上下文示例；从问题中抽取候选故障模式，分别通过独立LLM调用生成由故障到观测的链条；最后由决策模块将各链条与选项比对给出答案。

Result: 在FailureSensorIQ基准上，覆盖四类工业资产与三种LLM进行评估。相对直接提示精度提升7.8–19.6个百分点，相对常规RAG提升11.7–18.2个百分点，且在所有评估模型上均有提升。

Conclusion: 当图检索与故障传播的上下文示例相结合时，结构化工程知识能够有效增强LLM的诊断推理能力，说明知识表示与推理组织方式比单纯检索更为关键。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis in complex systems requires linking observed changes in physical quantities to the failure mechanisms that produce them. Experienced engineers perform this task by combining sensor information with knowledge of system behavior and fault propagation. Large Language Models (LLMs) can support this process, but their diagnostic reasoning depends on the availability of asset-specific engineering knowledge and on how this knowledge is represented and used during inference. This work introduces Chain of Physics (CoP), an LLM-based fault-diagnosis framework built around a Constrained Knowledge Graph (CKG) generated automatically from asset-specific reference documentation. CoP addresses failure-sensor association from textual engineering knowledge. The CKG stores diagnostic chains connecting failure modes, changes in physical quantities, and monitoring sensors. During inference, semantically similar graph paths are retrieved for each question and used as both information and in-context examples for the reasoning process. Candidate failure modes are extracted from the question and analysed independently through dedicated LLM calls, which generate plausible failure-to-observation chains. A final decision module compares these chains with the available answer choices. CoP is evaluated on the FailureSensorIQ benchmark across four industrial asset classes and three LLMs. Compared with direct prompting and conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), CoP improves diagnostic accuracy across all evaluated models, with gains ranging from 7.8 to 19.6 percentage points and from 11.7 to 18.2 percentage points, respectively. The results show that structured engineering knowledge can improve LLM-based diagnostic reasoning when graph retrieval is combined with in-context examples of fault propagation.

</details>


### [393] [Physics-Constrained Autoencoder for Interpretable Health Representation Learning in Turbofan Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14266)
*Leonardo Baldo,Jokin Alcibar Ibarzabal,Daniel Cisneros Acevedo,Kai Goebel,Manuel Arias Chao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理约束自编码器，将系统知识以归纳偏置（可微物理解码器）和损失偏置的形式嵌入，使潜变量具有明确物理意义并可分解到部件级，用于构建可解释的健康指数（HI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自编码器仅以重构误差驱动训练，潜变量缺乏物理含义，导致HI的可解释性与诊断价值受限。

Method: 解码器结构内嵌可微物理方程，以潜变量与工况为输入，结构化地引导每个潜变量对应预设物理量；同时在损失函数中加入物理学习偏置；先做工况修正，再将部件级物理量聚合为系统级HI。

Result: 在N-CMAPSS上重构R²=0.9993、MAE=0.0036，与纯数据驱动模型相当；工况修正后物理量可追踪退化，聚合HI与真值Spearman相关0.9791、RMSE=0.0286、MAE=0.0233；单一模型跨四种退化场景训练，无需诊断标签即可保留区分压气机侧与膨胀侧退化的部件级特征。

Conclusion: 物理约束自编码器在保持重构精度的同时赋予潜变量物理可解释性，支持部件级诊断与跨场景泛化，并可将退化趋势可视化于部件图，贴合工程专家使用习惯。

Abstract: Health Indices (HIs) summarize degradation from operational data and support diagnostic and prognostic tasks in Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Their usefulness depends on capturing degradation and interpreting system condition. Autoencoders learn such representations from unlabelled monitoring data; however, when training is driven only by reconstruction, the physical meaning of latent variables remains undefined, limiting their interpretation and diagnostic value. This work proposes a physics-constrained autoencoder that embeds system knowledge as an inductive bias in the decoder architecture and as a learning bias in the loss function. The decoder contains differentiable physical equations that receive latent variables and operating conditions as inputs, structurally guiding each latent variable to represent a predefined physical quantity and enabling component-level analysis before aggregation into a system-level HI.The methodology is benchmarked on the N-CMAPSS dataset against alternative representations. Reconstruction reaches an $R^2$ of 0.9993 and an MAE of 0.0036, comparable to fully data-driven models. After operating-condition correction, the inferred physical quantities track degradation, and their aggregation produces an HI with a Spearman correlation of 0.9791 with the ground truth, an RMSE of 0.0286, and an MAE of 0.0233. The methodology is further evaluated using a single model trained across four datasets representing distinct degradation scenarios. This model preserves component-level signatures that distinguish compression-side from expansion-side degradation without diagnostic labels.Finally, the proposed framework enables degradation trends to be visualized on component maps, providing a health representation in a form familiar to engine operators and domain experts.

</details>


### [394] [Federated Learning for Cross-Regional Epidemic Forecasting with Asynchronous Outbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14272)
*Ruibin Liu,Francisco Fernandes,Xuanfeng Li,Zige Liu,André Salgado,Chitin Hon,Zifeng Yang,Arlindo Oliveira,Ana Teresa Freitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该概念验证研究构建了基于Flower的联邦学习框架，在广东、澳门、葡萄牙三地异步疫情下训练LSTM进行跨区域COVID-19短期预测，在保持数据本地化的前提下取得了接近集中式训练的精度（nRMSE 0.028，较本地模型提升88.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情在不同区域呈异步暴发，跨区域协同预测本可互惠，但受隐私法规与公共卫生干预异质性的阻碍，数据难以集中共享，亟需在数据主权约束下实现协作建模的方案。

Method: 采用WHO与中国CDC的广东、澳门、葡萄牙三地每日新增确诊病例数据，构建LSTM网络并以联邦学习方式训练，用于短期预测；以RMSE、MAE、sMAPE为指标，与本地化训练和集中式训练进行基准对比；全部实验基于Flower联邦学习框架，各站点超参数一致，并因使用公开聚合病例数数据而可计算集中式参照结果。

Result: 数据集包含2021年11月24日至2023年1月8日共411天、1,233条每日观测。葡萄牙较早出现的Omicron波次所蕴含的传播动力学提升了澳门、广东等后发地区的预测效果。联邦模型归一化RMSE为0.028，较本地模型（0.240）改善88.3%，与集中式模型相当；网络开销主要取决于模型规模与聚合轮数。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在保持数据驻留的同时可实现接近集中式训练的短期预测精度，开销可接受；利用区域间异步疫情或为隐私保护下的协同预测提供可行路径，但仍需前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Objective: Epidemic outbreaks are often asynchronous across regions, yet collaborative forecasting is hampered by privacy laws and heterogeneous public health interventions. We developed and evaluated, as a proof of concept, a federated learning (FL) framework for cross-regional COVID-19 forecasting under asynchronous outbreaks among Guangdong, Macau, and Portugal, while maintaining data sovereignty.
  Methods: Using daily case data from WHO and China CDC for the three regions, whose outbreaks were asynchronous and interventions heterogeneous, we trained Long Short-Term Memory networks via FL for short-term forecasting, benchmarked against localized and centralized training using Root Mean Square Error, Mean Absolute Error, and Symmetric Mean Absolute Percentage Error. All experiments used the Flower FL framework with consistent hyperparameters across sites. Publicly available, aggregated case-count data were used so that the centralized benchmark could be computed as a reference.
  Results: The dataset comprised 1,233 daily observations (411 days) of new confirmed COVID-19 cases from November 24, 2021, to January 8, 2023. The earlier Omicron wave in Portugal supplied transmission dynamics that improved forecasts in the later-onset regions of Macau and Guangdong. The FL model achieved a normalized Root Mean Square Error of 0.028, an 88.3% improvement over the local model (0.240) and comparable to the centralized model, with network overhead mainly determined by model size and aggregation rounds.
  Conclusion: FL achieved short-term forecasting accuracy close to centralized training while maintaining data residency, with acceptable overhead. Leveraging asynchronous outbreaks across regions may offer a practical path to privacy-preserving collaborative forecasting, though prospective validation is required.

</details>


### [395] [MMDM: Measuring Massive Multitask Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14309)
*Junhao Shen,Qing Zong,Yue Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MMDM（Massive Multitask Decision Making）基准，含5,448道高质量题目、覆盖16类任务，评测28个System-1决策模型，揭示其在选项逻辑、约束规划与多选任务上的显著短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: System-1决策模型（单次前向传播直接输出结构化概率决策）已有评测多依赖现有基准，存在训练语料预暴露导致泛化性被掩盖的问题；且真实决策常涉及长上下文、大选项空间与复杂逻辑依赖，现有基准难以同时覆盖这些挑战。

Method: 构建MMDM基准：5,448道题、16个任务类别，混合新构造任务与基于来源的重新组合/改写，覆盖单选、多选、软标签决策；场景包括智能体状态追踪、工具与技能选择、偏好判断、评分量表（rubric）打分；选项空间2–1,024，输入长度达64,817 token；并对28个决策模型进行系统性定量评测。

Result: 模型在常识类任务表现良好，但在相互依赖选项、受约束决策与多选任务上明显退化：Perplexity Decider-V1.2在世界知识难单选准确率78.75%，而选项逻辑仅53.95%、约束规划仅47.95%；所有模型最佳难多选准确率仅36.81%。

Conclusion: 联合推理、约束感知选择与多选完整恢复是决策模型发展的关键方向，MMDM为衡量相关进展提供了测试平台。

Abstract: System-1 Decision Models directly make structured probabilistic decisions over query-specified options given a state in a single forward pass, without autoregressively generating reasoning trajectories or textual responses. Recent evaluations cover diverse tasks but often rely on existing benchmarks, where prior exposure to test material can obscure generalization. Moreover, real-world decision making often involves long contexts, large option spaces, and complex logical dependencies, challenges that existing benchmarks do not adequately capture together. Thus, we introduce MMDM (Massive Multitask Decision Making), a benchmark of 5,448 high-quality questions across 16 task categories, combining newly constructed tasks with source-grounded recompositions and rewrites for single-select, multi-select, and soft-label decision making in fresh contexts. It covers realistic scenarios such as agent state tracking, tool and skill selection, preference judgments, and rubric scoring, with option spaces ranging from 2 to 1,024 and inputs extending to 64,817 tokens. With MMDM, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative evaluation of 28 decision models, showing that many perform well on common knowledge yet struggle with interdependent options, constrained decisions, and multi-select tasks. For example, Perplexity Decider-V1.2 reaches 78.75% Hard Single-select Accuracy on world knowledge, but only 53.95% on option logic and 47.95% on constraint planning, while the best Hard Multi-select Accuracy across all models is 36.81%. These results highlight joint reasoning, constraint-aware selection, and complete multi-select recovery as key priorities for decision model development, with MMDM providing a testbed for measuring this progress.

</details>


### [396] [PSL-RGHA: Parallel Split Learning with Reference Gradient Half-Space Alignment for Mitigating Gradient Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14312)
*Han Li,Zheng Lin,Miao Yang,Zehang Lin,Jing Yang,Zihan Fang,Qiyang Zhang,Zhaolu Kang,Jianmin Li,Lin Li,Mingwei Hong,Shunzhi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PSL-RGHA框架，通过参考梯度半空间对齐机制缓解并行分割学习在异构数据下的训练发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络复杂度上升，使联邦学习在资源受限设备（如端侧图像分类、语音识别等多媒体场景）上部署困难。并行分割学习（PSL）通过逐层划分将大部分计算卸载到服务器，适合资源受限场景；但PSL缺少客户端侧聚合，在数据异构分布下易发生严重训练发散甚至收敛失败。

Method: PSL-RGHA为梯度对齐的PSL框架，含两个新组件：(1) 参考梯度渐进构造（RGPC），评估各客户端梯度的方向一致性，并通过Weiszfeld迭代更新逐步合成稳健的参考梯度，以逼近全局下降方向；(2) 梯度半空间对齐（GHA），将客户端梯度约束在RGPC定义的参考半空间内，以缓解梯度冲突并校正本地更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，PSL-RGHA在多种设置下均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过参考梯度半空间对齐约束客户端梯度方向，可有效抑制PSL在异构数据下的梯度冲突与训练发散，为资源受限多媒体场景下的分割学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: The escalating complexity of deep neural networks hinders the deployment of federated learning (FL) on resource-constrained devices, especially for multimedia applications such as on-device image classification and speech recognition. Parallel split learning (PSL) has emerged as a promising solution by offloading a substantial portion of the computational workload to the server through layer-wise model partitioning, making it particularly suitable for resource-limited visual and speech intelligence scenarios. However, the absence of client-side aggregation renders PSL sus ceptible to severe training divergence and potential convergence failure under heterogeneous data distributions. To address this challenge, we propose PSL-RGHA, a gradient-aligned PSL framework that employs reference gradient half-space alignment mechanism to mitigate training divergence among client devices. PSL-RGHA comprises two novel components: reference gradient progressive construction (RGPC) and gradient half-space alignment (GHA). Specifically, RGPC evaluates the directional consistency of client gradients and progressively synthesizes a robust reference gradient via Weiszfeld updates, effectively approximating the global descent direction. Then, GHA constrains client gradients within the reference half-space defined by RGPC to mitigate gradient conflicts and rectify local updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PSL-RGHA framework outperforms existing baselines in various settings.

</details>


### [397] [Auditing the Judge: Internal Signals for LLM Reasoning Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14323)
*Mayank Gulati,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较"判决token连续分数"与"基于注意力头激活训练的监督正确性探针"两种保障分数，指出AUROC掩盖固定预算下的表现差异，并证明探针在5%复核预算下能多捕获1.3–22.5个百分点的错误，但缺乏跨域泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型系统需通过自我判断决定答案的发布、复核或重算，而干预成本高，因此保障分数必须在实际可用预算内有效排序错误，而非仅追求全排序上的整体表现；AUROC等全局指标无法反映固定预算下的效用。

Method: 在三个开源7–12B模型上，于GSM8K与MATH两个数据集比较两类评分：judge的verdict-token连续得分，以及在同提示注意力头激活上训练的监督正确性探针；用精确恒等式将固定预算错误召回率与残余风险联系，并说明AUROC等价于对所有预算的召回率取平均；另做跨数据集迁移实验。

Result: 在全部6个模型–数据集设置中，5%复核预算下探针比judge多找出1.3–22.5个百分点的错误（中位数7.4）；10%路由预算下，探针引导分配在两个GSM8K实验中均优于等成本随机分配，但仅在judge较弱时才优于judge引导路由；双向跨数据集迁移均使探针AUROC相对目标域训练下降。

Conclusion: 内部信号可改善有监督的域内预算分配，但并非领域通用的置信度估计器；由于数据集、权重精度与生成预算同时变化，跨域下降度量的是联合协议偏移而非单纯数据集效应。

Abstract: Language-model systems use self-judgment to decide which answers to release, review, or recompute. Because intervention is costly, an assurance score must rank errors well at the available budget, not merely over the full ranking. We compare the judge's continuous verdict-token score with a supervised correctness probe trained on attention-head activations from the same prompt. Exact identities link fixed-budget error recall to residual risk and show that AUROC averages recall over all budgets. Across three open 7-12B models on GSM8K and MATH, the probe finds 1.3-22.5 additional percentage points of errors at 5% review in all six model-dataset settings (median 7.4 points). At 10% routing, probe-guided allocation improves accuracy over equal-cost random allocation in both GSM8K experiments, but beats judge-guided routing only for the weaker judge. Cross-dataset transfer lowers probe AUROC for every model in both directions relative to target-domain training. Because dataset, weight precision, and generation budget change together, this measures a joint protocol shift rather than a dataset-only effect. Internal signals can improve supervised, in-domain budget allocation, but are not domain-general confidence estimators.

</details>


### [398] [Forward-Value Discretization: Faster Stochastic Diffusion Sampling Without High-Order Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14328)
*Yuchen Jiao,Na Li,Changxiao Cai,Yuting Wei,Gen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出随机扩散采样器采用高阶离散化仍只能保持一阶收敛，据此提出免训练的“前向值离散化”框架，含 F-DPMSolver 与 F-DDPM 两个变体，在相同 NFE 预算下取得更优的采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机扩散采样器达到与确定性采样器相当质量需显著更多 NFE，而高阶离散对确定性采样的加速效果明确、对随机采样是否有效尚不清楚，作者由此探究随机情形下提高离散阶数的实际收益。

Method: 先建立匹配的上界与最坏情形下界，证明即使 score 精确，随机采样器使用高阶近似仍为一阶收敛；进而提出 forward-value discretization：用由历史预测构造的前视（lookahead）点评估数据/噪声预测模型。确定性分支为基于 DDIM 变体的 F-DPMSolver；随机分支为 F-DDPM，在经后续噪声注入修正的更“干净”前视点处评估模型，并复用上次模型预测与上次注入噪声，每步仅一次模型评估。

Result: 两种变体均具一阶收敛保证且单步单次评估。在 CIFAR-10、ImageNet、FFHQ、LSUN 上，F-DDPM 在相同 NFE 预算下 FID 一致低于所比随机基线；以约 1.5 倍于确定性基线的 NFE 即可达到有竞争力质量，缩小随机与确定性采样的实际效率差距。

Conclusion: 提高离散阶数无法普遍提升随机扩散采样收敛阶，而通过前视值评估（不改训练、不增评估次数）可在低 NFE 预算下改善质量，为弥合随机与确定性采样的效率鸿沟提供简洁途径。

Abstract: Stochastic diffusion samplers often require substantially more neural function evaluations (NFEs) than deterministic samplers to achieve comparable sample quality. Although higher-order discretization accelerates deterministic sampling, its benefits for stochastic sampling remains unclear. We establish matching upper and worst-case lower bounds showing that stochastic samplers with higher-order approximations remain first-order convergent, even with exact score functions. Thus, increasing the approximation order does not generally improve the convergence order. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce forward-value discretization, a training-free framework that evaluates the data/noise prediction model at a lookahead estimate constructed from previous predictions. For deterministic sampling, the resulting Forward DPMSolver (F-DPMSolver), improves sample quality at low NFE budgets through its DDIM-based variant, despite remaining first-order convergent. For stochastic sampling, we develop Forward DDPM (F-DDPM), which evaluates the model at a cleaner lookahead point adjusted for subsequent noise injection. This estimate reuses both the previous model prediction and the previously injected noise. Both variants admit first-order convergence guarantees with one model evaluation per iteration. Across CIFAR-10, ImageNet, FFHQ, and LSUN, F-DDPM consistently achieves lower FID than the evaluated stochastic baselines at matched NFE budgets. With 1.5 times as many NFEs as deterministic baselines, it achieves competitive sample quality, narrowing the practical efficiency gap between stochastic and deterministic sampling. Code is available at https://github.com/Na-Li66/F-DDPM.

</details>


### [399] [Predicting Time-to-conversion for Dementia of Alzheimer's Type using Multi-modal Deep Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2205.01188)
*Ghazal Mirabnahrazam,Da Ma,Cédric Beaulac,Sieun Lee,Karteek Popuri,Hyunwoo Lee,Jiguo Cao,James E Galvin,Lei Wang,Mirza Faisal Beg,the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于ADNI数据库401名受试者的63个特征，采用扩展Cox回归的深度学习生存分析模型预测阿尔茨海默型痴呆（DAT）的转化时间，发现认知/人口学/CSF（CDC）特征贡献最大，遗传特征贡献最小，且仅用CDC特征的预测效果即可媲美更昂贵的多模态组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAT由多种因素共同影响，但各因素对疾病进展的贡献尚不明确；深入剖析这些因素有望更准确地估计不同疾病阶段患者的DAT转化时间，并评估昂贵检测（基因、CSF）与常规临床评估的相对价值。

Method: 从ADNI数据库纳入401名受试者，提取MRI、遗传、CDC（认知测试、人口学、CSF）三类模态共63个特征；构建基于深度学习的生存分析模型（对经典Cox回归的扩展）预测DAT转化时间，并比较不同特征组合的预测性能。

Result: 遗传特征对生存分析贡献最小，CDC特征贡献最大；MRI与遗传特征结合优于任一单模态，但在任意特征组合上加入CDC，其效果仅与单用CDC相当。

Conclusion: 现有临床流程中的认知测试等CDC数据即可取得优于或等同于昂贵遗传或CSF数据的生存分析效果，提示常规临床评估在DAT转化预测中具有较高性价比。

Abstract: Dementia of Alzheimer's Type (DAT) is a complex disorder influenced by numerous factors, but it is unclear how each factor contributes to disease progression. An in-depth examination of these factors may yield an accurate estimate of time-to-conversion to DAT for patients at various disease stages. We used 401 subjects with 63 features from MRI, genetic, and CDC (Cognitive tests, Demographic, and CSF) data modalities in the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) database. We used a deep learning-based survival analysis model that extends the classic Cox regression model to predict time-to-conversion to DAT. Our findings showed that genetic features contributed the least to survival analysis, while CDC features contributed the most. Combining MRI and genetic features improved survival prediction over using either modality alone, but adding CDC to any combination of features only worked as well as using only CDC features. Consequently, our study demonstrated that using the current clinical procedure, which includes gathering cognitive test results, can outperform survival analysis results produced using costly genetic or CSF data.

</details>


### [400] [The Unused Input: The Initial State as a Conditioning Interface for Recurrent TSFMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14357)
*Bernhard Voggenberger,Christian Ganhör,Levente Zólyomi,Sebastian Böck,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环式时间序列基础模型（TSFM）中"初始循环状态"这一被忽略的输入通道，以xLSTM骨干的零样本预测器TiRex为例，冻结骨干、仅训练小型头部将上下文向量写入初始状态，证明初始状态可作为数据集自适应界面与外部信息通道，带来小幅但一致的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环骨干的TSFM预测由"观测上下文窗口"和"初始循环状态"两部分输入决定，但惯例上初始状态未被使用。作者提出疑问：初始状态能否被有效利用？为排除权重适配的干扰，需要一种仅改变状态而不改变骨干参数的设计。

Method: 以TiRex为案例，冻结骨干网络，仅训练一个小型头部，将上下文向量写入mLSTM的初始状态。（1）用输入窗口的谱摘要作为写入信号，学习一次后对所有序列不加修改地复用；（2）对每条序列附带的文本做嵌入，作为外生信息写入状态；（3）对照实验：参数匹配的LoRA适配器与可学习的静态状态；在GiftEval与六个MoTime数据集上评估，并做组成分析（mLSTM块定位、上下文长度敏感性）。

Result: 谱摘要初始状态使GiftEval平均MASE降低0.175%，每个随机种子均有改善，且参数匹配的LoRA与可学习静态状态均无法复现该效果。文本嵌入实验显示状态具有两种作用：作为逐数据集自适应界面，静态状态使MASE较零样本降低0.04–6.96%（相当于全量微调增益的8–70%）；作为外生信息通道，文本条件状态在六个数据集中的四个上再降低0.15–0.41%，与LoRA结合时在其中之一上超过无文本访问的全量微调。效果集中于mLSTM块，且在短上下文时最强。

Conclusion: 初始循环状态是循环式TSFM中一个被忽视但有效的输入通道，兼具数据集自适应与外生信息注入两种功能。尽管结果为初步且提升幅度较小，仍为后续研究指出新方向。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast unseen series without task-specific training. Increasingly they are built on recurrent backbones, whose constant per-step cost makes long-context inference cheap, and which, unlike transformers, carry a state. A recurrent backbone computes its prediction from two inputs: the observed context window and the initial recurrent state. In practice only the first is utilised and the initial state is unused by convention. We ask whether the initial state can be utilised in recurrent TSFMs. Using the xLSTM-based zero-shot forecaster TiRex as a case study, we freeze the backbone and train only a small head that writes a context vector into the initial state, so that any change in accuracy is attributable to the injected state rather than to adapted weights. A spectral summary of the input window, learned once and applied unchanged to every series, lowers mean GiftEval MASE by 0.175%, with every seed improving, which neither a parameter-matched LoRA adapter nor a learnable static state reproduces. An embedding of text attached to each series, evaluated on six MoTime datasets, reveals two roles for the state. As a per-dataset adaptation surface, a static state lowers MASE by 0.04-6.96% of the zero-shot baseline (8-70% of the gain of full fine-tuning). As a channel for exogenous information, the text-conditioned state lowers it by a further 0.15-0.41% of the zero-shot baseline on four of six datasets and, combined with a LoRA adapter, exceeds full fine-tuning, which has no access to the text, on one of them. In TiRex, the effect concentrates in the mLSTM blocks and is strongest at short context. The results are preliminary and the improvements small, but they point to the initial state as a direction for further research.

</details>


### [401] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Offline Reinforcement Learning with Online Action Selection Enabled by Sparse Gaussian-Process Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14374)
*Marco Chan,Christos Evripidou,Liqiu Dong,Benoit Chachuat,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析 HOFLON（离线学习+在线优化）中 AAE 惩罚项的作用，并用稀疏变分高斯过程（sGP）critic 替代树集成 critic 与 AAE，在聚合反应器基准上验证熵正则/不确定性惩罚（μ-κσ）对开车成功率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开车与牌号切换高度依赖人工操作，而基于模型的动态建模工程成本高。HOFLON 的原始实现中，在线目标由树集成 critic 的预测回报与 AAE 对陌生状态-动作对的惩罚组成；作者希望厘清该惩罚项的真实贡献，并考察更简洁、带不确定度量化的 GP critic 能否替代原有结构。
